We share practical lessons on data engineering, debiasing, and architectural choices that shaped our ranking system at scale.

</details>


### [118] [The Price of Precision: A Calibrated Cost Model for Mixed-Precision LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15629)
*Mukund Athmaram,Manoj Rao,Liang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对混合精度LLM服务中速度与精度的权衡，指出现有分配器使用平均位宽或内存预算作为速度代理的缺陷，提出考虑绑定资源（计算、权重加载、KV缓存访问）的成本模型，并用于延迟预测和配置分配。在三种硬件上评估了模型准确性，并与字节感知和敏感度方法比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度分配器使用平均位宽或内存预算作为速度代理，忽略了实际限制执行的资源（计算、权重加载、KV cache访问），导致位宽减少不一定加速服务。

Method: 论文提出一个校准的成本模型，用于预测LLM服务在不同混合精度配置和操作点下的延迟。模型考虑了三种可能的绑定资源：计算、权重加载和KV缓存访问，根据阶段、批大小和上下文长度确定当前限制执行的资源。模型使用针对特定模型、GPU和服务栈校准的常数来估计延迟。在分配器模式下，该方法结合建模的时间成本与测量的每张量精度成本，使用动态规划在给定延迟或精度预算下选择混合精度配置。实验评估了成本模型在三种硬件类别上的预测准确性，并将分配器的配置与字节感知和仅敏感度分配方法进行比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出考虑绑定资源（计算、权重加载、KV cache访问）的成本模型，能够预测不同配置下的服务延迟；将模型时间成本与实测的每张量精度成本结合，使用动态规划在延时或精度预算下选择混合精度配置；在三种硬件类别上评估了成本模型的准确性，并与字节感知和仅精度敏感度分配方法进行比较

Abstract: Choosing a mixed-precision configuration for large language model (LLM) serving trades speed against accuracy. Allocators that use average bit-width or a memory budget as a speed proxy can miss changes in the resource that limits execution. We argue that served speed can be limited by compute, weight loading, or key-value (KV) cache access, depending on the phase, batch size, and context length. A byte-based proxy does not capture all of these costs, so fewer bits do not always mean faster serving. To address this gap, we build a cost model that accounts for the binding resource and use it in two ways. First, as a predictor: given a configuration and operating point, it estimates serving latency using constants calibrated for the model, GPU, and serving stack. We evaluate its accuracy across three hardware classes. Second, as an allocator: we combine modeled time costs with measured per-tensor accuracy costs and use a dynamic program to select a configuration under a latency or accuracy budget. We compare its configurations against byte-aware and sensitivity-only allocation.

</details>


### [119] [The Gradient That Top-k Distillation Never Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15589)
*Rasul Khanbayov,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明 top-k 蒸馏中的重归一化并不能控制尾部概率质量：截断目标的梯度在尾部质量方向上恒为零，全词表梯度含有一个被忽略的尾部加权残差。实验表明无交叉熵时尾部质量远离教师，加入交叉熵后差距缩小约四倍但仍存在，且分布精确恢复要求 k ≥ d+1，而常用缓存大小低于该阈值。对部署蒸馏流水线的工程师有直接警示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: top-k 蒸馏中重归一化的截断目标是否真正补偿了被丢弃的尾部概率质量？其梯度能否控制尾部质量？

Method: 论文分析的是已有蒸馏流水线的目标函数，而非提出新的网络架构。核心流程：教师模型对每个位置输出全词表概率分布 p；蒸馏时选取 top-k 概率，重新归一化为 q；学生模型输出对应位置的概率分布，通过最小化 q 与学生分布之间的散度（如 KL 散度）进行训练。论文从梯度角度证明：重归一化后的目标仅依赖于保留 token 之间的 log-odds，因此沿着改变尾部质量（即被丢弃 token 的总概率）的方向，其梯度严格为零；真实教师上该分量约为全梯度的 2-3×10^-15。全词表梯度可分解为截断梯度加上由尾部质量加权的残差项，训练仅优化截断项，导致尾部质量不受控。若额外加入交叉熵损失（学生分布与真实标签），尾部质量漂移有所缓解（差距缩小约四倍），但仍无法完全消除。论文进一步在七个开放权重教师上实验，发现当保留集大小 k ≥ d+1（d 为隐藏维度）时，保留集可精确恢复教师分布，而常用缓存大小往往低于此阈值。推理或服务阶段使用 top-k 缓存时，该分析说明了缓存大小对分布保真度的影响。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "尾部质量梯度分量", "value": "2-3×10^-15（相对全梯度）", "setting": "真实教师模型，重归一化 top-k 目标的梯度测量"}, {"metric": "尾部质量差距缩小倍数", "value": "约 4 倍", "setting": "加入交叉熵项后，top-k 学生与教师尾部质量差距相比无交叉熵项"}, {"metric": "分布精确恢复的 k 阈值", "value": "k = d+1（d 为隐藏维度）", "setting": "在七个开放权重教师上，保留集恰好恢复教师分布的最小 k"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明重归一化后的 top-k 目标仅依赖于保留 token 间的 log-odds，对控制尾部质量的方向梯度恒为零，并在真实教师上测得该梯度分量约为 2-3×10^-15。；推导全词表梯度等于截断梯度加上由尾部质量加权的残差，指出训练仅作用于第一项。；实验显示无交叉熵项时 top-k 学生的尾部质量远离教师，全词表控制则略微靠近；加入交叉熵后差距缩小约四倍但仍存在。；在七个开放权重教师上发现，保留集从 k=d+1（d 为隐藏维度）起才能精确恢复教师分布，而常用缓存大小通常低于该阈值。；离线实验表明实际影响真实但小于教师-学生对选择的影响。

Abstract: Many deployed distillation pipelines show the student a truncated view of the teacher: keep the top-k probabilities, renormalize, and minimize a divergence on what remains. Renormalization is assumed to account for the missing mass. We prove that it removes the visibility of tail mass, not the freedom to move it. The renormalized objective depends only on log-odds among the retained tokens, so at each position its gradient along the directions that control tail mass is exactly zero; on a real teacher that component measures $2$-$3\times10^{-15}$ of the gradient. The full-vocabulary gradient equals the truncated gradient plus a residual weighted by the tail masses, and training acts only on the first term. Without the cross-entropy term, a top-k student's tail mass drifts away from the teacher's while a full-vocabulary control moves slightly toward it; with it, the gap shrinks about fourfold but persists. On seven open-weight teachers, the retained set determines the teacher's distribution only from $k=d+1$ ($d$ the hidden dimension): recovery is exact from there on all seven, and commonly cached sizes sit below it. In our off-policy runs the practical effect is real but smaller than the choice of teacher-student pair. Renormalization is a normalization, not a safeguard. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [UNISON: A Unified Framework for Learning from Scored Bags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15564)
*Ofek Kirshenboim,Or Shkuri,Shani Finkelshtein,Yoram Louzoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNISON统一多实例学习与推荐系统，将二者视为带分数袋集合学习，通过三阶段框架学习通用物品表示和袋特定偏好，冷启动有闭式解，在多个领域达SOTA，计算高效且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何统一多实例学习与推荐系统，在袋级分数监督下学习物品表示和袋特定偏好，并解决冷启动问题。

Method: UNISON将多实例学习和推荐系统统一为学习带分数袋集合，每个袋包含(item, score)对。框架分三阶段：1) 学习通用物品表示，通过投影函数φ将物品映射到共享嵌入空间；2) 用轻量级袋特定参数(w_j, b_j)对袋内物品偏好模式建模，对袋内物品的表示进行线性评分；3) 从学到的偏好中推断袋级特征（如分类标签）。线性袋特定评分由一阶分析支撑：当物品嵌入对分数足够平滑时，线性函数可近似真实评分，误差由偏好函数的曲率控制。冷启动时，固定通用表示，袋特定参数拟合简化为无岭线性回归，有闭式解；迭代变体有收敛保证。推理时，对新袋使用已学通用物品表示，通过闭式解快速求解袋特定参数，再聚合得到袋级输出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUC", "value": ">0.998", "setting": "合成实验，恢复潜在因子，投影φ无原始评分机制信息"}, {"metric": "性能", "value": "匹配或超过SOTA", "setting": "娱乐（书籍、电影）、免疫学（TCR分类）、微生物组等跨域实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将多实例学习和推荐系统统一为学习带分数袋集合的框架；三阶段分解：通用物品表示、轻量袋特定参数、袋级特征推断；用一阶泰勒分析论证线性袋特定评分的合理性；冷启动拟合简化为无岭回归，有闭式解和迭代收敛保证；合成实验验证袋特定参数可恢复潜在因子

Abstract: We introduce UNISON, a unified framework that bridges multiple instance learning and recommendation systems by treating both as instances of learning from scored bag collections of (item, score) pairs. Unlike traditional approaches that address bag-level classification and personalized recommendation as separate problems, UNISON decomposes learning into three interconnected stages: (1) learning universal item representations, (2) modeling bag-specific preference patterns through lightweight personalized parameters, and (3) inferring bag-level characteristics from learned preferences. We justify the architectural choice of linear bag-specific scoring through a first-order analysis: when item embeddings are sufficiently smooth in the score, a linear function approximates the score up to a Taylor remainder controlled by the curvature of the underlying preference function. For cold-start scenarios, we show that the cold-bag fitting problem reduces to ridge-less linear regression with a closed-form solution, and provide convergence guarantees for the iterative variant. Empirically, UNISON matches or outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse domains: entertainment (books, movies), immunology (T cell receptor (TCR) based classification), and microbiome, offering superior computational efficiency and interpretability. A controlled synthetic experiment further confirms that the bag-specific parameters ($w_j, b_j$) recover the latent factors that drive item scores even when the projection $\phi$ has no access to the original scoring mechanism (AUC > 0.998). UNISON is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [GA-SVD: Generation-aware Singular Value Decomposition for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15398)
*Shengkun Tang,Cong Zeng,Zongfang Liu,Dan Alistarh,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GA-SVD是一种面向大语言模型生成任务的低秩压缩框架，通过生成感知权重白化和进化全局秩分配，在Mistral-7B、Qwen3和Llama-3.3等模型上优于现有SVD方法，并扩展到70B参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SVD的一次性低秩LLM压缩方法主要针对多选题基准设计，在生成任务上性能保持不佳，且秩分配策略通常依赖启发式规则。

Method: GA-SVD是一个一次性低秩压缩框架。输入为预训练LLM的权重矩阵及通过on-policy知识蒸馏获得的梯度信息。首先，Generation-Aware Weight Whitening（GAWW）模块利用这些梯度构造白化变换，使权重在低秩分解前被重新缩放，从而与生成行为对齐，减少对生成任务性能的破坏。随后，框架对每个权重矩阵进行SVD分解，并采用基于进化的全局秩分配方法：在给定全局压缩率约束下，搜索各层的秩分配方案，以最大化压缩后模型在生成任务上的表现。训练目标是最小化压缩模型与原始模型在生成分布上的差异，通过on-policy知识蒸馏实现。推理时，压缩后的低秩模型直接部署，降低显存和计算开销。整体流程为：梯度计算→白化变换→SVD分解→进化秩分配→压缩模型评估与微调。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "持续优于现有SVD基线并达到SoTA", "setting": "Mistral-7B、Qwen3系列、Llama-3.3模型，多选题与生成基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Generation-Aware Weight Whitening（GAWW），利用on-policy知识蒸馏的梯度构建与生成行为对齐的白化变换。；提出基于进化的全局秩分配方法，在给定全局压缩率下搜索有效的逐层秩分配。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generalization across a broad range of reasoning and generation tasks, but their large parameter counts impose substantial memory and computation costs, hindering deployment in practical resource-constrained settings. One-shot low-rank compression is a promising solution since it reduces model size and inference cost effectively. However, existing SVD-based methods primarily target multiple-choice benchmarks and often fail to preserve performance on generation tasks. Moreover, their rank allocation strategies are typically heuristic. We propose GA-SVD, a generation-aware low-rank compression framework for LLMs. First, we introduce Generation-Aware Weight Whitening (GAWW), which leverages gradients from on-policy knowledge distillation to construct whitening transformations aligned with generative behavior. Second, we develop an evolutionary global rank allocation method that searches for effective layer-wise rank allocation under a given global compression ratio. Across extensive experiments on Mistral-7B, Qwen3 series, and Llama-3.3 models, evaluated on both multiple-choice and generative benchmarks, GA-SVD consistently outperforms existing SVD-based baselines and achieves state-of-the-art (SoTA) results. We further demonstrate scalability by applying our method to models with up to 70B parameters.

</details>


### [122] [E8 Lattice Key Quantization for Low-Bit KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15384)
*Sasho Nedelkoski,Alexander Acker,Soeren Becker,Dominik Scheinert,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于 E8 格点的向量量化用于 KV-Cache 的 key 压缩，配合表驱动非对称打分避免反量化开销，在五类 LLM 上以 k2v2 实现约 6 倍 KV 压缩、接近基线的长上下文困惑度，并在 80% 设置中优于标量 k3v2，解码吞吐与标量基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量量化 KV-Cache 方法在低比特下存在质量-保真度权衡：2-bit key 性能不稳定，通常需要 3-bit key 才能维持模型质量，因此需要一种在同等或更低比特下保持质量且不损失解码吞吐的 key 量化方案。

Method: 论文以长上下文 LLM 推理的 KV-Cache 为对象：输入是注意力层缓存的 key 与 value 张量，传统标量量化对每个维度独立取整，忽略了维度间相关性。作者对 key 采用基于 E8 格点的向量量化：将相邻多维 key 向量映射到 E8 格点码本，利用格点结构在多个维度上联合编码，从而在相同 key 载荷率下获得比独立标量量化更高的 SQNR。为了在注意力计算中高效使用量化后的 key，论文设计了表驱动的非对称打分算法：不先显式反量化重建 key，而是通过预计算查表直接计算 query 与量化 key 的近似内积，从而避免反量化开销并保持解码吞吐与标量基线持平。value 侧采用 2-bit 标量量化，整体形成统一的 k2v2 配置，实现约 6 倍 KV 压缩。训练目标与优化方面，论文聚焦推理期压缩，未披露任何训练流程；推理路径为：量化 key/value 存入 KV-Cache，解码时 query 与量化 key 通过查表打分，value 经标量反量化后参与加权求和，最终输出注意力结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "KV 压缩率", "value": "约 6 倍", "setting": "E8 k2v2 配置，对比标量 k3v2 约小 20%"}, {"metric": "长上下文困惑度（改善比例）", "value": "在 80% 的 model–dataset–context 设置中优于标量 k3v2", "setting": "全量困惑度评测，覆盖五种 LLM 家族"}, {"metric": "SQNR", "value": "高于同 key 载荷率下的独立标量量化", "setting": "结构化向量量化 vs 标量量化对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用 E8 格点向量量化（VQ）对 KV-Cache 的 key 进行量化，利用相邻维度相关性获得高于独立标量量化的 SQNR；提出表驱动的非对称打分算法，在注意力计算中无需显式反量化码本，保持与标量基线相当的解码吞吐；实现统一低比特 k2v2（2-bit key + 2-bit value）配置，在五类 LLM 家族上达到约 6 倍 KV 压缩且接近基线的长上下文困惑度

Abstract: Compressing the key-value (KV) cache is essential for serving long-context large language models (LLMs) under memory constraints. While current scalar quantization methods achieve significant compression, they typically face a quality-fidelity trade-off, either suffering from performance instability at 2-bit precision or necessitating 3-bit keys to maintain model quality. In this work, we demonstrate that a uniform low-bit k2v2 configuration (2-bit keys and 2-bit values) can preserve near-baseline quality by using vector quantization (VQ) based on the $E_8$ lattice for KV keys. We show that structured VQ exploits correlations across adjacent dimensions to achieve signal-to-quantization-noise ratios (SQNR) higher than those of independent scalar quantization at the same key payload rate. To ensure practical utility, we adapt a table-based asymmetric scoring algorithm that avoids explicit codebook dequantization during attention computation and preserves decode-throughput parity with scalar baselines. Across five LLM families, our $E_8$ k2v2 approach yields \textasciitilde$6\times$ KV compression, roughly 20\% smaller than scalar k3v2 (3-bit keys and 2-bit values), while retaining near-baseline long-context perplexity. In our full-suite perplexity evaluation, $E_8$ improves over scalar k3v2 in 80\% of model--dataset--context settings. These results indicate that $E_8$ lattice VQ improves quality per byte while keeping throughput at parity, suggesting that structured key quantization is a useful primitive for KV-compression stacks. Overall, our work provides a low-overhead primitive for expanding the quality-memory frontier in long-context LLM inference.

</details>


### [123] [ANTE: Anticipate, Then Execute -- Learning Internal World Models for Coding Agents from Verified Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15368)
*Yi Ge,Kangrui Ruan,Jifan Chen,Manish Prajapat,Christian Bock,Xiaoyang Wang,Rein Houthooft,Aritra Sengupta,Varun Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ANTE 提出一种智能体式强化学习框架，通过内部世界模型在动作前进行预测，并用真实执行结果验证，进而为编码智能体的动作提供逐步骤信用分配与信任/放弃决策；配合 BAPO 和 ANTE-TTO，在 SWE-bench Verified 上将 Qwen3-32B 的 pass@1 从 25.0% 提升至 57.2%，世界模型准确率从 44% 升至 69%，并展现出跨 harness 和领域的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体缺乏前瞻能力，策略不预测动作后果，终端奖励难以将失败归因到早期步骤，导致错误编辑在收到信号前已不可逆地破坏仓库状态。

Method: ANTE 是一个智能体式强化学习框架。在容器化环境中，编码智能体接收工具响应或执行结果作为输入，在每次动作前生成一个内部信念状态，即对动作后果的预测。随后策略执行动作，环境给出真实执行结果，系统将预测与实际结果进行验证比对。Belief-Anchored Policy Optimization (BAPO) 利用这些经过验证的预测，将信用锚定在策略自身的预测上，为每个动作步骤生成逐步骤优势，并据此更新策略，同时支持信任或放弃决策。训练过程中，世界模型的预测准确率逐步提升。在推理阶段，ANTE-TTO 测试时优化使用训练好的预测来在候选动作中进行选择，无需外部评分器，从而实现测试时扩展。整体目标是构建一个知道已经发生什么、当前什么成立以及下一步动作将达成什么的代码世界模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass@1", "value": "从 25.0% 提升至 57.2%", "setting": "Qwen3-32B 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，与未使用信念状态的匹配 GRPO 基线相比高 6.2%"}, {"metric": "世界模型准确率", "value": "从 44% 提升至 69%", "setting": "RL 训练过程中"}, {"metric": "泛化能力", "value": "学到的前瞻能力可跨 harness 和分布外领域迁移", "setting": "未披露具体量化指标"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ANTE 框架：通过真实执行验证策略动作前预测，并将其转化为逐步骤信用与信任/放弃决策。；Belief-Anchored Policy Optimization (BAPO)：将已确定的预测转化为逐步骤优势，把信用锚定在策略自身预测上。；ANTE-TTO 测试时优化：利用训练好的预测在推理时选择候选动作，无需外部评分器。

Abstract: Rapid advances in large language models have made software engineering agents capable of tackling complex coding tasks. Within a containerized environment, a coding agent receives tool responses or execution results, and determines its later actions by those earlier results. However, most existing coding agents never look ahead: their policies do not anticipate the consequences of actions, and the terminal reward barely attributes a failure to certain earlier steps, so an ill-judged edit can corrupt the repository state irreversibly before any signal arrives. Recent code world models aim to address these challenges, but they are either trained and then frozen before reinforcement learning (RL), or reduced to an auxiliary next-token loss on the environment's outputs. We present ANTE (Anticipate, Then Execute), an agentic RL framework that verifies the policy's pre-action predictions by real execution and turns them into per-step credit for its actions and trust-or-abstain decisions. With an internal belief stated before each action, Belief-Anchored Policy Optimization (BAPO) turns the settled predictions into per-step advantages by anchoring credit on the policy's own forecasts. To turn the trained forecasts into better actions at inference, thereby facilitating effective test-time scaling, ANTE-TTO (test-time optimization) selects among candidate actions without an external scorer. ANTE raises Qwen3-32B from 25.0% to 57.2% pass@1 on SWE-bench Verified, 6.2% above a matched GRPO baseline trained without the belief state; its world model's accuracy climbs from 44% to 69% during RL, and the learned foresight capability transfers across harnesses and out-of-distribution domains. In summary, we aim to build a code world model that knows what has happened, what holds now, and what the next action will achieve.

</details>


### [124] [CodeWalk: Generating Coding Benchmarks by Walking a Problem Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15345)
*Jiaxin Fang,Runyuan He,Arnav Gupta,Sahil Kumawat,Huanzhi Mao,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeWalk提出通过组合现有编程问题来近乎零成本地生成新编程基准，无需LLM生成或验证。在四种编程设置中实例化，生成的任务对前沿模型具有挑战性，且能作为训练数据提升Qwen3.5-9B性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量编程问题获取成本高昂，人工整理和合成生成均代价不菲，如何以近乎零成本生成具有挑战性的编程基准和训练数据。

Method: 方法架构为程序化构建：输入为已验证的原子问题集合，通过组合操作生成第N级任务。组合后的任务需满足两个条件：所有原子问题单独均未被模型解决，且组合后的联合参考解通过评估检查。整个过程不依赖语言模型，输出为可用于评测和训练的编程任务。在拒绝采样微调中，利用生成的任务进行训练，提升模型性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "frontier models degradation", "value": "所有前沿模型随组合深度增加性能下降", "setting": "在生成的组合任务上评估"}, {"metric": "Qwen3.5-9B performance improvement", "value": "在每个组合级别上提升", "setting": "拒绝采样微调（rejection-sampling fine-tuning）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将问题生成定义为代码组合：验证过的原子问题组合成第N级任务。；保持条件：仅当每个原子问题均未被解决且联合参考解通过评估检查时才保留该任务。；整个过程程序化，无需LLM生成或验证。；在四种编程场景中实例化：多错误修复、证明补全、复杂度分析和克隆等价。

Abstract: High-quality coding problems are expensive to collect. Benchmarks such as SWE-bench rely on substantial human curation, while synthetic alternatives typically rely on costly model generation and validation. In this work, we show that new coding problems can be created at almost no cost by composing existing ones. We formulate problem generation as code composition: verified atomic problems are combined into level-$N$ tasks, and a task is kept only if every atomic problem remains unsolved and a joint reference solution passes the same check used at evaluation time. The entire task-construction process is programmatic and requires no language-model generation or validation. We instantiate this approach in four coding settings: multi-bug repair, proof completion, complexity analysis, and clone equivalence. The generated tasks are challenging for frontier models, which all degrade as compositional depth increases. The generated tasks are also useful as training data: rejection-sampling fine-tuning on them improves Qwen3.5-9B at every composition level.

</details>


### [125] [ZOIF: Zeroth-Order Influence Function Estimation for LLM Data Selection Using Only Forward Passes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15336)
*Lisong He,Yunxuan Jiang,Haishan Ye,Xiangyu Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZOIF 提出纯前向传播的零阶影响函数估计框架，用于 LLM 数据选择，在显著降低峰值显存的同时保持与基线相当甚至更优的下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影响函数（IF）在十亿级参数 LLM 上难以扩展：Hessian 矩阵不可解，且传统 IF 近似仍需反向传播（BP），导致中间激活巨量显存开销，阻碍规模化数据选择。

Method: ZOIF 将影响函数估计分解为三个无需反向传播的阶段。输入为训练样本与模型参数；第一阶段通过零阶估计（基于前向传播的有限差分/随机扰动）近似平均代理梯度，避免为反向传播保存中间激活。第二阶段把逆 Hessian-向量积（iHVP）重写为一个辅助的零阶优化子问题并求解，从而绕过对十亿级 Hessian 的显式求逆与存储。第三阶段在第二阶段得到的参数更新方向上，沿该方向做前向有限差分，逐样本计算影响分数。整个流程仅使用前向传播，因此峰值显存大幅降低；训练目标即估计每个样本对模型的影响值以用于数据选择，推理/服务流程即为对候选训练样本进行前向影响打分并据此筛选数据子集。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游任务效用（数据子集选择质量）", "value": "与评估基线竞争，部分场景超过基线", "setting": "论文实验中的下游效用评估"}, {"metric": "峰值显存", "value": "显著低于基线", "setting": "与依赖反向传播的 IF 近似方法对比"}, {"metric": "整体计算时间", "value": "与基线相当", "setting": "与依赖反向传播的 IF 近似方法对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出完全前向传播（BP-free）的零阶影响函数估计框架 ZOIF；用零阶估计近似平均代理梯度，避免反传激活存储；将逆 Hessian-向量积重写为辅助零阶优化问题，绕过显式矩阵求逆；沿已计算参数更新方向用前向有限差分推导逐样本影响分数，实现三阶段全前向估计

Abstract: Influence functions enable principled data selection for large language models (e.g., identifying influential training samples for model editing, debugging, or efficient fine-tuning). However, scaling IFs to LLMs is hindered by the intractability of billion-scale Hessian matrices and the prohibitive memory overhead of backpropagation (BP). While many classical IF approximations reduce Hessian complexity, they still rely on BP for gradient computation, requiring massive storage for intermediate activations that impedes practical data selection at scale. To address this, we propose ZOIF, a zeroth-order framework for influence estimation that operates entirely via forward passes, enabling memory-efficient data selection under resource constraints. ZOIF decomposes influence estimation into three BP-free stages: (1) approximating the average proxy gradient via zeroth-order estimation; (2) reformulating and solving the inverse Hessian-vector product as an auxiliary zeroth-order optimization to bypass explicit inversion; and (3) deriving per-sample influence scores via a forward finite difference along the previously computed parameter update direction. Experiments show that ZOIF selects data subsets with downstream utility competitive with, and in some cases exceeding, the evaluated baselines, yet at substantially lower peak memory cost and with comparable overall computation time.

</details>


### [126] [Imperfect Verifiers, Exact Targets: Inference-Time Scaling for Continuous Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15281)
*Kijung Jeon,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CS-VGB 提出一种用于连续生成模型的推理时扩展方法，通过正确配对的去噪与重噪回溯，在验证器不完美时仍能精确采样目标奖励倾斜分布，并在扩散模型与多任务实验中验证了推理预算增加带来的奖励提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中间验证器不完美的情况下，如何通过推理时扩展从目标奖励倾斜分布中精确采样，并保证收敛到期望分布而非被验证器误差扭曲。

Method: CS-VGB 是一种推理时扩展方法，用于从奖励倾斜分布中采样。它以连续生成模型（如扩散模型）为基础：输入为噪声或中间含噪状态，经过去噪转换（denoising）生成更干净的中间状态，随后通过重噪转换（renosing）回到含噪状态，形成可重复的回溯过程。验证器在中间检查点对状态打分，用于引导搜索方向；与既有验证器引导采样不同，CS-VGB 要求去噪与重噪转换正确配对，从而构造出一个马尔可夫链，其平稳分布在干净检查点上恰好等于目标奖励倾斜分布，即使中间验证器不准确。理论分析表明，在给定正则条件下，该方法随验证器误差呈指数收敛到目标分布，且达到干净样本的期望更新次数仅随检查点数量线性增长。推理时，通过增加去噪-重噪-验证的迭代次数（推理预算）来逐步逼近目标分布，最终输出干净样本；框架还实例化了一步随机后验映射，用于扩散模型采样。该架构解决的核心问题是：中间验证器误差会扭曲采样，单纯重复精修无法保证恢复奖励倾斜分布，而 CS-VGB 通过配对转换与回溯搜索消除验证器误差的稳态偏差。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Continuous-State Verifier-Guided Backtracking (CS-VGB)，通过正确配对的去噪与重噪转换进行重复回溯；证明在给定正则条件下，CS-VGB 的干净检查点平稳分布等于目标奖励倾斜分布，即使中间验证器不准确；证明 CS-VGB 随验证器误差仍呈指数收敛到目标，且达到干净样本所需期望更新次数仅随检查点数量线性增长；在扩散模型与一步随机后验映射上实例化框架，并在 ImageNet、CelebA、多机器人规划任务上验证推理预算增加可提升奖励与任务成功率

Abstract: Verifier-guided inference-time scaling uses additional computation to score intermediate states and steer generation toward better outputs. However, imperfect intermediate verifiers can distort sampling, and repeated refinement alone does not ensure recovery of the desired reward-tilted distribution. We introduce Continuous-State Verifier-Guided Backtracking (CS-VGB), an inference-time scaling method for sampling from reward-tilted distributions with continuous generative models through repeated denoising and renoising. With correctly paired denoising and renoising transitions, the stationary distribution of CS-VGB at the clean checkpoint equals the desired reward-tilted target, even when intermediate verifiers are inaccurate. Under stated regularity conditions, CS-VGB converges exponentially to the target despite verifier error, and the expected number of updates needed to reach a clean sample grows only linearly with the number of checkpoints. In analytically tractable settings, we empirically demonstrate that CS-VGB recovers the desired reward-tilted distribution despite verifier error, whereas the compared verifier-based sampling methods fail to recover the target under the same verifier errors. We also instantiate the framework with diffusion models and one-step stochastic posterior maps. Across prompt-guided ImageNet generation, facial-attribute generation on CelebA, and multi-robot planning, recurrent CS-VGB search improves reward and task success as inference budgets grow, with image-generation rewards continuing beyond the observed plateaus of denoising-only baselines.

</details>


### [127] [ObsSpec: Accelerating Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation Speculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15246)
*Kyle Montgomery,Wang Zhang,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObsSpec 提出用小型世界模型在工具执行期间预测工具输出，让策略提前生成，从而加速智能体强化学习的 rollout 收集。预测仅在完全匹配时保留，保证无损。0.6B 世界模型在终端任务上达到 66.4% 精确匹配，速度约快16倍；8B 和 32B 策略分别降低延迟9.3%和14.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体强化学习中，策略生成与工具执行严格串行，大量多轮rollout导致端到端延迟高、吞吐低。

Method: ObsSpec 是一个用于智能体强化学习的观测猜测流水线。输入为当前策略模型的生成上下文以及待执行的工具调用；系统包含一个小型世界模型（0.6B）和一个策略模型（8B 或 32B）。在环境执行工具调用的同时，世界模型根据当前状态预测该工具调用的输出（观测），策略模型基于预测观测提前开始下一轮文本生成。当实际工具执行完成并返回真实观测后，系统进行严格一致性校验：仅当预测观测与实际观测完全一致时，才保留推测生成的轨迹片段；否则丢弃该推测结果并回退到真实观测继续执行。这种无损校验保证了收集到的 rollout 数据与串行执行等价。为了应对策略更新导致预测分布漂移，世界模型与策略协同训练（co-training），随策略学习不断更新世界模型。训练目标是智能体强化学习目标，使用无损推测 rollouts 进行策略优化。推理/执行路径中，工具执行与策略生成重叠，且该架构可与已有的 RL 加速技术（如并行化）叠加使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测与实际观测完全匹配率", "value": "66.4%", "setting": "终端使用任务，0.6B世界模型"}, {"metric": "预测到达速度提升", "value": "约16倍", "setting": "0.6B世界模型对比实际工具执行"}, {"metric": "智能体延迟降低", "value": "9.3%", "setting": "8B策略模型，使用ObsSpec"}, {"metric": "智能体延迟降低", "value": "14.2%", "setting": "32B策略模型，使用ObsSpec"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用小型世界模型在环境执行工具时预测工具输出，使策略能够提前开始下一轮生成。；仅在预测观测与实际观测完全一致时保留推测性生成的工作，保证rollout收集无损。；通过协同训练（co-training）随策略学习持续更新世界模型，保持预测准确性。

Abstract: Tool-using agents alternate between model generation and tool execution, waiting for each tool call to complete before starting the next generation. This sequential execution is costly during agentic reinforcement learning (RL), which requires large batches of multi-turn rollouts. We introduce ObsSpec, an observation speculation pipeline for agentic RL that uses a small world model to predict tool outputs while the environment executes, allowing the policy to begin its next generation early. ObsSpec retains speculative work only when the predicted and actual observations match exactly, making rollout collection lossless, while co-training keeps the world model up to date as the policy learns. On terminal-use tasks, predictions from a 0.6B world model exactly match actual observations in 66.4% of attempts and arrive roughly 16 times faster. Using these predictions, ObsSpec reduces agent latency by 9.3% and 14.2% for 8B and 32B policies. Across controlled experiments, we characterize the regimes in which ObsSpec reduces agent latency under shared policy resources. Finally, because ObsSpec overlaps policy generation with tool execution, it is architecturally compatible with existing techniques for accelerating RL. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [128] [Exploring Jailbreak Vulnerability Across Depth-Based Dynamic Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15207)
*Lass Najmuldeen,Seyedhamidreza Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究深度动态推理（中间层提前退出）对LLM越狱鲁棒性的影响，发现浅层退出更鲁棒、深层退出更易被对抗后缀攻破，且对抗后缀对效用影响跨深度一致，为设计更鲁棒的动态推理方法提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度动态推理（允许从中间层提前退出）在带来推理效率弹性的同时，是否会改变LLM抵御越狱攻击的鲁棒性，以及退出深度与对抗后缀优化难度之间的关系。

Method: 论文围绕深度动态推理展开：模型在每一层（或若干中间层）设置早期退出（early-exit）分支，输入经过Transformer编码后，可在中间层通过退出分支输出预测，从而根据资源预算灵活选择计算深度；浅层退出使用更少的层计算，深层退出使用更多层。作者在不同退出深度上评估LLM对越狱攻击（对抗性提示/对抗后缀）的鲁棒性，并比较攻击成功率、对抗后缀优化后的攻击损失以及任务效用；通过跨退出深度的对比实验，得出浅层退出更鲁棒、深层退出使对抗后缀优化更容易、以及对抗后缀对效用影响跨深度一致的结论。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次系统考察深度动态推理（中间层提前退出）对LLM越狱鲁棒性的影响；发现浅层退出比深层退出对越狱攻击更鲁棒，且深度越深对抗后缀越容易优化（攻击损失更低）；发现对抗后缀对模型效用（utility）的影响在不同退出深度上基本一致

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to be computationally expensive and vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, in which adversarial prompts bypass safety alignment and induce harmful responses. To address this inefficiency, a range of techniques have been proposed to enable efficient LLM inference. Depth-based dynamic inference has demonstrated promising results by enabling outputs to be generated from intermediate layers. It offers the flexibility to adjust model behavior based on the available resource budget. However, how this flexibility affects the robustness of LLMs against jailbreak attacks remains unexplored. In this paper, we address this gap via a comprehensive investigation into the impact of depth-based dynamic inference on the robustness of LLMs against jailbreak attacks. Specifically, we make the following key observations: 1) Shallow exits are consistently more robust to jailbreak attacks than deeper ones; 2) Exit depth affects how easily the adversarial suffix can be optimized, with deeper exits reaching lower attack loss; and 3) Adversarial suffixes affect utility uniformly across exit depths. These insights can help design dynamic inference methodologies that are more robust to jailbreak attacks. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [129] [DeltaNet-PC: Test-Time Memory Without Backpropagation Through Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15185)
*Todd Morrill,Richard Zemel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaNet-PC 将每步记忆更新视为潜变量，从预测编码概率模型推导出无需 BPTT 的测试时序列记忆更新规则，并与 DeltaNet 等线性注意力机制建立联系。实验表明其可扩展、内存占用低，并能以完美召回精度存储单序列、多序列及键值关联记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不需要通过时间反向传播（BPTT）的情况下，让模型在测试时具备对序列信息的快速记忆与检索能力。

Method: DeltaNet-PC 基于预测编码（Predictive Coding, PC）框架：PC 通过局部更新优化一个预测误差能量函数，而非依赖全局的 BPTT。论文将每个时间步的记忆状态更新 ΔS_t 重新解释为潜变量（latent variable），而不是需要缓慢学习的权重参数。具体地，对标准 PC 概率模型取对数概率，推导出一种与 PC 原生的逐字（verbatim）序列记忆更新规则。该更新规则在形式上与线性注意力文献（例如 DeltaNet）中常用的选择相关联，从而可以把 DeltaNet 类的线性注意力机制与 PC 的局部更新思想结合。模型在推理时逐步执行该局部记忆更新，实现测试时的序列记忆与检索；训练过程中不进行 BPTT，因此显著降低内存需求。论文通过一系列经验测试考察该系统的性质，包括容量随规模的扩展、内存占用、以及从证据中推断上下文（例如 bank 表示河岸还是银行）的能力，并展示其可用于存储单条序列、多条序列以及键值关联记忆对，且在这些任务上达到完美召回。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "召回准确率", "value": "perfect recall accuracy", "setting": "单序列、多序列及键值关联记忆对的存储实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每步记忆更新 ΔS_t 视为潜变量而非慢速学习权重，从预测编码（PC）的概率模型对数概率推导出 PC 原生的逐字序列记忆更新规则；将该更新规则与线性注意力（如 DeltaNet）中的常见选择建立联系，实现无 BPTT 的测试时记忆；支持单序列、多序列及键值关联记忆存储，并报告完美召回准确率

Abstract: Test-time memorization of sequential information is a foundational capability for any intelligent system that needs to rapidly store and retrieve data, as demonstrated by the attention mechanism in large language models. However, Transformers and linear attention variants are trained via backpropagation through time (BPTT), which has drawbacks such as its enormous memory requirements. Predictive coding (PC), an algorithm that optimizes a prediction-error energy function with local updates, offers a compelling solution but still lacks test-time memory for sequential data. We develop a solution to this problem by treating the per-step memory update $\Delta S_t$ as a latent variable, as opposed to a slow weight to be learned. By taking the log probability of a standard PC-based probabilistic model, we derive a PC-native solution for verbatim sequence memory, and relate our update rule to common choices in the linear attention literature (e.g., DeltaNet). We present an extensive set of empirical tests to probe our system's properties, including its scaling capacity, memory footprint, and ability to infer a context from evidence (e.g., bank (river) vs. bank (money)). We show how DeltaNet-PC can be used for storing single sequences of data, multiple sequences of data, and key-value associative memory pairs with perfect recall accuracy.

</details>


### [130] [Only What You've Signed up For: Secure Workflows with Constrained Diffusion Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15177)
*Michael Cardei,Anjun Hu,Chenrui Ma,Francesco Pinto,Chawin Sitawarin,Chulin Xie,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoDA，将任务安全策略以约束投影方式嵌入扩散语言模型的去噪过程，在静态与自适应prompt injection攻击下显著降低攻击成功率，且保持任务效用和可接受的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent防御方法依赖检测或推理恶意指令，面对自适应攻击仍脆弱；如何在不依赖注入检测的情况下，于生成过程中强制执行任务相关安全策略。

Method: CoDA框架在扩散语言模型的去噪过程中强制任务相关的安全策略。输入为任务指令、上下文以及安全策略约束；扩散语言模型在每一步去噪时对全序列并行预测token分布；CoDA在该步的token分布上施加投影操作，将其映射到满足策略约束的集合中，从而保证生成过程中序列始终满足策略。该过程贯穿整个迭代去噪步骤，最终输出策略合规的响应。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练目标和更多实现细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Policy-Constrained Diffusion Agents (CoDA)：在扩散语言模型每个去噪步中将token分布投影到策略合规集合，实现全程约束满足；利用dLLM序列级迭代精炼特性实现 broader-scope constraint enforcement，而非仅检测注入；在静态和自适应prompt injection攻击下评估多种SOTA扩散语言模型家族

Abstract: As agentic AI systems take on increasingly complex and consequential tasks, securing them against prompt injection becomes critical. Existing defenses often rely on detecting or reasoning about injected malicious instructions and can therefore remain vulnerable to adaptive attacks that optimize the injection itself. Diffusion language models (dLLMs), an emerging framework for language generation, offer a new opportunity for security enforcement: by iteratively refining their sequence wide predictions, they enable broader-scope constraint enforcement throughout generation. This paper introduces Policy-Constrained Diffusion Agents (CoDA), a framework that enforces task dependent security policies directly within the denoising process of diffusion language models. At each denoising step, CoDA projects token distributions onto a policy-compliant set, maintaining constraint satisfaction throughout generation. We evaluate CoDA across state-of-the-art diffusion language model families under both static and adaptive prompt-injection attacks. Results demonstrate substantial reductions in attack success rate with competitive task utility and practical inference overhead.

</details>


### [131] [Compute-Optimal Scaling Laws for Spiking Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15146)
*Dan Saunders,Benjamin Ren,Catherine He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对脉冲语言模型进行Chinchilla风格缩放研究，得出计算最优参数-数据缩放指数约为0.56，与稠密Transformer的0.50接近；并将与稠密基线的损失差距分解为主干成本0.16 nats与随计算量减小的脉冲成本（0.34至0.23 nats），量化了脉冲稀疏性的精度代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规模化条件下，脉冲语言模型的效率-精度权衡尚未被刻画，缺乏类似Chinchilla的计算最优缩放规律，不清楚脉冲稀疏性对参数与数据分配的影响。

Method: 论文研究SpikeGPT家族模型，采用RWKV作为主干网络，并引入脉冲神经网络机制，使用稀疏二值激活以实现能效语言建模。模型宽度从384扩展到1536，在FineWeb-EDU数据集上，针对计算预算3×10^17至10^19 FLOPs进行IsoFLOP扫描。训练目标为语言建模损失。通过拟合计算最优前沿，得到N_opt与计算量C的缩放关系；同时与非脉冲RWKV和Transformer基线在相同训练设置下对比损失，将损失差距分解为主干成本与脉冲成本两部分。推理时利用稀疏二值激活降低能耗，但论文未披露具体推理硬件、延迟、吞吐或内存数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "计算最优参数-数据缩放指数", "value": "N_opt ∝ C^0.56", "setting": "脉冲语言模型（SpikeGPT家族，RWKV主干），FineWeb-EDU数据集，IsoFLOP扫描，FLOPs从3e17到1e19"}, {"metric": "与稠密Transformer缩放指数的比较", "value": "接近0.50（Hoffmann等人报告值）", "setting": "相同计算预算下参数与数据权衡对比"}, {"metric": "主干成本损失差距", "value": "约0.16 nats（不随计算量变化）", "setting": "与非脉冲RWKV和Transformer基线按相同方式训练对比"}, {"metric": "脉冲成本损失差距", "value": "从0.34降至约0.23 nats后趋于平稳", "setting": "与非脉冲RWKV和Transformer基线按相同方式训练对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次对脉冲语言模型进行Chinchilla风格IsoFLOP缩放研究，基于SpikeGPT家族（RWKV主干）从宽度384到1536、计算预算3e17到1e19 FLOPs进行系统扫描。；发现计算最优前沿为 N_opt ∝ C^0.56，与Hoffmann等人对稠密Transformer报告的0.50接近，表明脉冲机制不改变计算预算下参数与数据的权衡方式。；将损失差距分解为主干成本（约0.16 nats，不随计算量变化）与脉冲成本（从0.34降至约0.23 nats后趋于平稳），量化了脉冲稀疏性的精度代价。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks target energy-efficient language modeling through sparse, binary activations, but the efficiency-accuracy tradeoff of spiking language models is uncharacterized at scale. We present the first Chinchilla-style IsoFLOP scaling study of a spiking language model, sweeping SpikeGPT-family models (RWKV backbone) from width 384 to 1536 across compute budgets from $3\times10^{17}$ to $10^{19}$ FLOPs on FineWeb-EDU. We report two findings. First, the compute-optimal frontier is $N_{opt} \propto C^{0.56}$, close to the 0.50 that Hoffmann et al. report for dense Transformers, so spiking does not change how parameters and data should be traded off under a compute budget. Second, against non-spiking RWKV and Transformer baselines trained the same way, the loss gap splits into a backbone cost of about 0.16 nats that does not change with compute and a spiking cost that falls from 0.34 to about 0.23 nats and then levels off. These results give the first compute-optimal recipe for spiking language models and quantify the accuracy cost of their sparsity.

</details>


### [132] [When Dynamic Scaling Fails: A Scale-State Feedback Governs Recurrent-State Quantization in Linear-Attention and SSM LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15139)
*Yuhang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究线性注意力/SSM LLM 中循环状态量化的失败模式，指出量化尺度与状态之间的反馈导致范数爆炸与长程功能退化，二者可分离；单纯定位离群值或学习层级比特分配均无法解决，32K 时 group-wise INT4 已退化到丢弃状态的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力与 SSM LLM 的循环状态在每步被重写，逐步量化会嵌入递归并导致误差累积，INT4 在长解码后期彻底破坏合成召回；需要区分数值不稳定与功能退化两类失败，并设计能控制尺度而非仅定位离群值的量化方案。

Method: 论文针对线性注意力与状态空间模型（SSM）类 LLM，其长上下文信息被压缩进固定大小的循环状态。推理时每步解码都会重写该状态，量化器在每一步作用于状态张量，因此量化误差进入递归并被逐步放大。论文分析了量化尺度（scale）与状态之间的反馈回路：scale 由状态导出，状态又被 scale 量化，导致范数爆炸。方案一将量化尺度绑定到全精度参考状态，从而切断该反馈并完全消除范数爆炸，但离线实验显示仍损失 5.4 nats 中的 3.6 nats，说明还存在无法用数值稳定性解释的功能退化。方案二探索离群值感知量化，发现其在各配置下均不敌朴素持久缩放（persistent scaling）。方案三尝试跨层比特分配，但在 article-disjoint 数据划分下学习到的分配退化为优先保护前几层。最终在 32K 解码长度下 group-wise INT4 的效果等同于直接丢弃状态，论文据此主张量化研究应报告位宽被认证有效的解码时域。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nats 损失恢复量", "value": "5.4 nats 中恢复 1.8 nats", "setting": "将量化尺度绑定到全精度参考后，在长解码轨迹上的损失恢复"}, {"metric": "INT4 召回保持", "value": "合成召回在约 1000 步后完全破坏", "setting": "INT4 逐步量化循环状态，起始时合成召回完好"}, {"metric": "group-wise INT4 与丢弃状态对比", "value": "32K 解码时 group-wise INT4 不优于丢弃状态", "setting": "扩展到 32K 解码长度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示量化尺度与状态本身之间的反馈机制，解释状态范数爆炸与长程退化；将量化尺度绑定到全精度参考以消除范数爆炸，但仅恢复 5.4 nats 中的 1.8 nats，证明数值不稳定与功能退化可分离；证明剩余损失是分辨率问题，任何离群值感知方案均不如朴素的持久缩放；在 article-disjoint 划分下，学习到的层级比特分配退化为保护前几层的平凡规则；扩展到 32K 时 group-wise INT4 的效果不优于直接丢弃状态，并提出应按认证位宽报告解码时域

Abstract: Linear-attention and state-space LLMs carry long context in a fixed-size recurrent state, and quantizing that state is the natural way to cut its cost during long decode. Unlike a weight tensor, the state is rewritten at every step, so a quantizer applied at each step sits inside the recurrence and its errors compound. INT4 that leaves synthetic recall perfectly intact at the start destroys it a thousand steps later. The mechanism is a feedback between the quantizer scale and the state it is derived from. Yoking that scale to a full-precision reference removes the resulting norm explosion entirely, yet recovers only 1.8 of the 5.4 nats lost, so numerical instability and functional degradation are separable failures. The remainder is a resolution problem that locating outliers does not solve, since every outlier-aware scheme here loses to plain persistent scaling. Allocating bits across layers does help, but under article-disjoint splits a learned allocation collapses onto the trivial rule of protecting the first few layers. Run out to 32K, group-wise INT4 is worth no more than discarding the state. Contain the scale rather than locate outliers, and report the decode horizon at which a bit-width was certified.

</details>


### [133] [Swarm Telepathy: Prefill-Free KV-Cache Transfer Across Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15095)
*Abdulrahman Alabdulkareem,Irene Tenison,Yang Zhang,Yada Zhu,Boris Katz,Dan Gutfreund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwaT提出首个跨模型族、无预填充的KV-cache传输方法，用于多智能体LLM通信。它通过直接传输和转换KV缓存替代文本通信，结合KV-cache压缩，在17个基准、12个LLM、6个模型族上平均准确率提升40%，墙钟时间快4倍，且训练成本低于50M tokens和1 GPU-day，模型保持冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统通过离散文本通信，接收方需要重新预填充，造成带宽受限和冗余计算；已有KV-cache传输方法局限于同族模型或仍需接收方预填充文本，无法跨模型族实现无预填充通信。

Method: SwaT面向多智能体LLM系统的智能体间通信。发送方智能体在自身模型中处理输入并生成KV缓存。SwaT将该KV缓存（可能先经KV-cache压缩）转换为接收方模型可用的形式，并直接注入接收方。接收方模型无需对原始文本进行预填充，直接基于转换后的KV缓存继续解码或生成响应。该方法支持跨模型族传输，发送方与接收方模型均保持冻结，仅训练传输/转换模块。若发送方为视觉语言模型，其包含视觉信息的KV缓存可被转换并迁移至纯文本接收方，使接收方无需多模态训练即可利用视觉信息。训练目标为最小化接收方基于传输KV缓存生成结果与理想结果之间的差异，训练数据仅为纯文本。推理时，通信过程跳过文本序列化和接收方预填充，降低冗余计算与延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "平均高40%（相对文本到文本通信）", "setting": "智能体到智能体通信设置，结合KV-cache压缩，跨17个基准、12个LLM、6个模型族"}, {"metric": "墙钟时间", "value": "平均快4倍（相对文本到文本通信）", "setting": "智能体到智能体通信设置，结合KV-cache压缩"}, {"metric": "训练成本", "value": "少于50M tokens，少于1 GPU-day", "setting": "SwaT训练，发送方和接收方模型冻结"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首个跨模型族、无预填充的KV-cache传输方法SwaT，支持多族多智能体通信。；结合KV-cache压缩技术，在保持准确率的同时实现4倍墙钟加速。；发送方为视觉语言模型时可将其视觉能力迁移到纯文本接收方LLM，即使SwaT仅在纯文本数据上训练。；训练成本极低，仅需少于50M tokens和少于1 GPU-day，且发送方与接收方模型均保持冻结。

Abstract: LLM multi-agent systems split work across agents that communicate through text. An agent summarizes its findings, another agent receives the text. This constrains communication to discrete tokens and costs redundant compute, limiting bandwidth and capabilities of multi-agent systems. Recent works demonstrate KV-cache transfer between LLMs but they either require the LLMs to be within the same family or cannot function across context, thus requiring the receiver to prefill text. We present SwaT (Swarm Telepathy), the first cross-family KV-cache transfer method that allows prefill-free communication in multi-family multi-agent systems. In an agent-to-agent communication setting, combining SwaT with recent advances in KV-cache compaction beats text-to-text communication in both accuracy and speed, with a mean of $40\%$ higher accuracy and a mean of $4\times$ faster wall-clock time. Experiments show SwaT achieves high performance on 17 benchmarks across 12 LLMs and 6 families. Additionally, given a sender Vision-Language Model (VLM), SwaT is able to transfer the vision capabilities to a text-only receiver LLM exceeding text-to-text communication despite SwaT being trained on text-only data. SwaT keeps both sender and receiver models frozen without fine-tuning and only requires $<50M$ tokens and $<1$ GPU-day to train.

</details>


### [134] [Anchors Enhance Controllability and Reasoning of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15097)
*Feiyu Qu,Zheng Huang,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorDLM 通过 RL 训练 anchor generator，为每个 query 生成短片段及其位置作为控制 token，插入冻结扩散语言模型以引导去噪，在代码生成和数学推理上提升可控性与推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的可控性不足：如何在保持原始生成能力的同时满足任务约束，且控制 token 的内容与放置位置需要相互兼容。

Method: AnchorDLM 由 anchor generator 与冻结的扩散语言模型（DLM）组成。输入为 query，anchor generator 生成一个 anchor，即一段短输出片段及其在输出序列中的位置。该 anchor 作为控制 token 被放置在指定位置，用于初始化冻结 DLM 的输出序列，并在后续去噪过程中提供引导。anchor generator 通过强化学习训练，奖励基于 DLM 生成输出的答案正确性与约束满足度联合优化 anchor 的内容与位置。作者还从离散流匹配（DFM）的角度解释 anchor 放置的作用，即帮助重塑引导生成的初始速度场。推理时，冻结 DLM 在 anchor 初始化后执行去噪生成最终输出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "controllability 与 reasoning performance", "value": "在五个 DLM backbone 上于代码生成和数学推理任务中均有提升", "setting": "五个 DLM backbone，代码生成与数学推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: AnchorDLM 框架：为每个 query 生成包含短输出片段及其位置组成的 anchor；将 anchor 作为控制 token 插入冻结 DLM 的输出序列指定位置以初始化并引导去噪；使用强化学习联合优化 anchor 内容与位置，奖励由生成输出的答案正确性和约束满足度计算；从离散流匹配（DFM）视角将 anchor 放置解释为重塑引导生成的初始速度场

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer new opportunities for controllable text generation. However, their controllability, defined as the ability to satisfy task constraints while preserving their original capabilities, remains underexplored. DLMs' bidirectional attention allows control tokens to guide generation from arbitrary positions in the output sequence, but effective guidance depends on the compatibility between their content and placement. To this end, we propose AnchorDLM, a framework in which an anchor generator produces an anchor consisting of a short output fragment and its position for each query. The anchor is placed as control tokens at the specified position to initialize the output sequence of a frozen DLM and guide subsequent denoising. We train the anchor generator with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly optimize anchor content and placement, using rewards computed from the DLM's generated outputs based on answer correctness and constraint satisfaction. Moreover, through the lens of discrete flow matching (DFM), we interpret anchor placement as helping to reshape the initial velocity field that guides generation. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning across five DLM backbones show that AnchorDLM improves controllability and further yields gains in reasoning performance across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [135] [Information-Theoretic Foundations of Online Temperature Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15043)
*Jin Sima,Nikolaos Papagiannis,Ananth Grama,Wojciech Szpankowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度研究LLM解码中的在线温度采样问题，提出EOS算法，通过在线估计温度以保证生成多样性并避免退化，具有理论最优性保证和初步实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线温度采样问题：条件概率逐个揭示，需因果地选择采样分布，在满足整个时域的交叉熵预算约束下最大化熵，以避免重复退化生成。

Method: 论文未给出模型架构细节。EOS是一种在线采样算法：对于每个时间步t，已知条件分布p_1,...,p_t，通过求解一个约束优化问题（最大化采样分布的熵，满足累积交叉熵不超过预算）重新估计温度参数，从而因果地确定q_t。离线最优解为温度采样，在线问题中温度是全局影子价格。算法包含标准版本和预算收紧变体，后者通过调整求解策略保证精确满足预算。整个推理路径不修改模型参数，仅调整采样分布。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "熵追踪效果", "value": "EOS能够追踪离线温度并优于固定温度采样", "setting": "实验验证，具体数值未在摘要中披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明离线最优解是温度采样，逆温度是预算的拉格朗日乘子。；提出Entropic Online Sampling (EOS)算法，对每个前缀重新求解温度。；理论证明EOS的熵损失和预算超出均为O(R log n)，且任何精确可行在线算法的损失下界为Ω(R)，因此EOS在log n因子内最优。；提出预算收紧变体，以相同速率实现精确可行。；在条件分布来自有限混合时，给出R = O~(√n)的界。

Abstract: Repetitive, degenerate generation is a persistent failure of LLM decoding, and the standard remedies (temperature, nucleus, and top-$k$ sampling) are static heuristics. By Kac's lemma and the AEP, a stationary source takes time exponential in its entropy to repeat a block. We therefore maximize the entropy of the sampling distributions under one cross-entropy budget that keeps the output plausible under the model. Offline, the solution is temperature sampling, with the inverse temperature as the budget's Lagrange multiplier. \emph{Our contribution is the online problem}: the conditionals $p_1, p_2, \dots$ are revealed one at a time, and the sampling distribution $q_t$ must be chosen causally from $p_1, \dots, p_t$ while the budget couples the entire horizon. The optimal temperature is thus a global shadow price that must be estimated from a prefix. We propose Entropic Online Sampling (EOS), which re-solves for the temperature on each prefix. We prove it loses $O(R\log n)$ entropy against the offline optimum while overshooting the budget by $O(R\log n)$, with $R$ an instance-dependent drift parameter. A budget-tightened variant is exactly feasible at the same rate. Conversely, every exactly feasible online algorithm must lose $\Omega(R)$, so EOS is optimal up to a $\log n$ factor. When the conditionals are drawn from a finite mixture, $R=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$. Experiments confirm that EOS tracks the offline temperature and beats fixed-temperature sampling in entropy.

</details>


### [136] [BSIPipe: Bidirectional Sequence-Level Interleaved Pipeline Parallelism for Long-Sequence Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14935)
*Yuzhou Ni,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BSIPipe提出双向序列级交错流水线并行，通过协同调度两个相反的V形交错流水线、基本单元拼接以及通信优化，提升长序列LLM训练吞吐，并在GPT与LLaMA3上相比SOTA序列级基线实现1.11×-1.13×平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级流水线并行方法单向调度子序列，导致长序列LLM训练吞吐受限。

Method: BSIPipe是一种用于长序列LLM训练的双向序列级交错流水线并行方法。它把序列切分为多个子序列，并为每个子序列协同调度两个方向相反的V形交错流水线，从而提升流水线吞吐。为避免子序列数量增加导致激活内存累积，引入基本单元拼接机制，将多个子序列的基本单元拼接在一起，使激活内存不随子序列数增长。双向调度可能带来通信死锁风险，因此采用结合拓扑感知设备映射与通信-计算重叠的通信优化策略来消除该风险。此外，BSIPipe可与张量并行组合，突破单维并行的内存限制，在维持吞吐的同时中和双向调度带来的额外内存开销。训练在GPT与LLaMA3模型上进行，与SOTA序列级流水线并行基线对比，取得平均1.11×至1.13×的加速。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练吞吐加速", "value": "平均1.11×至1.13×", "setting": "在GPT和LLaMA3模型上进行长序列训练，与SOTA序列级流水线并行基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 双向序列级交错流水线并行：在子序列级别协同调度两个相反的V形交错流水线；基本单元拼接机制：使激活内存不随子序列数量增长；通信优化策略：拓扑感知设备映射与通信-计算重叠，消除双向调度的通信死锁风险；与张量并行组合，克服单维并行的内存限制并中和双向内存开销

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism has become a critical technique for training large language models. To address the throughput limitation of existing sequence-level methods that schedule subsequences unidirectionally, we propose bidirectional sequence-level interleaved pipeline parallelism (BSIPipe), which co-schedules two opposing V-shaped interleaved pipelines at the subsequence level. To bound the activation accumulation from subsequence scaling, BSIPipe introduces a basic-unit concatenation mechanism that keeps activation memory invariant to the subsequence count. To eliminate communication deadlock risk from bidirectional scheduling, BSIPipe employs a communication optimization strategy that combines topology-aware device mapping with communication-computation overlap. In addition, BSIPipe composes with tensor parallelism to overcome the memory limitation of single-dimensional parallelism, neutralizing its bidirectional memory overhead while maintaining throughput. Experiments on GPT and LLaMA3 demonstrate that BSIPipe achieves average speedups of 1.11$\times$ to 1.13$\times$ in long-sequence training over SOTA sequence-level baselines.

</details>


### [137] [Terrarium: Growing Personal Assistants via Scalable Synthesis of App Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14860)
*Mengzhou Wu,Yuzhe Guo,Jiawen Tao,Xiaokun Yuan,Yuan Cao,Gang Cao,Yuetang Deng,Zhi Gong,Dezhi Ran,Linyi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Terrarium 通过合成可执行的应用世界和任务包，利用基于结果的强化学习训练通用个人助理 LLM。在 τ²-bench 和 AppWorld bench 上，训练后的 Qwen3 模型（4B、8B、14B）平均提升 18.34 和 10.94 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实应用不易支持大规模训练，因为访问受限且状态难以重置，需要可扩展的合成应用世界和任务来训练 LLM 助理。

Method: Terrarium 是一个框架，用于合成可执行的应用世界（App Worlds）并从中生成训练任务包，以支持基于结果的强化学习。其流程包括：（1）应用世界合成：将公共 Web 信息和应用内观察组织成结构化的应用规范，指导构建自包含的运行时环境，这些环境具有可调用的操作和可变、可重置的状态。（2）任务合成：基于应用世界状态构建任务，并生成评估器侧的结果评分标准，该标准评估成功与否而不规定参考轨迹。（3）迭代验证与优化：通过 agent rollout 迭代地验证和优化应用世界与任务。最终构建了一个包含 275 个应用世界和 13,344 个任务的训练套件，用于在三个 Qwen3 模型规模（4B、8B、14B）上进行基于结果的强化学习。训练后的模型在 τ²-bench 和 AppWorld bench 上评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "平均 18.34 个百分点", "setting": "在 τ²-bench 上，对比对应 Qwen3 模型（4B、8B、14B）"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "平均 10.94 个百分点", "setting": "在 AppWorld bench 上，对比对应 Qwen3 模型（4B、8B、14B）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从公共 Web 信息和应用内观察合成可执行的应用世界（自包含运行时，具有可调用操作和可变、可重置状态）；从应用世界状态生成基于结果评估器评分标准（evaluator-side outcome rubrics）的训练任务包，不预设参考轨迹；通过 agent rollout 迭代验证和优化应用世界与任务

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have brought the vision of a general-purpose personal assistant closer to reality. Such assistants could provide a unified natural-language interface to a broad ecosystem of applications. However, real-world applications do not readily support training at scale because access is often restricted and their state is difficult to reset. To address this challenge, we introduce Terrarium, a framework that synthesizes executable App Worlds from real-world application information, generates training task packages with evaluator-side rubrics from these worlds, and iteratively validates and refines both through agent rollouts. For App World synthesis, Terrarium organizes public Web information and in-application observations into structured app specifications that guide the construction of self-contained runtimes with callable operations and mutable, resettable state. For task synthesis, Terrarium constructs tasks grounded in App World state together with evaluator-side outcome rubrics that assess success without prescribing reference trajectories. We construct a training suite of 275 App Worlds and 13,344 tasks for outcome-based reinforcement learning across three Qwen3 model scales (4B, 8B, and 14B parameters). Across these scales, models trained with Terrarium outperform the corresponding Qwen3 models by an average of 18.34 percentage points on the $\tau^2$-bench and 10.94 percentage points on the AppWorld bench. These results suggest that app-grounded world and task synthesis offers a scalable path toward general-purpose assistants capable of operating across broad application ecosystems.

</details>


### [138] [PTGPO: Prefix Tree Group Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14737)
*Ruolin Chen,Yang Li,Jihang Wang,Zhuoya Zhao,Yuchen Liu,Jiaming Xu,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PTGPO 针对长程交互式语言智能体的稀疏奖励信用分配问题，在锚点状态组内对未来轨迹后缀构建前缀树，用父子节点经验平均回报之差定义每条边的信用，从而在无评论家、无额外 rollout 的情况下获得细粒度动作级学习信号，并在 ALFWorld、WebShop、Sokoban 上稳定优于 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程交互式任务中终端稀疏奖励导致中间动作信用分配信号薄弱；现有组方法要么共享轨迹级优势，要么从状态组估计步级优势，可能把当前动作与后续决策混为一谈。

Method: 该方法以语言智能体在交互环境中的轨迹为输入，在锚点状态（anchor state）处将具有相同前缀状态的轨迹归为一组。对每个锚点状态组，把各条轨迹的后缀展开成前缀树，树的每条边对应从父节点状态执行一个动作后的转移。每条边的信用由该边子节点与父节点的经验平均回报之差给出，刻画每增加一个动作所带来的条件回报变化。这些前缀树信用被用作细粒度的动作级学习信号，用于策略优化，无需训练额外的评论家网络，也无需增加额外的环境 rollout。训练在 ALFWorld、WebShop、Sokoban 等交互式基准上进行，策略在环境中采样轨迹后按上述方式计算信用并更新；推理/部署时仍按普通语言智能体策略与环境交互执行，不引入额外模型组件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出在锚点状态组内对轨迹后缀构建前缀树，用树边上的信用表示条件回报变化；用子节点与父节点经验平均回报之差定义每条边的信用，无需 critic 也无需额外 rollout；将前缀树信用作为细粒度学习信号融入无评论家策略优化目标

Abstract: Training language agents on long-horizon interactive tasks is challenging because sparse terminal rewards provide weak credit signals for intermediate actions. Existing group-based methods either assign a shared trajectory-level advantage or estimate step-level advantages from state groups, which may conflate the current action with subsequent decisions. We propose Prefix Tree Group Policy Optimization (PTGPO), a critic-free method that constructs prefix trees over trajectory suffixes within anchor-state groups. PTGPO assigns each tree edge a credit given by the difference between its child and parent empirical mean returns, capturing the conditional return change revealed by each additional action. These prefix-tree credits provide fine-grained learning signals without requiring a critic or additional rollouts. Experiments on interactive benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop and Sokoban show that PTGPO consistently improves over RL baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [139] [Accelerating Cross-Model LLM Inference via Attention Map Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14688)
*Zhan Jin,Yuze Luo,Bin Cui,Xupeng Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MapFlow 提出跨模型注意力图复用以加速异构大语言模型推理服务，通过部分重计算和线性变换实现高效复用，并引入块级稀疏压缩和融合检索内核支持长上下文，实验表明能降低预填充延迟并提升端到端效率且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构大语言模型在共享上下文（如模式、工具规范、交互历史、中间结果）时的预填充开销高，现有前缀缓存仅能复用同一模型的KV状态，无法跨模型复用。

Method: MapFlow 是一种用于异构大语言模型高效服务的系统。其核心思想是复用注意力图（attention maps）而非 KV 状态，因为注意力图捕获词元依赖模式且更易跨模型迁移。架构包含以下组件：1）输入处理：接收多模型共享的长上下文（如模式、工具规范、交互历史等）。2）注意力复用模块：结合部分重计算（对部分层/头重新计算注意力）和轻量线性变换（跨层、跨头的注意力图转换），以适配不同模型。3）长上下文优化：块级稀疏压缩降低注意力图存储和计算开销，融合检索内核加速注意力图检索。4）推理流程：在预填充阶段，利用缓存或计算得到的注意力图进行跨模型复用，减少重复预填充计算；生成阶段正常进行。训练目标或优化未明确披露，推测为保持生成质量并降低延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出跨模型注意力图复用，而非KV状态复用，利用注意力图对模型依赖模式的捕捉和跨模型可迁移性。；结合部分重计算和跨层、跨头的轻量线性变换，实现高效且准确的注意力复用。；引入块级稀疏压缩和融合检索内核，使注意力复用适用于长上下文场景。

Abstract: LLM-augmented data workflows often involve multiple heterogeneous models operating over substantial shared context, such as schemas, tool specifications, interaction history, and intermediate results. Although prefix caching can reduce prefilling overhead, existing approaches are largely limited to reuse within the same model because KV states are tightly coupled to model-specific architectures. We present MapFlow, a system for efficient heterogeneous LLM serving via cross-model attention map reuse. Instead of reusing KV states, MapFlow reuses attention maps, which capture token dependency patterns and are more transferable across models. To enable efficient and accurate reuse, we combine partial recomputation with lightweight linear translation across layers and heads. To make attention reuse practical for long contexts, we further introduce block-level sparse compression and a fused retrieval kernel. Experiments show that MapFlow reduces prefilling latency and improves end-to-end serving efficiency while preserving generation quality across heterogeneous model pairs and multi-turn routing workloads. These results suggest that attention maps provide an effective reusable state for heterogeneous LLM serving, especially in prefix-heavy data workflows. Our code is available: this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Reward-Aligned Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14572)
*Yilan Chen,Zian Su,Manish Prajapat,Dingmin Wang,Rein Houthooft,Rui Song,Vijay Lingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAD 提出一种奖励对齐的双层蒸馏框架，根据每个 token 对更新后奖励的预测影响来加权 on-policy distillation 信号，使权重可增强、抑制或反转单个梯度贡献；在六个数学竞赛基准和 LiveCodeBench-v6 上，对 Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B 和 OLMo3-7B 取得一致的离线准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy distillation 中，标准做法对所有 token 一视同仁地施加教师监督，但这些监督并不都提升学生模型的任务奖励；现有基于不确定性和师生差异的 token 选择方法只是代理指标，不能直接识别对奖励有改进作用的监督信号。

Method: RAD 是一个用于 on-policy distillation 的双层优化框架。输入为学生模型与更强教师模型在训练响应上的 token 级序列；标准 OPD 对每个 token 施加教师监督，而 RAD 为每个 token 的蒸馏监督分配一个可学习的权重。其数据流与优化过程如下：首先，在给定当前学生参数和一批训练响应时，对每个 token 的蒸馏梯度贡献进行评分。评分方式是通过一个临时优化器步（temporary optimizer step）进行微分，估计该 token 监督对学生更新后奖励的影响。然后，基于该预测影响，从初始 token 权重出发，采用投影上升（projected ascent）更新 token 权重。最终权重可以增强、抑制或反转单个蒸馏梯度贡献，从而改变学生参数更新方向。训练目标是在匹配训练响应预算下最大化学生更新后的任务奖励，而不是单纯拟合教师分布。推理或服务流程与常规微调后的学生模型一致，论文未披露额外推理开销或部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean accuracy on six competition-math benchmarks", "value": "比最强基线提升 6.5 percentage points", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B (non-thinking)，匹配训练响应预算"}, {"metric": "mean accuracy on six competition-math benchmarks", "value": "比最强基线提升 2.5 percentage points", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507，匹配训练响应预算"}, {"metric": "LiveCodeBench-v6 pass@1", "value": "54.69%", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B (non-thinking)，比最强基线高 8.7 points"}, {"metric": "LiveCodeBench-v6 pass@1", "value": "58.67%", "setting": "OLMo3-7B-Instruct，比最强基线高 6.4 points"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Reward-Aligned Distillation (RAD)，一个双层优化框架，根据每个 token 对学生更新后奖励的预测影响来加权蒸馏信号。；通过临时优化器步进行微分，为每个 token 的监督信号打分，然后从初始 token 权重出发做投影上升更新 token 权重。；所得权重可以增强、抑制甚至反转单个蒸馏梯度贡献，而非简单筛选或丢弃 token。；提出简化变体 RAD-Simple，在相同训练响应预算下取得可比数学准确率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models, but remains computationally expensive and sample inefficient. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher, yet standard OPD distills every token regardless of whether the teacher's preference actually improves task reward. Existing token-selection methods rely on proxies such as uncertainty or teacher--student divergence, which need not identify reward-improving supervision. We introduce Reward-Aligned Distillation (RAD), a bilevel framework that weights distillation signals based on their predicted effect on the student's post-update reward. RAD differentiates through a temporary optimizer step to score each token's supervision, then updates the token weights by projected ascent from the initial token weights. The resulting weights can strengthen, suppress, or reverse individual distillation-gradient contributions. At a matched training-response budget, RAD improves mean accuracy on six competition-math benchmarks over the strongest baselines by 6.5 percentage points for Qwen3-1.7B (non-thinking) and 2.5 points for Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. RAD-Simple, a simplified variant, achieves comparable math accuracy at the same training-response budget. On LiveCodeBench-v6, RAD reaches 54.69\% and 58.67\% pass@1 with Qwen3-1.7B (non-thinking) and OLMo3-7B-Instruct, respectively, exceeding the strongest baselines by 8.7 and 6.4 points. These results support reward-guided weighting of teacher supervision across model families and tasks.

</details>


### [141] [Query-Adaptive Neuron Steering for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14519)
*Yanxi Pan,Jingwen Wu,Yan Ma,Xiang Chen,Xinyi Zeng,Xuetong Wang,Yunyan Zhang,Yue Zhang,Yu Tian,Jianfeng Pan,Hongyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-NeuS提出查询自适应神经元干预框架，通过主题引导候选空间与轻量门控网络动态选择并调节神经元，结合Neu-GRPO训练门控，基座LLM全程冻结，在多个LLM和基准上取得一致提升并展现跨基准迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在LLM推理时针对每个输入查询自适应地决定干预哪些神经元以及干预强度，以更好激发模型能力，同时保持基座模型冻结且无需任务或主题标签。

Method: Q-NeuS是一个查询自适应的神经元干预框架。输入为用户查询，经基座LLM前向计算得到内部激活。首先基于预定义或统计得到的主题引导信息，构建一个紧凑的候选神经元空间，减少干预搜索范围。然后轻量级Query-Adaptive Gate根据查询诱导的激活模式，为候选空间内每个神经元输出调制系数，决定是否增强、抑制或保持不变。干预后的激活继续参与后续层计算，最终生成输出。训练阶段，基座LLM完全冻结，仅训练Query-Adaptive Gate；采用Neu-GRPO，对同一查询采样多组不同的神经元干预配置，评估各组相对表现，利用组相对优化更新门控网络，从而学习到针对不同查询的有效干预策略。推理阶段同样冻结基座LLM，无需任务或主题标签，门控网络直接根据当前查询生成调制系数，实现即时自适应干预。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现主题与层相关的神经元激活模式，并验证放大主题相关神经元可提升模型表现；提出Q-NeuS：基于主题引导的紧凑候选神经元空间与轻量Query-Adaptive Gate，为每个输入生成神经元级调制系数；提出Neu-GRPO：将组相对策略优化用于训练Query-Adaptive Gate，利用多个神经元干预配置的相对表现学习干预策略；推理时基座LLM保持冻结，无需任务或主题标签，具备跨基准迁移能力

Abstract: Effectively eliciting the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) at inference time remains an important challenge. Existing studies address this challenge through context engineering, which shapes user queries, or fixed steering, which directly intervenes in model computation. However, predefined contexts and fixed intervention strategies may not adequately accommodate the diverse knowledge and reasoning requirements of individual queries. This raises a natural question: Can internal steering in LLMs be made query-adaptive? In this work, we investigate whether query-induced internal activations can guide adaptive steering in LLMs. Our pilot studies reveal subject- and layer-dependent neuron activation patterns and show that amplifying subject-relevant neurons can improve model performance. Based on these findings, we propose Q-NeuS, a query-adaptive neuron steering framework that dynamically determines which neurons to modulate and by how much for each input. Specifically, Q-NeuS constructs a compact subject-guided candidate neuron space and employs a lightweight Query-Adaptive Gate to generate neuron-wise modulation coefficients. To learn effective intervention strategies, we introduce Neu-GRPO, which adapts group-relative optimization to train the Query-Adaptive Gate using the relative performance of multiple neuron intervention configurations. The base LLM remains frozen throughout training and inference, and no task or subject labels are required at inference time. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that Q-NeuS consistently improves model performance and exhibits broad applicability across model scales and strong cross-benchmark transferability.

</details>


### [142] [From Transfer Probes to Expert Grouping: Evidence from LoRA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14437)
*Xueyang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究固定预算 LoRA 后训练中，迁移探针能否发现优于均匀默认的分组，并发现基于初始状态的探针分组在后续训练中未能带来显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定预算的 LoRA 后训练中，能否通过迁移探针（在源任务上训练并测量所有源任务的配对变化）自动发现未知的业务线分组，使分组优于统一默认路由？

Method: 论文采用 LoRA 后训练框架：从一个共享的预热状态出发，将七个公开源（五个数学子领域、两种编程，作为业务线代理）分配到四个 LoRA 专家上。每个源在固定曝光和固定每专家预算下进行训练。评估时使用源标签树作为参考分组（评测者持有而方法不知）。比较三种分组：纯探针推导的分组、无信息均匀默认（所有源共享一个专家，采用 tied routing）、以及标签树参考分组。训练一次持续一个区间，路由规则由每个分配固定。评估指标为总体效用（utility），以百分比点表示。主要结果：探针分组与均匀默认相比参考分组分别低 -2.43 和 -2.54 个百分点，95% 置信区间均低于零；探针分组与均匀默认无显著差异（+0.11 pp，[-1.25, +1.54]）。结论：初始状态单源探针发现的分组在后续训练状态、有限预算和固定路由下未带来预期优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出用迁移探针（单源训练后测量所有源的配对变化）发现分组，并与标准标签树参考对比。；设计固定每源曝光和每专家预算的受控实验，比较探针分组、无信息均匀默认（共享一个专家）与标签树参考分组。；发现从初始状态单源探针得到的分组在后续训练状态、有限预算和固定路由下未能带来预测收益。

Abstract: A platform that post-trains one base model for many business lines often has more lines than experts it can maintain, so lines must share experts, yet lines seldom come with a taxonomy saying which should share. We ask whether transfer probes (train on one source, measure paired changes on all sources) can discover a grouping when the standard one is unknown. To test probes against a reference, we use seven public sources that do have a label tree (five mathematics subfields and two kinds of programming, standing in for business lines) and treat the tree's grouping as a label reference that the evaluator holds and the method does not. With four LoRA experts under fixed per-source exposure and per-expert budgets, we compare a purely probe-derived grouping and an uninformed uniform default (whose tied routing serves every line from one expert) against this reference. Trained once for one interval from a shared warm-up state and routed by a rule fixed by each allocation, neither reached the prespecified practical gain of 2 percentage points, and both had lower overall utility than the reference ($-2.43$ and $-2.54$ pp; 95\% intervals below zero). In a post hoc comparison, the probe-derived grouping was indistinguishable from the uniform default ($+0.11$ pp, $[-1.25,+1.54]$). In fixed-budget LoRA post-training, a grouping built from initial-state single-source probes thus did not deliver the advantage predicted from the probes once carried to a later training state, a finite budget, and specified routing.

</details>


### [143] [ConformalGuard: False-Positive-Controlled Action Gating for LM Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14365)
*Bishnu Bista,Dhanraj Haibatrao Jadhav,Vibha Choudhary,Gaurav Saxena,Arun Pandiyan Perumal,Arjun Subedi,Jignesh Ammineni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConformalGuard提出模型无关的保形校准层，为LM分类器动作门控提供有限样本误报率控制。在邮件语料上对比微调BERT、Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o，显示保形校准可显著降低FPR（如Claude FPR 0.040→0.008），但召回下降；同时揭示零样本LM API分数离散导致传统校准失效，需随机平均等操作技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证集阈值无法对部署时误报率提供有限样本控制，尤其在低误报率动作门控场景中，零样本LM API分数离散度高，导致温度缩放和保序回归退化为原始阈值，亟需模型无关的校准层来控制合法输入触发干预的比率。

Method: ConformalGuard是一个模型无关的校准层，包裹固定打分函数f(x)（如微调BERT、Claude Sonnet 4或GPT-4o的零样本分数）。输入为待分类样本x（如邮件），输出为是否触发干预层级（如拦截、送审）的二元决策。校准流程：在标定集上计算每个合法样本的非一致性得分（如1 - f(x)），取(1-α)分位数作为阈值q，部署时若非一致性得分超过q则触发干预。该方法对合法样本触发干预的期望比率提供有限样本控制（在可交换性假设下）。论文比较三种校准方法：原始阈值、温度缩放、保序回归与保形校准；并指出当零样本LM API分数离散（10-11唯一值）时，温度缩放和保序回归在低FPR操作点上等价于原始阈值。对确定性GPT-4o，采用三次随机运行平均来降低分数离散性，从而在FPR 0.005时提升召回率至0.732。推理时仅需比较分数与阈值，计算开销极低。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "FPR", "value": "从0.040降至0.008", "setting": "Claude Sonnet 4，保形校准对比基线"}, {"metric": "Recall", "value": "从0.938降至0.875", "setting": "Claude Sonnet 4，保形校准对比基线"}, {"metric": "Recall", "value": "0.564", "setting": "确定性GPT-4o分数，1%操作点"}, {"metric": "Recall", "value": "0.732（FPR 0.005）", "setting": "确定性GPT-4o分数，三运行随机平均"}, {"metric": "每10,000封邮件减少假阳性审核数", "value": "25（95% CI [10, 43]）", "setting": "BERT，2%患病率，保形校准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出ConformalGuard模型无关校准层，包装固定打分函数，对合法事件触发干预层级的比率提供有限样本控制。；实证揭示零样本LM API分数离散性（10-11个唯一值）导致温度缩放/保序回归在低FPR门控中操作上等同原始阈值。；在公共邮件语料上系统比较保形校准与温度缩放/保序回归，覆盖微调BERT、Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o三种打分器。；提出三运行随机平均策略缓解确定性GPT-4o分数在1%操作点过于保守的问题。

Abstract: Language model classifiers are increasingly deployed for high-stakes decisions such as phishing screening, content moderation, and fraud triage, yet validation-set thresholds provide no finite-sample control of deployment-time false positives. We present ConformalGuard, a model-agnostic calibration layer that wraps a fixed scoring function with finite-sample control over the rate at which legitimate inputs trigger an intervention tier. Our contribution is empirical and operational, not a new conformal theorem: zero-shot LM API scores can be coarse enough (10--11 unique values here) to make temperature scaling and isotonic regression operationally identical to raw thresholding for low-FPR action gating. We compare these methods with conformal calibration for fine-tuned BERT, Claude Sonnet 4, and GPT-4o on public email corpora. For Claude, conformal calibration reduces FPR from 0.040 to 0.008 while recall falls from 0.938 to 0.875. For deterministic GPT-4o scores, the 1% operating point is too conservative to deploy (recall 0.564); three-run stochastic averaging raises recall to 0.732 at FPR 0.005. At 2% prevalence with BERT, conformal calibration removes 25 false-positive reviews per 10,000 emails (95% CI [10, 43]). The guarantee is legitimate-event-rate control under exchangeability; discrimination, workload effects, and robustness to shift remain empirical.

</details>


### [144] [Embedding Graph Kernel Entropy (EGKE): Fine-grained Hallucination Detection for LLMs from Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14214)
*Dilrangi Sankalpana,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Uthayasanker Thayasivam,Kamal Premaratne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EGKE通过将KLE中的NLI语义图替换为基于RBF核的句子嵌入相似度图，降低了幻觉检测的计算开销，并在30个设置中达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义熵和核语言熵幻觉检测方法依赖外部NLI模型计算语义相似度，带来显著计算开销。

Method: EGKE方法将KLE框架中的NLI语义图替换为基于RBF核的稠密句子嵌入相似度图。输入为LLM对同一问题的多次生成回答，首先使用BGE嵌入模型将每个回答编码为稠密向量；然后通过RBF核计算所有回答对之间的相似度，构建归一化的图拉普拉斯矩阵；随后应用热核生成扩散矩阵；最后计算该图的冯·诺依曼熵作为不确定性度量，用于判断是否发生幻觉。训练目标非本文重点，属于无监督检测方法。推理时只需嵌入模型前向传播，无需额外NLI分类器，显著降低计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用RBF核直接作用于BGE稠密句子嵌入来构建语义图，替代NLI分类器；保留KLE的图拉普拉斯、热核和冯·诺依曼熵公式不变；在连续嵌入空间中计算相似度，实现对称且计算高效的语义比较

Abstract: Hallucination detection is critical for ensuring the reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world deployments. While recent works, such as Semantic Entropy (SE) and Kernel Language Entropy (KLE), identify hallucinations by measuring semantic disagreement across multiple generated responses using Natural Language Inference (NLI) models, these methods depend on separately loaded NLI classifiers to estimate pairwise semantic similarity, introducing significant computational overhead. We propose Embedding Graph Kernel Entropy (EGKE), a variant of Kernel Language Entropy (KLE) that replaces its NLI-derived semantic graph with a Radial Basis Function (RBF) kernel applied to dense sentence embeddings generated by a BGE embedding model, while retaining KLE's graph Laplacian, heat kernel, and von Neumann entropy formulation unchanged. By computing similarities directly in a continuous embedding space, the proposed method provides a symmetric and computationally efficient alternative to NLI-based semantic comparison. We evaluate our approach across 30 experimental setups (covering multiple question-answer datasets and open-weight LLM families) using both AUROC and AUARC, and demonstrate that this RBF-based method achieves competitive hallucination detection performance against state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [145] [Learnable Low-Rank Polynomial Sketch for Effective Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14219)
*Zongyue Qin,Haoran Deng,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLoPS，一种可学习的低秩多项式草图方法，用于线性注意力，可证明地降低近似误差，在多项基准上超越现有线性注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力方法使用近似多项式核，存在不可忽略的近似误差，且高维高次多项式核无法精确计算，导致性能次优。

Method: LLoPS 是一种线性注意力框架，旨在通过可学习的低秩多项式草图来近似多项式核。其核心思想是学习一个低秩矩阵分解，使得多项式核的特征映射能够以线性复杂度计算，同时保证近似误差严格小于现有方法。模型输入为序列特征，通过可学习的低秩投影生成近似多项式核的草图，然后利用线性注意力机制进行加权求和。训练目标为最小化近似误差并优化下游任务损失。具体模块包括：低秩草图生成、多项式核近似、线性注意力计算。推理时，通过预计算的低秩矩阵实现线性时间与空间复杂度，适用于长上下文场景。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从多项式核近似角度分析，证明现有线性注意力方法次优；提出可学习的低秩多项式草图（LLoPS）框架，可证明地实现更小的近似误差；在多个基准上取得优于现有线性注意力基线的性能

Abstract: Softmax attention in Transformers suffers from quadratic complexity in sequence length, making it impractical for long-context applications. Linear attention alleviates this issue by replacing the exponential kernel with alternative functions that enable linear-time computation. Among existing linear attention approaches, recent studies have shown that polynomial kernels are particularly effective, as they exhibit sparse and spiky behavior similar to softmax attention, which emphasizes large dot products while suppressing irrelevant interactions. However, the exact computation of high-degree polynomial kernels is infeasible for high-dimensional representations. As a result, prior work relies on approximate polynomial kernels. This introduces a non-negligible approximation error. In this paper, we show that, from the perspective of polynomial kernel approximation, existing linear attention methods are still suboptimal. We propose \textbf{L}earnable \textbf{Lo}w-Rank \textbf{P}olynomial \textbf{S}ketch (LLoPS), a principled and flexible framework for approximating polynomial kernels with linear attention. Our method learns a low-rank polynomial sketch that provably achieves a strictly smaller approximation error than existing approaches. Experiment results show that LLoPS achieves the highest performance across extensive benchmarks, comparing with various linear attention baselines.

</details>


### [146] [Adaptive-Depth Is a Finishing Stage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14252)
*Andrei Piterbarg,Sean McLeish,Dhruv Pai,Benjamin Keigwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出固定优先训练策略，通过仅在训练末期变化循环Transformer的深度，在200M和1B参数规模上分别节省45%和31%-37%训练计算量，墙钟时间减少32%-39%，而NLL与推理深度变化下的性能几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应深度循环Transformer需要在训练全程变化深度，导致训练计算开销大；能否将深度变化推迟到训练末期以节省计算，同时保持推理时深度自适应的性能？

Method: 论文基于循环Transformer架构，同一层被多次重用，从而允许在推理时调整计算深度而不改变模型参数。传统自适应深度方法在训练全程变化循环深度，导致训练不稳定且计算开销大。本文提出固定优先训练：模型首先以固定深度（如200M规模下的深度2，1B规模下的深度4）训练整个训练过程的大部分（如67%），仅在一小段最终阶段才变化深度（即训练深度调度）。这样模型在大部分训练中保持简单快速的固定深度前向传播，节省计算；在最后阶段再适应多种推理深度，从而保持推理时自适应深度的能力。训练目标为最小化负对数似然（NLL），并通过比较不同推理深度下的NLL以及下游任务表现来评估。推理时模型可以灵活选择循环次数（深度）以平衡计算与性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练计算量减少", "value": "45%", "setting": "200M参数模型，固定深度2训练前67%迭代，NLL在推理深度上平均差异<0.5%"}, {"metric": "训练计算量减少", "value": "31%-37%", "setting": "1B参数模型，初始深度4训练，NLL最终差异<0.8%，下游差异在种子变化范围内"}, {"metric": "墙钟时间减少", "value": "32%-39%", "setting": "1B参数模型，固定深度4训练后自适应深度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 固定优先训练策略：仅在训练最后短阶段变化模型深度，前期固定深度训练；在200M和1B参数规模上验证固定优先训练可显著减少训练计算量和墙钟时间；发现训练全范围深度可保留到短finishing阶段，性能仅有微小变化

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse the same layer multiple times, allowing inference compute to be adjusted without changing the model. Recent adaptive-depth methods train the model across multiple loop depths from the beginning of training. We introduce fixed-first training, which varies model depth only during a short final phase of training. At 200M parameters, training a model of fixed-depth 2 for the first 67% of the run reduces modeled training compute by 45% while keeping mean negative log-likelihood (NLL) across inference depths within 0.5% of training across depths throughout. At 1B parameters, training initially at depth 4 reduces modeled compute by 31% to 37% and wall-clock time by 32% to 39%, while keeping final NLL within 0.8% and downstream differences within the observed seed variation. These results show that training over the full range of depths can be reserved for a short finishing phase with only small changes in performance.

</details>


### [147] [ULPack: Reference-Conditioned Lossless Compression of Checkpoint Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14184)
*Aniket Srinivasan Ashok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ULPack 是一种针对模型检查点轨迹的无损压缩编解码器，通过用参考权重的指数条件化有序整数残差的熵模型，在 bfloat16、float16 和 float32 轨迹上相比 ZipNN、zstd-19 和 FM-Delta 最高减少存储 27%、21% 和 11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lossless compression of checkpoint trajectories for model training, where storage cost limits temporal resolution and existing codecs ignore trajectory-specific conditional structure.

Method: ULPack is a lossless codec for sequences of model checkpoints (trajectories). It takes as input a reference checkpoint and a subsequent checkpoint. The key observation is that the exponent of a weight in the reference checkpoint predicts the bit length of the displacement of the corresponding weight in ordered floating-point space, without side information. The codec first maps floating-point weights to ordered integers (presumably a monotonic mapping that preserves numeric order). Then it computes residuals between the reference and target checkpoint in this ordered-integer domain. These residuals are entropy-coded with a model conditioned on the reference exponent. The exponent serves as a context that indicates the magnitude of the residual, enabling more efficient coding. The compression improves as the updates between checkpoints shrink (i.e., the trajectory becomes more predictable), while a floor is set by near-uniform low-order bits. The exact entropy coder and implementation details beyond this are not disclosed.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "storage reduction", "value": "up to 27%", "setting": "against ZipNN across bfloat16, float16, and float32 trajectories"}, {"metric": "storage reduction", "value": "21%", "setting": "against zstd-19 across bfloat16, float16, and float32 trajectories"}, {"metric": "storage reduction", "value": "11.5%", "setting": "against FM-Delta across bfloat16, float16, and float32 trajectories"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Conditioning entropy models of ordered-integer residuals on the reference weight's exponent.；Exploiting the property that a reference weight's exponent predicts the bit length of its displacement in ordered floating-point space without side information.；A trajectory-aware lossless codec ULPack that improves compression as updates shrink.

Abstract: Studying model training requires checkpoint trajectories, whose storage cost limits temporal resolution. Although adjacent checkpoints are numerically close, existing lossless codecs either operate on XOR bit patterns or ignore trajectory-specific conditional structure. We show that a reference weight's exponent predicts the bit length of its displacement in ordered floating-point space without side information. We introduce \textbf{ULPack}, a lossless codec which exploits this dependence by conditioning entropy models of ordered-integer residuals on the reference exponent. Across bfloat16, float16, and float32 trajectories, ULPack reduces storage by up to 27\% against ZipNN, 21\% against zstd-19, and 11.5\% against FM-Delta. Across two dense trajectories, compression improves as updates shrink and reference conditioning strengthens, while near uniform low-order bits set the remaining compression floor and earlier checkpoints add little information.

</details>


### [148] [Rotate the Loop: Blockwise Conformality for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14030)
*Alexey Zemtsov,Albina Klepach,Vadim Lomshakov,Andrei Polubarov,Nikita Lyubaykin,Alexander Derevyagin,Vladislav Kurenkov,Alexander Nikulin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 looped transformer 中循环间残差的线性映射几何，提出 blockwise conformal injections，将残差分组并在组内旋转以赋予各组独立时间尺度，在状态跟踪与语言建模的早期信息末端检索任务上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer 在循环复用时，前一步写入的信息能否在后续步骤被读取，取决于循环间残差上的线性映射形式；本文将该问题形式化为几何问题。

Method: 论文采用 looped transformer 结构：单一共享 block 在多个循环步中反复复用，从而把深度变成可在运行时花费的量，而非堆叠不同层。循环间对残差施加线性映射，该映射决定前一步写入是否在后续循环步仍可读。作者将是否学会状态跟踪形式化为该线性映射的几何特性，并提出 blockwise conformal injections：将残差拆分为坐标对齐的多个组，在每组内部进行旋转（保持组内几何精确），使每个组具有各自的时间尺度，从而在不破坏精确几何的前提下允许不同信息以不同时间尺度被保留与读取。实验评估两个任务：一是 demanding 状态跟踪，仅在中前序前缀已正确的前提下考察最后 token 准确率；二是语言建模，要求序列早期的写入在序列末端被检索。摘要未给出具体的数据集、模型规模、训练硬件或推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将循环 Transformer 是否学习到状态跟踪归结为残差注入线性映射的几何形式问题；提出 blockwise conformal injections：把残差拆分为坐标对齐的组，并在每组内旋转，使每组拥有独立时间尺度且组内几何保持精确；在 last-token accuracy（前缀已正确时预测最后 token）与语言建模的早期信号末端检索任务上验证性能提升

Abstract: Looped transformers reuse one shared block across many recurrent steps, so depth becomes a quantity that can be spent at runtime rather than a stack of distinct layers. Those extra steps help only if a write made on one recurrent step is still readable on later ones, and that depends on the linear map applied to the residual between recurrent steps. We show that whether the loop learns to track state is a geometric question about the form of this map, and we propose blockwise conformal injections: the residual splits into coordinate-aligned groups and rotates inside each one, so every group has its own timescale while the geometry stays exact within it. Our experiments show a performance increase on a demanding form of state tracking - last-token accuracy when the rest of the prefix is already correct - and on language modelling, where a signal written earlier has to be retrieved at the end of the sequence.

</details>
