### [149] [Pick the Mixture, Not the Samples: Combinatorial Data Selection for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14028)
*Luyang Zhang,Jialu Wang,Bebei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出组合数据选择（CDS）框架及Mixture-then-Select算法，通过先分配领域配额再领域内排序，有效利用跨领域互补性并减少冗余，在指令微调数据池上比强基线提升20-54%，且理论保证选择差距与数据池规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法忽略了两个事实：数据池来自多个不同领域，以及评估任务多样，导致同一样本对不同任务价值不同，且样本在训练中相互作用使得子集价值不可简单加和。

Method: Mixture-then-Select为两阶段数据选择算法。第一阶段：根据数据池的领域划分，决定从每个领域抽取的样本数量（分配阶段）。第二阶段：在每个领域内，根据给定的归因方法（如影响函数等）对样本打分并排序，选取前k个样本。该设计基于对数据池的观测：跨领域存在互补性（联合效用超过单例之和），领域内存在冗余。算法将样本选择分解为领域级分配和样本级排序，从而避免直接组合优化的复杂度。理论分析表明，该算法与最优子集的差距随选择大小线性增长，且独立于数据池大小。此外，方法可扩展至预算约束下的数据购买场景。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "20% 至 54%", "setting": "真实指令微调数据池，与强基线对比"}, {"metric": "鲁棒性", "value": "跨归因方法、不同模型和数据池大小均有效", "setting": "多种配置实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化组合数据选择（CDS）框架，将归因（样本打分）与选择（挑选子集）分离，并建模样本间的非可加交互。；提出Mixture-then-Select两阶段算法：先决定各领域采样数量，再基于归因分数在领域内排序样本。；理论证明Mixture-then-Select与最优子集的差距随选择大小线性增长且与数据池大小无关。；在真实指令微调数据池上发现跨领域互补性（联合效用超过单例之和）和领域内冗余，并扩展至预算约束下的数据购买问题。

Abstract: Data selection for LLM fine-tuning faces two facts about real training: data pools draw from many distinct domains, and evaluation spans diverse tasks. As a result, the same sample helps different tasks differently, and samples interact during training so that a subset's value is not the sum of its parts. Current data-selection methods leave these realities largely unaddressed. We formalize them as Combinatorial Data Selection (CDS), a framework that separates attribution (how samples are scored) from selection (which subset to pick) and models the non-additive interactions among samples. On a real instruction-tuning data pool, we find substantial cross-domain complementarity (joint utility exceeds the sum of singletons), alongside redundancy within domains. Inspired by this, we propose Mixture-then-Select, a two-stage algorithm that first decides how many samples to take from each domain, then ranks samples within each domain by attribution score. Theoretically, the gap to the optimal subset is linear in the selection size and independent of data pool size. Empirically, Mixture-then-Select outperforms strong baselines by 20 to 54%, is robust across attribution methods, and works across different models and data pool sizes. The framework also extends to the data purchasing problem under a budget constraint when sample prices vary. Our findings suggest that allocating selection across domains offers a promising path toward more efficient and effective data selection for modern LLMs.

</details>


### [150] [Can We Trust This Evaluation? Instance-Level Reliability of LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14029)
*Yang Ba,Michelle V. Mancenido,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SCUR，一种无标签的实例级 LLM-as-a-judge 评估可靠性估计方法：以潜在测量模型为骨架，融合面板不确定性、测量交叉熵与扰动敏感性三路信号生成风险分，在五个真实基准上通过误差排序、选择性信任与风险分层验证，并更擅长发现多数投票难以察觉的高共识风险项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge 多评委多数投票只能给出聚合结论，无法保证单个评估实例（单条样本）的评判是否可靠，存在高共识但高风险的盲点。

Method: SCUR 以潜在测量模型为骨干：将每个被评实例视为一个待测量对象，将多个 LLM 评委的离散判决视为对同一潜在真值的重复测量，从而显式区分实例难度（真值不确定）与评委偏差/噪声。基于该测量模型，方法导出三个互补信号：(1) 面板不确定性，刻画评委间在该实例上的判决分布离散程度与潜在真值后验的方差；(2) 测量交叉熵，度量评委判决与测量模型预测分布之间的一致/失配程度；(3) 扰动敏感性，度量输入在扰动下评委判决的稳定性。三路证据被合成一个实例级风险分数，用于对实例可靠性排序。训练/拟合采用交叉拟合（cross-fitted）策略，以避免用同一批数据同时估计评委可靠性与实例风险导致的过拟合；整个流程不需要人工标签，仅依赖评委面板的判决行为。推理阶段只需收集评委判决（及扰动下的判决）即可计算风险分，属于轻量后处理，不涉及额外大模型训练。实验通过多数投票误差排序、选择性信任（按风险分保留低风险实例后的评估准确度）与风险分层验证风险分的有效性，并重点评估对高共识风险项的检测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "majority-vote error ranking / selective trust / risk stratification", "value": "在五个真实基准上优于对比的 LLM-as-a-judge 评估方法", "setting": "五个真实世界基准的选择性信任与风险分层实验"}, {"metric": "高共识风险项检测", "value": "相比多种其他 LLM-as-a-judge 评估方法更有效", "setting": "高共识风险项检测实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 SCUR（Structured Cross-fitted Uncertainty Risk）无标签实例级评估可靠性估计方法；以潜在测量模型作为测量系统分析骨架，将评委面板行为结构化建模；从模型中导出三种互补信号：面板不确定性、测量交叉熵、扰动敏感性，并合成单一风险分；采用交叉拟合（cross-fitting）避免过拟合，支持无标签场景下的可靠性排序；针对高共识风险项（多数评估方法的盲点）设计检测能力

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge evaluation commonly aggregates multiple judges' decisions into a majority vote, yet this panel decision process does not guarantee that the evaluation of an individual item is reliable. We propose Structured Cross-fitted Uncertainty Risk (SCUR), a label-free approach for estimating instance-level evaluation reliability based on the judge panel's behavior. A latent measurement model serves as the backbone for measurement system analysis, from which SCUR derives three complementary signals capturing panel uncertainty, measurement cross-entropy, and perturbation sensitivity. These three sources of evidence are combined into a risk score. We evaluate the SCUR approach on five real-world benchmarks using majority-vote error ranking, selective trust, and risk stratification. Furthermore, we show our approach to be more effective at detecting high-consensus risk items, which are the blind spots for many other LLM-as-a-judge evaluation methods.

</details>


### [151] [OPMD: On-Policy Mechanism Distillation Beyond Output Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14022)
*Yingdong Shi,Zhihong Li,Changming Li,Jingyi Yu,Quan Li,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPMD 提出在 on-policy distillation 中超越输出监督：先构造与学生计算兼容的教师中间状态，再在自生成轨迹上让学生内化这些机制级监督信号，在三个 teacher-student 设置中取得最高平均准确率，并可扩展到多教师蒸馏与跨家族 SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation（OPD）仅在输出空间用教师预测监督学生，无法监督产生这些预测的中间计算；而直接匹配中间表示又因师生表示几何不一致、且教师隐藏状态未必可被学生后续计算利用而效果有限。

Method: OPMD 是一个面向 on-policy distillation 的训练框架，不改变标准 Transformer 的推理结构。其流程为：1）学生模型在自身策略生成的 on-policy 前缀/轨迹上运行，获得学生自身的中间状态与输出；2）构造 student-compatible teacher：将教师的中间表示通过变换映射为学生下游计算可用的中间状态，以缓解师生表示空间的几何差异以及教师隐藏状态不可直接复用的问题；3）在这些 on-policy 轨迹上，用变换后的教师中间状态作为监督信号，让学生在其自身计算路径中内化这些中间状态；4）监督目标在原有输出空间蒸馏之外加入机制级（中间计算）监督，使学生不仅匹配教师的预测，还学习产生预测的内部机制。训练完成后，学生按常规自回归方式推理，不额外引入教师或变换模块。论文未披露具体变换形式、损失函数细节、模型规模或训练资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average accuracy", "value": "在全部三个 teacher-student 设置中取得所有评估蒸馏基线中的最高平均准确率（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "三个 teacher–student 蒸馏设置，与评估过的蒸馏基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 OPMD（On-Policy Mechanism Distillation），在输出监督之外迁移教师派生的中间计算（机制），并显式考虑其与学生网络的兼容性。；先构造 student-compatible teacher，将教师表示变换为学生下游计算可用的中间状态，再在 on-policy 轨迹上让学生内化这些状态。；验证机制级监督可迁移至多教师蒸馏与跨家族 SFT，并分析其改变学生中间计算与解题策略，证明传递了输出监督之外的信息。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model by supervising a student on prefixes sampled from its own policy, but its supervision remains confined to the output space. Consequently, OPD transfers what a teacher predicts without supervising the intermediate computation that produces those predictions. Simply distilling intermediate representations like representation matching is insufficient since teacher and student representations may differ geometrically, and even well-aligned hidden states need not be usable by the student's subsequent computation. We introduce On-Policy Mechanism Distillation (OPMD), which transfers teacher-derived intermediate computation while explicitly accounting for its compatibility with the student. OPMD first constructs a student-compatible teacher that transforms teacher representations into intermediate states usable by the student's downstream computation, then it internalizes these states from on-policy trajectories. OPMD achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated distillation baselines in all three teacher--student settings, and generalizes to multi-teacher distillation and cross-family supervised fine-tuning. Further analyses show that mechanism supervision yields more effective reasoning and meaningfully alters the student's intermediate computation and solution strategies, supporting that it transfers information beyond output supervision alone. Together, these results establish mechanism-level supervision as an effective way to move on-policy distillation beyond output supervision, enabling students to learn inner mechanisms they can use and internalize.

</details>


### [152] [Coding LLMs Can Be Harnessed to Learn Interpretable Models from Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14006)
*Aditya Kumar,Cassandra A. Cohen,William W. Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A harness repeatedly prompts a coding LLM to learn interpretable rule-set classifiers, matching or beating rule-learning baselines on tabular data and winning by large margins on text, and introduces interpretable-classifier construction as a new coding benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Can coding LLMs be harnessed to learn compact, interpretable classifiers-as-programs from labeled data, competitively with existing rule-learning methods?

Method: The paper proposes a harness that repeatedly prompts a coding LLM to produce the next rule in a rule set, given a sample of labeled examples. Inputs are labeled examples (tabular or text) and the current partial rule set; the LLM acts as a coding agent to generate the next interpretable rule. The process iterates to build a complete rule set. Training objective is implicit: the LLM is used as a program generator rather than fine-tuned. Inference is the iterative rule construction via LLM prompting. The harness enables search over a broader hypothesis space than restricted rule-learning algorithms, producing new kinds of interpretable models. The paper evaluates test-set error against state-of-the-art rule-learning methods and another LLM-based rule learner.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "test-set error", "value": "reported as competitive with or superior to state-of-the-art rule-learning methods", "setting": "tabular data, especially small training sets"}, {"metric": "test-set error", "value": "large margins of improvement", "setting": "text data, including against another LLM-based rule learner"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: A simple repeated-prompting harness that asks a coding LLM to produce the next rule in a rule set given labeled examples.；Demonstration that this LLM-based rule learning is competitive with and often superior to state-of-the-art rule-learning methods, especially in small-data tabular settings and with large margins on text.；Introduction of a new class of coding benchmarks: construction of interpretable classifiers with quantitative output-quality metrics and mature specialist coding algorithms as baselines.

Abstract: Since compact, interpretable classifiers correspond to simple programs, it is natural to ask if LLM-based coding agents can be used to learn interpretable models from data. In this paper we present a simple harness that repeatedly prompts a coding LLM to produce the next rule in a rule set, given a sample of labeled examples. We show that this approach is competitive with, and often superior to, state-of-the-art rule-learning methods on tabular data, especially with small training sets, and wins by large margins on text, including against another LLM-based rule learner; that this approach outperforms standard harnesses, and that the approach immediately leads to new kinds of interpretable models, since coding agents are not restricted to search a highly restricted hypothesis space. In addition to the advances made in learning interpretable models, these results are of interest because they introduce a new class of coding benchmarks - construction of interpretable classifiers - which includes quantitative measurements of output quality (i.e., test-set error) and existing mature ``specialist coding algorithms'' (i.e., existing learning methods).

</details>


### [153] [SemTree: Judge-Aware Semantic Decision Trees for Inspectable and Repairable Rule Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13999)
*Jincheng Gao,Wei Ding,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemTree提出一种语义决策树框架，将规则决策建模为可插拔LLM判断器回答的自然语言问题树，执行路径可解释，支持节点级修复和LLM编辑器自动改进。在ShARC、BANKING77和中文电商审核条款上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则决策涉及多个条件、豁免和缺失信息，错误难以定位和修复；需要一种可检查、可修复的决策表示和自动修复机制。

Method: SemTree将规则决策表示为一棵语义决策树：每个节点是一个自然语言问题，附带显式的Yes/No/Unknown判断标准；一个可插拔的语义判断器（如LLM）回答节点问题；整棵树基于该判断器实际给出的答案进行编译（即分支结构根据判断器的回答动态确定）。执行路径本身即决策的推理链，因此错误可以在发生的节点处修复：审查者修正单个节点并重新执行规则；修正后的节点成为判断器的训练标签；一个工具使用型LLM编辑器在观察执行结果的同时修订问题和子树。训练目标是通过节点级修正生成监督信号来微调判断器，以及通过编辑器改进树结构。推理时，输入案例从根节点开始，由判断器回答当前节点问题，沿相应分支前进，直到到达叶子节点得到最终决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "67.70%", "setting": "ShARC测试集8276个案例，由托管判断器执行的树，平均2.99个问题/案例"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "68.23%", "setting": "ShARC测试集，直接向同一判断器询问最终标签"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "47.87%", "setting": "ShARC测试集，为不同判断器编译的早期树由该判断器执行"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "64.39%", "setting": "ShARC测试集，基于该判断器对12个问题初始池的回答编译的新树"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "82.04% → 86.16%", "setting": "BANKING77数据集，节点级修复后"}, {"metric": "假阳性数", "value": "24 → 5", "setting": "中文电商审核条款，添加显式豁免节点后"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "64.47% → 73.35%", "setting": "开发集，训练后的编辑器编辑的树"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将规则决策表示为语义决策树，节点为自然语言问题，带有显式Yes/No/Unknown标准；可插拔的语义判断器执行节点判断，分支结构基于判断器实际回答编译；执行路径即决策理由，支持节点级审查和修复；修正后的节点可作为判断器的训练标签；工具使用型LLM编辑器通过观察执行结果来修订问题和子树

Abstract: Rule decisions such as content moderation, advertising compliance, and eligibility checks combine several conditions, exemptions, and missing-information cases, and an error can be fixed only once the failing condition is known. We introduce SemTree, a framework that represents a rule decision as a semantic decision tree: each node is a natural-language question with explicit Yes/No/Unknown criteria, a pluggable semantic judge answers it, and the branching structure is compiled on the answers that this judge actually gives. The executed path is the reasoning behind every decision, so errors are repaired where they occur: a reviewer corrects a single node and replays the rule, corrected nodes become training labels for the judge, and a tool-using LLM editor revises questions and subtrees while observing execution results. On all 8,276 ShARC test cases, a tree executed by a hosted judge reaches 67.70% accuracy with 2.99 questions per case, on par with asking the same judge for the final label (68.23%); an earlier tree compiled for a different judge scores 47.87% when executed by this judge, while a new tree compiled on this judge's answers to a 12-question initial pool already scores 64.39%. Node-level repair raises BANKING77 accuracy from 82.04% to 86.16%, an explicit exemption node cuts the false positives of a Chinese e-commerce moderation clause from 24 to 5, and a trained editor raises the development accuracy of edited trees from 64.47% to 73.35%.

</details>


### [154] [GameSmith: Scaling Zero-Sum Self-Play with Synthesised Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13995)
*Weixian Waylon Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Han Zhou,Chengzu Li,Ke Wang,Fengxiang He,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GameSmith通过LLM合成并验证零和游戏，结合CRAB优势估计器与蒸馏+自博弈RL流程，将Qwen3-4B-Base的13基准平均pass@1从45.5提升至64.9，展示可扩展的LLM后训练路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于双人零和博弈的LLM后训练流程依赖少量手写游戏，且优势估计器与特定游戏绑定，导致可扩展游戏供给与大规模语料上的优势估计受限。

Method: GameSmith构建端到端LLM自我提升流程。输入为在固定模板内由LLM合成的双人零和游戏，模板固定奖励、观测与隐藏信息代码，保证可验证奖励。生成的游戏先经种子配对回放与独立重实现验证，再扩展为编码、规则和规模变体，共从三个SPIRAL模板得到26个新游戏、29个家族、336个已验证变体。训练分两阶段：首先从Qwen3-32B教师在合成游戏上的自博弈轨迹蒸馏到Qwen3-4B-Base学生模型；随后学生模型在全部游戏变体上进行自博弈强化学习，使用CRAB优势估计器。CRAB将每个决策的贡献归因于其位置期望收益的收缩估计，从而在不同游戏变体间提供统一、可用的优势信号，解决了优势估计受限于特定游戏的问题。推理时，训练后的模型在13个基准上评估，展示从游戏自博弈到通用推理的迁移。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "13-benchmark mean pass@1", "value": "45.5", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Base基线"}, {"metric": "13-benchmark mean pass@1", "value": "62.5", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Base蒸馏后，同一预算下高于三个SPIRAL游戏"}, {"metric": "13-benchmark mean pass@1", "value": "64.9", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Base自博弈强化学习（CRAB）后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LLM在固定奖励、观测和隐藏信息模板内合成游戏，并通过种子配对回放与独立重实现进行验证；生成26个新游戏、29个游戏家族、336个已验证变体；提出CRAB优势估计器，将每个决策的贡献归因于其位置期望收益的收缩估计，且在任意变体上均有定义；采用从Qwen3-32B教师自博弈蒸馏到Qwen3-4B-Base，再以CRAB进行自博弈强化学习的训练流程

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) on two-player zero-sum games provides a verifiable reward, and the gains have been shown to transfer to general reasoning. Existing pipelines, however, rely on a handful of hand-written games and on advantage estimators tied to them, so both the supply of games and the advantage estimate on a large corpus limit scaling. We present GameSmith, which scales both. An LLM synthesises games inside templates whose reward, observation, and hidden-information code is fixed. Each game is verified by seed-paired replay and by an independent re-implementation of its specification, then expanded into encoding, rule, and size variants, giving 26 new games in 29 families and 336 verified variants from the three SPIRAL templates. The model is distilled from the self-play of a larger teacher (Qwen3-32B) on these games and then trained by self-play reinforcement learning with CRAB, an advantage estimator that credits each decision against a shrinkage estimate of the expected return of its position and is defined on every variant. On Qwen3-4B-Base, distillation raises the 13-benchmark mean pass@1 from 45.5 to 62.5, above the three SPIRAL games at the same budget, and self-play with CRAB reaches 64.9. The ordering holds across models, and transfer grows with the number of game families.

</details>


### [155] [DoSA: Dots Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13990)
*Fanxu Meng,Taosong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DoSA 提出一种无损的稀疏注意力索引加速方法，利用证书机制避免对全部 key 进行完整打分，只在必要时对可能入选的候选精确计算，从而保持精确 Top-K 选择。GPU 实现使完整 Indexer 相对 SGLang 和 LiteTopK 分别最高加速 2.74 倍和 1.69 倍，端到端在 GLM-5.2 与 DeepSeek-V3.2 上 TTFT 最高加速 1.94 倍和 1.50 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力虽然只访问少量 key，但用每个 indexer head 对每个 key 打分以找到这些 key 仍然开销很大；如何在保持精确 Top-K 选择的前提下，使打分计算变为条件计算。

Method: DoSA 面向稀疏注意力中索引器（Indexer）的 Top-K 选择过程。输入是 query 与 key，由多个 indexer head 分别计算分数，最终需要选出得分最高的 K 个 key。传统做法需要对每个 key 用每个 head 计算完整分数，再排序或选择。DoSA 的核心思想是：Top-K 选择不需要完整分数，只需要足以拒绝某候选的证据即可。其流程大致为：首先通过一个种子集合（seed set）计算出一个获胜阈值的下界；然后利用非负 head 投影和联合残差界来上界正贡献，即那些可能提高候选分数的 head 的输出；同时通过 activation-informed aggregation 来上界负 head 所减去的分数幅度。综合这些界，可以构造一个证书（certificate），判断某个候选是否仍然可能进入 Top-K：如果上界低于下界阈值，则候选可被安全拒绝，无需完整打分；否则需要对候选进行完整打分。证书的精确选择条件被形式化建立，论文还分析了向量几何与选择边际（selection margin）如何影响证书的有效性。经过证书筛选后，只有仍符合条件的候选会执行完整打分；随后通过精确分数过滤进一步减少写回和选中操作的开销。最终在 GPU 上实现，将完整 Indexer 的多个步骤融合加速，并在 GLM-5.2 和 DeepSeek-V3.2 模型上评估了端到端 TTFT 的改进。整体架构是算法与系统协同设计，目标是无损保持精确 Top-K 选择的同时降低计算量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "完整 Indexer 加速比", "value": "最高 2.74x", "setting": "相对 SGLang 基线，英语、中文、代码工作负载"}, {"metric": "完整 Indexer 加速比", "value": "最高 1.69x", "setting": "相对 LiteTopK 基线，英语、中文、代码工作负载"}, {"metric": "端到端 time-to-first-token 加速比", "value": "最高 1.94x", "setting": "GLM-5.2 模型，相对两个基线之一"}, {"metric": "端到端 time-to-first-token 加速比", "value": "最高 1.50x", "setting": "DeepSeek-V3.2 模型，相对两个基线之一"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 观察到 Top-K 选择只需要足够证据来拒绝候选，而不一定需要其完整分数；利用种子集合给出获胜阈值的下界；利用非负 head 投影和联合残差界对正贡献给出上界；通过 activation-informed aggregation 界定负 head 减去的幅度；证书（certificate）引导仅对仍可能入选的候选进行完整打分；精确分数过滤进一步减少写回和选择工作；建立证书及其精确选择条件，并分析几何与选择边际对有效性的影响；GPU 实现加速完整 Indexer

Abstract: Sparse attention visits few keys, yet finding them can still require scoring every key with every indexer head. We introduce DoSA (Dots Sparse Attention), a lossless method for making this computation conditional while preserving exact token selection. Its central observation is that Top-K selection requires sufficient evidence to reject a candidate, not necessarily its full score. A seed set supplies a lower bound on the winning threshold. Nonnegative head projections and joint residual bounds upper-bound positive contributions, while activation-informed aggregation bounds the magnitude subtracted by negative heads. The certificate directs full scoring toward candidates that remain eligible; exact-score filtering further reduces writeback and selection work. We establish the certificate and its exact-selection conditions, and analyze how geometry and selection margins govern its effectiveness. Our GPU implementation accelerates the complete Indexer by up to 2.74 times over SGLang and 1.69 times over LiteTopK across English, Chinese, and code workloads. End-to-end evaluation on GLM-5.2 and DeepSeek-V3.2 achieves up to 1.94 times and 1.50 times time-to-first-token speedups over the two baselines, respectively.

</details>


### [156] [PRIME: Frequency-Aware Unified Pruning and Merging for MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13982)
*Shanfeng Huang,Rongqian Zhang,Zhou Fang,Song Xiao,Hai Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRIME compresses MoE LLMs by jointly optimizing expert pruning and merging as a global ILP with a frequency-weighted objective, showing strong accuracy retention on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B plus vLLM memory/throughput gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE models have massive parameter footprints that strain deployment memory and throughput; existing expert pruning and merging are disjoint, greedy, and prone to irreversible information loss or representational pollution.

Method: PRIME treats MoE compression as a joint pruning-and-merging optimization. It formulates the discrete assignment of experts to be pruned or merged as a global Integer Linear Programming problem. The objective is a frequency-weighted surrogate that captures expert usage frequency, so high-frequency experts are favored as centroid experts during merging. An error-bound analysis under fixed representative assignments motivates this frequency-aware weighting and shows that the formulation encourages routing stability. Instead of sequentially pruning then merging with greedy heuristics, PRIME solves the combined assignment globally, selecting which experts to remove and which to merge into shared representatives in one optimization, thereby reducing irreversible information loss and representational pollution. Experiments compress Qwen3-30B-A3B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B and evaluate retained accuracy on knowledge, coding, math, and long-context tasks, with serving benchmarks in vLLM measuring memory and throughput of the compressed models.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy retention", "value": "strong retention reported across knowledge, coding, math, and long-context tasks", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B, merged/compressed MoE models on knowledge, coding, math, long-context benchmarks"}, {"metric": "memory and throughput benefits", "value": "quantified", "setting": "serving benchmark of merged models in vLLM"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Unified prune-merge assignment formulated as a global Integer Linear Programming problem over a frequency-weighted surrogate objective；Frequency-aware objective derived from an error-bound analysis under fixed representative assignments, favoring high-frequency experts as centroids to promote routing stability；Joint selection of pruning and merging decisions that accounts for expert usage instead of treating them as separate greedy heuristics；Serving-level evaluation of compressed merged models in vLLM to quantify memory and throughput benefits

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have emerged as an effective paradigm for scaling large language models, yet their massive parameter footprint poses severe memory challenges for deployment. Existing compression methods typically treat expert pruning and merging as disjoint operations and rely on greedy heuristics, often leading to irreversible information loss or representational pollution. In this paper, we propose PRIME, a unified optimization framework for MoE compression via adaptive PRunIng and MErging. PRIME casts the discrete prune-merge assignment step as a global Integer Linear Programming over a frequency-weighted surrogate objective, jointly selecting pruning and merging decisions while accounting for expert usage. We motivate the frequency-aware objective through an error-bound analysis under fixed representative assignments, and show that the formulation favors high-frequency experts as centroids, promoting routing stability. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Qwen3.5-122B-A10B demonstrate that PRIME achieves strong accuracy retention across knowledge, coding, math, and long-context tasks, while serving benchmarks of merged models quantify the memory and throughput benefits of expert compression in vLLM.

</details>


### [157] [LACRM: LLM-Guided Mining of Directed Advertiser Competition Relationships in E-Commerce Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13959)
*Xiang Shi,Yitian Kou,Bing Li,Shengsheng Niu,Chun Gan,Linghui Meng,Jie He,Zhangang Lin,Ching Law*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LACRM提出三阶段流水线，利用LLM角色分离画像、非对称多路检索和基于拍卖行为的listwise排序，构建有向连续强度的广告主竞争图，并在京东生产A/B测试中使竞价和激励分配分别提升广告收入1.73%和1.14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商广告平台通常忽略广告主之间的外部性，而广告主实际表现受竞对影响；需要构建显式、可复用、有向且连续的广告主竞争关系图来刻画这类竞争。

Method: LACRM是一个三阶段流水线，用于构建具有连续边强度的有向广告主竞争图。第一阶段进行LLM驱动的角色分离画像，将广告主分别刻画为不同竞争角色，从而避免传统对称相似度假设，为有向关系奠定基础。第二阶段采用非对称多路检索，基于角色分离的画像分别召回已实现竞对和潜在竞对，以覆盖竞争关系的多面性和潜在性。第三阶段使用与时间拍卖行为对齐的listwise learning-to-rank模型，对候选竞争对进行排序并输出连续的竞争强度，形成最终的有向竞争图。该图随后用于指导广告竞价和激励分配决策。训练目标或优化过程未详细披露，推理或服务流程亦未具体说明。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索性能", "value": "优于强有向GNN基线和消融变体", "setting": "工业基准上的离线检索实验（未披露具体数值）"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "广告收入", "value": "提升1.73%", "setting": "生产A/B测试，LACRM引导的竞价"}, {"metric": "广告收入", "value": "提升1.14%", "setting": "生产A/B测试，激励分配"}]}

Conclusion: LLM驱动的角色分离画像，打破对称相似度假设，支持非对称多路检索以发现已实现和潜在竞对。；基于拍卖行为的listwise learning-to-rank，用于精确建模有向竞争强度并生成连续边强度。；三阶段流水线构建有向竞争图，并验证潜在竞对反映真实市场竞争而非模型伪影。；将竞争图信号用于指导竞价和激励分配，并在生产A/B测试中取得广告收入提升。

Abstract: In e-commerce advertising, ad platforms typically fail to model the externalities among advertisers when making optimization decisions: an advertiser's actual performance is largely shaped by rivals competing for the same users; these rivals typically operate in overlapping product categories and at comparable budget scales. However, capturing this rivalry as an explicit, reusable signal is challenging because it is directed, multi-faceted, and partially observable. Existing paradigms such as interaction graphs, taxonomy rules, and single-embedding models do not fully capture these properties. In this paper, we propose LACRM (LLM-driven Advertiser Competition Relationship Mining), a novel framework that constructs directed competition graphs with continuous edge intensities via a three-stage pipeline. LACRM moves beyond symmetric similarity through LLM-driven role-separated profiling, which enables asymmetric multi-route retrieval to uncover both realized and latent rivals. The framework further incorporates listwise learning-to-rank aligned with temporal auction behavior to accurately model directed competition intensity. In offline retrieval experiments on an industrial benchmark, LACRM outperforms strong directed GNN baselines and ablation variants. Multi-LLM and human studies provide complementary evidence that the recovered latent rivals reflect meaningful market competition rather than merely model-generated artifacts. In production A/B tests, LACRM-guided bidding and incentive allocation increase advertising revenue by 1.73% and 1.14%, respectively. The results demonstrate the practical value of explicit competition modeling in large-scale advertising systems.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient Resource-Shiftable RL Post-training with Generative Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16866)
*Yanfeng Zhao,Haoyang Li,Sheng Lin,Yuming Zhou,Jiawei Jiang,Bin Cui,Fangcheng Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PivotRL 提出可动态迁移资源的 RL 后训练系统，解决 rollout、GenRM 奖励评估与策略训练的 GPU 资源竞争问题，在 128-GPU 集群上相对 colocated 与 disaggregated 部署分别提升端到端吞吐 1.81-2.05 倍和 1.50-1.95 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 后训练系统（colocated 与 disaggregated）对 policy rollout、GenRM 奖励评估、policy training 三个角色采用静态资源分配，无法随各角色运行时负载变化而调整，导致资源利用率低下。

Method: PivotRL 是一个面向 RL 后训练的动态资源迁移系统。系统将 policy rollout、GenRM 奖励评估和 policy training 三个角色运行在同一集群中，但资源分配不再静态固定。核心组件包括：(1) Global Parameter Storage，为各角色提供统一的参数存储，使得角色切换或迁移时无需重新加载参数；(2) 统一的上下文迁移机制，高效地在角色之间迁移各自的运行时上下文（如 KV cache、优化器状态等）；(3) 迁移感知调度器（shift-aware scheduler），在发生资源迁移时执行序列迁移，保证正在处理的序列不丢失；(4) 规划器（planner），根据聚合吞吐收益与迁移开销的权衡，决定何时以及如何进行资源迁移。训练流程上，系统交替执行 rollout 生成、GenRM 奖励计算和策略更新，规划器持续监控各角色负载，当某角色成为瓶颈时动态重新分配 GPU 资源，从而缓解静态分配下的资源闲置。系统在 128-GPU 集群上使用四组 dense 和 MoE 模型进行端到端评估，表明资源迁移可在数秒内完成，并保持 RL 收敛。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "end-to-end throughput", "value": "1.81-2.05x", "setting": "相对于 colocated 部署，128-GPU 集群，四组 dense 与 MoE 模型"}, {"metric": "end-to-end throughput", "value": "1.50-1.95x", "setting": "相对于 disaggregated 部署，128-GPU 集群，四组 dense 与 MoE 模型"}, {"metric": "resource shifting time", "value": "within seconds", "setting": "128-GPU 集群实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于 Global Parameter Storage 的可资源迁移运行时；高效迁移角色专属上下文的统一机制；迁移感知调度器，支持迁移时的序列迁移；规划器权衡聚合吞吐收益与迁移开销，决定何时及如何迁移

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training with generative reward models (GenRM) has become a central recipe for improving large language models (LLMs), and it is also increasingly resource-intensive: policy rollout, GenRM-based reward evaluation, and policy training all compete for GPU resources. However, existing colocated and disaggregated systems rely on static resource allocation for these roles. Such allocation cannot adapt as each role's workload changes during RL and therefore leaves a role's resources underutilized. We present PivotRL, an RL post-training system that dynamically shifts resources among different roles according to their runtime demands. PivotRL introduces a resource-shiftable runtime built on a Global Parameter Storage, together with a unified mechanism that efficiently shifts role-specific context. A shift-aware scheduler enables sequence migration upon a shift, while a planner considers the aggregate throughput benefit and the shift overhead to determine when and how to shift. We evaluate PivotRL on a 128-GPU cluster using four model groups spanning dense and mixture-of-experts models. PivotRL enables resource shifting within seconds and improves end-to-end throughput by 1.81-2.05 times over colocated deployment and 1.50-1.95 times over disaggregated deployment, while preserving RL convergence.

</details>


### [159] [Mean Accuracy Is the Wrong Estimand for a Release Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16825)
*Jaewon Choi,Jihye Kang,Namhyuk Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出现有基准评估的均值精度无法支持发布决策，因为微调会导致双峰模式坍缩，高均值常伴随高失败率。通过审计2124个微调模型，作者发现基于均值精度发布会在48%的情况下违反10%部署失败预算。为此提出recipe cards框架，利用精确二项界认证重跑尾部失败率，将违例率降至0.6%，并强制样本下限和偏移拒绝机制，为LLM发布提供统计上严格的评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准报告仅给出过去种子的平均精度，无法回答发布决策所依赖的下一轮重跑灾难性尾部风险，导致高平均精度常掩盖严重失败率。

Method: Recipe cards是一个评估框架，输入为任意微调模型在多个随机种子下的评估分数（如准确率）。框架首先将发布决策形式化为对下一轮重跑时模型是否违反部署失败预算的预测。核心模块包括：1）基于二项分布的精确有限样本边界，计算在给定置信水平下尾部失败率的上界；2）样本量下界校验，若评估样本数少于22则拒绝认证；3）领域与任务偏移检测，当测试分布与参考分布不一致时显式拒绝给出通过结论。处理流程为：收集多种子评估结果，计算失败率，应用二项边界得到认证区间，最终输出通过/拒绝/无法认证三种决策。训练目标无，推理时仅需评估分数和样本量。该架构解决的问题是将发布决策从关注均值转移到尾部风险，并提供统计保证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "部署失败预算违反率", "value": "从48.0%降至0.6%", "setting": "审计2,124个微调模型，覆盖11个语言模型，10%部署失败预算"}, {"metric": "样本下限", "value": "N>=22", "setting": "数学推导的样本量要求"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 引入recipe cards评估框架，直接认证重跑尾部失败率；采用精确有限样本二项界进行尾部风险认证；强制数学样本下限N>=22，防止在薄弱证据上认证；在领域和任务偏移下明确拒绝未经验证的声明

Abstract: A release decision about a fine-tuned model is an operational commitment about the \emph{next} rerun of its training recipe. Yet standard benchmark reporting gives only the mean accuracy of past seeds. These target fundamentally different questions: release decisions depend on catastrophic tail risk, whereas standard evaluation reports only central tendency. Because fine-tuning induces bimodal mode collapse, high average accuracy frequently conceals severe failure rates. Auditing $2\,124$ fine-tuned models across eleven language models, we show that shipping models based on mean accuracy violates a $10\%$ deployment failure budget $48.0\%$ of the time. Common heuristics like $\bar b - 2\sigma$ prove equally dangerous: by underestimating variance on small samples, they become most permissive when evidence is weakest. We introduce \textbf{recipe cards}, an evaluation framework that directly certifies rerun tail failure rates using exact finite-sample binomial bounds. Recipe cards reduce held-out budget violations to $0.6\%$, enforce a mathematical sample floor ($N \ge 22$) that prevents certification on thin evidence, and explicitly refuse unverified claims under domain and task shifts.

</details>


### [160] [Efficient Preconditioned Optimization Methods with Frequent Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16792)
*Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Yibo Wang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用 frequent directions 矩阵草图加速预条件优化，提出 FTSL、FTSL-Shampoo 及单边变体，降低预条件矩阵开销并获得更紧 regret 界，对大规模模型训练优化器有潜在参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应/预条件优化方法需要维护完整预条件矩阵，在大规模训练中计算与存储开销过高，难以实用。

Method: 方法以 frequent directions（FD）矩阵草图为核心，用低秩草图矩阵近似维护预条件矩阵，从而避免 O(d^2) 的完整预条件子开销。FTSL 在 primal-dual 框架下将 FD 自适应次梯度方法结合，并显式加回 FD 在更新过程中丢弃的累积信息，以修正草图偏差，获得更紧 regret 界；随后用 doubling trick 按需要对草图规模翻倍，进一步降低时间复杂度并保持相同 regret 保证。对于矩阵值决策变量，利用其结构性质构造草图预条件子，提出 FTSL-Shampoo，将 FD 适配到 Shampoo 的二阶预条件更新并给出 primal-dual 分析。为进一步降低复杂度，设计 mirror descent 和 primal-dual 两种单边变体，仅对矩阵的一侧做草图预条件。方法还扩展到离线随机凸优化，利用目标光滑性获得更快收敛率，最后给出实用变体并在多任务上做经验验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FTSL，将 frequent directions 矩阵草图与自适应次梯度方法在 primal-dual 框架下结合，并加回 FD 丢弃的累积信息，取得更紧的 regret 界；引入 doubling trick 进一步降低 FTSL 时间复杂度且保持相同 regret 保证；针对矩阵值决策变量利用结构性质构造草图预条件子，提出 FTSL-Shampoo 并给出 primal-dual 框架下的新分析；设计 FTSL-Shampoo 的 mirror descent 与 primal-dual 单边变体，降低计算复杂度同时获得更紧 regret 界；将方法扩展到离线随机凸优化，利用光滑性获得更快收敛率

Abstract: Adaptive optimization methods have received considerable attention in recent years due to their strong empirical performance. However, they generally require maintaining a preconditioning matrix, which incurs prohibitive computational overhead in large-scale applications. To address these limitations, we investigate the acceleration of preconditioned optimization methods through frequent directions (FD), a widely used matrix sketching technique. We first propose FTSL by integrating FD with adaptive subgradient methods under the primal-dual framework and adding back the cumulative information discarded by FD. FTSL obtains a tighter regret bound than existing state-of-the-art results. We then apply a doubling trick that further reduces the time complexity of FTSL, while maintaining the same regret guarantee. We also consider optimization over matrix-valued decision variables and exploit their structural properties to construct sketched preconditioners. We develop FTSL-Shampoo by adapting FD to Shampoo and give a novel analysis under the primal-dual framework. To further mitigate its computational complexity, we design one-sided variants of FTSL-Shampoo under both mirror descent and primal-dual frameworks. Our algorithms not only reduce the computational cost of FTSL-Shampoo, but also achieve tighter regret bounds. We extend our methods to offline stochastic convex optimization and exploit smoothness to achieve faster convergence rates. Finally, we introduce practical variants of our algorithms and empirically demonstrate their efficiency and effectiveness across a broad range of tasks.

</details>


### [161] [Learning Deployable Causal Action Geometry under Temporal Non-Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16644)
*Changjian Liu,Tianyu Wang,Yuwei Xu,Xiaoxuan Deng,Zhilin Zhang,Yong Gao,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究时间非平稳下连续决策的响应学习，提出可部署因果动作几何与两阶段正交估计器，在淘宝A/B测试中实现GMV +1.16%、购买数 +1.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结果随时间漂移的观测时间序列中学习连续决策的响应规律，避免将动作效应与漂移基线混淆甚至反转动作排序；即使一步响应可识别，不受限的跨时间泛化仍是病态问题。

Method: 该框架面向连续决策的响应学习。输入端为观测时间序列，包含动作、结果以及随时间变化的基线。第一阶段使用交叉拟合获得锚定响应的试点估计，以消除过拟合和动作无关水平漂移。第二阶段在链接函数、表示和形状类上进行诚实的剖析选择（profiled selection），从而在选定的链接尺度上分离出动作响应形状。估计器通过正交化减少干扰参数影响，并具备受限目标的oracle保证；在近似持续性条件下，所得决策规则在未来时期保持近最优。最终部署的决策规则在淘宝A/B测试中验证了在线收益。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "目标与迁移行为验证", "value": "验证了所提出目标及其迁移行为", "setting": "合成数据与Dominick's数据集实验"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "GMV", "value": "+1.16%", "setting": "淘宝随机A/B测试，部署系统"}, {"metric": "购买数", "value": "+1.70%", "setting": "淘宝随机A/B测试，部署系统"}]}

Conclusion: 提出“可部署因果动作几何”：在选定链接尺度上锚定掉动作无关的水平位移，并剔除正幅度变化后保留稳定的动作-响应形状；设计两阶段正交估计器：先获得锚定响应的交叉拟合试点估计，再在链接、表示和形状类上进行诚实的剖析选择；证明该估计器对受限目标具有oracle保证，并在近似持续性下产生未来时期近最优的决策规则；在合成数据、Dominick's数据和淘宝随机A/B测试中验证该目标及其迁移行为

Abstract: Learning a response law for continuous decisions from observational time series is central to many real-world decision systems, yet temporal non-stationarity poses a fundamental challenge: as outcomes drift over time, naively pooled models can conflate action effects with shifting baselines and even reverse action rankings. Even when the one-step response is identifiable, unrestricted cross-time generalization remains fundamentally ill-posed. We therefore seek the part of the response that remains stable for decisions even when outcome levels do not. We propose deployable causal action geometry: the action-response shape that remains after anchoring away action-independent level shifts on a selected link scale and profiling out positive amplitude variation. Building on this target, we develop a two-stage orthogonal estimator that first obtains cross-fitted pilot estimates of anchored responses and then performs honest profiled selection over link, representation, and shape classes. The resulting estimator admits oracle guarantees for the restricted target and, under approximate persistence, produces decision rules that remain near optimal in future periods. Experiments on synthetic data and Dominick's support the proposed target and its transfer behavior. In a randomized Taobao A/B test, the deployed system improves GMV and purchase counts by \(1.16\%\) and \(1.70\%\).

</details>


### [162] [SkillScaffold: Scaffolding Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Policy-Adaptive Skill Credit Assignment and Selective Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18807)
*Wenlin Zhong,Hao Wang,Kun Yuan,Fan Yang,Jinluan Yang,Dian Jin,Xu Tan,Yiquan Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillScaffold 提出策略自适应训练脚手架，通过低成本效用估计与选择性技能注入改进LLM智能体RL，在ALFWorld、WebShop等基准上超越强基线，并支持技能内化实现无技能推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的强化学习要么默认技能注入有益，要么依赖间接反馈或昂贵的 rollout 评估来判断技能效用；而 rollout 估计在有限采样下成本高且不稳定，技能收益因候选和当前策略能力而异。

Method: SkillScaffold 是一个策略自适应训练脚手架。输入为多轮交互轨迹与可验证结果。核心模块包括：(1) Generator：产生候选技能；(2) 效用估计模块：复用 outcome-verified 训练轨迹，通过 outcome-aware 似然比较估计候选技能效用，无需候选特定环境 rollout；(3) 选择性注入机制：根据效用决定是否注入及注入哪个技能，若无候选被估计为有用则保留无技能执行；(4) Executor：执行任务并接受注入技能。训练时联合优化 Generator 与 Executor，使技能逐步适应 Executor 演化中的策略；Scaffolded RL 鼓励技能内化，使推理时可无技能运行。推理路径为无技能执行，降低依赖。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "超过强智能体RL和基于技能RL基线", "setting": "ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld、τ²-Bench 短程与长程任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 策略自适应的训练脚手架：复用 outcome-verified 训练轨迹，通过 outcome-aware 似然比较低成本估计技能效用，无需候选特定环境 rollout；基于估计效用指导是否注入及注入哪个技能，当无候选有用时保留无技能执行（选择性注入与弃用）；联合训练 Generator 与 Executor，使技能适应不断演化的执行策略；Scaffolded RL 鼓励技能内化，实现无技能推理

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) enables large language models (LLMs) to learn from multi-turn interactions with verifiable outcomes. Textual skills can improve exploration by distilling prior interactions into reusable guidance. However, existing skill-based RL approaches often either assume skill injection is beneficial or rely on indirect feedback or rollout-intensive evaluation to assess its utility. Our pilot studies show that rollout-based utility estimation for skill credit assignment is costly and unstable under limited sampling, while skill benefits vary across candidates and depend on the current policy's competence on each query. To address these challenges, we introduce SkillScaffold, a policy-adaptive training scaffold that combines low-cost skill-utility estimation with selective skill injection. By reusing outcome-verified training trajectories, SkillScaffold generates candidate skills and estimates their utility through outcome-aware likelihood comparisons, without candidate-specific environment rollouts. The resulting utility guides whether and which skill to inject, retaining skill-free execution when no candidate is estimated to help. Joint training enables the Generator to produce skills tailored to the evolving Executor. Scaffolded RL encourages skill internalization for skill-free inference. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, ScienceWorld, and $\tau^2$-Bench demonstrate gains over strong agentic-RL and skill-based RL baselines across both short- and long-horizon tasks. Ablations and further analyses support the importance of utility-based selection and abstention. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [163] [Does self-distillation improve reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18613)
*Jason Ken Adhinarta,Raina Wu,Ashutosh Sharma,Avinash Balakrishnan,Rameswar Panda,Polina Golland,Yoon Kim,Zhang-Wei Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对两种自蒸馏方法（SDPO和OPSD）在代码、科学、工具使用和数学推理任务上进行重新评估，发现其报告增益无法一致地泛化到未见问题，且主要源于过拟合或迁移已有推理行为。梯度分析表明自蒸馏梯度与策略梯度对齐较弱，质疑其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏方法（基于特权信息）报告了优于强化学习的样本效率和泛化能力，但这些增益是否在分布外评估中成立、是否真正来自特权蒸馏仍不明确。

Method: 论文未提出新架构，而是对现有自蒸馏方法进行实证分析。方法上考察了SDPO（Self-Distillation Policy Optimization）和OPSD（On-Policy Self-Distillation），它们通常以特权信息（如正确答案或环境反馈）为条件，训练模型模仿自身预定义的预测分布。输入为问题-答案对或环境状态，模型输出候选答案分布，目标是最小化模型在特权信息条件下的预测分布与自身输出分布之间的差异。训练过程为在线策略蒸馏，使用Qwen3系列和OLMo-3模型在代码、科学、工具使用和数学推理任务上进行评估。推理流程为标准自回归生成。论文还分析了自蒸馏梯度与策略梯度的余弦相似度，显示弱对齐。未涉及端到端新模块设计或系统部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统复现并评估了两种代表性自蒸馏方法SDPO和OPSD，揭示了其报告增益主要源于过拟合或迁移已有推理行为。；通过梯度对齐分析，发现自蒸馏梯度与直接优化期望奖励的策略梯度对齐较弱，质疑其作为策略改进方向的有效性。

Abstract: Self-distillation is an emerging approach that trains a model to imitate its own predictions when conditioned on privileged information, such as correct answers or environmental feedback. Recent methods have reported better sample efficiency and generalization than reinforcement learning (RL), but it remains unclear whether these gains extend beyond in-distribution evaluations and whether they stem from privileged distillation. In this paper, we revisit two representative methods, Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) and On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), across code, science, tool use, and math reasoning tasks. We find that their reported gains do not consistently generalize to unseen problems and are largely explained by overfitting or transferring existing reasoning behavior, rather than effectively using privileged information. Moreover, self-distillation gradients show weak alignment with policy gradients that directly optimize expected reward, questioning whether they provide an effective direction for policy improvement. Our experiments across Qwen3 models (1.7B-14B) and OLMo-3-7B suggest that effectively leveraging privileged information to improve reasoning remains an open challenge. Our code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [164] [StructZO: Structured Zeroth-Order Optimization for Memory-Efficient LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16451)
*Xuanlin Yang,Jia Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StructZO提出结构化零阶优化框架，通过激活诱导子空间估计和谱控制，在仅需前向传播的内存高效微调中显著缩小与一阶Adam的性能差距，在多个LLM上平均相对提升8.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化器在内存高效微调大语言模型时性能远逊于Adam等一阶优化器。

Method: StructZO框架：首先通过前向传播获取激活，构建激活诱导的信息子空间；在该子空间内进行大查询零阶梯度估计，将原始高维参数空间（维度mn）的估计问题降维至低维空间（维度mr）；然后对降维后的更新量施加谱控制（spectral control），以改善条件数和更新方向；最后将优化后的低维更新映射回原始参数空间。整个训练过程仅需前向评估，避免反向传播和优化器状态的内存开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均相对提升", "value": "8.7%", "setting": "对比最强现有零阶基线，在OPT系列和Llama3.1-8B多种下游任务上"}, {"metric": "单任务最大相对提升", "value": "26.9%", "setting": "某些任务上对比最强零阶基线"}, {"metric": "平均性能差距", "value": "与一阶Adam相差1.5%以内", "setting": "同上实验条件"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在激活诱导的信息子空间内进行大查询零阶估计；对降维后的更新应用谱控制后再映射回原参数空间；理论证明将有效零阶估计维度从mn降至mr

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models at scale is increasingly limited by the memory cost of backpropagation. Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimizers reduce this burden by relying only on forward function evaluations, but they still exhibit a substantial performance gap to first-order (FO) optimizers such as Adam. We argue that this gap does not arise solely from the absence of explicit gradients: FO fine-tuning also benefits from informative low-rank optimization geometry, large-scale signal aggregation, and well-conditioned update directions. Motivated by this, we introduce StructZO, a structured Zeroth-Order optimization framework for memory-efficient LLM fine-tuning. StructZO performs large-query ZO estimation within an activation-induced informative subspace and applies spectral control to the resulting reduced-space update before mapping it back to the original parameters. This lets ZO aggregate useful directional signals in a compact, geometry-aware space while avoiding the activation and optimizer-state memory costs of backpropagation. We show theoretically that, when the induced subspace captures concentrated optimization-relevant directions, StructZO reduces the effective ZO estimation dimension from $mn$ to $mr$, with the remaining projection error governed by the spectral tail of the activation Gram matrix. Empirically, across OPT-series and Llama3.1-8B on diverse downstreamtasks, StructZO improves over the strongest existing ZO baselines by up to $8.7%$ relative on average and up to $26.9%$ relative on individual tasks, while remaining within $1.5%$ of FO-Adam on average. These results suggest that structured and spectrally controlled ZO updates can substantially narrow the gap between ZO and FO optimization.

</details>


### [165] [Drafting Agents, Not Just Tokens: Speculative Recursive Self-Improvement for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16491)
*Xin Xu,Ke Cheng,Hao Wang,Yangru Huang,Yanghao Xiao,Zhichao Chen,Zhisheng Zhong,Xun Liu,Yi Li,Qian Li,Haibin Lv,Jun Zhang,Jie Jiang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpecRSI，在父代智能体完全评估前先草拟可执行后继，并用VATS分配预算、GVO学习改进器；在科学主干对比中超过Janus 33.3%–40.0%、超过MetaRSI-v1 1.85%–2.38%，编码验证成功率达69.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限预算下，如何平衡递归自我改进智能体的探索（继续编辑）与评估（验证暂存候选），避免窄搜索错过有效编辑序列，或过早耗尽预算在未验证的后继上。

Method: SpecRSI将智能体改进建模为在有限预算下的搜索过程。输入为当前可执行智能体及其评估信号，输出为经过完整验证的改进后继。流程：改进器（improver）先对当前智能体进行编辑，生成可执行的草拟后继（speculative successor），这些后继在父代尚未完全评估时即被创建；所有草拟后继在正式采纳前必须经过真实评估器的完整验证。VATS（Verification-Aware Tree Search）维护一棵搜索树，其中节点为智能体版本、检查为未完成的验证任务；它利用续接的预测验证回报来在“继续编辑产生新草拟”与“完成已有待验证检查”之间分配预算，从而避免将预算过早耗尽。GVO（Group Verification Optimization）在已完成的搜索轨迹上训练改进器，但真实评估器保持固定，从而学习到如何提出更可能通过验证的编辑。推理/服务路径为：在预算约束下反复迭代草拟-验证，仅当候选通过完整验证后才被采纳为新的当前智能体。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "terminal gains", "value": "超过Janus 33.3%–40.0%", "setting": "主要科学对比（main scientific comparison）"}, {"metric": "50个科学主干-任务对上的表现", "value": "全部优于Janus", "setting": "额外评估，50 scientific backbone–task pairs"}, {"metric": "macro-average system score", "value": "超过MetaRSI-v1 1.85%–2.38%", "setting": "二十个主干（twenty backbones）"}, {"metric": "coding-validation success", "value": "69.4%", "setting": "对比HGM 64.3%，target-cost index 低20.8%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Speculative Recursive Self-Improvement (SpecRSI)：在父代完全评估前先行草拟可执行后继智能体，但采纳前必须完整验证；Verification-Aware Tree Search (VATS)：使用续接的预测验证回报在进一步编辑与待验证检查之间分配预算；Group Verification Optimization (GVO)：在已完成搜索上学习改进器，同时保持真实评估器固定

Abstract: Improving an agent through successive revisions requires balancing exploration and evaluation under a limited budget. A narrow search can miss useful sequences of edits, while fully evaluating every intermediate version can exhaust the budget before those sequences yield a verified improvement. We introduce Speculative Recursive Self-Improvement (SpecRSI), which drafts executable successor agents before their predecessors are fully evaluated, while requiring complete verification before adoption. Verification-Aware Tree Search (VATS) allocates the budget between further edits and outstanding checks using the predicted verified return of a continuation. Group Verification Optimization (GVO) learns the improver from completed searches while keeping the real evaluator fixed. In the main scientific comparison, terminal gains exceed Janus by 33.3%--40.0%. Additional evaluations show that SpecRSI outperforms Janus in all 50 scientific backbone--task pairs and exceeds MetaRSI-v1 by 1.85%--2.38% in macro-average system score across twenty backbones. Coding-validation success reaches 69.4% versus HGM's 64.3% at a 20.8% lower target-cost index.

</details>


### [166] [SLEM: Spectrum-Aware Latent Expert Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16484)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Bruno Andreis,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLEM 提出无校准的专家合并框架，用 VAE 将 MoE 专家权重编码到结构化潜空间，基于潜相似度在层内与跨层分组专家，并从权重谱推导合并系数，经几何感知潜插值后解码为合并专家权重；在通用知识、数学推理与代码生成基准上取得相当或更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts 模型虽然按输入只激活部分专家以高效扩展容量，但仍需在内存中保存全部参数，导致显著内存开销；现有专家合并方法存在两个关键限制：参数空间中的直接凸插值不保证得到有意义的专家函数插值，且专家相似度或合并系数估计通常依赖校准数据，带来对校准分布的依赖性。

Method: SLEM 是一个无校准数据的专家合并框架。输入为 MoE 模型中各专家的权重；首先通过 VAE 将专家权重编码到结构化潜表示空间，得到专家权重的潜变量表示。随后在该潜空间中依据专家之间的潜相似度进行分组，分组范围同时涵盖同一层内以及跨层的专家。合并系数不是基于校准数据估计，而是从专家权重的谱（spectra）中推导得到。得到分组与合并系数后，SLEM 在潜空间执行几何感知插值，将同一组的专家表示合并为新的潜表示，最后通过 VAE 解码器将合并后的潜表示解码回参数空间，生成合并后的专家权重。该流程通过潜空间表示避免了参数空间直接凸插值不保证函数有意义插值的问题，并通过谱推导合并系数避免了对校准分布的依赖，从而在保持或提升基准性能的同时减少所需存储的专家参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "通用知识基准性能", "value": "与现有专家合并方法相比达到相当或更好", "setting": "通用知识基准，与现有 expert merging 方法对比"}, {"metric": "生成基准平均性能", "value": "更高", "setting": "数学推理与代码生成生成基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 SLEM（Spectrum-Aware Latent Expert Merging），一个无校准数据的专家合并框架；使用 VAE 在专家权重的结构化潜表示空间中进行合并，而非直接在参数空间做凸插值；基于潜相似度在层内与跨层对专家分组，并从专家权重谱中推导合并系数；进行几何感知的潜空间插值，再解码回合并后的专家权重

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale model capacity efficiently by activating only a small subset of experts for each input, while still requiring all parameters to be stored in memory, resulting in substantial memory overhead. Expert merging techniques have recently emerged as a promising compression paradigm that reduces this overhead by consolidating multiple experts into a smaller set. However, existing approaches have two key limitations: (i) direct convex interpolation in parameter space is not generally guaranteed to yield meaningful interpolation of expert functions; and (ii) estimating expert similarity or merging coefficients often relies on calibration data, introducing dependence on the calibration distribution. To address these limitations, we propose Spectrum-Aware Latent Expert Merging (SLEM), a calibration-free expert merging framework that occurs in a structured latent representation of expert weights using a VAE. SLEM groups experts within and across layers based on latent similarities and derives merging coefficients from expert weight spectra. It then performs geometry-aware latent interpolation and decodes the resulting representations into merged expert weights. Compared with existing expert merging methods, SLEM achieves comparable or better performance on general knowledge benchmarks and higher average performance on generation benchmarks involving mathematical reasoning and code generation.

</details>


### [167] [Asymptotic Risk of Learning-Rate Schedules for Least-Squares Regression with Discretely Distributed Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16518)
*Noé Colomban,Francis Bach,Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对SGD在流式最小二乘中采用常数与WSD学习率调度的最后迭代风险进行了精确的有限时间和渐近分析。结果表明，在幂律条件下，调优基学习率的WSD能以Θ(T^{-q})衰减，且任何非零cooldown都能带来多项式改善，而线性衰减最优；最优风险仅通过bT依赖于批大小与步数。该理论为LLM训练中广泛使用的WSD调度提供了严谨的数学解释和超参数设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离散分布特征（Slock模型）的流式最小二乘回归中，常数学习率与Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) 调度下SGD最后迭代的精确有限时间风险和渐近行为，以及cooldown长度、批大小与训练步数对风险的影响。

Method: 论文考虑流式最小二乘回归问题，特征服从离散分布（Slock模型），使得不同特征方向上的SGD动态解耦。分析对象为SGD的最后迭代，学习率调度包括常数和Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD)。在幂律源条件和容量条件下，作者推导了有限时间风险的精确表达式和渐近等价，重点关注风险随训练步数T的缩放规律。关键模块包括：对每个特征方向独立分析SGD迭代；利用随机矩阵理论或渐近分析工具处理最后迭代的风险；比较不同调度（常数vs. WSD）下的风险衰减指数和常数因子。主要结论是：调优基学习率后，风险以Θ(T^{-q})衰减，q与WSD的cooldown相对长度c无关，但常数调度对应更小的q；任何c>0的cooldown都带来多项式因子改善；cooldown长度只影响常数项，线性衰减(c=1)最优；最优风险仅通过bT依赖批大小和步数（直到临界批大小）。训练目标是最小化期望风险，推理即使用训练得到的模型进行预测，但论文主要关注训练动态的理论分析。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导了常数和WSD调度下SGD最后迭代的精确有限时间风险与渐近等价表达式；证明了在调优基学习率下，风险随步数T以Θ(T^{-q})衰减，指数q与cooldown相对长度c无关，但常数调度严格更差；任何固定相对长度的cooldown（即使极短）都能将风险改善一个多项式因子，且cooldown长度仅影响常数项，线性衰减(c=1)最小化该常数；揭示了最优风险仅通过bT依赖于批大小b和训练步数T（直到临界批大小）

Abstract: We study the last iterate of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) on streaming least squares with discretely distributed features (Slock's model), which decouples the dynamics across eigendirections. Under power-law source and capacity conditions, we derive the exact finite-time risk and asymptotic equivalents of constant and Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedules. With a tuned base learning-rate, the resulting scaling law decays as $\Theta(T^{-q})$, where $T$ is the horizon, and $q$ is independent of the cooldown length $c \in (0,1]$ but is strictly smaller for the constant schedule: any cooldown of fixed relative length $c > 0$, however short, improves the risk by a polynomial factor, while its length only affects the constant, which is minimized by linear decay ($c = 1$). Finally, we show that the optimal risk depends on the batch size $b$ and horizon $T$ only through $bT$, up to a critical batch size.

</details>


### [168] [LoopQuant: Accurate and Efficient Post-Training Quantization for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16368)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Xing Hu,Lifeng Liu,Guangyu Sun,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopQuant 提出面向循环语言模型（LoopLM）的循环感知后训练量化框架，通过跨循环分布规范化、交互感知精度分配和循环条件任务校准，在 Ouro-2.6B 上实现 W4A4 超过 W4A8 基线的精度，同时降低 65.3% 内存、仅增加 3.4% 存储开销并获得 1.7 倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向前馈 Transformer 的 PTQ 方法无法处理 LoopLM 因递归复用共享模块而产生的跨循环激活动态、异质循环敏感性和递归误差传播，导致低精度下性能严重下降。

Method: LoopQuant 是针对循环语言模型（LoopLM）的后训练量化框架。LoopLM 通过递归复用共享 Transformer 模块来提升参数效率，但重复计算演化中的循环状态导致激活分布跨循环动态变化、不同循环对量化敏感性异质、且误差在递归过程中传播。LoopQuant 包含三个核心组件：1）跨循环分布规范化（CLDC）：学习一个共享的、对量化友好的表示，作用于循环状态之间，在保持参数共享的前提下统一量化分布；2）交互感知的循环级精度分配（ILPA）：在固定比特预算下，通过建模任务级循环敏感性和循环间交互，为不同循环分配激活精度；3）循环条件任务校准（LCTC）：通过轻量级任务空间校准，将量化推理与低比特递归轨迹对齐，减少递归误差传播。在推理时，模型以 W4A4 低比特执行，实现内存压缩和解码加速。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能（未指明具体指标）", "value": "在 Ouro-2.6B 上 W4A4 超过所有 W4A8 基线", "setting": "LoopLM 后训练量化，W4A4 激活/权重4比特，对比 W4A8 基线"}, {"metric": "模型内存", "value": "降低 65.3%", "setting": "相对 BF16 基线，LoopQuant W4A4"}, {"metric": "额外存储开销", "value": "3.4%", "setting": "相对朴素 INT4 量化"}, {"metric": "解码速度", "value": "1.7 倍加速", "setting": "相对 BF16，LoopQuant W4A4"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 跨循环分布规范化（CLDC）：学习跨循环状态共享的量化友好表示，同时保持参数共享。；交互感知的循环级精度分配（ILPA）：在固定比特预算下，建模任务级循环敏感性与循环间交互，分配激活精度。；循环条件任务校准（LCTC）：通过轻量级任务空间校准，使量化推理对齐低比特递归轨迹。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) improve parameter efficiency by recursively reusing shared Transformer modules, yet their repeated computation over evolving recurrent states poses new challenges for low-bit deployment. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods, largely designed for feed-forward Transformers, fail to account for cross-loop activation dynamics, heterogeneous loop sensitivity, and recursive error propagation, leading to substantial performance degradation at low precision. To address these challenges, we propose LoopQuant, a loop-aware PTQ framework for accurate and efficient low-bit quantization of LoopLMs. LoopQuant is built on three core components: 1) Cross-Loop Distribution Canonicalization (CLDC) learns a shared quantization-friendly representation across recurrent states while preserving parameter sharing; 2) Interaction-Aware Loop-wise Precision Allocation (ILPA) allocates activation precision under a fixed bit budget by modeling task-level loop sensitivity and cross-loop interactions; and 3) Loop-Conditioned Task Calibration (LCTC) further aligns quantized inference with the low-bit recursive trajectory through lightweight task-space calibration. Extensive experiments across LoopLM architectures demonstrate that LoopQuant consistently outperforms existing low-bit PTQ methods. On Ouro-2.6B, LoopQuant under W4A4 surpasses all W4A8 baselines in performance, while reducing model memory by 65.3% with only 3.4% additional storage overhead over naive INT4 and achieving a 1.7 times decoding speedup over BF16. These results demonstrate a strong balance among accuracy, compression, and inference efficiency, providing a practical and deployment-friendly solution for low-bit LoopLM inference. The code is available at [ this https URL ].

</details>


### [169] [Readout Scaling Shapes Sparse Feature Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18462)
*Brain Xiao,Sarah Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究读写缩放对稀疏自编码器学习稀疏特征的影响，提出RC-DS坐标方法改善特征恢复，在语言模型检查点上验证增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 读写缩放如何影响稀疏自编码器学习到的稀疏特征恢复，以及能否通过坐标变换消除奇异值增益的影响。

Method: 论文使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为分析对象。输入为语言模型检查点的激活或logit空间。方法上，RC-DS坐标变换首先应用于输入特征，通过规范化读写矩阵的奇异值，消除读写缩放造成的增益但保留决策相关方向。SAE编码器将输入映射到稀疏隐空间，解码器重构输入。训练目标为重构误差最小化，在固定读写条件数下比较不同训练坐标系对已知因子恢复的影响。实验中还对比了中心logit训练，并使用精确包装器确保RC-DS训练与标准训练在数值上一致，以隔离几何效应。推理或评估时，测量SAE重构输出logit的保真度以及在18种任务-稀疏度设置下的增益自由重建性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均稀疏因子恢复率", "value": "0.506 提升至 0.913 (条件数 100)", "setting": "RC-DS 与中心logit训练在相同预算下对比"}, {"metric": "平均增益自由重建", "value": "在18个任务-稀疏度设置中全部更高", "setting": "三个固定语言模型检查点"}, {"metric": "输出logit重建差距", "value": "0.0198 降低至 0.0016", "setting": "小标签头控制实验，延长训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出读写规范化决策空间（RC-DS）坐标，在保留决策信息的同时移除奇异值增益。；通过受控的坐标匹配实验证明恢复效果最好时训练恢复因子生成所在的空间。；设计精确包装器以数值精度复现RC-DS训练，隔离训练几何效应而非信息增益。

Abstract: Readout scaling changes what a sparse autoencoder (SAE) learns, but the benefit of removing it depends on the data. We use readout-canonicalized decision-space (RC-DS) coordinates to remove singular-value gains while preserving retained decision information. At a fixed readout condition number, changing the generating distribution reverses which training coordinates recover known factors best. In our controlled coordinate-matching study, recovery is highest when training restores the space in which the factors were generated. When factors are generated before readout scaling, RC-DS increases mean sparse-factor recovery from 0.506 to 0.913 at condition number 100, compared with centered-logit training under the same budget. An exact wrapper reproduces RC-DS training to numerical precision, isolating training geometry rather than additional information. In neural policies, RC-DS improves mean logit reconstruction under the tested budgets. On three fixed language-model checkpoints, RC-DS has higher mean gain-free reconstruction in all 18 tested task-sparsity settings, while centered-logit SAEs reconstruct output logits better in 17. Small-label-head controls show that longer training reduces the reconstruction gap from 0.0198 to 0.0016. Together, these results show how readout scaling, the data distribution, and training shape sparse learning. RC-DS provides an information-preserving method for studying and controlling the readout's contribution. Separate label-detection and steering tests distinguish these reconstruction effects from task-specific feature usefulness.

</details>


### [170] [PRISM: Calibrating Black-Box Judges with Counterfactual Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18389)
*Kakei Yamamoto,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM 提出一种无 rubric 的 LLM 评委校准方法：固定内容不变，通过强调/弱化属性探测评委分数变化，将响应建模为代理 Jacobian，并用 Ridge 回归在人类标签上学习修正。在 SummEval 和 HANNA 上显著提升与人类排序的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 评委的隐式评分偏差会导致其分数与人类判断系统性不一致，且传统 rubric 方法需要显式准则级评分，成本高且可能无法覆盖全部偏差。

Method: PRISM 首先固定被评估内容，对同一回复施加显式的评价偏差：强调或弱化某些属性（如风格、质量），并记录 LLM 评委在这些扰动下的分数变化。这些响应被表示为一个代理 Jacobian，用于刻画评委对属性扰动的局部敏感度。随后，以未校准的基线分数为基准，使用 Ridge 回归在人类标注数据上学习一个线性修正项，将代理 Jacobian 特征映射为分数修正量。最终评分协议不依赖 rubric，仅使用总体分数，无需准则级评分。训练目标是最小化修正后分数与人类排序/评分之间的一致性损失（具体损失函数未在摘要中说明）。推理时，对给定回复执行基线打分和扰动探针打分，获得 Jacobian 特征后经 Ridge 回归修正，输出校准后的分数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "与人类排序的一致性", "value": "显著优于未校准分数，并与基于 rubric 的方法具有竞争力", "setting": "SummEval 摘要任务与 HANNA 故事评估任务，Jev 和 Sol 评委对比"}, {"metric": "与人类排序的一致性", "value": "rubric 导出的质量探针与风格探针表现相似", "setting": "受控 SummEval 对比实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 PRISM：在保持被评估内容不变的前提下，通过强调/弱化属性来测量评委分数变化，将评委对显式评价偏差的响应作为校准信号。；用局部敏感度模型将响应表示为代理 Jacobian，并用 Ridge 回归在人类标签上学习对基线分数的修正。；无 rubric 评分协议：仅使用总体分数，不要求准则级评分，仍能改善与人类排序的一致性。；发现不同评判模型之间存在共享和相反的修正方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as judges to reduce reliance on costly human evaluation, yet their scores can systematically disagree with human judgments. We ask whether responses to an explicitly imposed evaluative bias can help correct a judge's implicit scoring biases. We introduce PRISM (Probe-based Residual Inference of Scoring Misalignment), which holds the evaluated content fixed and measures score changes when attributes are emphasized or de-emphasized. A local sensitivity model motivates representing these responses as a surrogate Jacobian. Ridge regression on human labels learns a correction to the baseline score. The resulting scoring protocol is rubric-free: it uses overall scores without requesting criterion-level ratings. In the main Jev and Sol comparisons on SummEval summarization and HANNA story evaluation, PRISM and its learned-baseline variant significantly improve agreement with human rankings over uncalibrated scores while remaining competitive with rubric-based methods. A controlled SummEval comparison yields similar agreement for rubric-derived quality probes and style probes, supporting calibration without reproducing rubric criteria. The learned coefficients reveal shared and opposing correction directions across judges.

</details>


### [171] [LoOPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation with Looped Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18162)
*Wei Fan,Qingyue Yang,Chao-Hong Tan,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Xiangang Li,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoOPSD 提出通过搜索模型自身的层执行顺序（重复层）构建更强教师，并将教师优势蒸馏到学生模型中。在 GSM8K 上，Qwen3-1.7B 的准确率从 42.30% 提升至 79.61%（400 token 思考预算），且多轮蒸馏可持续提升。该方法无需外部教师或额外上下文，为自蒸馏提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不需要外部更强教师或额外上下文的情况下，通过模型自身计算（层执行顺序）构建更强教师，并将优势蒸馏回学生模型，从而提升推理能力。

Method: LoOPSD 的端到端架构如下：输入为数学和逻辑推理任务的问题文本，学生模型为 Qwen3-1.7B。主要模块包括：（1）教师构建模块：固定模型权重，在层的执行顺序空间中进行搜索，选择需要重复执行的层，形成循环执行路径，从而构建一个比标准前向传播更强的教师模型。（2）学生蒸馏模块：学生模型在生成前缀（on-policy 轨迹）时，教师模型对相同前缀给出预测，学生通过最小化与教师预测的差异进行学习。（3）交替优化：完成一轮蒸馏后，重建教师模型基于更新后的学生重新搜索层执行顺序，重复搜索-蒸馏过程。训练目标为蒸馏损失（如 KL 散度或交叉熵），优化学生参数。推理时，学生模型使用标准前向传播（不循环层），因此不增加推理成本。该架构解决了自蒸馏中缺乏更强教师的问题，利用模型自身层的额外计算作为监督信号。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "从 42.30% 提升至 79.61%", "setting": "GSM8K 数据集，Qwen3-1.7B 模型，400 token 思考预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过搜索层重复顺序构建教师模型，保持权重不变，仅改变层执行顺序。；交替执行教师搜索与学生蒸馏，每轮从更新后的学生重建教师，逐步吸收替代层执行带来的增益。；利用学生生成的前缀上教师的预测来指导学习，实现 on-policy 自蒸馏。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation improves a model using guidance from the model itself, but it still requires a teacher that provides more useful predictions than the student. Existing approaches obtain such guidance by giving the teacher additional context or instructions. We ask whether it can instead come from changing how the same model uses its own layers. To address this, we introduce LoOPSD, which searches over a model's layer execution order to construct a stronger teacher and distills its advantage into the student. Specifically, we keep the model weights unchanged while searching for layers to repeat, and use the resulting teacher's predictions on student-generated prefixes to guide learning. We then alternate search and distillation, rebuilding the teacher from the updated student in each round so that the student progressively absorbs the gains from alternative layer execution. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning tasks show improvements across models. On GSM8K with a 400-token thinking budget, LoOPSD raises Qwen3-1.7B accuracy from 42.30% to 79.61%; subsequent distillation rounds yield further gains beyond the first round. Together, these findings show that by searching layer execution to construct teachers, we can turn a model's additional computation into improved student capabilities, opening a new avenue for self-distillation to derive supervision from the model's own computation.

</details>


### [172] [inLoRA-MINT: Internal Representations for Auditing Training Data in Domain-adapted LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18051)
*Gonzalo Mancera,Julian Fierrez,Aythami Morales,Ruben Tolosana,Daniel DeAlcala,Francisco Jurado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 LoRA-MINT 黑盒困惑度框架上引入中间激活与参数相关信号，训练 MINT 分类器审计样本是否被用于 LoRA 微调；在 Qwen、Phi-4、DeepSeek-V3、LLaMA 3.2-3B 与医学/数学/学术推理数据集上达到 95%-98% 准确率，面向数据隐私与 AI 合规审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑盒或半黑盒条件下判断某条样本是否被用于 LoRA 微调，用于数据隐私、泄漏检测与训练数据溯源审计。

Method: 方法在 LoRA 微调的 LLM 上执行成员推断测试。输入为待审计样本，先经被审计的 LoRA 微调模型前向计算，除输出困惑度外，还抽取中间模型表征，包括各层激活以及与 LoRA 参数相关的信号。这些中间表征与困惑度特征一并输入一个 MINT 分类器，由该分类器判定样本属于训练集还是非训练集。分类器训练阶段利用已知属于训练集的样本与已知非训练样本构造监督信号，推理阶段仅需访问被审计模型的前向输出与中间激活，而不需要完整模型参数，从而保持相对黑盒的审计设定。该方法在四个 LLM 与三个领域数据集上验证了跨架构、跨领域的稳定判别性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MINT 分类准确率", "value": "95%-98%", "setting": "在 4 个 LLM（Qwen、Phi-4、DeepSeek-V3、LLaMA 3.2-3B）上做 LoRA 微调，覆盖医学、数学、学术推理 3 个数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在已有仅依赖困惑度的 LoRA-MINT 黑盒框架之上，引入中间模型表征（激活与参数相关信号）作为审计特征。；这些表征使 MINT 分类器无需完整访问模型参数即可区分训练样本与非训练样本。；跨 Qwen、Phi-4、DeepSeek-V3、LLaMA 3.2-3B 四类架构与医学、数学、学术推理三领域数据集验证了方法的稳定性。；方法论可扩展到其他 LLM 适配技术及更广泛的 AI 模型审计。

Abstract: This work investigates the application of the Membership Inference Test (MINT) to large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned with LoRA, aiming to determine whether specific samples were used during training and address concerns about data privacy, leakage, and transparency. Building on our previous LoRA-MINT framework, which relies exclusively on perplexity under a strict black-box setting, we propose an approach based on intermediate model representations, including activations and parameter-dependent signals. These representations enable a MINT classifier to distinguish training from non-training samples without requiring full access to model parameters. We evaluated the method on four LLMs (Qwen, Phi-4, DeepSeek-V3, and LLaMA 3.2-3B) fine-tuned on three datasets covering medical, mathematical, and academic reasoning tasks. Results show consistent performance across architectures and domains, achieving scores between 95% and 98%, with robustness to variations in model configurations and data distributions. The proposed approach enables efficient auditing of sensitive training data and supports data provenance and compliance with emerging privacy and AI governance regulations. Although our experiments focus on LoRA-based fine-tuning, the methodology can be extended to other LLM adaptation techniques and, more broadly, to other AI models.

</details>


### [173] [Checking the Checkers: A Synthesis of Verifier Noise and Robustness in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17824)
*Savyasachi Gupta,Aniruddha Srinivas Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于 RLVR 验证器噪声与鲁棒性的综述，系统比较 58 项研究，指出错误结构比错误率更关键，并提出可比较的报告协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 依赖验证器正确性，但规则检查器、单元测试与 LLM 评判器都会产生错误，论文探究这些验证器错误的结构、影响与缓解方式。

Method: 该文为综述性工作，无单一端到端模型架构。其分析框架围绕 RLVR 训练回路：策略 LLM 生成响应，验证器（规则检查器、单元测试、LLM 评判器、rubric 或自生成奖励）对响应打分形成奖励，策略据此更新。论文按验证器类型与错误类型（假阳性/假阴性、随机/系统性）组织证据，比较不同缓解手段（如 KL 正则、预算约束、奖励塑形、集成验证等）在何种错误结构下有效，并讨论错误分布随策略提升而漂移的现象。最后提出报告协议以规范验证器噪声的度量与实验对比，未涉及具体模型规模或服务化推理路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统综述 58 项关于验证器错误测量、分析与缓解的研究；提出错误结构（如可被策略触发的假阳性）比错误率更重要的结论；汇总比较表格并调和文献中的表面矛盾；提出使未来结果可比较的验证器噪声报告协议

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a principal recipe for improving the reasoning of large language models (LLMs). It rests on the assumption that the checker scoring each response is itself correct. In practice, rule-based checkers reject correct answers in unexpected formats, unit tests accept incorrect programs, and LLM judges can be led to approve empty or adversarial responses. This work surveys 58 studies that measure, analyze, or mitigate such verifier errors. We ask what errors verifiers make, how they affect training, which mitigations work against which errors, and how the findings extend to self-generated, rubric- and judge-based, and verifier-free rewards. The evidence suggests that the structure of an error matters more than its rate. Random errors mostly slow learning, though under a fixed budget or KL regularization they also lower final accuracy. False positives that the policy can learn to trigger can cause plateaus or collapse, sometimes even with a low initial error rate. Whether a verifier wrongly rejects or accepts depends on what it checks and who built it, not on its family. Its errors shift as the policy improves, and most mitigations have been tested against only one kind of error. We compile the findings into comparison tables, reconcile apparent conflicts in the literature, and propose a reporting protocol to make future results comparable.

</details>


### [174] [HaloBlock: Faster and Better Decoding for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17948)
*Zhaohui Zheng,Chenhang He,Jinrui Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HaloBlock is an inference-time decoding method for diffusion large language models that uses bidirectional cumulative entropy to adaptively allocate halo regions around blocks, reducing boundary discontinuity and decoding difficulty. It improves accuracy and efficiency on math and code benchmarks across LLaDA-8B, Dream-7B, and LLaDA-1.5 without additional training.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Block-wise decoding in diffusion large language models suffers from high decoding difficulty for right-side tokens and boundary contextual discontinuity, leading to lower generation quality and slower convergence.

Method: HaloBlock operates during inference of diffusion large language models (dLLMs). The input is a sequence processed in blocks. For each block, the method computes bidirectional cumulative entropy over tokens to quantify the difficulty of decoding that block, especially the right-side tokens which are known to be harder. Based on this difficulty, it adaptively assigns a halo region on both sides of the block. The halo region provides additional context tokens from neighboring blocks, which are included during the denoising steps for the current block. This mitigates the boundary discontinuity that arises when blocks are decoded independently. The decoding process then proceeds with this expanded context, allowing bidirectional attention to leverage information from the halo tokens. The method does not require additional training; it is a plug-in for existing dLLM decoding pipelines. The adaptive allocation of halo size according to difficulty balances quality and efficiency: harder blocks receive larger halos, while easier blocks use smaller halos. By smoothing cross-block context propagation, the decoding difficulty for hard tokens is reduced, leading to better generation quality and faster convergence (fewer denoising steps). The paper evaluates on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B-Instruct, and LLaDA-1.5 across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, showing consistent accuracy improvements and higher inference efficiency.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "consistent improvements", "setting": "mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B-Instruct, and LLaDA-1.5"}, {"metric": "inference efficiency", "value": "higher efficiency", "setting": "same benchmarks and models"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Bidirectional cumulative entropy to characterize block decoding difficulty；Difficulty-aware adaptive halo mechanism that allocates bidirectional halo regions around each block to mitigate boundary context discontinuity；Smoothing cross-block context propagation to reduce decoding difficulty for hard tokens

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have attracted increasing research attention due to their bidirectional attention and arbitrary order decoding. However, there are two issues in dLLMs inference: the decoding difficulty is dominated by the tokens on the right side of the block, and the block-wise decoding inherently suffers from boundary contextual discontinuity. To tackle these limitations, we propose HaloBlock, a difficulty-aware adaptive halo mechanism for dLLM inference. We first leverage bidirectional cumulative entropy to characterize block decoding difficulty. Then, based on the bidirectional decoding difficulty, we adaptively allocate bidirectional halo regions on both sides of each block to mitigate boundary contextual discontinuity. The difficulty-aware design of HaloBlock smooths cross-block context propagation and lowers decoding difficulty for hard tokens, yielding better generation quality and faster convergence. Evaluated on LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B-Instruct and LLaDA-1.5 across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, HaloBlock demonstrates consistent accuracy improvements and higher inference efficiency. Source codes are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [175] [SelectTTRL: Representation-Guided Data Selection for Efficient Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16104)
*Yi Li,Linan Yue,Weibo Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectTTRL通过内部响应表征与正确性方向的余弦相似度（COS）来预筛选对测试时强化学习（TTRL）训练效用高的测试问题，仅对选定百分位区间的问题执行昂贵的多响应采样与RL更新，从而在多个推理基准上大幅降低测试时计算，同时保持或提升标准TTRL的性能。论文还提供了理论分析支持COS与问题可解性的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无真实答案的情况下，能否在昂贵的测试时训练之前识别出对TTRL训练效用高的测试问题，以避免对所有问题执行相同的多响应采样与强化学习更新，从而降低测试时计算。

Method: SelectTTRL在标准TTRL流程前增加一个基于内部表征的数据选择模块。输入为无标注测试问题集合，首先对每个问题，模型通过采样多个响应（标准TTRL所需）或仅需一次前向得到响应表征。利用一个独立问答数据集构建正确性方向：从该数据集提取问题对应响应表征与正确性标签，训练一个线性方向（正确性方向）。对每个测试问题，计算其响应表征与正确性方向的余弦相似度，即COS。根据COS对测试问题进行排序，选取特定百分位区间内的子集。仅对选中的子集执行标准TTRL：采样多个响应，基于多数投票等构造伪标签，并执行强化学习更新。未选中的问题跳过TTRL的多响应采样与RL更新。训练目标与TTRL一致，即在伪标签上优化策略。推理时，模型仅在选中问题上进行测试时自适应，最终在全部测试问题（或任务评估集）上评估。该设计不修改TTRL核心算法，仅通过表征引导的数据筛选降低测试时计算，同时通过选择高训练效用的样本维持或提升性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现并验证内部响应表征与问题级经验准确率之间的稳定关系，定义COS（响应表征与从独立问答数据集构建的正确性方向的余弦相似度）作为TTRL训练效用代理。；提出SelectTTRL：基于COS对测试问题排序，仅对选定的百分位区间应用标准TTRL，其余问题进行多响应采样与RL更新，不改变TTRL原始流程。；提供理论分析解释COS与问题可解性之间的关系。

Abstract: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning (TTRL) enables large language models to adapt directly on unlabeled test problems by constructing pseudo-labels from multiple sampled responses. However, existing TTRL methods typically apply the same expensive sampling and optimization procedure to all test problems, despite substantial differences in their training utility. In this paper, we study whether such problems can be identified before costly test-time training, without access to ground-truth answers. We uncover a stable relationship between internal response representations and problem-level empirical accuracy. Specifically, we define COS as the cosine similarity between a model's response representation and a correctness-related direction constructed from an independent question-answering dataset. COS consistently correlates with problem solvability across different models, datasets, and experimental settings, and we further provide a theoretical analysis explaining this relationship. Building on this finding, we propose SelectTTRL, a representation-guided data selection method that ranks test problems using COS and applies TTRL only to a selected percentile interval. SelectTTRL leaves the standard TTRL pipeline unchanged for the selected problems, while avoiding unnecessary multi-response sampling and reinforcement learning updates on the remaining ones. Experiments across multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SelectTTRL substantially reduces test-time computation while maintaining or improving the performance of standard TTRL. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Policy Rollouts: Embedding-Space Directional Exploration with Anchored Noise for LLM RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16105)
*Daijiao Liu,Huiyi Wang,Lina Yao,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDEN 在 RLVR 的 rollout 源头进行干预：在同一 rollout 预算内，将每组少量标准 rollout 替换为从「prompt 加一个带高斯噪声的附加 embedding 位置」生成的 rollout，并以共享锚点提供组内共同调节方向，扰动 rollout 以降权优势参与 GRPO 更新。模型架构与测试时推理不变，在多个模型规模和数学推理基准上优于 GRPO 与同 pipeline 的探索控制方法，采样解覆盖率增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 RLVR 中使用 GRPO 等组相对方法时，每个 prompt 的 rollout 组通常较小，同一策略重复采样会产生高度相似的轨迹，导致组内奖励方差低、组相对优势信号弱，探索不足；现有方法（提高温度、熵奖励、非对称裁剪、增大 rollout 预算）未改变 rollout 的条件分布。

Method: EDEN 是在 RLVR 训练阶段对 rollout 过程进行干预的方法，不改变模型本身架构与测试时推理。输入侧：对一个 prompt 的每个 rollout 组，标准 rollout 仍从 prompt 的 token 序列输入策略生成；同时将一小部分（小比例）标准 rollout 替换为「prompt 加一个附加的、被扰动的 embedding 位置」作为条件输入，由同一策略生成。扰动方式：向该附加 embedding 位置加入高斯噪声以驱动变化，同时在同一 prompt 组内共享一个锚点（anchor），使扰动 rollout 具有共同的调节方向，从而在组内产生有方向性的差异而非纯随机发散。训练目标与更新：仍然使用 GRPO 的组相对优势估计与更新；被选中的扰动 rollout 参与 GRPO 更新，但其优势值被降权（downweighted advantages），避免扰动样本过度影响策略更新。输出与推理：模型架构、离散解码过程和测试时推理路径保持不变。该方法通过改变 rollout 的条件分布，在同一 rollout 预算下增加组内奖励变化与有非零组相对学习信号的 prompt 组数量，从而增强探索。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学推理基准上的表现", "value": "优于 GRPO 及同一 pipeline 中的探索控制方法，在采样解覆盖率上增益最大", "setting": "多个模型规模与数学推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在 rollout 源头进行干预：用「prompt 加一个附加的扰动 embedding 位置」来生成部分 rollout，而非仅从 prompt 本身采样，从而探索策略自身 rollout 之外的空间。；高斯噪声提供变化性，同时使用共享锚点（anchored）在每个 prompt 组内提供共同的调节方向，噪声与锚定互补。；被选中的扰动 rollout 以降低权重的优势值参与 GRPO 更新，模型架构、离散解码和测试时推理流程保持不变。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically uses small rollout groups per prompt, where repeated sampling from the same policy can yield highly similar trajectories, reducing within-group reward variation and weakening group-relative advantage signals. Existing remedies such as higher sampling temperature, entropy bonuses, asymmetric clipping, and larger rollout budgets broaden sampling or modify policy updates but leave rollout conditioning unchanged. In this paper, we propose EDEN (Embedding-space Directional Exploration via anchored Noise), which intervenes at the rollout source through anchored input perturbations. EDEN explores beyond the policy's own rollouts. Within the same rollout budget, it replaces a small fraction of standard rollouts in each group with rollouts that the same policy generates from the prompt plus a single appended, perturbed embedding position, rather than from the prompt alone. Gaussian noise drives variation, while a shared anchor provides a common conditioning direction within each prompt group. Selected perturbed rollouts contribute to GRPO updates with downweighted advantages, and the model architecture, discrete decoding, and test-time inference remain unchanged. Across model scales and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, EDEN outperforms GRPO and the exploration controls evaluated in the same pipeline, with the largest gains in sampled solution coverage. Analyses show that more prompt groups provide non-zero group-relative learning signals and that noise and anchoring play complementary roles. Intervening at the rollout source is thus an effective lever for exploration in RLVR.

</details>


### [177] [BARTER: Compact Dynamic Sparse Training with Standard Dense Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16147)
*Yueyuan Sui,Payal Mohapatra,Qi Zhu,Stephen Xia*

Main category: cs.LG
