Repeated retrieval and write-back can let one historical claim occupy multiple retrieval positions and influence current answers without independent evidence. Existing work examines retrieval, updating, and question answering, leaving same-source record accumulation and post-correction persistence underexplored. Motivated by research in social cognition and memory psychology on repeated exposure, retrieval, and revision, we define \emph{Memory Echo} as the excess retrieval exposure gained by historical information from the same source without additional independent evidence, and introduce MEchoBench to evaluate its emergence, correction resistance, persistence, and effect on answers. To address this phenomenon, we introduce the Family--Provenance--Authority framework (FPA). Specifically, FPA selects one representative per source family, conditions the eligibility of old records for the answer context on verified corrections, and constrains final answers with authoritative evidence. In paired replay on 190 fixed questions across four memory frameworks, FPA raises accuracy from 0.825 to 0.920 with DeepSeek V4 Flash and from 0.782 to 0.916 with Kimi K2.6; mean Decision Contamination Effect falls from 0.106 to 0.030 and from 0.109 to 0.038, respectively. These results show that agent memory can preserve traceable history without allowing repeated historical information to govern current decisions. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [205] [DuplexAct: How Interactive Are Frontier Full-Duplex Spoken Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17738)
*Jiaheng Dong,Shihong Tan,Gongping Huang,Jean Honorio,Abhirup Ghosh,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuplexAct提出面向端到端全双工语音语言模型的交互动作级评测基准，含三层动作分类（8动作、24条件）、即时执行与后续跟进两类成功指标，以及4691条覆盖闲聊/会议/客服/访谈的测试实例，其中2531条来自真实录音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏结构化的动作级公式化，常关注动作是否发生而非是否成功，且对真实世界对话覆盖有限，无法系统评估E2E全双工语音语言模型在对话中采取适当交互动作的能力

Method: DuplexAct是一个评测基准而非模型架构。其端到端流程为：输入为完整语音对话上下文（包括模型自身与用户的语音流），要求E2E全双工语音语言模型在持续聆听与说话的同时，在对话演进中决定并执行适当的交互动作。基准构建三层动作分类体系：顶层为交互动作类别，向下细化为8种交互动作与24种对应的交互条件。评测时将模型输出映射到该动作空间，并针对每个动作分别计算两类指标：即时执行（是否在正确时机触发动作）与后续跟进（动作执行后的持续行为是否符合预期）。测试集包含4691条实例，覆盖闲聊、会议、客服、访谈四类真实场景，其中2531条来自真实对话录音，以保证真实世界接地性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出DuplexAct基准，定义三层动作分类体系，涵盖8种交互动作与24种交互条件；通过即时执行与后续跟进两个维度刻画成功动作，并开发动作专属评测指标；构建4691条测试实例，覆盖闲聊、会议、客服、访谈四类场景，其中2531条源于真实对话录音

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) full-duplex spoken language models (FD-SLMs) enable simutanous listening and speaking continously, requiring models to take appropriate interaction actions as conversations unfold. However, existing benchmarks lack a structured action-level formulation, often evaluate action occurrence rather than success, and provide limited coverage of diverse real-world conversations. We introduce DuplexAct, a benchmark for evaluating interaction action taking in E2E FD-SLMs. DuplexAct defines a three-level action taxonomy spanning eight interaction actions and 24 corresponding interaction conditions. We further characterize successful action-taking through immediate execution and subsequent follow-through, and develop action-specific evaluation metrics for both. DuplexAct contains 4,691 test instances spanning casual conversation, meetings, customer service, and interviews, including 2,531 instances derived from real-world conversational recordings. Together, DuplexAct provides a structured and real-world-grounded framework for systematically assessing how E2E FD-SLMs participate in ongoing spoken interaction.

</details>


### [206] [Evidence-First Agentic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17724)
*Reza Sanayei,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Sunjie Hou,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Haris Riaz,Robert Vacareanu,Minglai Yang,Ying Liu,Mihai Surdeanu,Daniel Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sauron 是一个证据优先的智能体验证器，通过同时调查环境状态与执行轨迹并对声明进行独立验证，为评分标准判定提供可审计证据。在 2,586 条人工标注标准上，它于所有 8 个主干模型上取得最高的 F1，并在成本上低于最强基线 Gandalf；在故障注入套件中能检测 97% 的注入故障且仅误报 2%，在 TRAIL 基准上领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、半可验证的智能体任务依赖评分标准评估，但证据可能散布在环境状态与执行轨迹中，评判者容易忽略证据或接受无依据的声明（包括智能体自报成功），当评分标准判定被用作强化学习奖励时会强化不良行为。

Method: Sauron 是一个证据优先的智能体验证器。其输入包括环境状态与智能体的执行轨迹。核心流程为：首先对评分标准中的声明（claim）进行识别；然后通过有界查询（bounded queries）从状态和轨迹中检索支持证据；对于相关标准进行联合调查，以减少上下文使用和冗余工作；仅在独立验证声明与其来源一致后才采信该声明。最终每个评分标准判定都附带可审计的证据回执（auditable receipt），记录支持该判定的证据。论文未披露具体的模型结构、训练目标或服务部署细节。验证结果可用于在强化学习中作为奖励信号，防止因评判错误而强化不良行为。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "F1", "value": "0.96", "setting": "GPT-5.6 Sol 主干，2,586 条人工标注标准，100 个个人助手回合"}, {"metric": "F1", "value": "0.92", "setting": "Gandalf 基线，相同评估集"}, {"metric": "成本降低", "value": "23% 更低", "setting": "Sauron 相对 Gandalf，GPT-5.6 Sol 主干"}, {"metric": "注入故障检测率", "value": "97%", "setting": "配对故障注入套件，覆盖 13 个故障族，含模拟奖励黑客场景"}, {"metric": "干净对照误报率", "value": "2%", "setting": "同一故障注入套件中的干净对照样本"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证据优先的智能体验证器 Sauron，同时调查环境状态与执行轨迹，并在独立验证来源后才采信声明；有界查询与相关标准联合调查，减少上下文占用和冗余工作；每个判定附带可审计的证据回执；面向奖励黑客等故障注入的鲁棒性评估方法

Abstract: Long-horizon, semi-verifiable agentic tasks often rely on rubric-based evaluation, but the evidence needed to assess these tasks can be spread across the environment state and the execution trace. A judge may overlook this evidence or accept unsupported claims, including an agent's own reports of success. When rubric verdicts serve as reinforcement-learning rewards, such errors can reinforce undesirable behavior. We introduce Sauron, an evidence-first agentic verifier that investigates both state and trace and relies on a claim only after independently validating it against its source. Bounded queries and joint investigation of related criteria reduce context use and redundant work, and each verdict carries an auditable receipt of its supporting evidence. Across 2,586 human-labeled criteria from 100 personal-assistant episodes, Sauron achieves the highest F1 of five verifiers on all eight backbones tested while costing less than Gandalf, the strongest baseline. With GPT-5.6 Sol, it reaches 0.96 F1 versus Gandalf's 0.92 at 23% lower cost, with a wider margin on process criteria. On a paired fault-injection suite covering 13 families, several of which simulate reward hacking, Sauron detects 97% of injected failures while flagging only 2% of clean controls, outperforming baselines even when they are given the full trace. On TRAIL, a benchmark for detecting and localizing real agent errors, Sauron leads both Gandalf and TRAIL's own judge on all three quality metrics.

</details>


### [207] [Adaptive-Group Process Rewards for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17710)
*Zirui Shang,Yang Liu,Xin Shu,Zhi Gao,Xinxiao Wu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向 GUI 智能体 RL 训练的稀疏奖励问题，提出自适应分组过程奖励框架：把轨迹切成语义子目标组，在动作级和组级分别评估并跨组细化，最终将过程奖励用于增强 GRPO 优势，在过程奖励基准与下游 RL 训练上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体强化学习仅依赖稀疏的结果奖励，难以对中间动作进行准确信用分配，且单个动作的价值往往依赖长程轨迹中超出当前状态与观测的信息。

Method: 输入为 GUI 轨迹，包含动作、状态与视觉观测。框架首先将整条轨迹动态划分为若干语义连贯的子目标组。动作级模块使用 critic，基于组内的视觉变化和后续动作，评估每个动作的正确性及其对当前子目标的贡献。组级模块聚合组内动作级评价，判断子目标是否完成以及该组对整体任务的贡献，并进一步利用轨迹中所有组的上下文对这些组级评估进行细化。最终将动作级评价与细化后的组级评价相结合，得到每个动作的过程奖励。训练阶段用该过程奖励增强 GRPO 的结果优势，为 GUI 智能体提供更细粒度的优化信号。推理/部署流程未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "过程奖励基准与下游 RL 训练的实验效果", "value": "论文报告所提框架有效，但摘要未给出具体数值", "setting": "过程奖励基准以及下游强化学习训练实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 自适应分组过程奖励框架：将 GUI 轨迹动态划分为语义连贯的子目标组；动作级评价：critic 基于组内视觉变化与后续动作评估动作正确性及对子目标的贡献；组级评价：聚合动作级评价以判断子目标完成度与对整体任务的贡献，并利用轨迹中所有组的上下文进行细化；将动作级与细化后的组级评价结合得到每个动作的过程奖励，并用于增强 GRPO 的结果优势

Abstract: Training graphical user interface (GUI) agents with reinforcement learning suffers from sparse outcome rewards, leading to inefficient learning. Process rewards offer a promising direction for credit assignment to intermediate actions; however, accurately evaluating an action requires identifying evidence across long-horizon GUI trajectories, as its value may depend on information beyond its immediate state and observation. In this paper, we propose an adaptive-group process reward framework that dynamically partitions a GUI trajectory into semantically coherent subgoal groups, enabling process reward estimation at both the action and group levels. At the action level, a critic evaluates each action based on the visual changes and subsequent actions within the group for action correctness and contribution to the subgoal. At the group level, the framework aggregates action-level evaluations to assess subgoal completion and contribution to the overall task, and further refines these assessments by leveraging the context of all groups in the trajectory. The process reward of each action is obtained by combining its action-level evaluation with the refined group-level evaluation. We further augment GRPO's outcome advantage with the proposed process rewards to provide finer-grained guidance for GUI agent training. Experiments on process reward benchmarks and downstream RL training demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [208] [Training LLMs for Multi-Agent Collaboration with Verifiable Information Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17893)
*Keru Chen,Sen Lin,Yingbin Liang,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Shaofeng Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoSIR，一种基于意外性信息奖励的多智能体LLM后训练方法，在可验证环境中自博弈训练，使模型学会共享证据、请求信息，并迁移到七个未见协作任务，取得最高聚合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过后训练让多个LLM智能体在协作中有效共享证据、请求缺失信息并整合队友贡献，而仅依赖最终任务结果进行训练难以对单个消息的价值提供细粒度反馈。

Method: CoSIR采用后训练框架，在可验证的简单协作环境中使用自博弈训练。每个智能体拥有私有证据，可向队友共享信息或请求缺失信息。贝叶斯评估器根据团队意外性（正确选项在给定各智能体可用证据下的负对数概率之和）的降低量来对共享信息评分；请求的评分则基于若所请求信息被如实提供时同一意外性量的潜在降低。奖励函数还鼓励智能体在私有决策和计算中实际使用接收到的信息。训练目标是通过强化学习最大化这些信息奖励。在推理时，训练后的模型在更复杂、未见过的自然语言协作任务上进行评估，以检验协作行为的泛化能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "聚合得分", "value": "CoSIR取得最高聚合得分（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "在七个未见自然语言协作任务上评估，与基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于Surprisal的信息奖励（CoSIR），用团队意外性降低量对共享信息进行可验证评分。；设计贝叶斯评估器，为共享信息和信息请求分别提供奖励信号。；在简单可验证环境中自博弈后训练Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Gemma-3-4B-it，并评估向七个未见自然语言协作任务的迁移。；通过行为分析显示模型增加了私有证据披露并减少了未使用队友输入的失败。

Abstract: Effective collaboration among large language model (LLM) agents in multi-agent systems requires sharing relevant evidence, requesting missing information, and incorporating teammates' contributions into decisions. Training these behaviors is challenging because final task outcomes provide limited feedback on the value of individual messages. We introduce CoSIR (Collaboration via Surprisal-based Information Rewards), a post-training approach that uses a simple, verifiable environment to teach collaboration behaviors that generalize to more complex, unseen tasks. We propose a Bayesian evaluator that scores shared information by its reduction in team surprisal, defined as the sum of the negative log probabilities assigned to the correct option given each agent's available evidence. Requests are scored by the potential reduction in the same quantity if the requested information were truthfully provided. Separate rewards encourage agents to use the received information in their private choices and calculations. We post-train Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-3-4B-it through self-play and evaluate transfer to seven unseen natural-language collaboration tasks. CoSIR achieves the highest aggregate score, while its performance in general tasks remains competitive. Behavioral analyses show increased disclosure of private evidence and fewer judged failures to use teammates' input.

</details>


### [209] [Stable Token-level Scheduling for Serverless LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17689)
*Hardi Ashishbhai Desai,Balaji N K,Sourav Kanti Addya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TWP是一种面向无服务器LLM推理的闭环token级调度框架，利用ITL、队列深度等反馈动态调节请求准入并配合chunk-based prefill，从而稳定TTFT、降低P99尾延迟和ITL波动，吞吐较ServerlessLLM提升最高45%、较传统调度器提升58%，且无需修改模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无服务器LLM推理调度主要优化首token延迟（TTFT），忽视了token间延迟（ITL）；而LLM推理中prefill计算密集、decode带宽受限，新请求的准入会干扰正在进行的decode并导致延迟尖峰。

Method: TWP是一个闭环调度框架，针对无服务器LLM推理。系统持续采集运行时反馈信号，包括ITL、队列深度和工作负载特征，并据此动态调整一个自调节的token窗口，在token粒度上对请求准入进行调控，而不是按batch粒度准入。为降低新请求对正在进行的decode阶段造成的干扰，TWP结合了chunk-based prefill，将prefill分块执行以平滑资源竞争。该架构不修改底层模型，仅通过调度层实现稳定TTFT、减少延迟尖峰、降低P99尾延迟、减小ITL变化并提升吞吐。摘要未披露具体模块细节、数据流、训练目标或部署参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Token Window Pacing（TWP），一种基于ITL、队列深度和工作负载特征等运行时反馈的闭环调度框架；在token粒度而非batch粒度上调控请求准入，动态调整自调节的token窗口；结合chunk-based prefill以最小化新请求对进行中decode的干扰

Abstract: Serverless inference is emerging as an efficient deployment model for Large Language Models (LLMs) due to its ability to elastically scale resources, improve GPU utilization through resource sharing, and reduce infrastructure costs. However, existing approaches primarily optimize time to first token (TTFT) while overlooking inter-token latency (ITL), a key determinant of user experience during response generation. We observe that LLM inference comprises a computation-intensive prefill stage and a bandwidth-bound decode stage, where admitting new requests can disrupt ongoing decoding and increase latency. To address this, we propose Token Window Pacing (TWP), a closed-loop scheduling framework that leverages runtime feedback from ITL, queue depth, and workload characteristics to regulate request admission at the token level instead of the batch level. TWP dynamically adjusts a selfregulating token window and incorporates chunk-based prefill to minimize interference from incoming requests. Experiments on realistic serverless workloads show that TWP delivers stable TTFT, significantly reduces latency spikes, lowers P99 tail latency, minimizes ITL variation, and improves throughput by up to 45% over ServerlessLLM and 58% over conventional schedulers. These results demonstrate that feedback-driven tokenlevel scheduling enables stable, high-throughput serverless LLM inference without modifying the underlying model.

</details>


### [210] [Cowork-Gym: Synthesizing Interactive Work Environments at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17670)
*Anni Zou,Cheng Chen,Jiaji Deng,Weikang Zhou,Xinji Mai,Yue Wu,Jianing Lin,Lipeng Xie,Lixiong Qin,Qingyue Zhao,Que Shen,Shuchang Tao,Xuchen Pan,Yaliang Li,Yunpeng Zhai,Yushuo Chen,Zhihai Wang,Zhuo Zhang,Zhaoyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cowork-Gym提出通过build-execute-revise循环合成大规模交互式工作环境，并用有状态用户模拟器模拟动态编辑，合成64.9K环境覆盖14个场景，对9B/27B/35B-A3B Qwen模型进行SFT、RL和在线蒸馏后训练，在六个智能体基准上平均分分别提升8.7、6.4、4.1分，27B模型整体排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模合成一致、可执行且能模拟真实用户动态编辑行为的交互式工作环境，用于训练鲁棒的协作智能体。

Method: Cowork-Gym框架通过build-execute-revise循环合成交互式工作环境。首先根据任务目标构建初始环境（包括工具、文件和共享工作空间），然后执行环境以收集执行证据，再通过评审反馈进行验证和修复，确保任务目标、可执行资源、用户行为和成功标准的一致性。有状态用户模拟器在智能体执行过程中通过对话和对共享工作空间的直接编辑来动态响应智能体动作，模拟真实用户行为变化。合成得到64.9K个环境，覆盖14个应用场景。这些环境被用于对9B、27B和35B-A3B Qwen模型进行后训练，训练流程包括监督微调（SFT）、强化学习（RL）和在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）。训练后的模型在六个智能体基准上进行评估，与基础检查点相比平均分提升8.7、6.4和4.1分，其中27B模型整体排名第二，并在PawBench和ClawEval上取得最高平均分，超越总参数量超过100B的模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "六基准平均分提升", "value": "+8.7", "setting": "9B Qwen模型，经SFT/RL/在线蒸馏后对比基础检查点"}, {"metric": "六基准平均分提升", "value": "+6.4", "setting": "27B Qwen模型，经SFT/RL/在线蒸馏后对比基础检查点"}, {"metric": "六基准平均分提升", "value": "+4.1", "setting": "35B-A3B Qwen模型，经SFT/RL/在线蒸馏后对比基础检查点"}, {"metric": "整体排名", "value": "第二", "setting": "Ours-27B在评估模型中总排名第二"}, {"metric": "PawBench平均分", "value": "最高", "setting": "Ours-27B在PawBench上取得最高平均分，超过总参数量>100B的模型"}, {"metric": "ClawEval平均分", "value": "最高", "setting": "Ours-27B在ClawEval上取得最高平均分，超过总参数量>100B的模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: build-execute-revise循环：利用执行证据和评审反馈验证并修复生成的环境，保证任务目标、可执行资源、用户行为和成功标准之间的一致性；有状态用户模拟器：通过对话和对共享工作空间的直接编辑来响应智能体动作，模拟真实的协作动态；大规模合成64.9K环境覆盖14个应用场景，并系统性地用于9B、27B、35B-A3B Qwen模型的SFT、RL和on-policy distillation后训练

Abstract: Cowork agents navigate dependencies across tools and files while adapting to changing user requirements and edits to shared workspaces. Scaling such environments requires maintaining consistency among task objectives, executable resources, user behavior, and success criteria. We introduce Cowork-Gym, a framework that synthesizes Cowork environments through a build-execute-revise loop that uses execution evidence and review feedback to validate and repair generated environments. To capture realistic Cowork dynamics, a stateful user simulator responds to the agent's actions through dialogue and direct edits to the shared workspace. We synthesize 64.9K environments across 14 application scenarios and use them to post-train 9B, 27B, and 35B-A3B Qwen models with supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation. Averaged across six agentic benchmarks, these models outperform their corresponding base checkpoints by 8.7, 6.4, and 4.1 points, respectively. Ours-27B ranks second overall among the evaluated models and achieves the highest average scores on PawBench and ClawEval, outperforming models with over 100B total parameters on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [211] [When Should Enterprise Policy Decisions Be Learned, and When Should They Be Reasoned?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17643)
*Hyojung Han,Ryangkyung Kang,Youngeun Kim,Seung-Hun Jeon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文比较企业策略代理在回答前动作决策中，生成JSON对象与单token约束两种接口，以及是否先推理的影响。在2800条分布内样本上精度无显著差异，但重启稳定性相差约十倍；在300条密封基准上推理带来+17.3点精确动作提升；边界目标监督显著提升覆盖边界且不损害保留边界；英文重渲染显示轻微性能下降且推理-监督交互未稳定复制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业策略代理在回答前应选择哪些动作，以及决策应该通过生成JSON对象还是约束为单个token，并且是否应先进行推理；同时比较不同路径在分布内精度、重启稳定性和分布外泛化上的表现。

Method: 论文基于一个固定的27B韩语企业模型，在回答前决定动作（回答、追问、确认、调用工具、升级、拒答）。两种输出接口：生成JSON对象或单token约束。可选的推理步骤在决策前进行。评估包括2800条分布内项目、300条密封基准（隐藏可执行策略标签）、token匹配对照实验、3种子训练×推理因子设计，以及独立渲染的英文版本。模型和带金标签的密封重渲染已公开。未见更详细的架构描述。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "93.46%", "setting": "2800条分布内样本，生成JSON对象的部署路径"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "94.14%", "setting": "2800条分布内样本，单token约束的部署路径"}, {"metric": "重启稳定性（动作翻转比例）", "value": "3.00%", "setting": "生成JSON对象的部署路径"}, {"metric": "重启稳定性（动作翻转比例）", "value": "0.29%", "setting": "单token约束的部署路径"}, {"metric": "推理收益（精确动作提升）", "value": "+17.3点 [+12.3, +23.0]", "setting": "密封基准300条，标签来自隐藏可执行策略"}, {"metric": "格式差异（无推理时）", "value": "0.0点 [-4.3, +4.0]", "setting": "密封基准300条，无推理条件下比较两种接口"}, {"metric": "边界目标监督提升（边界1）", "value": "+17.5点", "setting": "token匹配随机对照，两个覆盖边界之一"}, {"metric": "边界目标监督提升（边界2）", "value": "+20.0点", "setting": "token匹配随机对照，两个覆盖边界之一"}, {"metric": "保留边界变化", "value": "无变化", "setting": "token匹配随机对照，留出的边界"}, {"metric": "密封渲染交互效应", "value": "-4.9 [-8.1, -1.7]", "setting": "3种子训练×推理因子设计"}, {"metric": "英文版本性能差距", "value": "低0.82点 [-2.68, +0.93]", "setting": "独立渲染英文版本，推理开启"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 固定一个27B韩语企业模型，对比生成JSON对象与单token约束两种动作输出接口在部署路径上的精度与重启稳定性。；在密封基准上评估推理对精确动作的影响，并量化推理收益。；通过token匹配的随机对照，验证边界目标监督对覆盖边界的提升及对未覆盖边界的保持不变。；在3种子训练×推理因子设计中分析密封渲染的交互效应，发现部分替代现象。；独立渲染英文版本验证语言迁移性，并公开模型及带金标签的密封重渲染。

Abstract: An enterprise agent that answers under a written policy must choose an action before it says anything: answer, ask for a missing fact, ask for confirmation, call one or several tools, escalate, or refuse. Holding one 27B Korean enterprise model fixed, we vary whether the decision is a generated JSON object or a single constrained token, and whether the model may reason first. On 2800 in-distribution items we did not detect an accuracy difference between the two deployed paths (93.46% vs 94.14%), while they differ about tenfold in restart stability (3.00% of actions flip vs 0.29%); they also differ in reasoning, so this is an operational comparison. On a sealed benchmark of 300 items whose labels come from a hidden executable policy, reasoning is worth +17.3 points [+12.3, +23.0] on exact action. With reasoning off, the format difference was 0.0 points [-4.3, +4.0], a failure to detect an advantage for either interface, not equivalence; with reasoning on it is unresolved. Against a token-matched random control, boundary-targeted supervision moves the two covered boundaries by +17.5 and +20.0 points and leaves a held-out boundary unchanged. In a 3-seed training x reasoning factorial, the sealed-rendering interaction is -4.9 [-8.1, -1.7], consistent with partial substitution. All supervision was Korean. On independently rendered English versions, the model is 0.82 points lower [-2.68, +0.93] with reasoning on, the same order as re-rendering in Korean; the reasoning-off gain from targeted supervision is not stable in either language, so we do not claim transfer. The original text stays private as the confirmatory set. An open-model re-rendering made after these results reproduced the reasoning effect and the English verdict; the interaction kept its sign but did not replicate under item-clustered intervals. We release the model and that re-rendering with gold labels.

</details>


### [212] [Releasing the Inner Jev: A Simple Recipe for Open Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17205)
*Jonathan Geuter,Andrew Cheng,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单方法，使开放指令微调LLM无需训练即可从字母logits读出决策概率，并用LoRA加决策头微调进一步提升；Gemma 4 31B微调后在OpenDecisions和JevBench v1.7上均超过Jev，并开源模型、服务代码和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何让开放指令微调语言模型无需额外训练或仅用简单监督微调，就能在单次前向传播中输出选项概率，达到或超越System One决策模型Jev的性能，并改善校准。

Method: 输入为类型化状态、问题和一组选项；模型在最后一个token位置读取输出logits中对应字母（A、B、C等）的分布作为各选项概率，无需生成文本。训练配方采用LoRA微调开放指令微调模型，并额外引入一个决策头，该决策头从语言模型头的切片初始化，从而将预训练输出层知识迁移到决策任务。评估使用OpenDecisions验证集，结合准确率与校准指标计算Capability分数，同时在JevBench v1.7和公开决策数据集上验证。推理时仅需一次前向传播即可得到选项概率，并发布服务代码支持部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OpenDecisions Capability score", "value": "79.6", "setting": "Gemma 4 31B 未微调，对比Jev 80.1"}, {"metric": "OpenDecisions Capability score", "value": "84.4", "setting": "Gemma 4 31B 经本文微调配方后"}, {"metric": "JevBench v1.7 分数", "value": "81.5", "setting": "Gemma 4 31B 经本文微调配方后，对比Jev高出4.3 Capability点"}, {"metric": "公开决策数据集准确率", "value": "与Jev相当", "setting": "本文微调模型，校准误差更低"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现开放指令微调LM可直接从最后一个token位置对应A/B/C等字母的logits读出选项概率，无需额外训练即可接近Jev。；提出基于LoRA和从语言模型头切片初始化的决策头的监督微调配方，进一步提升决策性能。；构建并发布OpenDecisions验证集（2,352题），用于评估任务构成并计算结合准确率与校准的Capability分数。；证明开放模型在公开决策数据集上可达到与Jev相当的准确率且校准误差更低，并公开模型、服务代码及数据集。

Abstract: LLM-based decision-making systems, also called System One models, have recently gained popularity, most notably through Jev (Almeida, 2026). Given a typed input state, a question, and a set of options, they return a probability for each option in a single forward pass, without text generation. We show that existing open instruction-tuned language models can achieve comparable performance \textit{without any additional training}, by reading out answers from letters (A, B, C, etc.) in the model's output logits at the last token position. Furthermore, we show how a simple supervised fine-tuning recipe can improve their performance further. We evaluate four open instruction-tuned language models: Gemma 4 31B, Gemma 4 12B, Qwen3.6-27B, and Qwen3.5-4B. We fine-tune each using LoRA and a decision head initialized from a slice of the language model head. Furthermore, we introduce OpenDecisions, a novel 2,352-question validation dataset designed to reflect the task composition of JevBench (JevBench main- tainers, 2026) and which computes a Capability score based on accuracy and calibration metrics. Without fine-tuning, Gemma 4 31B scores 79.6 on OpenDecisions, compared to Jev's 80.1. After undergoing our fine-tuning recipe, it scores 84.4 on OpenDecisions and 81.5 on JevBench v1.7, outperforming Jev by 4.3 and 4.4 Capability points respectively and setting a new state-of-the-art on OpenDecisions. On a suite of public decision datasets, our models achieve accuracy comparable to Jev, with lower calibration error. Our results show that open language models already possess System One capabilities, and with simple fine-tuning they outperform Jev on a wide range of decision tasks. We release our models, serving code, and OpenDecisions.

</details>


### [213] [The Format is the Filter: Scoring Format Fixes What an LLM Judge Can Express](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17158)
*Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova,Sagarika Suresh,Jack Ji,Jithin Pradeep*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在固定候选池、评判提示、解码与选择预算下，系统消融五种 LLM 评判评分格式（二值、1–5、0–100、两种比较式）对指令微调数据筛选的影响：0–100 实际只有 7–12 个可用级别，二值判定在 77–78% 的提示上最高分并列导致随机选择，Kendall τ_b 会掩盖这些问题，比较式存在随评判模型变号的槽位偏差；筛选数据在各评判模型下均优于随机选择，但格式间无可检测排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于 LLM 评判者的指令微调数据筛选流程中，评分格式（二值判定、1–5 评分、0–100 打分、比较式）对筛选质量的影响从未被系统性地论证与消融，且常用的一致性指标会掩盖格式本身引入的缺陷。

Method: 论文研究的是一条“LLM 评判者驱动的数据筛选—微调—评估”端到端管线，而非提出新的模型结构。输入为 15,406 条 UltraFeedback 指令候选数据，所有格式消融实验共享同一候选池、评判提示、解码策略与选择预算，以保证格式是唯一变量。评判阶段使用三个不同的 LLM 作为评判者对候选逐条打分，评分格式共五种：二值判定、1–5 评分、0–100 打分，以及两种比较式（comparison）格式。随后按各格式的分数进行选择（curation），筛选出的数据用于微调，共训练 66 个模型。评估阶段使用四个评判模型对微调结果进行比较与打分，并额外做长度与 markdown 调整以检验稳健性。训练目标即标准的指令微调（SFT）。分析层面，论文计算各格式的分数分布（如 0–100 实际有效级别数、二值判定最高分并列比例）、评判者间一致性（Kendall τ_a、τ_b、top-1 一致率）以及比较式格式的槽位偏差，并据此提出四个筛选管线应报告的量化指标。核心逻辑是：评分格式决定了评判者能表达的区分度，进而决定选择阶段有多少比较是真正有信息的、多少是必须随机打破平局；因此格式选择本身构成一个数据筛选的隐含滤波器，必须在管线中被度量与报告。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "0–100 评分格式的有效使用级别数", "value": "7–12 个等频使用级别", "setting": "15,406 条 UltraFeedback 候选、三个评判模型、每种格式固定候选池/提示/解码/选择预算"}, {"metric": "二值判定在最高分并列的提示占比", "value": "77–78%", "setting": "二值判定格式下、同一提示内最高分并列的比例，导致大多数选择随机进行"}, {"metric": "0–100 相对 1–5 的有效粒度", "value": "约为 1–5 标度的 2–3 倍（而非 20 倍）", "setting": "同样的候选池与评判模型下比较两种评分格式的实际可用级别数"}, {"metric": "Kendall τ_b 与 τ_a、top-1 一致率的排序差异", "value": "τ_b 将二值判定排第一，τ_a 与 top-1 一致率将其排最后", "setting": "五种评分格式在三个评判模型下的一致性排序对比"}, {"metric": "筛选数据相对随机选择的性能", "value": "每评判模型下筛选数据均优于随机选择，且对长度与 markdown 调整稳健；但各格式之间无可检测的优劣排序", "setting": "训练 66 个模型、用 4 个评判模型评估的下游对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在固定候选池、评判提示、解码与选择预算的前提下，系统消融 5 种评分格式与 3 个评判模型，量化格式本身对筛选行为的影响。；揭示 0–100 标度实际仅提供 7–12 个等频使用级别，远少于名义上的 100 级；二值判定在 77–78% 的提示上最高分并列，导致多数选择近乎随机。；指出 Kendall τ_b 会因折扣选择器必须随机打破的平局而掩盖格式缺陷：τ_b 认为二值格式最好，而 τ_a 与 top-1 一致率将其排在最差。；发现两种比较式格式都存在槽位偏差，且偏差方向随评判模型不同而不同。；提出基于评判者的数据筛选流程应报告四个量化指标。

Abstract: When an LLM judge filters instruction-tuning data, its scoring format (binary verdict, 1--5 rating, 0--100 score, or comparison) is rarely justified or ablated. Holding the candidate pool, judge prompt, decoding and selection budget fixed, we score 15,406 UltraFeedback candidates under five formats with three judges, curate, fine-tune 66 models, and evaluate them with four judges. A 0--100 scale is worth only 7--12 equally used levels, two to three times a 1--5 scale rather than twenty, and a binary verdict leaves the top score tied in 77--78\% of prompts, so most selections are made at random. These properties are set by the format almost independently of the judge. Kendall's $\tau_b$ hides them: it ranks binary best while $\tau_a$ and top-1 agreement rank it last, because it discounts the ties a selector must break at random. Both comparative formats inherit a slot bias whose sign differs across judges. Downstream, curated data beat random selection under every evaluation judge, robust to length and markdown adjustment, but no ordering among formats is detectable. We propose four quantities that judge-based curation pipelines should report.

</details>


### [214] [SFT Leaves a Reproducible Signature in Activation Geometry: Calibrating Post-Training Interpretability Against Run-to-Run Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17093)
*Xupeng Chen,Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Runhao Li,Michael Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究后训练在激活几何上的可复现签名，提出以运行间噪声带校准模型差异的方法。在 Qwen3-1.7B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 上，SFT 显示出大且可复现的图位移，而 DPO/GRPO 结果不一致。通过预注册因果扫描验证位移源的负载作用，但未声称机制改变。为后训练评估提供了统计严谨的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在后训练模型对比中，如何区分真实的训练效果与运行间的随机波动，避免将单次差异误读为后训练带来的机制性变化。

Method: 论文基于稀疏共激活图分析后训练模型。输入为通用文本探针状态，通过前向传播收集激活并构建稀疏共激活图。主要模块包括：(1) 稀疏共激活图构建，捕捉神经元/特征间的共激活关系；(2) 图成对距离计算，度量不同检查点之间图结构的差异；(3) 校准模块，对每种后训练配方（LoRA-SFT, DPO, GRPO）生成多个独立运行，计算运行间距离分布作为噪声带；(4) 统计检验，将基座到后训练的对比与噪声带比较，并使用运行交换性推导认证上限；(5) 因果扫描，通过边预算控制识别对提示负载关键的边。训练目标为标准的 SFT/DPO/GRPO。推理时，使用同一套图构建和距离计算流程评估新检查点。未披露更多细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "图成对距离标准差倍数", "value": "17.8 SDs", "setting": "base → SFT 位移相对于四个独立 LoRA-SFT 运行间平均距离，Qwen3-1.7B，共激活图"}, {"metric": "位移范围差距", "value": "5.0倍（SFT）和5.6倍（DPO）", "setting": "29 步下，四个 SFT 和四个 DPO 运行占据互不重叠的位移范围，无噪声带"}, {"metric": "因果扫描平均负载", "value": "12倍于匹配随机控制", "setting": "10,000 边预算，4,816 条留出提示，三个边预算的预注册因果扫描"}, {"metric": "因果扫描中位数负载", "value": "3.5倍于匹配随机控制", "setting": "10,000 边预算，4,816 条留出提示"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用稀疏共激活图的成对距离作为后训练影响的结构度量，并校准运行间噪声带。；提出基于运行交换性的统计下限：k 次运行认证能力不超过 1/(k+1)，无论效应多大，因此噪声带 z 分数仅为描述性尺度。；在 Qwen3-1.7B 与 Llama-3.1-8B 上验证 SFT 签名可复现，且 DPO/GRPO 的效果不一致或为未检测。；通过预注册因果扫描验证被位移源在提示上的负载作用，但未声称机制改变。

Abstract: A model diff credits post-training with whatever separates a base checkpoint from one post-trained descendant, presuming a rerun would land close by. On the sparse co-activation graph of Qwen3-1.7B it does not: two LoRA-SFT reruns sit over half as far apart as either sits from the base. We score each base-to-post contrast against the run-to-run band of its own recipe, and show that $k$ runs certify no better than $p=1/(k+1)$ under run exchangeability, however large the effect, so the band $z$-score is a descriptive scale. SFT leaves a large, reproducible signature: base $\to$ SFT displaces the graph by 17.8 pairwise-distance SDs beyond the mean distance between four independent LoRA-SFT runs, and the pre-registered estimator replicates on Llama-3.1-8B with Llama-Scope. At 29 steps each, four SFT and four DPO runs occupy disjoint displacement ranges, a gap $5.0\times$ and $5.6\times$ the spreads of the two arms, without any band. All bands use graphs built on one GPU architecture. Three other readings stop short of a verdict: DPO band counts (3/4, 1/4, 3/4 contrasts outside at 12, 29, 60 steps) show no consistent dose ordering while its displacement stays flat; light GRPO is a secondary non-detection, not an absence, and we run no equivalence test; and the graph concentration read as an RL signature is already in the zero-RL base. A pre-registered causal sweep at three edge budgets on 4,816 held-out prompts found the displaced sources load-bearing ($12\times$ a matched random control in mean, $3.5\times$ in median at 10,000 edges), but their stage-specificity interaction reversed at two budgets and the third failed its faithfulness gate, so we claim calibrated structural displacement, not a changed mechanism. Scope: one backbone, one SFT recipe and general-text probe states for the full protocol.

</details>


### [215] [VL-DocIR: A Benchmark for Vision-Based Long Document Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17057)
*Simon Ott,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Junwei Zheng,Jiale Wei,Di Wen,Kunyu Peng,Jiaming Zhang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VL-DocIR 是一个页面级视觉长文档检索基准，包含 29,641 个文档、388,548 张页面图像、271,760 条查询，覆盖 23 个领域和 6 种证据结构，并评测单/多向量检索器及诊断设置，显示跨文档检索仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索评测偏重短文档和单相关页，无法反映真实长文档检索中证据跨多页甚至跨文档的场景，导致榜单性能与真实检索能力之间存在差距。

Method: 论文本身不提出新的模型架构，而是构建评测基准并评估现有视觉检索器。输入为从 Wikipedia、arXiv、PubMed 和 SEC proxy statements 采集的 HTML 文档，渲染为页面图像，并保留 HTML 元素标识符用于证据标注。查询基于页面和元素标识符生成，经过针对清晰性、正确性、闭包性和无显式布局引用的清洗流程过滤。评测时对单向量和多向量视觉检索器进行检索，并引入文档上下文评分、查询拆分、重排序和轮询集成等诊断设置，以页面级证据完整性指标（nDCGAll@10、RecallAll@10）评估，并按来源和证据结构进行细分分析。该基准旨在暴露跨文档检索等失败模式。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nDCGAll@10", "value": "69.3", "setting": "结构平衡 Macro 评测下最强评测配置"}, {"metric": "RecallAll@10", "value": "80.6", "setting": "结构平衡 Macro 评测下最强评测配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建 VL-DocIR 页面级证据完整的长文档视觉检索基准，覆盖单页、文档内多页和跨文档证据结构。；从 29,641 个 HTML 文档渲染出 388,548 张页面图像，并生成 271,760 条查询，覆盖 23 个领域和 6 种证据结构。；证据标注对齐到渲染页面和 HTML 元素标识符，并用清洗流程过滤查询的清晰性、正确性、闭包性和避免显式布局引用。；系统评测单向量与多向量视觉检索器，并诊断文档上下文评分、查询拆分、重排序和轮询集成等设置，揭示跨文档检索的困难。

Abstract: Vision-based document retrieval has improved rapidly, but current evaluation settings still emphasize short documents and single relevant pages. This leaves an important gap between leaderboard performance and realistic long-document retrieval, where the evidence needed to answer a query may span multiple pages or even multiple documents. We present VL-DocIR, a page-level benchmark for evidence-complete visual long-document retrieval. VL-DocIR is built from 29,641 HTML-born documents rendered into 388,548 page images from Wikipedia, arXiv, PubMed, and SEC proxy statements. It contains 271,760 queries across 23 domains and six evidence structures, covering single-page, within-document multi-page, and cross-document evidence configurations. Evidence annotations are grounded to rendered pages and HTML element identifiers, and queries are filtered with a cleaning pipeline targeting clarity, correctness, closedness, and the absence of explicit layout references. We evaluate leading single-vector and multi-vector visual retrievers together with diagnostic retrieval settings such as document-context scoring, query splitting, reranking, and round-robin ensembles. The strongest evaluated configuration reaches 69.3 nDCGAll@10 and 80.6 RecallAll@10 under structure-balanced Macro evaluation, but source- and evidence-structure breakdowns show that cross-document retrieval remains substantially harder than single-page retrieval, and that document-context scoring is especially helpful for within-document multi-page queries. VL-DocIR exposes these failure modes and provides a more demanding target for visual long-document retrieval. The new dataset and code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [216] [WeFair: Fidelity-Aware Alignment from Human Feedback for Personalized Query Rewriting in Short-Video Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16927)
*Yingtao Ren,Aolin Li,Cheng Cheng,Huiyun Hu,Huasheng Liang,Baoxing Xie,Jun Yuan,Haijun Wu,Chenxing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WeFair是微信短视频搜索的个性化查询改写框架，将真实用户行为组织为演示→偏好→验证三阶段，依次用监督课程、DPO和基于严格验证奖励的GRPO训练紧凑改写器，线上A/B显示有效点击查询率相对提升1.576%、查询改写率相对下降1.731%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格时延约束下，如何判断何时需要个性化，并生成保持用户即时信息需求忠实的查询改写，避免流畅或高互动优化带来的意图漂移与无依据细节注入

Method: WeFair是面向微信短视频搜索个性化查询改写的保真度对齐框架。输入端为用户简短、有歧义的搜索查询及搜索会话中的行为轨迹。首先在数据构建阶段，从真实搜索会话中挖掘用户实际发生的人工改写，形成个性化查询示范；再用多维LLM筛选与上下文验证过滤掉缺乏依据的转换，从而在少人工标注下得到高质量训练数据。训练分成三阶段：第一阶段监督课程学习，由大规模人工个性化查询示范逐步建立改写能力；第二阶段用直接偏好优化（DPO）对齐用户后续消费行为，使生成的改写偏向用户真实会继续消费的方向；第三阶段进行on-policy优化，以真实人工改写构造严格验证奖励，并采用分组相对策略优化（GRPO）进一步提升。推理时查询改写器接收原始短查询并输出个性化改写，供下游检索使用，需在严格时延预算内完成。作者进一步分析发现，在GRPO中引入辅助奖励虽能激活更多rollout分组，但会降低改写对用户意图的保真度，因此验证奖励的严格性对避免意图漂移至关重要。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "有效点击查询率（观看时长VV>10s）", "value": "相对提升1.576%", "setting": "微信短视频搜索线上A/B测试"}, {"metric": "查询改写率", "value": "相对下降1.731%", "setting": "微信短视频搜索线上A/B测试"}]}

Conclusion: 将真实用户行为信号组织为演示→偏好→验证的渐进式保真度对齐框架；从搜索会话挖掘真实人工改写，并用多维LLM筛选与上下文验证过滤不忠实转换，减少人工标注；三阶段训练课程：大规模人工个性化查询示范的监督课程学习、面向后续消费的DPO对齐、基于真实人工改写构建严格验证奖励的on-policy GRPO优化；发现辅助奖励可激活更多GRPO rollout分组但会降低意图保真度的分析结论

Abstract: Short-video search requires compact query rewriters that infer personalized intent from brief, ambiguous queries under strict latency constraints. The challenge is to decide when personalization is warranted and generate a reformulation that preserves the user's immediate information need. A rewriter optimized for fluency or engagement may still introduce unsupported details and redirect the search toward a different intent. Existing methods often underutilize users' actual reformulations and behavioral traces, relying instead on synthetic reasoning and indirect behavioral rewards that may produce more engaging candidates but cause intent drift. To build an effective rewriter that remains faithful to user intent, we propose WeFair, Fidelity-Aware Alignment from Human Feedback for Personalized Query Rewriting in WeChat short-video search. WeFair assigns real user behavioral signals to a progression from demonstration to preference to verification. We first mine actual human reformulations from search sessions, then apply multidimensional LLM screening and contextual verification to filter unsupported transitions without extensive manual annotation. During training, we begin with a supervised curriculum that progressively builds rewriting capabilities from large-scale human personalized query demonstrations, and then use direct preference optimization to align with users' subsequent consumption. In the final on-policy optimization stage, we construct a strict verification reward based on actual human reformulations to further boost performance with group relative policy optimization. Further analysis shows that auxiliary rewards can activate more rollout groups in GRPO but reduce intent fidelity. Online A/B testing in WeChat short-video search shows a 1.576% relative increase in effective clicked-query rate (VV>10s) and a 1.731% relative reduction in query reformulation rate.

</details>


### [217] [Choosing LLMs for Strategic Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18354)
*Arastun Mammadli,Herbert Woisetschläger,Jawad Fayaz,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将LLM选择形式化为元博弈，比较了嵌入、自我反思和bandit等选择器，发现轻量级MAB较可靠但未超越最佳固定模型，且非平稳bandit未能处理动态对手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在对手未知且可能随时间适应、导致反馈信号偏移的情况下，在线动态选择LLM以优化博弈交互中的累积收益。

Method: 论文提出的系统是一个战略选择器（strategic selector），位于基础博弈之上，构成元博弈。每轮选择器从包含10个LLM的组合中选择一个模型参与博弈，每个LLM在基础博弈中与对手交互，产生收益。选择器维护对潜在博弈收益矩阵的在线估计，并基于历史反馈更新选择策略。选择器方法包括：基于查询嵌入的方法、基于LLM自我反思的方法，以及轻量级多臂老虎机算法。训练目标为最大化累积收益。评估通过在每个博弈中重复多轮种子运行（N次）来衡量选择器的表现，并与最佳固定模型和基线bandit进行比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将战略选择定义为叠加在基础博弈之上的元博弈，其中选择器每轮从模型组合中选出一个LLM应对潜在的收益矩阵；发现评估信号具有博弈特异性，可靠信号需要远高于传统基准的种子运行次数N；比较多种选择器方法（查询嵌入、LLM自我反思、多臂老虎机），发现轻量级MAB最可靠但仍不及最佳固定模型；实验表明非平稳和对抗性bandit变体未能超越平稳bandit基线，揭示了当前方法在处理动态对手时的不足

Abstract: Game-theoretic benchmarks claim that large language model (LLM) strategic decision-making varies across game types, interaction structures, and capability dimensions. However, they only evaluate strategic performance across individual fixed models. In parallel, contemporary LLM routers pick models under quality-cost trade-offs, but with no notion of a strategic structure. They do not account for an opponent that might be unknown, and that can adapt over time and shift the feedback signal. To address this, we extend the game-theoretic evaluations and define strategic selection as the meta game layered on top of the base game. In each round, a strategic selector fields an LLM from a portfolio of models to navigate a game payoff matrix that is latent and learned online. We find that the evaluation signal is game-specific, and that extracting a reliable signal requires a much higher number of seed runs $N$ compared to traditional benchmarking setups. Evaluating across 5 games and 10 LLMs, we show a model's strategic competence varies significantly depending on the game setup and the opponent faced. Building on this, we implement a suite of selector methods and find that approaches that rely on query embeddings or LLM self-reflection do not consistently capture the underlying strategic signal. A lightweight multi-armed bandit algorithm performs more reliably across games, though it still falls short of the best fixed model. Finally, we show that simple non-stationary and adversarial bandit variants fail to outperform a stationary bandit baseline, pointing to a gap in how current methods handle a shifting opponent in strategic settings.

</details>


### [218] [Geospatial Vector Geometry as a Native Spatial Modality for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16769)
*Huang Fei,Boyi Chen,Hao Xue,Yang Yue,Yehong Xu,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoVec-VLM 提出将地理空间矢量几何作为视觉语言模型的原生空间模态，通过结构化几何表示和几何交互策略，在五个空间推理任务上平均准确率相对最强基线提升约11%，并展现出更好的几何一致性，为VLM空间推理提供了新的内部空间基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLM）主要依赖语言和视觉信息进行空间推理，但在大地理尺度下，有限的视觉观测难以建立准确的空间参考和恢复精确的空间关系，成为VLM空间感知和推理的瓶颈。

Method: GeoVec-VLM 模型以地理空间矢量几何作为额外的空间模态。输入包括视觉内容、语言参考和地理空间矢量几何数据。首先，通过结构化几何表示模块将矢量对象编码为保留空间结构和排列的显式空间基。然后，几何交互策略将该空间基融入多模态计算中，使其在Transformer架构中与视觉特征和语言特征根据查询的空间关系进行交互。最终模型基于共享的几何参考进行感知和推理。训练目标可能为多任务学习，但论文未详细披露。推理时，模型接收视觉、语言和几何输入，通过几何交互生成空间推理结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "相对最强基线提升约11%", "setting": "在五个空间推理任务上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出将地理空间矢量几何作为VLM的一种原生空间模态；设计结构化几何表示，保留矢量对象空间结构和排列，构建显式空间基；引入几何交互策略，使几何基在多模态计算中与视觉内容和语言参考根据查询的空间关系进行交互

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning links VLMs to real-world action and decision making. Reliable reasoning requires accurate spatial information and effective spatial representations. However, current VLMs obtain this information mainly from language and vision. At larger geographic scales, limited visual observations make it difficult to establish accurate spatial references and recover precise spatial relations. This leads to a bottleneck for VLM spatial perception and reasoning. This paper proposes that geospatial vector geometry can become a native spatial modality within a VLM and introduces GeoVec-VLM. First, we develop a structured geometry representation that preserves the spatial structure and arrangement of vector objects, building an explicit spatial basis. Second, we introduce a geometry interaction strategy that brings this basis into multimodal computation, where it interacts with visual content and language references according to the queried spatial relations. Together, these designs allow perception and reasoning to operate on shared geometric references. Across five spatial reasoning tasks, GeoVec-VLM improves mean accuracy by about 11\% relative to the strongest baseline, maintains its gains under geographic transfer, and shows greater geometric consistency. Our results show that geospatial vector geometry offers a promising internal spatial basis for VLM spatial reasoning. this https URL

</details>


### [219] [Separating Prompt Harmfulness from Interaction Safety via Partial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16761)
*Ram Vikas Mishra,Shashank Aggarwal,Jahnavi Kumar,Amit Awekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于部分生成的LLM安全护栏方法，区分提示危害性与交互安全性。通过监控生成过程中的部分响应，使用微调分类器提前预测响应安全性，从而避免不必要的拒答并减少生成开销。实验基于41,944个提示-响应对（7个开源LLM），表明提示级和响应级危害标签仅50.46%一致，且仅约三分之一响应token即可恢复近乎完整响应的AUPRC（最佳0.885），展示了早期停止的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prompt-level harmfulness classification rejects many prompts that would receive safe responses, wasting system resources and denying helpful responses; need to distinguish prompt harmfulness from interaction safety and detect harmful responses early during generation.

Method: The paper proposes a system architecture for LLM safety guardrails based on partial generation. The system starts generating a response to a given prompt and monitors the partial output. A fine-tuned classifier processes the partial response (e.g., first third of tokens) to predict whether the full response would be harmful. If the partial response is predicted safe, generation continues; if harmful, generation is stopped early. The classifier is trained on a dataset of 41,944 prompt-response pairs generated by seven open-weight LLMs from three families (Llama, Gemma, Qwen). The training objective is response-level harm classification. The method leverages the observation that partial responses carry most of the signal needed for harm prediction, enabling early stopping and reducing unnecessary rejections of safe prompts. The architecture includes: input prompt, LLM generation module (which produces partial responses), safety classifier module (which takes partial response as input), and a control module that decides whether to continue or stop generation based on the classifier's output. No specific model parameters, training hardware, or inference hardware are disclosed.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Cohen's κ", "value": "0.06", "setting": "Agreement between prompt harmfulness labels and response harmfulness labels on 41,944 prompt-response pairs"}, {"metric": "agreement percentage", "value": "50.46%", "setting": "Prompt labels and response labels agreement"}, {"metric": "percentage of prompts harmful across all 7 LLMs", "value": "3.5%", "setting": "Among prompts that make any model respond harmfully"}, {"metric": "AUPRC", "value": "0.885", "setting": "Best fine-tuned classifier for response-level harm prediction"}, {"metric": "F1", "value": "0.807", "setting": "Best fine-tuned classifier for response-level harm prediction"}, {"metric": "AUPRC recovery", "value": "almost full-response AUPRC", "setting": "Using about one-third of response tokens"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Demonstrating that prompt harmfulness and interaction safety diverge (only 50.46% agreement, Cohen's κ=0.06; only 3.5% of prompts harmful across all 7 LLMs).；Proposing partial-generation-based safety inference: start generating, monitor partial response, stop only if harmful, reducing unnecessary rejections.；Building a dataset of 41,944 prompt-response pairs with 7 open-weight LLMs (Llama, Gemma, Qwen) and showing fine-tuned classifiers predict response-level harm well (best AUPRC 0.885, F1 0.807).；Showing that about one-third of response tokens recover almost full-response AUPRC, enabling early stopping with minimal generation.

Abstract: LLM-based information systems need safety guardrails. Most guardrails classify the prompt alone. If the prompt looks harmful, the system rejects it before any answer is generated. This is costly. Many rejected prompts would have received a safe response. Users lose help they should have received from the information system. This cost grows as conversational and generative search becomes common. Prompt-level labels treat harm as a property of the query. But harm depends on what the model actually writes. Existing input-output guards do look at responses. But they judge a finished response, so the decision comes after full generation. However, we observe that safety of model interaction with the input prompt can be inferred early with only partial output. So the system can start generating, watch the partial response, and stop only if it turns harmful. We test this idea in three steps. First, we build a dataset of 41,944 prompt-response pairs with seven open weights LLMs from three families: Llama, Gemma, and Qwen. Second, we show that prompt harmfulness and interaction safety differ. Prompt labels and response labels agree on only 50.46\% of items (Cohen's $\kappa$ = 0.06). Of the prompts that make any model respond harmfully, only 3.5\% do so for all seven LLMs. Third, we show that partial responses carry most of the signal. Fine-tuned classifiers predict response-level harm well (best AUPRC 0.885, F1 0.807). About one third of the response tokens recover almost full-response AUPRC.

</details>


### [220] [Double-Rollout Reinforcement Learning with Sampling-based Reward Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16593)
*Haifeng Qian,David Mosallanezhad,Christian Munley,Adithya Renduchintala,Oleksii Kuchaiev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对 RLVR/GRPO 中结果奖励无法准确反映推理质量、导致奖励噪声与训练不稳定的问题，提出 double-rollout 强化学习：截断推理轨迹并从截断前缀采样多条 continuation，用 continuation 奖励均值替代原始奖励以获得更稳健的推理质量估计。该方法仅增加中等计算开销，却在数学与代码任务上、跨 Qwen 与 Nemotron 模型取得比 GRPO 基线更高的准确率且所需 RL 步数更少；例如在仅用 DAPO 数据、30 个 RL 步内将 Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B 在 AIME 2026 上提升至 95.4%（GRPO 为 91.5%）。结合长度奖励后进一步改善准确率-效率帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证的结果奖励无法准确衡量推理质量：正确答案可能缺乏合理推理轨迹支撑，错误答案也可能包含几乎正确的推理过程，由此产生的奖励噪声导致 RL 训练在推理准确率与 token 效率上不稳定，同时存在信用分配困难。

Method: 方法建立在 GRPO 风格的 RLVR 训练流程之上。输入为采样得到的 rollout（包含推理轨迹与最终答案），原流程仅用最终结果的可验证奖励作为监督信号。Double-rollout 算法在每条 rollout 的推理轨迹上进行截断，保留前缀，并从该截断前缀出发采样多条 continuation，随后计算这些 continuation 的奖励均值，并用该均值替换原始 rollout 的奖励，作为更稳健的推理质量估计参与策略优化。这种设计将单条轨迹的结果奖励替换为对前缀后续推理质量的期望估计，从而降低因最终答案正确但推理不充分、或最终答案错误但推理接近正确所带来的奖励噪声，改善信用分配。训练目标仍沿用 GRPO 的相对优势估计框架，但奖励信号被重新定义；推理/生成阶段不改变模型结构，仅在训练时增加额外的 continuation 采样开销。论文进一步将 double-rollout 与基于长度的奖励结合，以同时优化准确率与 token 效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AIME 2026 准确率", "value": "95.4%（GRPO 91.5%，且显著高于该模型规模的现有最好水平）", "setting": "Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B，仅使用 DAPO 数据，30 个 RL 步内"}, {"metric": "数学与代码任务准确率提升及所需 RL 步数减少", "value": "在 Qwen 与 Nemotron 模型上均取得比基线算法更高的准确率且使用更少的 RL 步", "setting": "数学与代码可验证奖励任务，跨 Qwen 与 Nemotron 模型"}, {"metric": "准确率-效率帕累托前沿改善", "value": "结合长度奖励后进一步优于现有效率优化方法", "setting": "加入基于长度的奖励设置，未披露具体数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Double-rollout 奖励估计：对每条采样 rollout 截断其推理轨迹，从截断前缀采样多条 continuation，用 continuation 奖励均值替换原始奖励，从而得到更稳健的推理质量估计。；实证展示结果奖励与推理质量之间的错配，并将其与 RL 训练不稳定（准确率与 token 效率）联系起来。；将 double-rollout 与基于长度的奖励结合，进一步改善准确率-效率帕累托前沿。；以仅中等计算开销的代价，在更少 RL 步数内取得更高准确率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) implemented with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has driven substantial progress in training reasoning language models. However, verifiable outcome rewards provide an imperfect measure of reasoning quality: a correct final answer may not be supported by the reasoning trace, whereas an incorrect rollout may contain a nearly correct reasoning trajectory. We present empirical evidence for this mismatch and hypothesize that the resulting reward noise contributes to instability in reinforcement learning for both reasoning accuracy and token efficiency. To address this credit assignment challenge, this paper proposes double-rollout reinforcement learning. For each sampled rollout, we truncate its reasoning trace, sample multiple continuations from the truncated prefix, and replace the original reward with the mean continuation reward, yielding a more robust estimate of reasoning quality. This double-rollout algorithm incurs only a moderate increase in compute cost and yet reaches substantially higher accuracies across math and coding tasks and across Qwen and Nemotron models than baseline algorithms with fewer RL steps. For example, using only DAPO data it trains Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B to reach 95.4% on AIME 2026, versus 91.5% by GRPO and substantially above state of the art for this model size, within 30 RL steps. When combined with length-based rewards, the same algorithm further improves the accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier over existing efficiency optimizations. These findings suggest that the double-rollout reward estimation is an effective and cost-efficient way to improve both the accuracy and token efficiency of reasoning RL.

</details>


### [221] [Overtaught, Underlearned: Learner-Centered Distillation from Multi-Agent Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16543)
*Mengtao Zhou,Jianxiao Wang,Xiupeng Chen,Cailiang Liu,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出现有多智能体讨论图的全图蒸馏存在 over-teaching 与 under-learning 问题，并提出 FAR 流程：训练前折叠正确解为代表性课程，训练后根据学生尝试与表征聚类定位遗漏并生成针对性课程。实验表明 FAR 在更少训练目标下优于全图蒸馏，并降低 under-learning。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图蒸馏将多智能体讨论图中每个正确解都当作独立训练目标，导致教师提供的监督与学生实际能学到的内容不匹配：over-teaching（多个正确解在学生表征中可能对应同一知识）和 under-learning（部分训练问题学习后仍被遗漏）。

Method: FAR 是一个以学生为中心的蒸馏流程。输入是多智能体讨论图，其中每个讨论状态包含多个正确解。训练前阶段（Fold）：将每个讨论状态内的多个正确解折叠（fold）为代表课程，减少监督目标数量，缓解 over-teaching。训练阶段：学生模型基于折叠后的课程进行学习。训练后阶段（Attempt）：让学生模型对训练问题自行尝试作答。Revisit 阶段：根据学生自身尝试找出仍被遗漏的问题（under-learning），同时通过聚类分析发现表征覆盖不佳的簇，表明折叠可能丢失了有用变化；然后利用保留的讨论上下文为这些遗漏问题写出针对性课程。整体目标是用学生自身状态组织监督，使蒸馏更有效且更高效。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理任务表现", "value": "优于全图蒸馏，且训练目标更少、under-learning 更低（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "两个学生模型、四个推理任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出量化 over-teaching 与 under-learning 的比率指标，并在讨论图上验证其高企。；提出 FAR（Fold--Attempt--Revisit）以学生自身状态为中心的蒸馏流程：训练前折叠每个讨论状态内的多个正确解为代表性课程。；训练后利用学生自身尝试识别遗漏问题，并通过表征不佳的聚类发现折叠可能丢失的有用变化，从保留的讨论上下文中写出针对性课程。

Abstract: Multi-agent discussion produces reasoning collaboratively, while deployment still relies on a smaller student that must answer alone. Full-graph distillation transfers such discussions by treating each correct solution as a separate training target, but what teachers provide need not match what the student learns. This mismatch appears in two ways: several correct solutions may amount to the same lesson in the student's representation (over-teaching), while some training questions remain missed after learning (under-learning). We quantify both with rates that are high on discussion graphs. To address them, we introduce FAR (Fold--Attempt--Revisit), a learner-centered distillation procedure that organizes supervision around the student's own state. Before training, FAR folds solutions within each discussion state into representative lessons. After training, the student's own attempts identify missed questions, while poorly represented clusters indicate where folding may have lost useful variation; FAR then writes targeted lessons from retained discussion contexts. Across two students and four reasoning tasks, FAR outperforms full-graph distillation with far fewer training targets and lower under-learning. Further analyses examine the behavior of FAR and the sources of its gains. Overall, the results show that organizing discussion supervision around the student can make distillation both more effective and more efficient.

</details>


### [222] [Phantom Merge: When Your Large Language Model Agents Pick One but Tell You About Another](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16141)
*Wangshu Zhu,Qianwen Guo,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别并形式化了 LLM 智能体中的 Phantom Merge 实体-证据绑定失败，提出 claim 级风险估计与修正框架 AGR+FAC。在 Shopping 检测任务上 AGR-slot 达到 AUROC 96.2%、AP 97.3%，端到端缓解中 FAC 将轨迹级 PM 从 51.4% 降至 6.2%，输出与原始 claim 比为 80.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在多实体任务中会出现实体-证据绑定失败（Phantom Merge），把不支持的主张归因于其已承诺的实体，即使任务被正确完成也可能发生。

Method: 论文提出两阶段框架用于 claim 级风险估计与修正。第一阶段 Anchor-Grounded Risk (AGR) 对智能体预发射隐藏状态进行表征读出，并计算基于 Jacobian 的功能信息，生成互补的 slot 级与 anchor-value 级风险分数。第二阶段 Fixed-Anchor Correction (FAC) 对标记的 claim 进行审计，利用观测到的 anchor 证据选择性修正这些 claim；当没有受支持的替换内容时则移除。整体流程从智能体生成过程出发，在 claim 发射前进行风险估计，再对高风险 claim 实施证据约束的修正或删除，以降低实体-证据绑定错误。论文在四个智能体领域和四个骨干模型家族上刻画 PM 的流行度与错误子类型，并在 Shopping 检测与端到端缓解评估中报告了结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "96.2%", "setting": "AGR-slot 在 held-out Shopping 检测队列上"}, {"metric": "average precision", "value": "97.3%", "setting": "AGR-slot 在 held-out Shopping 检测队列上，优于所评估的检测基线"}, {"metric": "trajectory-level PM", "value": "从 51.4% 降至 6.2%", "setting": "FAC 在端到端 Shopping 缓解评估中"}, {"metric": "output-to-original claim ratio", "value": "80.7%", "setting": "FAC 在端到端 Shopping 缓解评估中"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化 Phantom Merge 为 claim 级实体-证据绑定失败，并刻画其流行度与错误子类型。；提出 Anchor-Grounded Risk (AGR)，结合预发射隐藏状态表征读出与 Jacobian 功能信息，提供 slot 级和 anchor-value 级风险分数。；提出 Fixed-Anchor Correction (FAC)，对标记的 claim 进行审计，并用观测到的 anchor 证据选择性修正，无支持替换时则删除。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate over multiple entities, where reliable response generation requires not only retrieving relevant evidence but also preserving its association with the correct entity. We identify Phantom Merge (PM), an entity-evidence binding failure in which an agent attributes unsupported claims to its committed entity, potentially even when the task is completed correctly. We formalize PM at the claim level and characterize its prevalence and error subtypes across four agent domains and four backbone families. To address this problem, we propose a two-stage framework for claim-level risk estimation and correction. First, Anchor-Grounded Risk (AGR) combines a representational readout of the agent's pre-emission hidden state with Jacobian-based functional information, providing complementary slot-level and anchor-value risk scores. Second, Fixed-Anchor Correction (FAC) audits flagged claims and selectively revises them using observed anchor evidence or removes them when no supported replacement is available. On the held-out Shopping detection cohort, AGR-slot achieves an AUROC of 96.2% and an average precision of 97.3%, outperforming the evaluated detection baselines. In the end-to-end Shopping mitigation evaluation, FAC reduces trajectory-level PM from 51.4% to 6.2%, with an output-to-original claim ratio of 80.7%. These results demonstrate the prevalence of entity-specific grounding failures and the effectiveness of combining internal risk estimation with evidence-constrained claim correction. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Perfect Teammates: Multi-Agent Collaboration with Unreliable Colleagues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16117)
*Chen Huang,Yiyang Zhang,Yuhao Wu,Yang Deng,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出LLM多智能体协作常隐含‘完美同事’假设，提出UnCoBench基准，用Misaligned、Incomplete、Missed三类失败刻画不可靠同事，并给出带答案唯一性/角色必要性可执行校验的自动生成流水线。对十个模型的评测显示，可靠场景表现相近的模型对不可靠证据的鲁棒性差异明显，轨迹分析暴露了纠正性跟进遗漏、低效等待和冗余查询等恢复行为问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体协作系统与评测隐含‘完美同事’假设：协作者提供正确完整信息并及时响应。论文要分离任务解决能力与在不可靠证据获取过程中的恢复能力，即在同事返回错配、信息不足或未响应时，主智能体能否诊断证据失败、恢复相关信息并整合到决策中。

Method: 论文提出UnCoBench基准与配套的自动化生成与验证流水线。任务设定包含一个主智能体与若干同事智能体协作解题；同事可能产生三类不可靠行为：Misaligned（返回信息不针对目标问题）、Incomplete（信息不足以支撑决策）、Missed（未响应请求）。主智能体的处理流程需要：1）验证同事返回信息与当前上下文的正确性；2）诊断信息缺口，识别哪些子问题未被满足；3）追踪未解决依赖并执行有针对性的后续行动（追问、重查、等待或替代查询），从而获取正确证据；4）将恢复后的证据整合进最终决策。为规模化和可验证地构造基准，作者设计了自动化生成流水线：先用LLM生成场景与角色/任务描述，再通过可执行检查保证答案唯一性和各角色必要性，剔除不唯一或角色可省略的样本，以形成可判定、可复现的评测集。评测在十个模型上进行，关注可靠设定与不可靠设定下的表现差异，并通过轨迹分析刻画恢复行为中的失败模式（如遗漏纠正性跟进、低效等待、冗余查询）。文中未披露具体模型参数、训练硬件、延迟/吞吐等系统指标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Unreliable Colleagues Benchmark（UnCoBench），按Misaligned、Incomplete、Missed三类行为失败组织不可靠场景。；定义主智能体需验证上下文正确性、诊断信息缺口并追踪未解决依赖以获取正确证据的任务形式。；设计可验证、可扩展的自动生成流水线：LLM生成场景结合可执行检查（答案唯一性、角色必要性）。；对十个模型做评测并给出轨迹分析，揭示相似可靠场景表现下不同的不可靠证据鲁棒性，以及纠正性跟进遗漏、低效等待与冗余查询等恢复行为。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent collaboration is increasingly used to solve complex tasks by dividing work among specialized agents and integrating complementary expertise. Yet existing systems and evaluations largely rest on an implicit perfect-colleague assumption: collaborators provide correct and complete information and respond promptly to requests. In realistic teamwork, however, collaborators may return information that does not answer the intended question, provide insufficient detail to support a decision, or fail to respond to a request. To separate task-solving competence from the ability to recover an unreliable evidence-gathering process, we introduce the Unreliable Colleagues Benchmark (UnCoBench), which organizes behavioral failures through Misaligned, Incomplete, and Missed settings. The main agent need to verify contextual correctness, diagnose information gap and track unresolved dependencies in order to obtain the correct evidence for solving the task. We also introduce a verifiable and scalable automated generation pipeline that pairs LLM-based scenario generation with executable checks of answer uniqueness and role necessity. Results across ten models show that similar reliable-setting performance can result in different robustness to unreliable evidence. Trace analysis reveals frequent omissions of corrective follow-up, and the recovery behavior involves inefficient waiting and redundant queries. These findings show that robust collaboration requires not only sound reasoning, but also targeted action to diagnose evidence failures, recover relevant information, and integrate it into decisions. Our data and source code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [Heterogeneous Agentic Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16078)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Zi Yang,Shivani Modi,Cici Cong Wu,Yunlong He,Yuxin Chen,Zhengyu Zhang,Liang Luo,Venkatesh Ranganathan,Jianhui Sun,Yantao Yao,Wenlin Chen,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HACE 提出异构智能体协同进化框架，让专用基础模型与 LLM 智能体通过互惠学习共同提升：智能体用验证增益做强化学习，专用模型用智能体探索经验做监督训练，并用进化搜索优化交互接口。在推荐系统等三个领域超过现有自改进方法，但无公司披露、无线上实验、无模型规模细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专用基础模型（如推荐系统模型）依赖人工设计改进，难以像通用LLM智能体一样递归自我改进；因为当专家模型进步时，提升它的策略也必须随之适应。

Method: HACE 包含两个主要组件：一个领域专用基础模型（specialist）和一个 LLM 智能体（agent）。专用模型负责目标任务预测（如推荐、蛋白质功能预测、晶体稳定性预测），通过监督学习在领域数据上训练。智能体则通过 on-policy 强化自训练进行优化，其奖励信号基于智能体提出的改进策略在专用模型上实际验证得到的性能提升；即智能体探索候选的改进方案（如数据增强、训练策略调整），在专用模型上执行并度量增益，用该增益作为强化学习奖励更新智能体策略。同时，智能体的探索经验会反馈到专用模型的后续监督训练中（例如筛选出的高质量训练样本），使专用模型适应智能体行为。两者形成互惠循环。为了维持这种循环，HACE 进一步使用进化搜索来优化“交互接口”（interaction harness），该接口定义了两个模型之间如何交换信息、如何选择训练经验。随着双方能力进化，接口参数通过进化算法（如变异、选择）不断更新，以适配新的能力状态。论文在推荐系统、蛋白质功能预测和晶体稳定性预测三个领域进行评估，报告 HACE 在主要任务指标上优于所评估的自改进方法。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 异构智能体协同进化（HACE）框架：专用基础模型与LLM智能体相互学习、共同进化；使用经当前专用模型验证的性能增益作为奖励，以在策略强化自训练训练智能体；智能体的探索结果反过来指导专用模型的后续监督训练，形成双向闭环；引入进化搜索优化交互接口（交互方式与训练样本选择策略），随模型能力变化自适应调整

Abstract: Recent advances in self-improving LLM agents have demonstrated the potential of AI systems that drive their own intelligence. However, many domain-specific applications rely on specialized foundation models that possess strong domain expertise but remain dependent on human-designed improvements. Extending recursive self-improvement to these models presents a key challenge: as a specialist improves, the strategies used to advance it must also adapt. To address this challenge, we propose Heterogeneous Agentic CoEvolution (HACE), a framework that jointly evolves specialized foundation models and LLM agents through reciprocal learning. HACE improves the specialist through supervised learning while training the agent with on-policy reinforced self-training, guided by verified performance gains on the current specialist. The agent's exploration, in turn, informs subsequent specialist training, enabling both models to adapt to each other's progress. To sustain this reciprocal learning, HACE further employs evolutionary search to optimize their interaction harness, adapting how the models exchange information and select training experiences as their capabilities evolve. We evaluate HACE across three challenging domains: recommendation systems, protein function prediction in biology, and crystal stability prediction in material discovery. We show that HACE outperforms the evaluated self-improving methods on the primary task metrics across all three domains. These results shed light on heterogeneous coevolution as a universal approach to improving specialized foundation models through reciprocal interaction with a learning agent.

</details>


### [225] [Understanding and Reducing Misalignment in Persuasion Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16070)
*Boyi Wei,Ruta Desai,Peter Henderson,Wenyue Hua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究RL说服训练导致模型证据编造与信念胁迫等不对齐行为增加，并比较多种缓解方法，发现引导与惩罚结合最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在针对说服任务进行强化学习优化时，模型会产生证据编造、信念胁迫等不对齐策略，如何理解并减少这种说服力—不对齐权衡。

Method: 论文未披露具体模型结构细节。其框架为多智能体说服任务：发送方（sender）模型在博弈中向接收方（receiver）模型传递信息以改变其信念。使用强化学习对发送方进行优化，奖励信号基于接收方信念偏移程度。训练目标即最大化说服力（信念变化）。随后通过多种干预措施减少不对齐行为：加入更强策略引导提示、提供干净示范、在奖励中对使用不对齐策略施加惩罚，以及增强模型检测编造内容的能力。评估指标包括每局游戏中使用的不同不对齐策略平均数量、每局虚构次数等。推理路径为发送方生成说服性消息，接收方更新信念，统计不对齐策略发生情况。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建评估RL说服训练导致不对齐的测试床（多智能体说服博弈）；量化RL优化对虚构与不对齐策略使用频率的影响；系统比较多种缓解手段：更强策略引导、干净示范、对错位策略惩罚、提升虚构检测能力；发现结合对齐说服策略引导与错位奖励惩罚能取得最佳权衡

Abstract: Frontier models are now trained for complex multi-agent tasks, from running a company to engaging in negotiations. These tasks necessitate training models to be persuasive. But what does this optimization pressure mean for model alignment? In this work, we study how reinforcement learning (RL) training for persuasion affects models' use of misaligned strategies such as evidence fabrication and belief coercion, and what techniques can reduce such misalignment. We find that optimizing sender models solely for receiver belief shifts causes the average number of distinct misaligned strategies used per game to increase by more than 25%, and fabrications per game increase to more than 4 times relative to pre-RL models. We compare various mitigation approaches, including stronger strategy guidance, clean demonstrations, penalizing misaligned strategy usage, and improving the model's ability to detect fabrications. We find that navigating the persuasiveness-misalignment trade-off in this setting is challenging, with the best trade-offs coming from combining guidance on aligned persuasion strategies with reward penalties on misalignment. Overall, our work provides a testbed for assessing misalignment stemming from RL-based persuasion training and shows concrete paths for reducing misalignment. Importantly, it highlights that without direct intervention, persuasion-involved RL environments are likely to naturally lead to large increases in the use of misaligned strategies.

</details>


### [226] [Engram Injection: A Study of Temporal Validity in Parametric Language-Model Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15882)
*Sam Siavoshian,Omar Ramadan,Amir K. Saeed,Benjamin A. Johnson,Benjamin M. Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM参数化记忆中的时间有效性，提出Engram Injection方法将事实存储在LoRA适配器中并通过FiLM注入时钟信号。实验发现，在七天后，适配器方法正确回答0/62对，而全历史提示和检索分别达到59/62和62/62，表明学习事实与学习何时使用事实是分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能否在参数中学习事实随时间变化的情况，并仅根据问题和当前时间正确回答。

Method: Engram Injection方法基于冻结的Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型。事实存储在LoRA适配器中，时钟信号通过FiLM条件注入模型。LoRA适配器学习事实的表示，FiLM层将时间信息调制到隐藏状态中。训练目标：模型需要根据问题和当前时间正确回答，即学习事实以及何时使用该事实。在推理时，给定问题和当前时间，模型通过适配器和FiLM条件生成答案。对比方法包括：加法时钟条件、全历史提示和检索。实验表明，适配器方法在时间有效性上表现不佳，而检索和全历史提示表现更好。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "正确回答的对数", "value": "0/62", "setting": "在七天的年龄下，适配器方法在主要盲审下正确回答0对。"}, {"metric": "正确回答的对数", "value": "59/62", "setting": "全历史提示方法在七天的年龄下正确回答59对。"}, {"metric": "正确回答的对数", "value": "62/62", "setting": "检索方法在七天的年龄下正确回答62对。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Engram Injection方法：将事实存储在LoRA适配器中，并通过FiLM条件将时钟信号注入冻结的Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct模型。；对比了加法时钟条件、全历史提示和检索四种方法。；训练了六个匹配的适配器（三个种子），并修复了可能将测试年龄样本纳入训练的采样器。；训练审计报告称在127,760个训练输入中没有此类样本。

Abstract: A stored fact may stop being valid when a user changes a preference or an event replaces an earlier one. We study whether a language model can learn these changes in its parameters and answer correctly from only a question and the current time. Engram Injection stores facts in LoRA adapters and supplies the clock through FiLM conditioning on a frozen Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct model. We compare it with additive clock conditioning, prompting with the full dated history, and retrieving four dated records. We train six matched adapters across three seeds after fixing a sampler that could admit test-age examples into training. The training audit reports no such examples among 127,760 evaluated training inputs. On 62 pairs of questions asked before and after an update, at an elapsed age of seven days excluded from training, both adapter methods answer zero pairs correctly under the primary blinded assistant review with order code 1 held fixed. Full-history prompting answers 59/62 pairs correctly, and retrieval answers 62/62. Separate experiments show that adapters can store facts and that removable memory units can support local deletion. Combining fact selection with time-based version selection remains unsuccessful. These results separate learning a fact from learning when to use it.

</details>


### [227] [History Replays Itself: Unleashing Long-Context Capability in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15715)
*Li Nanbo,Firas Laakom,Yilan Zhang,Piotr Piękos,Yimeng Chen,Liangyu Wang,Yifan Xu,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Dandan Guo,Wenyi Wang,Yanning Dai,Yuhui Wang,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NoteBank 提出训练免费、即插即用的稀疏情景记忆与原生重放机制，将长期存储从线性注意力的固定循环状态中解耦，使 Gated DeltaNet 在 100 万 token 下 RULER 六项检索任务平均准确率从 1.7% 升至 70.3%，且几乎不增加 FLOPs 与存储，可扩展至 Mamba-2-8B、Qwen3.5-9B 并在 LongBench、MemoryAgentBench 上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力的固定大小循环状态必须同时承担当前计算与历史信息存储，导致长上下文能力受限于状态记忆容量；论文挑战该容量上限假设，试图在不重新训练模型的前提下扩展预训练线性注意力模型的长上下文能力。

Method: NoteBank 是一种训练免费、即插即用的稀疏情景记忆模块，挂载在预训练线性注意力/循环模型（如 Gated DeltaNet、Mamba-2、Qwen3.5）之上。它在未来查询未知之前，从输入文本流中选择性归档片段形成稀疏情景记忆库；写入与寻址均使用主干网络自身的原生特征表示，不依赖外部嵌入模型或单独训练的检索器。当模型进行前向推理时，利用主干原生前向计算对检索到的证据进行重放（replay），使证据作为上下文被原生阅读，而无需单独的神经阅读器。该设计将长期存储从固定大小循环状态中解耦，循环计算只负责常规序列处理，长程信息由外部稀疏记忆承担，从而在几乎不增加分析性 FLOPs（≤0.025%）和存储开销（约为全 MHA KV cache 的 1/2,600）的情况下大幅扩展长上下文能力。整个接口无训练过程，直接适配预训练模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "六个 RULER 检索任务平均准确率", "value": "1.7% -> 70.3%", "setting": "100 万 token 长度，Gated DeltaNet"}, {"metric": "分析性 FLOPs 增加", "value": "≤0.025%", "setting": "相对基线线性注意力模型"}, {"metric": "持久上下文存储", "value": "约 Transformer 全 MHA KV cache 的 1/2,600", "setting": "匹配宽度、深度与精度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: NoteBank 稀疏情景记忆：在未来查询未知前归档选定文本，使用主干网络原生特征进行写入与寻址，无需外部嵌入模型或单独训练的检索器。；原生前向计算重放读取：通过主干自身的 forward 计算重放检索到的证据，无需单独的神经阅读器。；训练免费、即插即用的接口设计，可直接挂载到预训练线性注意力模型上。；在 100 万 token 长度下将 Gated DeltaNet 在六个 RULER 检索任务的平均准确率从 1.7% 提升至 70.3%，且 4K 到 1M token 未见显著检索退化。；分析性 FLOPs 增加 ≤0.025%，持久上下文存储约为同宽度、深度、精度下 Transformer 全 MHA KV cache 的 1/2,600。

Abstract: Linear attention promises the efficiency needed for long-context language modelling, yet its fixed-size recurrent state must simultaneously support ongoing computation and preserve information from an ever-growing history. This coupling has reinforced a prevailing view that the state's memory capacity defines the model's long-context capability. We challenge this presumed ceiling: \emph{recurrent computation need not bear the full burden of long-term storage}. We introduce NoteBank, a sparse episodic memory that archives selected text before future queries are known, uses the backbone's native features for writing and addressing, and reads retrieved evidence through its native forward computation via replay. This \emph{training-free, plug-and-play} interface requires no external embedding model, separately trained retriever, or separate neural reader. At one million tokens, it raises Gated DeltaNet's mean accuracy across six RULER retrieval tasks from 1.7% to 70.3%, with no observed significant retrieval degradation from 4K to 1M tokens. This recovery adds $\leqslant$0.025% to analytical FLOPs, with persistent context storage approximately 1/2,600 that of a Transformer's full MHA KV cache at matched width, depth, and precision. Experiments extend from three 1.3B recurrent backbones to Mamba-2-8B and post-trained Qwen3.5-9B, demonstrating gains on LongBench v1/v2 and MemoryAgentBench. Together, these results show that sparse evidence storage and native replay can substantially expand the long-context capability of pretrained linear attention models while retaining the efficiency of recurrent computation.

</details>


### [228] [Recall What Is Missing: Composing Evidence from Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15642)
*Qi Feng,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将长期智能体记忆的读取重构为查询条件下的证据组合问题，提出无训练机制 MemPursuit：用稀疏凸查询重构残差衡量记忆的增量证据价值，复用检索嵌入渐进组合嵌套证据上下文，无需 LLM 或 cross-encoder 逐对打分。在 LoCoMo 固定预算下 Top-10 准确率提升 7.2 个百分点、Top-20 提升 5.5 个百分点，并相对 fully batched BGE-v2-m3 取得 32.9 倍中位重排延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限上下文预算下，长期记忆智能体需要检索的不是各自相关的记忆，而是对当前查询集体充分的证据；传统检索-打分-截断会因冗余而浪费上下文槽位并遗漏互补证据。

Method: MemPursuit 面向长期记忆智能体的读取阶段。输入是当前查询以及检索阶段已经计算出的查询嵌入与候选记忆嵌入。机制将记忆集合视为对查询的稀疏凸重构：在表示空间中，用当前已选记忆组合对查询进行重构，重构残差被用作‘当前记忆集合还缺少什么’的代理信号。在此基础上，方法按需选择能最大程度降低残差的候选记忆，逐步组合出嵌套的证据上下文，而不是独立地对每条记忆打分并截断。由于完全复用检索阶段已有的嵌入，评分过程不调用语言模型或 cross-encoder，从而避免两两交叉编码的高延迟。整体为 training-free 机制，可嵌入多种记忆框架中，在固定上下文预算下完成证据组合与重排。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "+7.2 percentage points", "setting": "LoCoMo 基准，固定上下文预算 Top-10"}, {"metric": "end-to-end accuracy", "value": "+5.5 percentage points", "setting": "LoCoMo 基准，固定上下文预算 Top-20"}, {"metric": "median reranking latency", "value": "32.9x speedup", "setting": "相对于 fully batched BGE-v2-m3"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将长期记忆读取阶段形式化为查询条件下的证据组合，以‘超出已选证据的增量’衡量每条记忆的价值。；提出无训练的 MemPursuit 机制，用稀疏凸查询重构的残差作为‘当前记忆集合缺失内容’的表示空间代理。；复用检索阶段已有的查询与候选嵌入，渐进式组合嵌套证据上下文，避免对每个查询-记忆对调用语言模型或 cross-encoder。

Abstract: Under a limited context budget, a long-term agent must recall not merely memories that are individually relevant, but evidence that is collectively sufficient for the current query. Conventional memory access retrieves, scores, and truncates memories, often spending multiple context slots on redundant facts while leaving complementary evidence behind. We cast the read phase of long-term memory as query-conditioned evidence composition: each memory is valued by what it adds beyond the evidence already selected. We instantiate this operation with MemPursuit, a training-free mechanism that uses the residual of a sparse convex query reconstruction as a representation-space proxy for what the current memory set is missing. By reusing the query and candidate embeddings already available from retrieval, MemPursuit progressively composes nested evidence contexts without invoking a language model or cross-encoder to score each query--memory pair. The effectiveness of our method is verified on 3 popular benchmarks in comparison to strong baselines. In fixed-budget LoCoMo experiments, MemPursuit improves end-to-end accuracy by 7.2 percentage points at Top-10 and 5.5 percentage points at Top-20, with consistent gains across multiple memory frameworks, while achieving a 32.9$\times$ speedup in median reranking latency over fully batched BGE-v2-m3. Our implementation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [229] [Knowledge Interaction in Hypernetwork-based Continual Learning: Conflict and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15677)
*Jiaqi Zheng,Yi Hu,Yadong Mu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在超网络持续学习框架下研究LLM中基础模型、适配器与上下文三种知识来源的冲突与组合行为，发现冲突时模型偏好上下文且受位置影响，并可通过正交方向的steering vector控制知识来源；多跳推理中三源知识难以可靠组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于超网络的持续学习框架中，理解基础模型、参数更新（适配器）与输入上下文三者所携带知识之间的交互：冲突时模型优先遵循哪一来源，以及能否可靠地将三者知识组合以支持多跳推理。

Method: 该框架使用一个共享超网络，根据上下文动态生成适配器参数，注入到冻结的基础大语言模型中。基础模型参数保持冻结，不进行任务特定更新；超网络以输入上下文为条件，输出适配器的权重或变换，从而在不修改基础模型的情况下实现对新数据的适配。输入端同时包含基础模型已有知识、由超网络生成的适配器所携带的新知识以及提示中的上下文知识。推理时，三者共同影响模型预测。论文通过知识冲突实验观察模型在来源冲突时的选择倾向，通过表示分析发现上下文与适配器知识导致的表示变化与基础模型原始隐状态正交，并据此构造steering vector以控制模型更倾向于遵循哪一知识来源。多跳推理实验则检验三种来源的知识能否组合，结果表明组合常失败，瓶颈在于超网络与基础模型及上下文之间的交互。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统研究超网络适配框架中基础模型、适配器、上下文三种知识来源的冲突与组合行为。；发现知识冲突时模型偏好遵循上下文，且选择更多取决于知识呈现的位置而非内容。；揭示上下文与适配器知识沿与基础模型干净隐状态正交的方向扰动表示，并据此推导控制模型遵循哪种来源的steering vector。；在多跳推理中发现模型无法可靠组合三种来源的知识，关键失败源于超网络与基础模型及上下文的交互。

Abstract: Continual learning aims to adapt pretrained large language models (LLMs) to evolving data while preserving their pretrained capabilities. A central challenge is catastrophic forgetting: adaptation to new data can impair performance on previously learned knowledge. Since continual learning commonly changes model parameters, the model's predictions may draw on knowledge from the base model, the parameter update, and the input context. Understanding how these sources interact is essential: conflicts reveal whether new knowledge can override outdated knowledge, while multi-hop reasoning tests whether new knowledge can be composed with existing knowledge to support generalization. We study these interactions in a hypernetwork-based adaptation framework, where a shared network generates adapters from context, allowing a frozen base model to adapt without task-specific updates. We find that, under knowledge conflict, models preferentially follow the context, and their choices depend more on where knowledge is presented than on its content. We also show that context and adapter knowledge perturb the base model's representations along directions orthogonal to its clean hidden states, enabling us to derive steering vectors that control which source the model follows. In multi-hop reasoning, we find that models fail to reliably compose knowledge across the three sources, with the key breakdown arising from the interaction of the hypernetwork with both the base model and the context. Code and reproduction materials: this https URL

</details>


### [230] [Large Decision Models: Repurposing Large Language Models as Single-Pass Decision Makers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15501)
*Ziyi Chen,Yan Zhang,Jianhui Wei,Jianing Yin,Daoan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Large Decision Models，将LLM改造为单次prefill输出显式决策概率的接口，通过多样化决策监督与自设推理蒸馏缩小与推理模型的差距，并在Gemma-4-31B上实现182倍延迟加速；进一步用置信度路由在恢复92%准确率优势的同时仅生成27-58%的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM通过解码生成答案，存在解码开销且不直接暴露决策概率；任务特定分类器又需逐任务训练、难以泛化到未见决策schema。论文希望用一次prefill前向即可对运行时指定的标签输出显式概率。

Method: LDMs将语言模型改造为决策概率接口：输入为决策schema与候选标签，模型通过一次prefill前向直接输出各标签的概率分布，取代传统的自回归解码。训练分两阶段。首先使用Diverse Decision Supervision (DDS)，在覆盖不同领域、规则和标签的监督数据上微调，使模型学会对运行时指定的任意标签集合返回显式概率。然后Self-Posed Reasoning Distillation (SPRD)进一步缩小与推理模型的性能差距：由一个冻结的教师模型生成任务，经过答案一致性过滤后保留高质量样本，再通过重复教师推理获得标签分布，并以其为监督信号蒸馏学生模型。推理阶段，模型仅需一次前向即可得到概率，避免逐步解码开销；对于不确定的决策，引入置信度路由器，将部分样本路由给推理模型，以在准确率和token成本之间取得平衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "较step-matched baseline提升1.8个百分点", "setting": "跨三个基础模型，在DecisionVista上评估"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "较step-matched baseline提升1.5个百分点", "setting": "跨三个基础模型，在DecisionVista上评估"}, {"metric": "latency speedup", "value": "182倍", "setting": "相对reasoning rollout，在Gemma-4-31B上"}, {"metric": "accuracy recovery", "value": "中位数92%", "setting": "开发集上的置信度路由，相对推理模型的准确率优势"}, {"metric": "token generation ratio", "value": "27-58%", "setting": "开发集上的置信度路由，相对推理模型的token生成量"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Large Decision Models (LDMs)：将语言模型改造为单次prefill前向、对运行时指定标签返回显式概率的决策接口。；Diverse Decision Supervision (DDS)：覆盖多领域、多规则和多标签的多样化决策监督，使模型适配该接口。；Self-Posed Reasoning Distillation (SPRD)：由冻结教师模型生成任务，进行答案一致性过滤，并在重复教师推理得到的标签分布上蒸馏，以缩小与推理模型的差距。；置信度路由器：在开发集上对不确定决策进行路由，在恢复推理模型大部分准确率优势的同时显著减少token生成。

Abstract: Large language models make decisions through parallel prefill and sequential decoding. Although effective, this approach incurs decoding costs and does not explicitly expose decision probabilities. Existing task-specific classifiers require per-task training and offer limited generalization to unseen decision schemas. We propose Large Decision Models (LDMs), repurposing language models to return explicit probabilities over labels specified at run time using one prefill forward pass. We first adapt models to this interface through Diverse Decision Supervision (DDS), covering varied domains, rules and labels. To further narrow the performance gap to a reasoning model, Self-Posed Reasoning Distillation (SPRD) adds tasks generated by a frozen teacher, filters them for answer consistency, and trains on label distributions obtained from repeated teacher reasoning. We evaluate on DecisionVista, pairing new-schema tests with held-out human-labelled sources. Across three base models, the recipe improves accuracy over a step-matched baseline by 1.8 and 1.5 percentage points, respectively, achieving a 182-fold speedup in latency over reasoning rollout on Gemma-4-31B. Reasoning remains stronger on the new-schema test, motivating a confidence router for uncertain decisions. On development sets, routing recovers a median 92% of reasoning's accuracy advantage while generating 27-58% of its tokens. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [231] [SpecFit: Not All Draft Tokens Cost the Same in Offloaded MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15417)
*Hongliang Cao,Xiuyu Li,Siqi Wang,Ziming Wang,Jingmin Zhu,Hailong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecFit面向MoE专家权重卸载到主机内存的推理场景，指出草稿树越大则H2D专家传输越多，推测解码有时反而不如自回归。它改以预测吞吐量而非接受长度来选择验证草稿子树：用草稿路径概率估计提交token数，用节点数与每层激活专家并集预测周期时间，并把专家并集建模为次模覆盖函数，最后精确搜索祖先闭合子树，同时决定何时停止草稿。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，CNN/DailyMail与GSM8K子集分别比EAGLE-3快21.6%和7.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE模型专家权重卸载到主机内存的场景下，每个被验证的草稿token都会激活自己的专家，导致H2D专家传输进入解码关键路径；更大的草稿树带来更多专家传输，使得推测解码有时比自回归解码更慢。
