Method: SpecFit针对MoE模型专家权重卸载到主机内存、H2D专家传输进入解码关键路径的场景。推测解码一次前向验证多个草稿token，但每个被验证token会激活自己的专家，因此草稿树越大，需要传输的专家越多，H2D成本可能抵消推测解码收益。SpecFit在选择验证草稿子树时，以预测吞吐量为优化目标而非接受长度。它由草稿路径概率估计期望提交token数，并根据子树节点数以及每层所激活专家的并集来预测单次验证周期时间；由于每层激活专家并集是单调次模覆盖函数，每个节点只需为其新增的专家付费。算法对祖先闭合的草稿子树进行精确搜索，返回预测吞吐量最高的子树；该搜索在每个草稿层之后运行，同一搜索也用于决定何时停止草稿。最终由未修改的目标模型对所选子树进行验证。整个流程不改变目标模型，仅调整验证的草稿子树选择与草稿停止时机。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解码速度相对EAGLE-3（相同专家缓存）", "value": "快21.6%（CNN/DailyMail summarization）", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，CNN/DailyMail摘要任务"}, {"metric": "解码速度相对EAGLE-3（相同专家缓存）", "value": "快7.0%（GSM8K子集）", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，GSM8K子集"}, {"metric": "解码速度相对vanilla EAGLE-3", "value": "1.97x（CNN/DailyMail）", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，CNN/DailyMail摘要任务"}, {"metric": "解码速度相对vanilla EAGLE-3", "value": "1.72x（GSM8K子集）", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，GSM8K子集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 选择验证草稿子树时以预测吞吐量为目标而非接受长度；由草稿路径概率估计期望提交token数，并由节点数和每层激活专家并集预测周期时间；将每层激活专家并集建模为单调次模覆盖函数，使每个节点只需为其新增专家付费；对祖先闭合子树进行精确搜索返回预测吞吐量最高的子树，每层草稿后运行该搜索并据此决定何时停止草稿；目标模型未修改地验证结果

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models activate only a few experts per token, but when their expert weights are offloaded to host memory, host-to-device (H2D) expert transfers enter the critical path of decoding. Speculative decoding verifies several draft tokens in one forward pass, yet in an MoE model each verified token activates its own experts, so a larger draft tree transfers more experts. Because of this H2D cost, speculative decoding commits several tokens per verification cycle yet is sometimes even slower than autoregressive decoding. Based on this observation, we propose SpecFit, which chooses the verified draft subtree to maximize predicted throughput rather than acceptance length. SpecFit estimates the expected number of committed tokens from draft path probabilities and predicts cycle time from the node count and the per-layer union of the experts the subtree activates; this union is a monotone submodular coverage function, so each node pays only for the experts it adds. An exact search over ancestor-closed subtrees returns the subtree with the highest predicted throughput; run after each draft level, the same search also decides when to stop drafting, and the unmodified target verifies the result. On CNN/DailyMail summarization and a GSM8K subset with Qwen3-30B-A3B, SpecFit decodes 21.6% and 7.0% faster than EAGLE-3 with the same expert cache and 1.97x and 1.72x as fast as vanilla EAGLE-3. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [232] [Fewer Bits, More Tries: Recovering Capability Under Memory Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15372)
*Tomer Galanti,Josh Susskind,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 verified retry：在内存受限的本地部署中，用较小/压缩模型并行生成多个候选并借助测试选择答案。四个代码生成基准上，7B Qwen 8 次尝试基本匹配 32B 模型（MBPP+）并在其余三个上超过，内存约少 4 倍、速度最高快 6 倍、能耗更低（Nvidia GPU 测量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型比小模型能力更强，但其内存需求使本地部署困难。论文研究能否通过运行更小或更压缩的模型多次，并利用公开或模型生成的测试在输出中选取正确答案，从而恢复部分因模型变小或压缩而损失的能力。

Method: 论文提出 verified retry 框架：输入为代码生成任务，使用较小或压缩后的语言模型（如 7B Qwen）并行生成多个候选输出（例如 8 次尝试），然后利用公开的或模型生成的测试对候选进行验证，从通过测试的候选中选择最终答案。整体流程为：单用户本地推理硬件通常未充分利用，因此并行候选生成可以在适度延迟成本下换取准确率。训练目标未涉及（该方法不改变模型参数），推理阶段通过并行采样与测试筛选实现。论文在四个代码生成基准上评估，并在 Nvidia GPU 上测量内存、速度和能耗；还跨模型规模、压缩级别和硬件选择观察类似权衡，但具体收益依赖硬件配置和问题设定。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "8次并行尝试的7B Qwen 模型在 MBPP+ 上基本匹配 32B 模型，在另外三个基准上超过 32B 模型", "setting": "四个代码生成基准（含 MBPP+），7B Qwen 模型 8 次并行尝试对比 32B 模型，Nvidia GPU 测量"}, {"metric": "模型内存", "value": "约减少 4 倍", "setting": "7B Qwen 模型 8 次并行尝试对比 32B 模型，Nvidia GPU"}, {"metric": "运行速度", "value": "最高快 6 倍", "setting": "7B Qwen 模型 8 次并行尝试对比 32B 模型，Nvidia GPU"}, {"metric": "能耗", "value": "更少（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "7B Qwen 模型 8 次并行尝试对比 32B 模型，Nvidia GPU 测量"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 验证式重试（verified retry）：利用测试反馈在多次并行候选生成中选择输出，以额外的测试时计算换取准确率，同时降低模型内存需求。；将单用户本地推理中硬件未充分利用的空闲算力用于并行候选生成，以适度延迟代价提升准确率。；在多个模型规模、压缩级别和硬件配置上系统刻画了模型容量与验证式测试时计算之间的权衡，并给出 Nvidia GPU 上的内存、速度和能耗测量。

Abstract: Large language models are generally more capable than smaller ones, but their memory requirements can make local deployment difficult. We ask whether part of this capability gap can instead be recovered by running a smaller or more compressed model multiple times and selecting among its outputs using public or model-generated tests. Single-user local inference can leave hardware underutilized, allowing parallel candidate generation to trade additional test-time computation for accuracy at modest latency cost. Across four code-generation benchmarks, verified retry recovers substantial accuracy lost to model size or compression. A 7B Qwen model with eight parallel attempts essentially matches a 32B model on MBPP+ and exceeds it on the other three benchmarks, while using about $4\times$ less model memory, running up to $6\times$ faster, and using less energy, as measured on Nvidia GPU. We observe similar tradeoff across model sizes, compression levels and hardware choices, although exact benefit depends on the hardware profile and problem setting. These results show that model capacity can be traded for verified test-time computation when correct solutions remain reachable within a modest retry budget.

</details>


### [233] [SuperAI: Merging Capabilities Across Heterogeneous Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15322)
*Jingtong Dou,Chuancheng Shi,Wenhua Wu,Wei Bao,Fei Shen,Zhiyong Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SuperAI 提出一种跨架构多模型融合框架，通过PCA构建低维任务子空间，经正交Procrustes对齐源与宿主模型表示，并训练轻量残差MLP以联合监督注入能力增量。在3个宿主模型、15个能力域上优于现有跨架构融合方法，且随源模型数量增加更好地保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同基础模型之间存在架构差异与表示空间不兼容，难以通过统一接口复用和组合分散在各模型中的知识能力，需要一种跨架构、低维、保持几何结构的融合方法。

Method: SuperAI 是一个跨架构多模型融合框架，目标是将多个源基础模型的互补能力注入宿主模型。流程如下：首先，使用一组共享锚样本分别输入源模型和宿主模型，提取各模型低维任务相关表示；对每个模型的任务相关表示应用主成分分析（PCA），得到相同维度的低维功能子空间。其次，使用正交 Procrustes 映射对齐源模型子空间与宿主模型子空间的坐标系统，在保持每个源子空间内部几何结构的同时建立跨模型对应关系。最后，训练一个轻量级残差多层感知机（MLP），以任务输出和源模型增量为联合监督信号，学习输入相关的残差增量，并将该增量注入宿主模型，实现多模型能力融合。推理时，宿主模型结合注入的残差增量给出预测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用共享锚样本提取源模型与宿主模型的任务相关表示，并通过PCA构建同维度的低维功能子空间。；采用正交Procrustes映射对齐源模型与宿主模型坐标系统，在保持各源子空间内部几何的同时建立跨模型对应关系。；训练轻量级残差MLP，以任务输出和源模型增量为联合监督，学习输入相关的残差增量注入宿主模型，实现多模型能力融合。

Abstract: Enabling the reuse and composition of knowledge and capabilities distributed across foundation models is an important step toward building scalable artificial intelligence (AI) systems. However, architectural differences and incompatible representations make it difficult to transfer and integrate these capabilities through a unified cross-model interface. To address this challenge, we propose SuperAI, a cross-architecture multi-model fusion framework that integrates complementary capabilities from multiple source models into a host model through orthogonal alignment of low-dimensional task-relevant representations. Specifically, we first extract task-relevant representations from the source models and the host model using shared anchor samples, and apply principal component analysis (PCA) to construct low-dimensional functional subspaces with the same dimensionality. Next, we use orthogonal Procrustes mappings to align the source and host coordinate systems, establishing cross-model correspondences while preserving the internal geometry of each source subspace. Finally, we train a lightweight residual multilayer perceptron (MLP) with joint supervision on task outputs and source-model increments, learning input-dependent residual increments that are injected into the host model for multi-model capability fusion. Experiments on three host models across 15 capability domains show that SuperAI achieves higher average domain scores than the evaluated cross-architecture fusion methods and better preserves general capabilities as the number of source models increases. Ablation studies further support the effectiveness of orthogonal alignment, nonlinear residual mapping, and joint supervision.

</details>


### [234] [Understanding Unified VLMs: From Attention Probing to Guidance Tuning for Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15227)
*Zhengyang Luo,Xiaoyu Qiu,Zhiheng Tan,Yuchen Liang,Yingbin Liang,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过探测统一 VLM 中注意力头对条件引导的敏感性，揭示了功能分化模式，并提出推理时干预方法 HeadTuned-CFG，在 T2I 和 I2T 生成上无需训练即取得一致提升，尤其 T2I 显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉语言模型（VLM）如何在同一个 Transformer 骨干中处理文本到图像（T2I）和图像到文本（I2T）生成的双向条件引导，以及如何利用这种机制改进生成质量。

Method: 论文从条件引导角度研究统一 VLM。统一 VLM 使用共享 Transformer 骨干同时进行 T2I 和 I2T 生成。作者对每个注意力头进行激活差异探测，比较无分类器引导（CFG）两个分支（有条件和无条件）之间的激活差异，发现一部分头对条件变化敏感，另一部分头不敏感，且这种模式在 T2I 和 I2T 中均存在并跨样本稳定。基于此，提出 HeadTuned-CFG：在推理时，根据探测得到的条件变化感知，在注意力头级别选择并调整条件引导强度，从而无需训练即可提升生成质量。方法直接作用于推理阶段，不修改模型参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "T2I 生成性能", "value": "显著优于强基线", "setting": "在自回归和掩码扩散两种统一 VLM 范式上，T2I 生成任务中取得大幅提升"}, {"metric": "多模态生成方向一致性收益", "value": "一致提升", "setting": "在 T2I 和 I2T 两个方向上均获得一致的性能提升，且无需额外训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过探测每层注意力头在无分类器引导（CFG）的两个分支间的激活差异，揭示统一 VLM 中条件敏感头与条件无关头的功能分化模式；提出 HeadTuned-CFG，一种无需额外训练的推理时干预方法，根据探测到的条件变化感知在注意力头级别选择并调整条件引导

Abstract: Unified vision-language models (VLMs) perform both text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) generation with one shared Transformer backbone, yet how the backbone processes the condition in either direction remains poorly understood. We study this question from the perspective of conditional guidance: we probe every attention head to show the activation difference between two branches of classifier-free guidance (CFG) with different conditional guidance. On two unified VLMs of autoregressive and masked-diffusion paradigms, the probing reveals a subset of heads that are more sensitive to the change of condition while others are agnostic to it. This characteristic pattern holds in both T2I and I2T generation and is largely stable across data samples, demonstrating the internal mechanism of unified VLMs. Motivated by this, we propose a new inference-time intervention approach for unified multimodal generation, named \textit{HeadTuned-CFG}, which uses the probed condition-change awareness to select and tune the conditional guidance at the attention-head level. Experiments show consistent benefits in both multimodal generation directions of unified VLMs without additional training, and in particular substantial improvements over strong baselines in T2I generation. These results further support the key finding of our probing analysis: the observed pattern of functional specialization is an intrinsic property of unified VLMs.

</details>


### [235] [Harmony Harness: Multi-Objective Self-Evolution of Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15150)
*Weichen Yu,Lesly Miculicich,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Shuo Li,Matt Fredrikson,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Harmony Harness 是面向 LLM Agent Harness 的多目标自进化框架，通过分解式多目标进化搜索与因子化进化算子，分离通用机制与环境特定适配，并在推理时动态组合，在 SecRepoBench 和 BaxBench 上取得超过 10% 的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化 LLM Agent 方法通常优化单一目标，并将 Harness 作为单体产物进行演化，难以处理相互竞争的目标，也无法将可迁移的设计与环境特定适配分离。

Method: Harmony Harness 是一个多目标、组合式的 Agent Harness 进化框架。其输入为待优化的 Agent Harness 配置与执行反馈。框架首先将 Harness 演化建模为多目标优化问题，目标为多个竞争性指标。在进化搜索阶段，采用基于分解的方法（如 MOEA/D 风格）将多目标问题分解为多个子问题，维护一组解以覆盖帕累托前沿的不同区域，避免依赖手工设计的结构多样性度量。随后，因子化进化算子分别对通用型 Harness 机制（可跨环境迁移）和基准特定适配（环境专用）进行独立精化。最后，动态组合工作流在推理时根据当前任务将通用机制与特定适配组合成完整的 Agent Harness。训练与优化过程利用执行反馈进行迭代搜索，无梯度更新。推理时直接使用组合后的 Harness 执行任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "benchmark performance", "value": "超过 10% 的提升", "setting": "在 SecRepoBench 和 BaxBench 上，与 strong harness-evolution baselines 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 Harness 演化形式化为多目标优化问题，并采用基于分解的进化搜索来维护帕累托前沿上不同区域的解，避免依赖手工设计的结构多样性度量。；提出因子化进化算子，分别优化通用 Harness 机制和基准特定适配。；设计动态组合工作流，在推理时集成通用机制与特定适配。

Abstract: Recent work on self-evolving LLM agents treats the agent harness as an optimization target that can be iteratively improved through execution feedback. Existing approaches, however, largely optimize a single objective and evolve the harness as a monolithic artifact, limiting their ability to handle competing goals and to separate transferable design from environment-specific adaptation. We introduce Harmony Harness, a framework for multi-objective, compositional harness evolution. Harmony Harness formulates harness evolution as a multi-objective optimization problem and uses decomposition-based evolutionary search to maintain solutions across different regions of the Pareto frontier, avoiding reliance on a hand-designed structural diversity metric for repository-scale harnesses. In parallel, we introduce factorized evolution operators that separately refine general-purpose harness mechanisms and benchmark-specific adaptations, together with a dynamic composition workflow that integrates them at inference time. Across SecRepoBench and BaxBench, Harmony Harness consistently outperforms strong harness-evolution baselines, with gains exceeding 10% on both benchmarks. Our results show that explicitly modeling both objective trade-offs and harness structure yields substantially stronger and more transferable agent evolution than existing single-objective, monolithic approaches.

</details>


### [236] [Structural Chain-of-Thought: Reasoning with Executable Computational Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15107)
*Petr Senatorov,Karim Vafin,Elena Tutubalina,Oleg Somov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Structural Chain-of-Thought：让 LLM 生成可执行的 computational graph 而非自由文本推理，最终答案由图确定性执行得到；用 graph-based RL 同时奖励结构有效性、答案正确性和与参考图的节点重合度。在 5 个基准、4 个模型家族上稳定优于监督图训练，并相较标准 GRPO 和 CoT 监督训练可减少最多 5-6 倍生成长度；忠实性实验表明模型更依赖图推导答案，而非事后合理化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought prompting produces reasoning traces that are hard to verify, expensive to generate, and not always faithful; the paper asks whether models can instead generate verifiable, executable programs whose final answer is produced deterministically.

Method: The model is trained to generate an executable computational graph instead of a free-form chain of thought. At generation time, the LLM emits a structured graph representation. The final answer is then obtained by deterministically executing that graph, so answer correctness is separated from language generation. Training uses graph-based reinforcement learning. The reward combines structural validity of the generated graph, correctness of the final answer after execution, and node overlap with reference graphs. This shapes the model toward graphs that are both well-formed and semantically aligned with correct reasoning while still producing the right answer. The paper reports comparisons against supervised graph training, standard GRPO, and chain-of-thought supervised training. Faithfulness experiments indicate that with this approach, models tend to derive their answers from the generated graphs rather than producing post-hoc rationalizations, which is a common failure mode of chain-of-thought. The deterministic execution path also reduces generation length by up to 5-6x relative to free-form CoT-based generation in the reported settings.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "consistently improves over supervised graph training", "setting": "five benchmarks, four model families, graph-based RL vs supervised graph training"}, {"metric": "generation length", "value": "up to 5-6x reduction", "setting": "compared with standard GRPO and chain-of-thought supervised training"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Structural Chain-of-Thought framework where the model emits an executable computational graph rather than free-form text reasoning；Deterministic graph execution produces the final answer, decoupling reasoning structure from final-answer generation；Graph-based reinforcement learning objective rewarding structural validity, answer correctness, and node overlap with reference graphs；Demonstrated faithfulness improvement: answers are derived from generated graphs rather than post-hoc rationalizations, and generation length can be reduced by up to 5-6x versus standard GRPO and CoT supervised training

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting enables reasoning in large language models but produces explanations that are difficult to verify, expensive to generate, and not always faithful. We introduce a Structural Chain-of-Thought framework where models generate executable computational graphs instead of free-form reasoning. The final answer is obtained by deterministically executing the graph. The model is trained using graph-based reinforcement learning that rewards structural validity, answer correctness, and node overlap with reference graphs. Experiments across five benchmarks and four model families show that our method consistently improves accuracy over supervised graph training and can reduce generation length by up to 5-6x compared with standard GRPO and chain-of-thought supervised training. Our faithfulness experiments reveal that models trained with our approach tend to rely on deriving their answers from the generated graphs rather than producing post-hoc rationalizations as usually in chain-of-thought reasoning.

</details>


### [237] [Self-Evolving Visual Agent via Margin Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15084)
*Wenxi Yang,Yuzhong Zhao,Baochang Zhang,Yunfan Liu,Qixiang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Marvo 提出一种自演化视觉智能体训练方法，利用挑战者生成问答、求解者通过强化学习学习工具调用，并引入 margin reward（有无工具的性能差）构建自适应课程，在无监督图像上提升视觉搜索等任务准确率最高 7.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需人工标注的问答对或专家演示，从无标签图像中让视觉智能体学会有效调用工具（包括视觉搜索），同时适应问题难度。

Method: Marvo 包含挑战者（challenger）与求解者（solver）两个智能体。挑战者从无标签图像生成问答对；求解者通过强化学习解决生成的问答问题，学习在推理过程中调用工具。挑战者接收 margin reward，定义为求解者在有工具与无工具条件下的性能差异。该奖励促使挑战者生成难度适中（需要工具但仍在求解者能力范围内）的问题。据此形成自适应课程，迭代提升求解者的工具调用能力。训练在无标注图像上进行，无需人工问答对或专家演示。在覆盖智能体推理、视觉搜索、数学和通用真实世界推理的多个基准上评估，平均准确率最高提升 7.1%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "提升最高 7.1%", "setting": "覆盖 agentic reasoning、visual search、mathematical、general real-world reasoning 等基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Marvo 自演化框架，包含挑战者（生成问答对）和求解者（通过强化学习学习工具调用）两个智能体；设计 margin reward：用求解者有无工具时的性能差作为挑战者的奖励，鼓励生成需要工具但又不太难的问题；基于 margin reward 构建自适应课程，自动调节问题难度以促进求解者学习工具调用

Abstract: Visual agents that interleave reasoning with tool invocation have posed a promising direction for multimodal tasks. However, learning effective tool invocation usually relies on curated question-answer pairs or expert demonstrations at scale, which are expensive to acquire. In this study, we propose Marvo, a simple-yet-effective self-evolution approach that enables learning tool invocation from unlabeled images. Marvo consists of two agents: (1) a challenger that generates question-answer pairs from unlabeled images, and (2) a solver that learns tool invocation by solving questions through reinforcement learning. To encourage effective tool invocation, a good training question should be difficult enough to warrant tool use, yet remain within the solver's capabilities. To this end, we introduce a margin reward to the challenger, defined as solver's performance difference with and without access to tools. With the margin reward, we create an adaptive curriculum that encourages the solver to learn effective tool invocation. Across benchmarks covering agentic reasoning, visual search, mathematical, and general real-world reasoning, Marvo improves average accuracy by up to 7.1\% --- demonstrating margin reward as a preferred way to scale question complexity without human supervision.

</details>


### [238] [Can You Trust Your Preference Models? Stackelberg Games for Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15008)
*Riccardo Formenti,Aditya Agrawal,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将偏好模型评估中的误差利用问题建模为零和 Stackelberg 博弈，Leader 最小化集成分歧、Follower 最大化分歧，提出具有收敛保证的 SMIDA 算法及神经策略梯度版本，在合成 oracle 上验证分歧可作为预测误差代理，并提升反转偏好的比较比例，为 LLM 对齐中的偏好模型鲁棒性提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏真实人类偏好的情况下，如何利用偏好模型集成的不一致性作为预测误差代理，主动搜索能被评估器反转偏好的比较样本，以提供额外监督并提升策略对齐的鲁棒性。

Method: 论文提出一个基于 Stackelberg 博弈的偏好模型对抗搜索框架。输入是上下文比较样本，由两个策略网络组成：Leader 策略尝试生成使偏好模型集成分歧最小的比较，Follower 策略尝试生成使分歧最大的比较，形成零和博弈。训练时，利用偏好模型集成对比较样本的预测分歧作为预测误差的代理信号，无需真实人类偏好。针对有限上下文 Stackelberg 博弈，论文证明均衡存在性，并在正则化条件下给出唯一性和闭式解。算法层面，提出 Stackelberg Mirror Descent-Ascent（SMIDA），采用两时间尺度更新（Leader 和 Follower 学习率不同），在表格设定下具有线性最后迭代收敛。进一步，论文开发了神经策略梯度版本，将上述思想扩展到连续/高维策略空间，用神经网络参数化 Leader 和 Follower。训练目标是通过对抗搜索找到高分歧样本，并将其作为额外监督信号用于策略对齐。推理时，训练好的 Leader 策略可用于生成候选比较样本，交由偏好模型集成评估，进而筛选出潜在错误样本进行再训练或修正。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "超过分歧阈值的比较比例", "value": "增加（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "合成 oracle，留出数据，训练策略对比参考策略"}, {"metric": "反转 oracle 偏好的比较比例", "value": "增加（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "合成 oracle，留出数据，训练策略对比参考策略"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将偏好模型不一致性搜索形式化为 Leader 最小化分歧、Follower 最大化分歧的零和 Stackelberg 博弈。；针对有限上下文 Stackelberg 博弈证明均衡存在性，并在正则化下给出唯一性和闭式刻画。；提出两时间尺度的 Stackelberg Mirror Descent-Ascent（SMIDA）算法，在表格设定下具有线性最后迭代收敛。；开发神经策略梯度版本，并在合成 oracle 上验证分歧作为预测误差代理的有效性。

Abstract: Language models are frequently aligned to human preferences via imperfect evaluators whose errors can be exploited during optimization. We train policies to search for comparisons on which an evaluator may reverse the underlying preference, providing candidates for additional supervision. Since human preferences are unavailable during policy training, we use disagreement among an ensemble of preference models as a proxy for prediction error. We formulate this search as a zero-sum Stackelberg game between two opposing policies, a Leader which tries to minimize disagreement, and a Follower which maximizes it. For a general class of finite contextual Stackelberg games, we establish equilibrium existence and, under regularization, uniqueness and a closed-form characterization. We introduce Stackelberg Mirror Descent-Ascent (SMIDA), a two-timescale algorithm with linear last-iterate convergence in the tabular setting, and develop a neural policy-gradient analogue. Experiments with a synthetic oracle support disagreement as an informative proxy for prediction error. On held out data the trained policies outperform reference policies and increase the fraction of comparisons that exceed a disagreement threshold and reverse the oracle's preference.

</details>


### [239] [SkillHorizon: Constructing Long-Horizon Agent Tasks through Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14966)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Hangyu Guo,Shijue Huang,Zhixiong Zhang,Kawai Chung,Zhongwei Xie,Tianshi Zheng,Wei Fan,Qing Zong,Haochen Shi,Baixuan Xu,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillHorizon 通过技能组合在七个商业环境中构建长时程智能体任务，并评估五个语言模型在 1050 个任务上的表现，揭示随着工作负载增加，局部目标完成率从 90.4% 降至 46.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何扩展长时程智能体任务的需求和依赖关系，同时保持任务可执行且结果可验证。

Method: SkillHorizon 是一个基准测试框架，其架构围绕技能组合生成器展开。生成器使用领域特定规则将可重用的技能程序转化为具体商业活动。这些活动通过输入输出依赖、共享资源和状态更新相互连接，使得工作负载和协调需求同步增长。成功参考执行用于验证任务可行性并测量交互工作负载，程序化评估器则评估局部完成情况和整体任务成功。在七种商业环境中生成 1050 个任务，评估五个语言模型，参考交互工作负载范围为 64K 至 1M tokens。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均局部目标完成率", "value": "从 90.4% 降至 46.8%", "setting": "五个语言模型在参考交互负载 64K 至 1M tokens 之间的表现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过技能组合生成具有输入输出依赖、共享资源和状态更新的长时程任务；使用成功参考执行验证任务可行性并测量交互工作负载；程序化评估器评估局部完成和整体任务成功；在 1050 个任务上评估五个语言模型，参考交互负载达 64K 至 1M tokens

Abstract: Long-horizon agents must complete workflows in which later activities depend on earlier outputs and changes to shared state. A key challenge for evaluation is to expand task requirements and their dependencies while keeping tasks executable and outcomes verifiable. We introduce SkillHorizon, a benchmark that constructs long-horizon tasks through skill composition in seven business environments. Its generators use domain-specific rules to turn reusable skill procedures into concrete business activities. These activities are connected through input-output dependencies, shared resources, and state updates, allowing workload and coordination demands to grow together. Successful reference executions validate task feasibility and measure interaction workload, while programmatic evaluators assess local completion and whole-task success. We evaluate five language models on 1,050 tasks with reference interaction workloads ranging from 64K to 1M tokens. Across these scales, mean local-goal completion falls from 90.4% to 46.8%, as completed work fails to keep pace with growing requirements. At larger workloads, agents more often submit unsuccessful final answers with substantial work unfinished and tool-call allowance remaining. Trace analysis also identifies cases where scripts execute successfully but their outputs fail downstream business checks. SkillHorizon provides a configurable testbed for studying sustained progress, dependency coordination, and complete task delivery in growing workflows. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [240] [FleetPilot: Fine-Grained Steering Empowers Real-World LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14970)
*Kounianhua Du,Jianxing Liu,Kangning Zhang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Jiarui Jin,Jianghao Lin,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FleetPilot提出一种免训练的细粒度激活steering框架，从用户历史与查询中动态生成样本级干扰向量并注入LLM前向传播，实现查询相关的个性化生成，声称在短/长文本生成与web function calling上均有提升且推理与存储开销较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户交互历史稀疏、异构且快速演化时，如何以低推理与存储开销实现查询相关的LLM个性化适配，避免上下文管理、参数微调或多候选打分的高昂成本。

Method: FleetPilot以用户历史与当前查询为输入，先通过match contrast模块从用户数据中构造与查询相关的对比信号，作为个性化证据来源；随后细粒度steering组件挂接（hook）模型attention层与MLP层的激活，从不同网络深度提取反映用户偏好的样本级信号；query-conditioned aggregation模块将这些多层信号按查询条件聚合，生成样本级干扰向量（interference vector）；该向量在前向传播过程中被注入模型内部激活，从而在不修改权重的情况下实现实例级个性化适配。整个流程无需参数训练周期，仅在推理时动态生成并注入steering向量，目标是在短到长文本生成及web function calling等任务上提升个性化表现，同时控制存储与推理延迟开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 样本级干扰向量（interference vector）动态生成并注入前向传播的细粒度steering框架；match contrast机制生成与用户查询相关的信号；从attention与MLP层hook激活并聚合的细粒度steering组件；query-conditioned aggregation模块将多信号合成为上下文相关增强；无需训练周期且存储/推理延迟开销较小的可部署设计

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of large language models (LLMs), there is an increasing demand for effective personalization techniques that can adapt model behavior to individual user preferences. Personalized LLMs must infer a user's query-dependent preferences from limited, heterogeneous, and evolving interaction histories. Existing approaches rely primarily on in-context personalization, parametric adaptation, or personalized reward modeling, but respectively incur recurring costs in context management, model optimization and deployment, or multi-candidate generation and scoring. These limitations become particularly restrictive when user observations are sparse and the preference evidence relevant to each query varies across contexts and over time. To address these challenges, we propose \modelname, a fine-grained and instance-tailored steering framework that dynamically generates sample-level interference vectors from user data and injects them into the model's forward pass for personalized adaptation. Our approach introduces: match contrast that produces user-relevant signals, fine-grained steering component that captures nuanced signals by hooking activations from attention and MLP layers, and query-conditioned aggregation module that synthesizes these signals into contextually relevant enhancements. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios, including short-to-long text generation, and web function calling, validate the effectiveness and compatibility of \modelname. Results show that our method consistently brings significant performance gains, and show extreme superiority in fast-shifting and data-sparse environments. Additionally, \modelname rids us of training cycles and incurs light overhead of storage or inference latency. These make \modelname a strong and real-world deployable method. Implementation is available \footnote{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [241] [VC-OPD: Visual Counterfactual On-Policy Distillation for Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14939)
*Jiahe Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VC-OPD 通过原始图像与反事实图像两次教师评估估计 token 级视觉依赖，并据此重加权 on-policy distillation 损失，结合 mass-preserving normalization，使视觉语言推理训练更关注图像 grounded 的 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 式强化学习的序列级奖励难以对单个推理 token 做细粒度信用分配，而标准 on-policy distillation 对所有教师偏好的 token 给予相似重要性，导致模板短语和视觉无关的延续也获得学习容量，削弱视觉 grounding 能力。

Method: 方法包含三个阶段。首先，教师监督 warm start：对学生模型进行教师监督微调，以稳定响应格式并减少后续 on-policy rollout 中的噪声。其次，on-policy distillation：对学生生成的轨迹，用两个条件分别查询教师：一个在原始图像下，一个在实例特定证据被破坏的反事实图像下。原始图像下的教师分布作为蒸馏目标；原始与反事实教师分布之间的差异估计每个 token 的视觉依赖程度，并用于软重加权蒸馏损失，使视觉 grounded 的 token 获得更高权重。最后，对加权后的 token 权重进行 mass-preserving normalization，使每个响应的总权重保持不变，避免响应级更新尺度不稳定。该设计在保留 OPD 密集监督的同时，把学习容量转移到依赖图像证据的推理 token 上。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "视觉推理性能", "value": "提高（未披露具体数值）", "setting": "多模态推理基准实验"}, {"metric": "token 级蒸馏有效性", "value": "更有效（未披露具体数值）", "setting": "多模态推理基准实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用同一学生轨迹在原始图像与破坏实例证据的反事实图像下分别做教师评估，以原始-反事实教师分布差异估计 token 级视觉依赖；以原始图像教师分布作为蒸馏目标，并用视觉依赖软重加权蒸馏损失，将学习容量偏向视觉 grounded 推理 token；对 token 权重进行 mass-preserving normalization，避免响应级更新尺度不稳定；教师监督 warm start 稳定响应格式并减少噪声 rollout，再进入 on-policy distillation

Abstract: GRPO-style reinforcement learning has advanced vision-language reasoning, but its sequence-level rewards provide limited credit assignment for individual reasoning tokens. On-policy distillation (OPD) addresses this issue by providing dense supervision on student-generated trajectories, yet standard OPD assigns similar importance to all teacher-preferred tokens, including template phrases and visually irrelevant continuations. We introduce Visual Counterfactual On-Policy Distillation (VC-OPD), a grounding-aware distillation method that prioritizes tokens whose correctness depends on image-specific evidence. After a teacher-supervised warm start that stabilizes response formats and reduces noisy rollouts, VC-OPD performs on-policy distillation with two teacher evaluations for each student trajectory: one under the original image and one under a counterfactual image with corrupted instance-specific evidence. The original-image teacher distribution determines the distillation target, while the original--counterfactual teacher gap estimates token-level visual dependence and softly reweights the loss. To avoid unstable response-level update scales, VC-OPD further applies mass-preserving normalization to the token weights. This design preserves the dense supervision of OPD while shifting learning capacity toward visually grounded reasoning tokens. Experiments on multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that VC-OPD improves visual reasoning performance and produces more effective token-level distillation.

</details>


### [242] [What Survives When Activation Steering Is Used as Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14958)
*Vedant Jumle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于激活引导的记忆机制，发现冻结 LLM 旁存储的激活 shift 只能保留与问题无关的内容：约 98% 无关回复不变，但事实几乎无法使用（至多 15%，上下文为 96-98%），偏好仅在窄强度窗口迁移，且参考向量的选择会决定存储概念还是方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在冻结 LLM 旁使用训练无关的激活记忆时，哪些类型的内容（事实、偏好）能够存活并可用于后续会话，以及写入与回放机制如何影响记忆的选择性与可用性。

Method: 系统由冻结 LLM 和训练无关的激活记忆组成。对于每次经验，记录其在晚期层状态上引起的改变，作为 shift 存储。shift 以对话 gist 的白化 key 归档。在后续会话中，当当前对话的 gist 与某个 key 匹配时，将对应的 shift 加回晚期层激活。论文分析指出，这种 shift 是效果在上下文中的固定平均，而注意力会在上下文中针对每个问题重新计算该效果，因此只有与问题无关的内容（如事实、偏向）可能存活。实验在 Qwen3.5-2B 和 9B 上进行预注册研究，使用四个模型家族的评判模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "无关回复不变比例", "value": "约 98%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-2B 和 9B，预注册研究，多模型家族评判"}, {"metric": "事实干净使用率（记忆）", "value": "至多 15%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-2B 和 9B，预注册研究"}, {"metric": "事实干净使用率（上下文）", "value": "96-98%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-2B 和 9B，预注册研究"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出训练无关的激活记忆：将一次经验在晚期层状态上造成的改变存储为 shift，以白化后的 key 归档，仅在对话 gist 匹配时回加。；理论分析指出 shift 是效果在上下文中的固定平均，而注意力会针对每个问题重新计算，因此仅问题无关内容能存活。；预注册实验揭示事实以‘要说的词’形式仅存储于最后几层且几乎不被使用；偏好仅在窄强度窗口迁移；写入偏好时参考向量的选择决定存储的是概念还是方向。

Abstract: Activation steering has recently been used as a memory for skills: past experiences of an agent are stored as steering vectors and retrieved later. Facts and preferences about the user are assumed to need text instead. We test that assumption with a training-free activation memory beside a frozen LLM. The change an experience causes in late-layer states is stored as a shift, filed under a whitened key for the conversation's gist, and added back in a later session only when the gist matches. We argue that such a shift is a fixed average of an effect that attention, in context, recomputes for every question, so only question-independent content can survive. In a pre-registered study on Qwen3.5-2B and 9B, with judges from four model families, the memory is selective (about 98% of unrelated replies are unchanged), but facts are stored as the word to say, in the last layers only, and are almost never used (at most 15% clean use, against 96-98% in context). Leanings transfer only in a narrow strength window. Most strikingly, the reference subtracted when writing a preference decides whether its concept or its direction survives: unless the opposite is subtracted, a stored dislike is recalled as a like.

</details>


### [243] [How Should a Camera-First AI Agent Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14772)
*Xiaofan Wu,Xi Zeng,Miaoxia Chen,Peishan Chen,Shuyan Li,Chen Wang,Jiyun Yao,Hanyong Zhong,Jiahao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对相机优先智能体中瞬时视觉目标难以被捕获的问题，提出双截止时间架构，通过快速记忆提交和延迟推理分离来确保证据不丢失，在MOT17上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机优先智能体在视觉目标短暂出现时需要及时捕获和推理，而由于端到端推理延迟，证据可能在推理完成前消失。

Method: 该架构由三个主要模块组成：1）始终在线、有界的非语义快速路径，用于实时处理视觉流并检测短暂出现的目标；2）主动记忆提交机制，事件驱动地将关键时刻提交到持久记忆，避免被后续输入覆盖；3）延迟 deliberation 层，利用多模态模型和工具进行语义推理。数据流为：快速路径持续监控视觉输入，当检测到相关事件时触发记忆提交，之后延迟层异步处理已存储的证据。训练目标未明确披露，推理时快速路径需在硬截止时间内完成记忆提交，而推理层在软截止时间内生成答案。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化瞬时目标交互（Ephemeral-Target Interaction, ETI）问题；提出感知截止时间契约（Perceptual Deadline Contract），区分硬记忆截止时间和软推理截止时间；设计双截止时间架构：始终在线的非语义快速路径、主动记忆提交机制和延迟 deliberation 层

Abstract: Camera-first agents must act on the visual world as it unfolds. We argue that the central obstacle for future camera-first agents is not recognition ability - multimodal models (vision-language and live/streaming alike) generalize well through language and remain the right tool for semantic reasoning - but time. Many visual targets are ephemeral: they appear, are query-relevant, and leave within a short window. Binding memory to the same end-to-end path as reasoning means that by the time a monolithic pipeline completes, the evidence is gone. We formalize this as Ephemeral-Target Interaction (ETI) and a Perceptual Deadline Contract separating a hard memory deadline from a soft reasoning deadline, grounded in the bounded, passive nature of sensory and working memory. We propose a two-deadline architecture: an always-on, bounded, non-semantic fast path; an active remembering mechanism that commits a moment to durable memory before it is overwritten (event-driven in v1); and a deferred deliberation layer (multimodal model + tools). We instantiate the contract on real MOT17 track windows, a consistency check, and calibrate the single-path baseline from measured frontier-model latency: even a short answer takes 1.2-9.3 s, and an agentic identify-and-search longer. Answers can be late; evidence cannot be missed.

</details>


### [244] [GateSight: Recurrent Memory Anticipates Attention Memory for Query-Agnostic KV Eviction in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14754)
*Junjie Li,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GateSight利用混合LLM中GDN循环记忆在prefill阶段产生的write gate、decay和write magnitude信号，通过轻量per-head MLP预测KV条目的未来效用，实现query-agnostic的KV淘汰。在1/10 KV cache预算下，在SCBench和RULER-4096上对Qwen3.8-27B和Qwen3.8-Flash-Next的实验中，准确率领先KVzip 9.5至47.2个点，且额外开销相对prefill可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合LLM（Gated DeltaNet循环记忆层与全注意力层交错）中，全注意力层的KV cache随上下文增长，成为服务显存的主要开销；如何在未见query之前（query-agnostic）准确判断哪些KV条目对未来注意力输出有用并加以淘汰，从而在极低KV缓存预算下维持长上下文推理精度。

Method: 混合LLM将Gated DeltaNet（GDN）层与全注意力层交错。GDN层具有固定大小的循环记忆，在prefill阶段产生写入信号：write gate、decay和write magnitude。论文首先观察到GDN头的长记忆能力由权重而非文档决定。接着定义KV条目的未来效用：移除该条目对注意力输出在模型自身续写上的改变量，并发现GDN的写入信号在未见任何query前即可预测该效用。由于该关系分布在多个头和层上，且线性映射对高效用尾部的恢复不如非线性，GateSight为每个头训练一个轻量级MLP，输入为GDN写入信号，不读取注意力分数，输出预测的效用。这些效用决定每个注意力层保留哪些KV条目；每层保留的数量由模型原生注意力输出门的均值决定（跨层差异超过一个数量级）。在serving时，prefill后GateSight对KV cache打分一次，并物理释放被丢弃的条目，因此不引入持续的每步开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对于KVzip的准确率提升", "value": "9.5 到 47.2 个点", "setting": "在SCBench和RULER-4096上，使用Qwen3.8-27B和Qwen3.8-Flash-Next，在1/10注意力KV cache预算下，最长262K token，四种模型-基准组合中的四组均领先KVzip"}, {"metric": "在四种模型-基准组合中，三种上为五种query-agnostic方法中准确率最高", "value": "3/4", "setting": "1/10注意力KV cache预算，长上下文基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现GDN循环记忆的写入信号（write gate、decay、write magnitude）在prefill阶段即可预测KV条目的未来效用，且该关系跨多个GDN头和层分布。；定义KV条目的未来效用为：移除该条目对模型自身续写时注意力输出的改变量，并用该定义验证循环记忆信号与未来效用的关联。；使用轻量级per-head MLP（不读取attention score）从GDN写入信号中非线性学习效用预测，优于线性映射对高效用尾部的恢复。；利用模型原生注意力输出门（attention output gate）的层级均值（跨层差异超过一个数量级）来决定每层保留多少KV条目。；在serving时，prefill后对cache一次性打分并物理释放被丢弃的KV条目，开销相对prefill可忽略。

Abstract: Hybrid language models interleave Gated DeltaNet (GDN) layers, whose recurrent memory has a fixed size, with full-attention layers, whose key-value (KV) cache grows with the context and dominates serving memory. In this paper, we ask whether the recurrent memory already knows what the attention memory will need. We first show that which GDN heads of Qwen3.8-27B keep long memories is fixed by the weights rather than by the document. Next, we define the future utility of a KV entry by how much removing it changes the attention output over the model's own continuations, and find that the write gate, decay and write magnitude that GDN layers emit during prefill anticipate this utility before any query is seen. Because the relation is spread across many GDN heads and layers, and a linear map recovers its trend but less of its high-utility tail than a nonlinear one, we propose GateSight, which learns it with lightweight per-head multilayer perceptrons that read these signals and no attention score. Their predicted utility decides which entries each attention layer keeps, and how much each layer keeps is set by the model's native attention output gate, whose mean differs by more than an order of magnitude across layers. Finally, at serving time, GateSight scores the cache once after prefill and physically releases discarded KV entries. Experimental results on SCBench and RULER-4096 with Qwen3.8-27B and, with the same recipe, the sparse-attention model Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, up to the 262K-token limit of the models, show that with a tenth of the attention KV cache, GateSight is the most accurate of five query-agnostic methods on three of the four model-benchmark pairs and leads KVzip on all four, by 9.5 to 47.2 points. Notably, it scores a full-length context with negligible overhead relative to prefill.

</details>


### [245] [ProgAgent: Learning Tool Orchestration through Programmatic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14774)
*Hao Li,Keqing He,Longtao Zheng,Junlong Li,Weihao Zeng,Qian Liu,Yuzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProgAgent提出完整的程序化工具调用（PTC）训练栈，包含可验证长程任务合成、轻量PTC运行时与两阶段训练配方；仅用215个任务即在四个基准上超过同规模基线，并在LOCA-bench的64k-token环境状态下取得30.7%通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体多采用直接工具调用（DTC），工具响应不断堆入模型上下文，导致长程任务中的上下文腐化与编排崩溃；而程序化工具调用（PTC）此前主要作为推理时优化，其训练收益不明且缺少可验证的长程训练任务。

Method: ProgAgent由三部分构成端到端PTC训练栈。输入侧为长程任务与工具环境；不同于DTC将每次工具响应追加进模型上下文，PTC通过可执行代码调用工具，中间结果保存在运行时变量中而非上下文，从而避免上下文膨胀。第一模块是任务合成流水线：自动生成可验证的长程任务，并在四种环境规模下实例化为共215个训练任务。第二模块是轻量PTC运行时：负责执行模型生成的代码、管理工具调用与变量状态，使工具返回结果留在运行时而非模型上下文。第三模块是两阶段训练配方：先进行第一阶段训练，再进行第二阶段训练，使模型学会在代码中编排工具。训练目标是任务可通过运行时状态进行程序化验证的通过率目标。推理时模型输出可执行代码，由运行时执行并返回变量状态，模型据此继续编排，从而在64k-token环境状态下仍保持编排能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass rate", "value": "30.7%", "setting": "LOCA-bench，64k-token环境状态，ProgAgent-14B，同规模基线在该设置下为0"}, {"metric": "pass rate", "value": "高于同规模评估基线", "setting": "四个基准，模型规模与基线相当，使用215个任务、四种环境规模实例化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出ProgAgent完整PTC训练栈，包含可验证长程任务合成流水线、轻量PTC运行时与两阶段训练配方；将PTC从推理时优化扩展为可训练的智能体范式，并验证其收益可跨评估接口迁移；仅用215个任务在四种环境规模实例化即可获得优于同规模基线的通过率

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly expected to orchestrate diverse tools over long-horizon tasks. Existing agents typically use Direct Tool Calling (DTC), where the model invokes tools sequentially, and tool responses accumulate in the model context, which can lead to context rot and orchestration breakdown. In contrast, Programmatic Tool Calling (PTC) invokes tools through executable code and stores intermediate results in runtime variables rather than appending them to the context. Prior work has explored PTC mainly as an inference-time optimization, but its benefits for agentic training remain unclear, and suitable verifiable training tasks are scarce. In this work, we introduce ProgAgent, a full PTC training stack, comprising a pipeline for synthesizing verifiable long-horizon tasks, a lightweight PTC runtime, and a two-stage training recipe. Using only 215 tasks instantiated at four environment scales, ProgAgent yields higher pass rates than the evaluated baselines of comparable size across four benchmarks. On LOCA-bench, ProgAgent-14B achieves a 30.7% pass rate at a 64k-token environment state, where these baselines score zero. Cross-harness experiments show that PTC-trained models outperform DTC-trained models under both evaluation interfaces, suggesting that the benefits of PTC training extend beyond the interface.

</details>


### [246] [SAGE: Pushing 2-Bit Vector-Quantized LLMs toward Real-World Usability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14745)
*Xianglong Yan,Zenan Wu,Kewei Zhang,Kaicheng Yang,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE提出2-bit权重仅向量量化方法，通过能量分层分组（ESG）保护敏感通道，并用自适应比特拆分（ABS）在方向码本与半径精度间分配固定预算；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上保留95.3%的BF16平均基准分数，配合自定义CUDA内核实现最高2.55倍FP16端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2-bit极低位宽权重仅量化下，现有向量量化方法在真实任务上仍有明显精度退化，且存在两个未被充分探索的设计问题：(1)如何在联合编码的向量中保护敏感的权重通道；(2)如何在固定比特预算下，在方向与半径等表示分量之间分配比特以适应不同权重矩阵。

Method: SAGE是一种面向2-bit权重仅量化的向量量化方法，包含两个耦合模块。输入为LLM权重矩阵及校准数据产生的激活。首先，Energy-Stratified Grouping (ESG) 依据通道能量将通道分层，在每个子向量内混合来自不同能量层级的通道，并把分组过程与激活加权拟合耦合，使高敏感通道在联合编码中获得更强保护。其次，Adaptive Bit Split (ABS) 在固定比特预算下，为每个权重矩阵搜索方向码本容量与半径精度的最优比特拆分，以激活加权重建误差作为选择准则，从而适配不同权重矩阵的统计特性。量化后的权重以向量码本形式存储。推理时使用自定义CUDA内核完成向量的查表与反量化计算，实现非流式端到端加速。训练目标为最小化激活加权重建误差，并在固定比特约束下优化分组与比特拆分。整体流程为：校准激活输入→能量分层与子向量分组拟合→逐矩阵比特拆分配置选择→2-bit向量量化模型→自定义CUDA内核推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均基准分数保留率（相对BF16）", "value": "95.3%", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，2-bit量化，覆盖知识、数学、代码生成、指令遵循、工具使用等基准"}, {"metric": "平均基准分数", "value": "state-of-the-art（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "2-bit量化设置，多类基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Energy-Stratified Grouping (ESG)：将通道分组与激活加权拟合耦合，在每个子向量中按能量层级分组不同通道，以优先保护高敏感通道。；Adaptive Bit Split (ABS)：在固定比特预算下，按矩阵自适应平衡方向码本容量与半径精度，选择激活加权重建误差最低的比特拆分方案。；自定义CUDA内核：实现2-bit向量量化模型的高效推理，最高获得2.55倍非流式端到端FP16加速。

Abstract: Extreme low-bit weight-only quantization is an effective approach to reducing the deployment costs of large language models. Below 3 bits, scalar quantization often suffers severe accuracy loss, while vector quantization (VQ) better preserves accuracy through more expressive joint encoding. However, existing VQ methods still exhibit substantial degradation on real-world tasks, and two design challenges remain underexplored: (1) protecting sensitive weight channels within jointly encoded vectors; and (2) allocating a fixed bit budget among representation components, such as direction and radius, to suit different weight matrices. To address these challenges, we propose SAGE: Split Adaptation and Grouping by Energy for weight-only vector quantization. First, Energy-Stratified Grouping (ESG) couples channel grouping with activation-weighted fitting, grouping channels from different energy tiers within each subvector to prioritize sensitive channels. Second, Adaptive Bit Split (ABS) balances direction-codebook capacity and radius precision per matrix, selecting the split with the lowest activation-weighted reconstruction error under a fixed budget. Across benchmarks covering knowledge, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and tool use, SAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance in the 2-bit quantization setting. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, it retains 95.3% of the BF16 model's average benchmark score. With our custom-designed CUDA kernel, SAGE delivers up to a 2.55-fold non-streaming end-to-end speedup over FP16. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [247] [Shaping Latent Mixture-of-Experts: From Expert Geometry to Layerwise Capacity Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14644)
*Yaoxiang Wang,Kai Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Lanrui Wang,Guoliang Zhao,Junhui Li,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentMoE把专家计算搬进低维隐空间，使专家输入维度与中间宽度成为显式设计轴，并给出Jacobian秩上界证明近方形专家在固定参数量下最优；同时用逐层内在维度指导深度方向专家数量与形状的容量分配，在每层参数量、激活FLOPs与通信量恒定的条件下取得更低的验证损失和更强的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE通常通过缩小专家宽度来增加专家数量，把专家形状当作路由粒度的副产品，导致专家表达能力（Jacobian秩上限）与参数效率未被显式优化，同时各层容量分配缺乏原则性指导。

Method: LatentMoE把传统MoE中专家在原始高维隐藏状态上的计算改写为在低维隐空间中的计算：先通过一个下投影把输入映射到维度d_in较低的空间，再在该空间内以宽度d_mid进行专家变换，最后上投影回原隐藏维度。由此，专家的输入维度与中间宽度成为可独立设定的设计变量，而传统做法把两者隐含绑定于路由粒度。论文给出理论结果：专家局部Jacobian的秩同时被d_in与d_mid上界约束，因此在固定专家参数量下，将两者平衡到接近方形可最大化秩上限，提升单个专家的表达容量。作者在多个模型规模与不同路由粒度下做受控形状扫描，验证近方形专家配置一致占优。在此基础上，进一步利用逐层内在维度（ID）来指导深度方向的容量分配：对每一层，根据其ID协同决定专家数量与专家形状，同时约束每层总参数量、激活FLOPs与通信量保持不变，从而把“容量分配”问题与“专家形状”问题统一在固定预算下求解。训练目标即标准语言建模/下游目标，优化后模型在验证损失与下游指标上均优于层同构MoE基线。推理路径为路由→隐空间下投影→低维专家计算→上投影回原维度，通信开销因专家计算在低维空间进行而受到控制。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "验证损失", "value": "低于层同构基线", "setting": "ID引导容量分配 vs 层同构MoE基线，保持每层参数量/激活FLOPs/通信量恒定"}, {"metric": "下游任务性能", "value": "优于层同构基线", "setting": "多下游任务评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LatentMoE：把专家计算移入低维隐空间，使专家输入维度与中间宽度成为显式可设计变量；理论分析：专家局部Jacobian秩由输入维度与中间宽度共同上界约束，二者平衡可在固定专家参数量下最大化秩上限；受控形状扫描：跨模型规模与路由粒度实证近方形专家占优；基于逐层内在维度(ID)的深度容量分配：在每层参数量、激活FLOPs和通信量恒定的前提下协同调整专家数量与形状；将专家形状与逐层分配确立为MoE的实用设计轴

Abstract: Fine-grained mixture-of-experts (MoE) models commonly increase expert count by narrowing each expert, treating expert shape as a by-product of routing granularity. LatentMoE moves expert computation into a lower-dimensional space, exposing the input dimension and intermediate width of each expert as explicit design choices. We show that an expert's local Jacobian rank is jointly bounded by these two dimensions, and that balancing them maximizes the rank ceiling at fixed per-expert parameters. Controlled shape sweeps across model scales and routing granularities consistently favor near-square experts. We further use layerwise intrinsic dimension (ID) to guide capacity allocation across depth, co-varying the number and shape of experts while keeping per-layer parameter count, activated FLOPs, and communication volume constant. Compared with layer-homogeneous baselines, our ID-guided allocation achieves lower validation loss and stronger downstream performance. These results establish expert shape and layerwise allocation as practical design axes for MoEs.

</details>


### [248] [Iterate on an LLM, Serve on an SLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14576)
*Keegan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“在前沿LLM上迭代，在SLM上服务”的蓝图：先用实时生产流量在GPT-5.4-nano上开发排班安全决策行为，再将11.6k条真实生产决策蒸馏到Qwen3-8B，实现0.913一致率、相比教师更低延迟和成本，且盲测中更受专业排班员青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在生产环境中快速迭代小模型行为并保持低成本、低延迟推理，避免每次行为变化都重新训练。

Method: 系统采用两阶段生命周期。第一阶段：在GPT-5.4-nano（教师模型）上通过prompt工程开发排班安全决策行为，利用线上流量即时反馈（真实排班场景中的护理员-班次对）迭代prompt，直至行为收敛。第二阶段：将第一阶段中教师模型在真实生产流量上记录下来的决策（prompt与输出对，共11.6k条）作为监督数据，对Qwen3-8B进行LoRA监督微调（SFT），从而将收敛后的行为蒸馏到小模型。部署时，学生模型独立处理每个护理员-班次对的是/否安全决策，以低延迟、低成本提供推理服务。训练目标为标准SFT损失，即最小化学生模型输出与教师记录决策之间的差异。推理流程：输入护理员与班次的特征描述，输出二分类安全决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "与教师模型一致率", "value": "0.913", "setting": "在11.6k条生产记录决策上，LoRA微调的Qwen3-8B与GPT-5.4-nano的对比"}, {"metric": "教师模型自一致率", "value": "72.7%", "setting": "GPT-5.4-nano重新运行在自身记录的prompt上"}, {"metric": "专家偏好", "value": "48-29 (学生 vs 教师)", "setting": "盲测中专业排班员在两者不一致时的选择"}, {"metric": "中位延迟降低", "value": "约2.5倍", "setting": "SLM与教师LLM在生产环境中的对比"}]}

Conclusion: 提出先在前沿LLM上用prompt space开发行为并实时获取生产反馈，再将生产记录的决策轨迹（而非合成数据）蒸馏到SLM的完整生命周期。；使用真实生产决策数据（11.6k条）对Qwen3-8B进行LoRA SFT，实现与教师模型0.913的一致率，且教师模型自一致率仅72.7%。；通过盲测显示学生模型在与教师不一致时更受专业排班员青睐（48-29），同时中位延迟降低约2.5倍，成本大幅下降。

Abstract: As frontier LLMs gain more capabilities, the cost to run them also goes up. At the same time, small language models (SLMs) also gain more capabilities but have largely been able to keep their cost stable, offering 5-40 times cheaper inference for the narrow, repetitive subtasks that agents decompose work into. However, the standing limiting factor is getting the correct behavior out of a small model in a fast, efficient, and economical way. Unfortunately, in most cases, zero-shot small models fail narrow production tasks. Furthermore, retraining one every time the behavior changes is too slow to iterate on. We propose doing both in sequence. First, develop the desired behavior in prompt space, on a frontier model, with immediate feedback from live traffic. Then, once the behavior converges, distill the recorded production behavior (not synthetic imitations of it) into an SLM through supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We show a proper use case of this method through a scheduling agent for home-care agencies that fans out a single yes/no safety decision per caregiver-shift pair on GPT-5.4-nano. A LoRA-tuned Qwen3-8B trained on 11.6k recorded production decisions reaches 0.913 agreement, while the teacher (GPT-5.4-nano), re-run on its own recorded prompts, agrees with itself only 72.7% of the time, pointing to the fact that the SLM may also be more consistent. Furthermore, when the two disagreed, a blinded professional scheduler sided with the student 48-29. Finally, we find that the SLM has approximately 2.5 times lower median latency and is a fraction of the cost. We believe this lifecycle of quick building, live feedback, and cheap inference is a blueprint for others building similar systems.

</details>


### [249] [Learning after Execution with Action-Specific Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14573)
*Yufeng Tian,Jiahao Tang,Minghao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出执行后学习框架与动作特定信用分配方法，将程序执行结果作为中间状态支持额外学习交互，在Image-to-TikZ任务上单次渲染成功率提升至92.70%，并产生执行后纠正能力，为LLM在可执行任务上的强化学习提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行结果通常仅作为终端奖励，其状态未被用于进一步学习；如何将执行结果作为中间状态进行额外学习交互，并解决两个动作共享最终目标但角色不同的信用分配问题。

Method: 论文针对可执行任务（以Image-to-TikZ为测试平台）提出执行后学习框架。输入是图像，策略（LLM）生成对应的TikZ代码作为初始动作；执行该代码（编译渲染）得到执行结果，该结果作为中间状态而非仅终端奖励。然后同一策略基于中间状态（如图像与渲染结果的差异）生成纠正动作，对代码进行修正。训练时，初始动作的信用来自最终轨迹结果，纠正动作的信用来自中间状态的改进量，从而实现动作特定信用分配。优化目标结合两种信用信号，通过强化学习（如GRPO变体）更新策略。推理时，模型支持单次调用直接生成代码，也具备执行后纠正能力。实验在945个保留样本上进行，对比持续单阶段GRPO，显示单次调用渲染成功率提升至92.70%，并产生独特的执行后纠正能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "single-call render success", "value": "92.70%", "setting": "945个保留示例，相同额外训练迭代次数下与持续单阶段GRPO（88.25%）对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出执行后学习（Learning after Execution），将执行结果视为中间状态以支持同一策略的额外学习动作。；提出动作特定信用（Action-Specific Credit），为初始动作分配基于最终轨迹结果的信用，并为纠正动作分配基于中间状态改进的信用。；在Image-to-TikZ可执行视觉任务上验证，展示出单次调用渲染成功率的提升以及执行后纠正能力的产生。；表明替代信用定义会在相同轨迹上诱导不同的组相对偏好，强调信用设计的重要性。

Abstract: Execution is often used as terminal feedback: it scores a generated program, but the resulting state is not used for another learning action. Learning after execution instead uses the execution result as an intermediate state that supports an additional learning interaction for the same policy. This creates a credit-assignment problem because the two actions share the same final objective but play different roles in the trajectory. Accordingly, action-specific credit assigns the initial action credit from the final trajectory outcome and the corrective action credit for improvement over the intermediate state. Image-to-TikZ serves as an executable visual testbed for this setting. On 945 held-out examples, learning after execution strengthens the shared policy under single-call inference and also produces a post-execution corrective capability. Single-call render success reaches 92.70\%, compared with 88.25\% for continued single-stage GRPO after the same number of additional training iterations. This corrective capability is not reproduced by continued single-stage training and depends on the intermediate state. Alternative credit definitions also induce different group-relative preferences over the same trajectories. Together, these results suggest that executable tasks contain useful learning structure beyond a terminal reward. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [250] [FDE-Bench: Evaluating End-to-End Delivery from Underspecified Real-World Business Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14539)
*Huaiming Li,Can Huang,Chufan Wu,Danyu Liu,Xiaomin Yuan,Qinze Li,Yui Lo,Wenhui Bai,Qianlong Wang,Zengle Ge,Xiyu Yan,Yuheng Cheng,Jinhui Ren,Guokai Chen,Jiaqun Liu,Haoran Li,Qianhui Liu,Annan Li,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FDE-Bench 是首个基于真实客户项目构建的端到端交付基准，用于评估代理在需求不明确的前向部署工程任务中的交互式需求澄清与迭代优化能力。该基准包含 49 个案例，涵盖组合优化和机器学习。评估了 18 种代理配置，最高平均交付分数仅为 0.5510（FDE 参考为 1.0），最高交付成功率仅 6.12%，表明可靠的前向部署级端到端交付对当前代理仍遥不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化基准通常从明确目标开始，而澄清基准则是在现有任务中引入信息差。本文解决的是代理在真实、需求不明确的前向部署工程任务中，能否通过交互式需求澄清和迭代优化达到客户验收标准。

Method: FDE-Bench 是一个评估基准，其架构包括：1）输入：49 个来自真实客户项目的案例，涵盖组合优化和机器学习任务，每个案例包含初始需求描述（存在缺失需求）。2）交互模块：代理与模拟客户进行对话，主动询问以澄清缺失需求；澄清目标来自一线从业者与 FDE 的真实讨论。3）优化模块：代理根据澄清后的需求迭代优化解决方案。4）评估模块：案例特定的评估器使用客户认可的 FDE 解决方案作为质量参考，根据验收标准对最终交付物评分。评估指标包括平均交付分数和交付成功率（DSR）。整个流程模拟了 FDE 从交互需求澄清到迭代交付的完整过程，用于衡量代理的端到端交付能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最高平均交付分数", "value": "0.5510", "setting": "18 种代理配置中的最高值，FDE 参考为 1.0"}, {"metric": "最高交付成功率 (DSR)", "value": "6.12%", "setting": "计划运行中达到参考标准的比例"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首个基于真实客户项目构建的端到端交付基准，包含 49 个案例，涵盖组合优化和机器学习任务。；澄清目标来源于一线从业者与 FDE 的真实讨论，模拟客户交互环节。；使用客户认可的 FDE 解决方案作为质量参考，通过案例特定评估器对最终交付物进行验收标准评估。；对 18 种代理配置进行了系统评估，揭示了当前代理在端到端交付上的显著不足。

Abstract: Real-world business requests often leave key requirements unstated because frontline practitioners struggle to articulate rules they know from experience. Forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must elicit these requirements through dialogue and iteratively optimize solutions until they meet customer acceptance criteria. Existing optimization benchmarks typically start from explicit objectives, while clarification benchmarks often introduce information gaps into existing tasks. We introduce FDE-Bench, the first benchmark built from real customer projects to evaluate agents on end-to-end delivery tasks that require interactive requirement clarification and iterative optimization. Its 49 cases span combinatorial optimization and machine learning. Clarification targets are distilled from discussions between frontline practitioners and FDEs. Agents elicit missing requirements from a simulated customer, and case-specific evaluators assess final artifacts against acceptance criteria using customer-accepted FDE solutions as quality references. Across 18 agent configurations, the highest mean delivery score is only 0.5510, compared with an FDE reference of 1.0. The highest delivery success rate (DSR), defined as the fraction of planned runs reaching this reference, is only 6.12%. These results show that reliable FDE-level end-to-end delivery remains out of reach for the evaluated agents on underspecified real-world business tasks. The benchmark project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [251] [Frustratingly Easy Agentic RAG via Portable Skills: A Case Study in Customer Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14428)
*Istok Bako,Bonan Min,Tiffany Shao,Claire Cheng,Yashar Mehdad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个企业客服场景的 agentic RAG 部署案例：用一个可移植的技能（自然语言指令 + 轻量工具 + LLM 生成的文档导航层）替代复杂的 RAG 管线，在客服解决工作流生成任务上达到 99.2% 程序等价性和 83.3% 证据充分性，但推理延迟约 90 秒/案例，比生产助手慢一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产级客服场景中，如何用更简单、可移植的方案替代复杂的文档分块、索引、检索管线以及学习式重排器、查询改写器和定制化 agentic RAG 基础设施，同时保持高精度的解决工作流生成。

Method: 系统输入为具体客服案例或请求，以及一个经过精心维护的大规模操作文档语料（SOP、政策文档）。该语料被增强，添加了由 LLM 生成的结构化“Wikipedia 风格”导航层，即对底层文档的索引，用于辅助智能体在检索时快速定位相关章节。核心组件是一个便携式技能，包含：自然语言指令（定义任务与流程）、轻量工具（检索 API 用于从增强语料中获取信息、格式检查脚本用于验证输出格式）。该技能运行在通用智能体测试框架（如 Claude Code）上，由智能体根据指令自主调用检索 API 获取证据，并依据检索到的政策文档生成正确的解决步骤序列。生成结果通过格式检查脚本确保结构合规。整个流程不涉及学习式重排器或查询改写器，智能体的决策完全由自然语言指令和工具调用驱动。训练目标：无模型训练，完全基于提示与工具编排。推理流程：智能体接收案例，调用检索 API 查询增强语料，获取相关文档片段，然后综合生成解决工作流，最终输出经过格式检查的步骤序列。维护方式为编辑文本和数据，而非重新训练模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "程序等价性（procedural equivalence）", "value": "99.2%", "setting": "相对于人工批准的 ground truth，客服解决工作流生成任务"}, {"metric": "证据充分性（evidence sufficiency）", "value": "83.3%", "setting": "客服解决工作流生成任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出便携式技能（portable skill）范式：由自然语言指令、轻量工具（检索 API、格式检查脚本）和增强语料组成，可跨 agentic 框架（如 Claude Code）移植。；在底层文档上叠加 LLM 生成的导航层（类 Wikipedia 结构化索引），作为语料增强，以降低对复杂检索管线的依赖。；展示了该方案在客服解决工作流生成任务上达到 99.2% 程序等价性和 83.3% 证据充分性，且实现成本仅 1-2 工程师日。

Abstract: Enterprise workflow automation typically requires retrieving a complete and accurate set of relevant information from large operational document corpora (SOPs, policy documents) given a specific scenario or request. In customer support (CS), for example, human specialists must determine the correct sequence of resolution steps for each case, a task that demands precise grounding in policy documentation. Meeting this bar in production typically requires complex document chunking, indexing, and retrieval pipelines, often layered with learned re-rankers, query rewriters, and bespoke agentic RAG infrastructure. We report a deployment case study of a simpler alternative: a portable skill consisting of a natural-language instruction, lightweight tools (a retrieval API, format-checking scripts), and a corpus enriched with an LLM-generated navigation layer, which is a structured "Wikipedia-style" index over the underlying documents. This agentic RAG approach is straightforward to implement (1-2 engineer days), portable across capable agentic harnesses (e.g., Claude Code), and performs strongly on the challenging task of CS resolution workflow generation, achieving 99.2% procedural equivalence to human-approved ground truth and 83.3% evidence sufficiency. It is also roughly an order of magnitude slower per case than our deployed production assistant (~90 s vs. ~8-9 s), so we claim no latency advantage. Our claim is scoped to that setting: a mature, well-curated corpus, a latency-tolerant workflow, and human review of every case. There, the skill did the work of a complex RAG stack, and maintenance became a matter of editing text and data rather than retraining models.

</details>


### [252] [AlignPoison: Conditional Targeted Poisoning of Generative Recommenders via Decoding-Aligned Prefixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14487)
*Zhuoxuan Zhang,Jinhao Duan,Xinyu Zhao,Jinduo Guo,Shuai Shao,Rui Li,Luke Simon,Zhun Deng,Tianlong Chen,Bo Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlignPoison研究生成式推荐模型的条件性目标投毒：攻击者黑盒查询API并注入少于1%恶意交互，通过解码对齐的短前缀显著提升触发条件下恶意目标的推广成功率，ASR最高提升16.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型对条件性目标投毒攻击的脆弱性，即攻击者注入少量恶意交互并作为黑盒查询推荐API时，能否在触发条件下定向推广恶意目标。

Method: 论文针对基于自回归解码的生成式推荐模型。输入为交互序列，模型通过自回归解码生成推荐物品。攻击者作为黑盒查询推荐API并注入少于1%的合成交互。AlignPoison框架从API输出中发现并采样上下文对齐的prefix，这些prefix在模型诱导的转移动态下常见且与高频触发词强相关，从而在触发条件下定向推广恶意目标。训练目标为投毒优化，推理时通过触发词激活恶意推荐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ASR lift", "value": "up to 16.3 percentage points", "setting": "单模态和多模态生成式推荐基准，对比竞争基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出条件性目标投毒攻击设置：黑盒API访问、有限恶意交互注入、触发条件定向推广；从解码对齐和前缀流行度两个角度分析恶意交互合成，发现短小、上下文对齐且与高频触发词强相关的prefix能放大定向推广；提出AlignPoison黑盒框架，从API输出中发现并采样上下文对齐的prefix；在单模态和多模态生成式推荐基准上验证，ASR提升最高16.3个百分点

Abstract: Generative recommendation models based on autoregressive decoding have been adopted as alternatives to classical ranking-based systems. While their modeling flexibility has been widely studied, their resilience to data poisoning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we ask: how susceptible are generative recommenders to conditional targeted poisoning? Under a realistic setting where an adversary can inject limited malicious interactions and query a recommendation API as a black box, our empirical study shows that generative recommenders are vulnerable: injecting fewer than 1% synthetic interactions significantly increases the likelihood of promoting malicious targets under the trigger. To characterize this vulnerability, we analyze the synthesis of malicious interactions from two perspectives: (i) decoding alignment and (ii) prefix popularity. We observe that short, context-aligned prefixes that are both common under the model's induced transition dynamics and strongly linked to a frequent trigger consistently amplify targeted promotion. Motivated by this analysis, we develop AlignPoison, a black-box framework that discovers and samples context-aligned prefixes from API outputs. We conduct experiments on benchmarks in unimodal and multimodal generative recommendation settings. Results show that these prefixes outperform competitive baselines, improving ASR lift by up to 16.3 percentage points and highlighting a decoding-dependent, context-sensitive poisoning vulnerability in generative recommendation models.

</details>


### [253] [Should SLMs be in the Driver's Seat? A Study of Local Models as Interpreters in Cloud-Written Agent Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14209)
*Boris Radovič,Marco Canini,Veljko Pejović*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探索一种云边协同智能体架构：云端大模型编写程序，将运行时需要解释用户私有数据的步骤通过函数调用委托给本地小模型执行。在AppWorld上，该架构在六种骨干模型与测试分区组合中均提升了纯代码智能体的成功率，最高提升14.9个百分点，同时保持数据本地化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大模型驱动的智能体需要读取用户私有数据，而本地小模型能保护隐私但成功率低；如何在数据不出设备的前提下，让云端生成的程序在运行时利用本地小模型解释数据，从而提升任务成功率。

Method: 系统采用云-端协同架构。云端大模型接收用户任务描述并编写一个可执行程序，程序在编写时未见到用户私有数据。程序在执行过程中，遇到需要对运行时出现的私有数据进行解释的步骤时，不直接让云端模型访问数据，而是通过普通函数调用，将受限的问答委托给设备上的本地小模型。本地小模型在设备上执行该函数调用，仅将解释结果返回给程序，原始用户数据始终留在本地。程序继续执行直至完成任务。训练目标未明确披露，推理路径为：云端生成程序→设备执行程序→必要时调用本地小模型解释数据→返回结果继续执行。该设计旨在兼顾隐私保护与任务成功率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "提升最高14.9个百分点", "setting": "AppWorld基准，六种骨干模型与测试分区组合，与纯代码智能体对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一种中间架构：云端模型编写程序时将需要运行时数据解释的步骤延迟为对本地小模型的受限函数调用；在AppWorld上验证了该架构在多种骨干模型和测试分区上均提升代码智能体成功率；在保持用户数据本地化的同时，缩小了与云端ReAct循环的性能差距

Abstract: The most accurate agents executing user-provided tasks are driven by large, cloud-hosted models, which solve them by reading the private data of the user. Agents powered by models small enough to run on edge devices keep that data local, but yield far worse results. A third option is to have cloud models write a program that devices execute: the data stays local, but as the program is written before any data is seen, it fails whenever a step depends on interpreting content that appears only at run time. We explore an intermediate architecture, in which the cloud-written program defers bounded questions over user data to a small local model through an ordinary function call. On AppWorld this improves the success rate of a code-only agent in all six combinations of backbone model and test partition we evaluate, by up to 14.9pp, recovering a substantial part of the gap to a cloud-hosted ReAct loop while keeping user data on the device.

</details>


### [254] [Echo: Error-Compensated 4-Bit Tensor-Parallel Communication for LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14277)
*Xingyu Xie,Xiaoyong Xie,Zhouchen Lin,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Echo 提出一种误差补偿的 4-bit 张量并行 all-reduce 通信方法，通过高精度求和与跨调用误差反馈，将累积误差控制在深度无关的界内。在 A100 上相对 16-bit NCCL 实现 2.09x 加速，推理精度平均差距 0.4 点以内，10B token 预训练最终损失差距小于 0.9%，无需 FP8/FP4 硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量并行中每层依赖的 all-reduce 通信成本无法隐藏，16-bit 全归约成为瓶颈，而直接使用 4-bit 量化会因每次归约引入舍入误差并在层间累积，损害模型质量。

Method: Echo 是一种面向张量并行的 4-bit all-reduce 通信机制。在张量并行层结束时，传统做法需要将各 GPU 的部分结果以 16-bit 进行 all-reduce 归约求和。Echo 将传输数据压缩至 4-bit 以降低通信量，同时在接收端以高精度执行求和，使得每个数值在任何 GPU 数量下每次调用最多经历两次量化。关键创新在于：归约求和本身可以揭示每次量化注入的误差，Echo 捕获这些误差并在后续 all-reduce 调用中反馈，使先前引入的误差在后续层中相互抵消，而不是逐层累积。论文从理论上证明累积误差独立于模型深度保持有界。该机制可直接用于训练和推理，且不依赖 FP8 或 FP4 硬件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "all-reduce 加速比（相对 16-bit NCCL）", "value": "2.09x", "setting": "A100 GPU，1 GB 消息"}, {"metric": "平均准确率差距（相对 16-bit 推理）", "value": "0.4 点以内", "setting": "Qwen3-8B，七个基准平均"}, {"metric": "最终训练损失差距（相对 16-bit 训练）", "value": "<0.9%", "setting": "10B token 预训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 all-reduce 数据以 4-bit 传输、在高精度下求和，使每个值在任意 GPU 数量下每次调用最多被量化两次；利用求和过程显式提取每次引入的误差，并将其反馈到后续调用中，使旧误差相互抵消而非累积；证明累积误差独立于模型深度保持有界；无需 FP8/FP4 硬件即可实现 4-bit 通信压缩

Abstract: Tensor parallelism ends every layer with an all-reduce that sits between two dependent matrix multiplications, so its cost cannot be hidden and grows as compute outpaces interconnect bandwidth. Sending 4-bit instead of 16-bit values would cut this cost by nearly 4x, but the all-reduce must add the values it carries, and rounding the sum injects an error at every call. Model quality depends on how these errors accumulate over layers, yet existing methods treat each call on its own. We present Echo, a 4-bit all-reduce for training and inference that controls the accumulated error. Echo moves data in 4 bits and sums in high precision, so each value is quantized at most twice per call for any number of GPUs. The summation reveals each injected error, and Echo feeds it back into later calls, so old errors cancel instead of accumulating. We prove that the accumulated error stays bounded independently of depth. On A100 GPUs, Echo is 2.09x faster than the 16-bit NCCL all-reduce on 1 GB messages. It speeds up the time to first token by up to 1.16x and the training step by up to 1.09x on an idle interconnect, and by up to 1.44x and 1.38x as background traffic grows. It keeps the average accuracy over seven benchmarks within 0.4 points of 16-bit inference on Qwen3-8B. In 10B-token pretraining, its final loss is less than 0.9% above that of 16-bit training. Echo needs no FP8 or FP4 hardware.

</details>


### [255] [Harness^2: Recursive Agent Harnessing for an Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14224)
*Ruiyao Xu,Yanfei Chen,Zhongying CuiZhu,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Yifei Ming,Han Yu,Rujun Han,Chen-Yu Lee,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Harness^2 提出双层递归的即插即用 agent harnessing 框架：任务级用对比式 Propose-Probe-Compose 在无真值验证器时可靠编辑 harness，harness 级把编辑经验沉淀为持续改进 harness 以泛化到新任务；在三个专业基准、四种模型-框架配置上单步递归即取得最高 +11.5、+4.5、+11.8 的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界、测试时任务新颖且无真值验证器的条件下，如何可靠地对 LLM agent 的执行 harness 进行递归自我改进，并将单任务经验泛化到未来新任务。

Method: Harness^2 是即插即用的递归 agent harnessing 框架，包含两个相连的递归层级。其输入是当前任务以及 agent 现有的执行 harness；任务级递归在测试时针对当前任务对 harness 进行精炼，核心是对比式 Propose-Probe-Compose 步骤：先提出候选 harness 编辑，再探测其效果，最后组合出改进版本，从而在开放世界无真值验证器的情况下仍能可靠地指导编辑，并可通过并行或顺序方式扩大任务级递归预算。harness 级递归则把上述任务级编辑过程中获得的经验抽取出来，用于更新一个持续改进 harness，使 agent 在面对未来新任务时具备更好的适应能力。两个层级形成闭环数据流：任务级产生编辑经验，harness 级沉淀经验并反哺后续任务级递归。论文在三个专业基准和四种 model-harness 配置上验证，单步递归即可稳定超越 base harness，且收益随任务级递归预算增加而扩大。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WorkBuddy-Bench 得分提升", "value": "最高 +11.5 points", "setting": "GLM-5 与 Gemini 3.5 Flash，四种 model-harness 配置，单步递归，相对 base harness"}, {"metric": "JobBench 得分提升", "value": "最高 +4.5 points", "setting": "GLM-5 与 Gemini 3.5 Flash，四种 model-harness 配置，单步递归，相对 base harness"}, {"metric": "LAB 得分提升", "value": "最高 +11.8 percentage points", "setting": "GLM-5 与 Gemini 3.5 Flash，四种 model-harness 配置，单步递归，相对 base harness"}, {"metric": "任务级递归预算增大后的收益", "value": "进一步提升（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "随任务级递归预算增加"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 任务级递归：用对比式 Propose-Probe-Compose 步骤在无真值验证器时指导 harness 编辑，并可并行或顺序扩展递归预算；harness 级递归：从任务级编辑中抽取经验，更新一个持续改进 harness，提升对未来任务的适应能力；plug-and-play 的递归 agent harnessing 框架，统一两个层级的递归

Abstract: LLM agents' capabilities depend not only on their underlying models but also on their execution harnesses. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) offers a promising approach to automatically evolve these harnesses. However, deploying RSI in open-world settings still faces two critical challenges. First, open-world tasks are typically unseen during the harness design phase and novel at test time, leaving no ground-truth verifiers to reliably guide online harness edits. Second, the RSI process remains highly task-specific and fails to generalize to novel scenarios. We introduce Harness^2, a plug-and-play, recursive agent harnessing framework that addresses these challenges through two connected levels of recursion. Task-level recursion refines the current task's harness using a contrastive Propose-Probe-Compose step that reliably guides edits without ground-truth verifiers, and can be scaled further via parallel or sequential recursion. Harnessing-level recursion then extracts experience from these edits to update a continual improvement harness, enabling the agent to better adapt to future tasks. Across three professional benchmarks and four model-harness configurations, a single step consistently improves GLM-5 and Gemini 3.5 Flash over their base harnesses, yielding gains of up to 11.5 points on WorkBuddy-Bench, 4.5 points on JobBench, and 11.8 percentage points on LAB, with further gains as the task-level recursion budget increases.

</details>


### [256] [Unconstrained CTI-REALM with Open-Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14180)
*Sahil Garg,Ruhi Gottumukkala,Vishal Agarwal,Shubham Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在CTI-REALM安全智能体基准上对比了26个开放权重与闭源模型，发现开放权重模型在放宽回合限制后能以不到十分之一的成本匹配最强闭源模型，并指出原基准的消息限制会扭曲排名，提出分阶段评估与按阶段路由的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全智能体任务中，开放权重模型能否以更低成本匹配闭源前沿模型，以及如何更合理地评估多阶段智能体工作流中各模型的能力与成本。

Method: 该研究构建了一个统一的智能体评估框架，针对CTI-REALM基准。输入是威胁情报报告，智能体通过查询实时遥测存储来生成检测规则。评估框架包括统一的运行器、评分器和LLM评审团。实验对比了26个模型（19个开放权重，7个闭源），在原始消息限制和放宽限制两种设置下进行。此外，研究引入了分阶段指标：将智能体提交的检测查询在遥测存储中执行，并将输出与答案键对比进行评分。研究还分析了各模型生成的token数量，并探讨了将工作流各阶段路由到最佳模型的可能性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "奖励提升", "value": "开放权重模型在放宽回合限制后获得最高奖励", "setting": "CTI-REALM基准，20个开放权重与7个闭源模型对比"}, {"metric": "成本对比", "value": "开放权重模型成本低于最强闭源模型的十分之一", "setting": "匹配最强闭源模型性能时"}, {"metric": "价格与能力差异", "value": "相似奖励的模型成本相差超过一个数量级", "setting": "领先模型之间"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在统一测试框架下对26个开放权重与闭源模型进行大规模对比，揭示开放权重模型在放宽回合限制后能够匹配甚至超越闭源模型，且成本低于十分之一。；指出原基准的智能体消息数限制是一个混淆因素，会导致超限任务在所有检查点得零分，从而扭曲模型能力排名。；提出分阶段评估指标，通过实际执行检测查询并对照答案键评分，而非仅依赖检查点记录。；发现不同模型在不同阶段表现领先，提出按阶段路由至最佳模型而非选择单一整体最优模型。；报告各模型生成的token数量，发现思考输出占比与奖励不相关。

Abstract: Organisations that want language-model agents for security work face a choice between open-weight models, which they can run and audit themselves, and far more expensive closed frontier models. We study that choice on CTI-REALM ( arXiv:2603.13517 ), a benchmark in which an agent turns a threat-intelligence report into a working detection by querying a live telemetry store. We evaluate twenty-six models, nineteen open-weight and seven closed, under a common harness, scorer and panel of LLM judges. Our main finding is that an open-weight model can match the strongest closed models at less than a tenth of their cost, provided it is given enough turns. The benchmark's published limit on agent messages is a confound: a task that exhausts the limit scores zero on every checkpoint, including those it had already passed, so results at that limit partly measure which models finish in time. At the published limit the leading open-weight and closed models cannot be distinguished. With the limit relaxed, the models it was truncating recover, and the highest rewards belong to open-weight models. Among the leading models, price is a poor guide to capability: models with similar rewards differ in cost by more than an order of magnitude. Because the benchmark's checkpoint scores largely record what an agent did or mentioned, we also introduce stage-level metrics that score what the agent submits, by executing its detection queries in the telemetry store and judging its outputs against the answer key. Rather than combining them into one more overall reward, we show that different models lead different stages, which suggests routing each stage of the workflow to the model that performs it best rather than choosing a single model by its overall score. We also report how many tokens each model generates, and find that the share of output spent on thinking does not track the reward.

</details>


### [257] [ClawLoop: Self-Improving Agents via Harness-Native Training Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14118)
*Hansi Yang,Linji Hao,Hongyan Hao,An Wang,Han Wu,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ClawLoop 提出一个无需外部教师模型的自改进循环，用于训练 harness/sandbox 环境中的 LLM agent：任务合成（双构造流程、诊断驱动的难度增长、无 agent 与有 agent 验证）、SFT/RL 训练、以及 rollout 诊断反馈，循环迭代。用 Qwen3.6-27B 作循环模型在 SkillsBench、Claw-Eval-Live、ClawGym-Bench 上获得一致提升，并将精炼任务迁移到 4B/9B 模型验证数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing post-training pipelines for harness-based agents are one-shot and teacher-dependent: a stronger external model synthesizes tasks and collects trajectories, the target agent is trained once, and evaluation results are not fed back, bounding improvement by the external model and human priors with no room for self-improvement.

Method: ClawLoop is a self-sustaining cycle with three phases. Task Synthesis generates a diverse pool of training tasks via two complementary construction routines, applies diagnosis-conditioned difficulty growth (using a capability analysis report from the previous iteration), then performs both agent-free and agent-based task validation. Training updates the agent via SFT and/or RL on the synthesized tasks. Diagnosis and Feedback analyzes the agent's rollout behaviors to produce a capability analysis report that feeds back into task synthesis for the next iteration. Using Qwen3.6-27B as the loop model, the cycle yields consistent benchmark improvements, and the iteratively refined tasks transfer to smaller-scale Qwen3.5-4B and 9B models with gains across all benchmarks.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "benchmark performance", "value": "consistent improvements", "setting": "SkillsBench, Claw-Eval-Live, and ClawGym-Bench using Qwen3.6-27B as the loop model"}, {"metric": "benchmark performance", "value": "gains across all benchmarks", "setting": "iteratively refined tasks transferred to smaller-scale Qwen3.5-4B and 9B models"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ClawLoop self-sustaining cycle without any external teacher model and minimal prior dependencies, composed of Task Synthesis, Training, and Diagnosis & Feedback.；Diagnosis-conditioned difficulty growth during task synthesis driven by a capability analysis report from rollout behaviors.；Two complementary task construction routines plus both agent-free and agent-based task validation.；Iterative feedback from diagnosis to the next iteration's task synthesis, enabling self-improvement.；Demonstrated transfer of iteratively refined tasks to smaller-scale models, validating data quality independently of the loop model.

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) has driven their deployment toward general-purpose harness environments, where LLM-based agents operate in sandbox workspaces and interact directly with file systems and shells. While numerous post-training pipelines exist for tool-use agents, their counterparts for harness-based agents remain scarce and are predominantly one-shot and teacher-dependent: a stronger external model synthesizes tasks and collects trajectories, the target agent is trained once, and evaluation results are consumed by human researchers rather than fed back into the loop. Such pipelines are inherently bounded by the capability of the external model and the scope of human prior, leaving no room for self-improvement. To address these limitations, we propose ClawLoop, a framework that enables self-improving without any external teacher model and with minimal prior dependencies. ClawLoop composes three phases into a self-sustaining cycle: Task Synthesis, which generates a diverse pool of training tasks via two complementary construction routines, applies diagnosis-conditioned difficulty growth followed by both agent-free and agent-based task validation; Training, which updates the agent via SFT and/or RL; and Diagnosis and Feedback, which analyzes the agent's rollout behaviors to produce a capability analysis report that will be used as feedback for task synthesis in the next iteration. Using Qwen3.6-27B as the loop model, ClawLoop yields consistent improvements on SkillsBench, Claw-Eval-Live, and ClawGym-Bench. The iteratively refined tasks are further transferred to smaller-scale Qwen3.5-4B and 9B models with gains across all benchmarks, validating data quality independently of the loop model.

</details>


### [258] [One-Sided Verification Under Limited Expert Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18089)
*Fan Nie,Haotian Ye,Shurui Liu,Zihao Wang,Stefano Ermon,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSVerify面向有限专家评审的验证场景，通过分层错误提议与验证器解耦，在固定假拒绝率约束下最大化无效解拒绝率，在竞赛与研究基准上平均减少46%无效解人工评审，后训练验证器后达57%，并用于确认论文错误与辅助解决数学猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在候选生成规模超出人类专家评审能力时，如何以有限人工评审成本最大化过滤无效候选，同时保持对有效候选的低假拒绝率。

Method: OSVerify是一个将错误提议与错误验证解耦的验证框架。输入为候选解（来自竞赛或研究问题）及其上下文。错误提议者首先在不同粒度上对解进行分层分解，搜索可能存在的错误，生成错误候选集合。随后，验证器对每个错误候选进行判断，区分实质性错误与轻微或虚构错误。验证器可经过后训练以提升区分能力。系统通过调整提议与验证的阈值，在满足有效解假拒绝率（FNR）约束下最大化无效解拒绝率（TNR），从而减少需要人工评审的无效解数量。最终，仅有被验证器判定为含实质性错误的解才会进入人工评审环节，以降低人类工作量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "无效解人工评审减少比例", "value": "46%（平均）", "setting": "竞赛级与研究级基准，相同经验低FNR约束下，对比投票基线"}, {"metric": "无效解人工评审减少比例", "value": "57%", "setting": "对验证器进行后训练后，相同FNR约束下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 采用单侧验证目标：在约束有效解假拒绝率（FNR）的前提下最大化无效解拒绝率（TNR），将人工评审成本显式最小化。；OSVerify框架将错误提议与错误验证解耦，错误提议者在不同粒度上分层分解搜索错误，验证器区分实质性错误与轻微或虚构错误。；对验证器进行后训练，进一步将无效解人工评审减少量从46%提升至57%。；将框架应用于已发表研究论文中的错误识别（获原作者确认）及三个Langlands开放猜想的解决（获专家确认）。

Abstract: AI has become increasingly helpful in scientific discovery, yet human expert review remains essential for those problems without reliable automated evaluation, posing a fundamental challenge as candidate generation scales beyond available review capacity. This paper studies verification under limited human effort, where rare valid solutions need to be prioritized while invalid candidates should be filtered out to reduce human workload. We adopt the one-sided verification objective that maximizes the rejection rate of invalid solutions (TNR) under a constraint on the false-rejection rate of valid ones (FNR), making human review cost an explicit term to minimize. Drawing on the intuition that verifying a specific error can be easier than discovering one, we introduce OSVerify, an algorithmic framework that decouples error proposal from error validation. The error proposer searches for errors in solutions across different granularities by hierarchical decomposition, while the validator distinguishes substantial errors from minor or hallucinated ones. Experiments on competition- and research-level benchmarks show that OSVerify reduces the number of human reviews of invalid solutions by 46% on average compared to voting baselines under the same empirical low FNR constraint. Our post-training of the validator further improves the reduction to 57%. As high-impact real-world use cases, we apply OSVerify to identify errors in published research papers that are subsequently confirmed by the original authors and help resolve three Langlands open conjectures that are confirmed by experts, demonstrating its utility to facilitate human-AI research collaboration.

</details>
