Abstract: Structured knowledge retrieval acquires task-relevant evidence from structured knowledge sources for downstream tasks. Existing methods rely on unconstrained retrieval or predefined query-rule supervision, such as author-paper rules for scientific discovery and anatomy-drug rules for precision medicine. Such supervision is rarely available in practice. We propose Co-Adaptive Rule Routing and Constraining Retrieval (CoRE), which jointly learns which reasoning rule to assign to each query and how to retrieve evidence under that rule, without predefined query-rule pairs. A routing agent selects a rule, and its corresponding retriever traverses the knowledge base and ranks evidence that satisfies the rule's constraints. Because routing and retrieval are interdependent, CoRE alternates between reinforcement learning for the routing policy and contrastive learning for the rule-constrained retriever. The selected rules provide supervision for retriever training, while retrieval performance rewards the routing policy. This closed-loop process enables both components to co-adapt without explicit query-rule supervision. Experiments on STaRK-QA datasets across scientific discovery, biomedical reasoning, and e-commerce knowledge management demonstrate CoRE's effectiveness. Ablations show the benefit of co-adapting the router and retriever. Generation-based routing transfers across datasets and benefits from multi-domain pretraining followed by target-specific fine-tuning. Analysis of rule usage across queries also informs automatic rule refinement and discovery.

</details>


### [289] [Understanding and Mitigating Training Instability in Large-Scale Recommender Systems: A Cascade Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14394)
*Yuening Li,Zeyu Wang,Zhen Zhang,Jiayi Liu,Zhimeng Jiang,Yuji Roh,Weikai Xu,Mingyan Gao,Onkar Dalal,Chuan He,Liang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文基于工业短视频推荐系统，分析训练不稳定性的梯度-权重-损失级联，并提出分阶段缓解框架，对处理大规模动态推荐训练问题有实践参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动态推荐系统中，数据分布快速变化和模型复杂度增加导致训练不稳定，表现为梯度爆炸→权重爆炸→损失爆炸的G-W-L级联发散，阻碍模型收敛和性能。

Method: 论文从级联视角分析大规模推荐系统训练不稳定性：输入为动态平台上的用户交互数据，分布快速变化；模型为大规模推荐模型。训练过程中，由数据分布偏移或模型复杂度引发的梯度爆炸首先出现(G)，进而导致权重/激活值爆炸(W)，最终造成损失爆炸(L)，形成G-W-L级联，破坏收敛。所提框架将干预措施分类为：梯度控制(如梯度裁剪、归一化)、权重/激活稳定化(如权重归一化、激活正则化)、损失塑形(如损失函数调整、鲁棒损失)，分别对应级联阶段进行阻断。论文在工业短视频推荐系统上实证评估这些策略的有效性，但未披露具体网络结构、训练目标、推理路径等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统性地分析并形式化G-W-L级联发散现象，将其分解为梯度爆炸、权重爆炸、损失爆炸三个阶段；提出结构化缓解框架，将干预措施明确映射到级联的具体阶段：梯度(G)控制、权重/激活(W)稳定化、损失(L)塑形；在主要工业短视频推荐系统上进行实证验证

Abstract: Training large-scale recommender systems on dynamic industrial platforms, such as Short-Form Video (SFV) services with billions of daily interactions, faces critical stability challenges. Rapid distribution shifts in user data and increasing model complexity frequently trigger a divergence cascade, typically initiating with gradient explosion, propagating to weight explosion, and culminating in loss explosion. This G-W-L cascade severely impedes model convergence and performance. This paper provides a systematic analysis of this G-W-L cascade within an industrial SFV recommender context. We then propose and evaluate a structured mitigation framework where interventions are explicitly mapped to counteract specific stages of the cascade: gradient (G) control, weight/activation (W) stabilization, and loss (L) shaping. Our contribution provides a structured framework for understanding and tackling training divergence, offering practical insights for deploying robust, continuously updated recommenders in large-scale, non-stationary settings. We empirically validate the effectiveness of these strategies on a major industrial SFV recommendation system.

</details>


### [290] [LLM-based User-Product Relevance Evaluation for Dynamic Product Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14550)
*Wen Zhang,Xiao Bai,Hsiang-wei Chao,Mengfan Zhu,Tong Zhao,Mingxuan Ju,Xiaodong Liu,Congfei Zhang,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Snap Inc. 提出基于 LLM 的 DPA 检索离线评估指标：先将用户参与历史压缩为意图代表集合，再用 agentic 提示框架与教师-学生蒸馏的 LLM 语义评判器逐意图打分并聚合为用户级相关性，该指标与离线/在线业务指标强相关，可加速检索迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPA 检索系统的评估困难：传统离线参与度指标受稀疏、有偏反馈限制，在线评估成本高、耗时长，缺少稳定可解释且与业务指标强相关的离线评估手段。

Method: 该方法以用户参与历史为输入，先通过 Semantic-ID 分组与事件分层切分将历史压缩为紧凑的意图代表集合；随后对每个意图代表，用 LLM 作为语义相关性评判器（judge）评估候选推荐产品与该意图的功能与语义相关性，再将各意图的相关性聚合成用户级相关性分数。为规模化计算，论文提出 agentic、与人类对齐的提示框架，自动生成多类别相关性指南，并将教师模型的判断蒸馏到高效学生模型，使学生模型能低成本地在大规模上复现教师判断。最终得到的离线相关性指标与离线及在线业务指标强相关，服务于 DPA 检索系统在 A/B 测试前的迭代。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将用户参与历史通过 Semantic-ID 分组、事件分层切分为紧凑的意图代表集合，并逐意图评估推荐产品相关性后聚合为用户级相关性分数；提出 agentic 且与人类对齐的提示框架，自动生成多类别相关性指南；将教师模型判断蒸馏到高效学生模型，实现规模化 LLM 语义相关性评判；该度量与离线和在线业务指标强相关，可用于 A/B 测试前的检索迭代

Abstract: Dynamic Product Advertising (DPA) retrieval systems are a core component of modern e-commerce advertising; however, their evaluation remains challenging. Conventional offline engagement metrics are limited by sparse, biased feedback, while online evaluation is costly and time-consuming. In this work, we propose a user--product relevance metric for offline evaluation of DPA retrieval systems. The metric first compresses the engagement history into a compact set of intent representatives via Semantic-ID-grouped, event-stratified segmentation, then assesses the relevance of a recommended product to each individual intent and aggregates them as a user-level relevance score, yielding a stable and interpretable measure of retrieval quality. To compute this metric at scale, we leverage large language models (LLMs) as semantic relevance judges, capturing nuanced functional and semantic relationships that heuristic methods often miss. We propose an agentic, human-aligned prompting framework that generates multi-category relevance guidelines and distills teacher-model judgments into an efficient student model for scalable evaluation. The resulting metric strongly correlates with offline and online business metrics, enabling faster and more reliable DPA retrieval iteration before A/B testing.

</details>


### [291] [EVISTA: Unified Multimodal Video Search with Evidence-Aware Temporal Path Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14510)
*Minh-Huy Tran,Van-Thinh Vo,Minh-Khoi Nguyen,Duy-Tan Nguyen,Anh-Quan Nguyen-Tran,Loi Nguyen Khanh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EVISTA 是一个面向大规模视频检索的统一多模态系统，集成了视觉语义、OCR、ASR、物体和元数据搜索，并提出 TEMPO-P 证据感知的时间路径优化方法，通过跨事件候选聚合、跨模态联合关键帧打分和动态规划实现有序多事件检索。在 HCMC AI Challenge 2026 的五个官方查询和36个额外多事件查询上，TEMPO-P 在视觉事件上与视觉主导的时间对齐方法持平（R@1 0.42），并能检索视觉方法无法表示的文本定义事件（R@1 0.38）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视频集合中，针对同一视频内多个按时间顺序描述的事件进行检索，且不同事件由视觉内容、屏幕文字（OCR）、语音（ASR）、物体和元数据等不同证据描述。独立检索每个事件会导致某事件召回弱时整体视频被漏检，且独立召回的候选事件在时间上不一致或无法表示主要由文本/语音定义的事件。

Method: EVISTA 是一个统一的多模态视频检索系统，以共享关键帧索引为核心。系统输入包括视频关键帧、视觉嵌入、OCR 文本、ASR 转写、物体检测结果以及元数据。检索时，系统在共享索引上同时支持视觉语义、OCR、ASR、物体和元数据搜索，形成多模态候选。针对有序多事件检索，TEMPO-P 首先跨事件和跨模态聚合候选视频，避免因单个事件弱召回而导致整个视频被漏检；然后对每个视频内的关键帧使用视觉、OCR 和 ASR 证据进行联合打分；最后通过动态规划在视频内选择一条紧凑的、按时间排序的关键帧路径，使得所选路径同时满足事件顺序、证据模态匹配和局部得分最优。TEMPO-P 作为 EVISTA 的有序事件检索模式集成到系统中。论文未披露训练目标、模型规模、训练资源、推理硬件或延迟等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "R@1", "value": "0.42", "setting": "在五个组织者查询和36个额外多事件查询上，TEMPO-P 与视觉主导的时间对齐方法在视觉描述事件上持平，均为0.42。"}, {"metric": "R@1", "value": "0.38", "setting": "在包含文本定义事件的查询上，TEMPO-P 能够检索，而视觉主导的时间对齐方法无法表示此类事件。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: EVISTA 统一多模态视频检索系统，在共享关键帧索引上集成视觉语义、OCR、ASR、物体和元数据搜索。；TEMPO-P 证据感知的时间路径优化方法，用于有序多事件检索：跨事件和跨模态聚合候选视频，联合使用视觉、OCR 和 ASR 证据对关键帧打分，并通过动态规划在每个视频内选择紧凑的按时间排序路径。；针对独立事件检索的两个局限提出系统级解决方案：跨事件候选聚合缓解弱事件漏检，跨模态联合打分与动态规划保证时间一致性并支持文本/语音定义事件。

Abstract: In the era of big data, precise event retrieval from large video collections has become increasingly important and challenging. In particular, locating multiple events from the same video in their temporal order is difficult when different events are described by different types of evidence, such as visual content, on-screen text, and spoken words. We develop EVISTA, a unified multimodal video retrieval system for the Ho Chi Minh City AI Challenge 2026, which integrates visual-semantic, OCR, ASR, object, and metadata search over a shared keyframe index, and propose TEMPO-P, an evidence-aware temporal path optimization method for ordered multi-event retrieval. Building EVISTA for this task reveals two limitations of independent event retrieval: a relevant video may be missed when one event is weakly retrieved, and independently retrieved event candidates may be temporally inconsistent or unable to represent events defined primarily by text or speech. TEMPO-P addresses these limitations by aggregating candidate videos across events and modalities, jointly scoring keyframes using visual, OCR, and ASR evidence, and selecting a compact temporally ordered path within each video through dynamic programming. On five organizer queries and 36 additional multi-event queries, TEMPO-P matches visual-only temporal alignment on visually described events (R@1 0.42 for both) and retrieves queries containing text-defined events, which visual-only alignment cannot represent (R@1 0.38). TEMPO-P is integrated into EVISTA as its ordered-event retrieval mode.

</details>


### [292] [Simple Linear Corrections for Compression in Late Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14196)
*Mihir Agarwal,Sabyasachi Basu,Viraj Agrawal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种轻量级线性修正方法，用于恢复late-interaction多向量检索模型在压缩后的检索质量。方法冻结编码器和压缩器，通过蒸馏未压缩模型的分数分布学习全局线性修正，可在搜索时或离线应用。在ColBERTv2、Jina v4、ColPali2及MS MARCO、BEIR、ViDoRe v3上验证，域内BEIR设置中154/160优于基线压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: late-interaction多向量模型索引存储和评分成本高，池化和剪枝等压缩技术在大幅压缩时检索质量明显下降。

Method: 输入为查询和文档，分别由冻结的late-interaction编码器（如ColBERTv2、Jina v4、ColPali2）生成token级多向量表示。文档向量经过免训练压缩器（池化或剪枝）压缩至指定向量预算。在评分阶段，对压缩后的文档向量与查询向量的交互分数应用一个全局线性修正：该修正矩阵通过蒸馏未压缩模型在候选文档上的分数分布学习得到，训练时编码器和压缩器均冻结。修正可应用于搜索时每个查询，或离线预先应用于存储的文档向量。模型在MS MARCO、BEIR和ViDoRe v3基准上评估，覆盖四种压缩方法、多种向量预算及跨域迁移。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "改进设置比例", "value": "154/160", "setting": "域内设置下，修正后优于基线压缩的BEIR配置比例"}, {"metric": "检索质量", "value": "改善", "setting": "ViDoRe v3上，使用合成查询训练的矩阵可优于基线压缩"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 为每个编码器、压缩器和向量预算组合学习一个全局线性修正矩阵，通过蒸馏未压缩模型的分数分布来恢复压缩后的检索质量。；编码器和压缩器保持冻结，线性修正可在搜索时逐查询应用，也可离线一次性应用于存储向量。；在ColBERTv2和Jina v4上跨MS MARCO和BEIR，以及ColPali2在ViDoRe v3上验证，覆盖四种免训练压缩方法、多种向量预算、域内适配和迁移设置。；发现使用合成查询训练的修正矩阵在ViDoRe v3上能超过基线压缩。

Abstract: Late-interaction (multi-vector) models represent documents using a set of token-level vectors, providing a middle ground between cross-encoders and dense encoders in terms of quality and cost of embedding. However, compared to the latter, the indices are costly to store and score. Pooling and pruning based compression techniques are deployed widely but deteriorate in quality noticeably with aggressive compression. We investigate whether a lightweight change to the scoring function can recover retrieval quality after document-vector compression. For each combination of encoder, compressor, and vector budget, we learn a global linear correction by distilling the uncompressed model's score distributions over candidate documents. The encoder and compressor remain frozen, and the correction can be applied to each query at search time or once, offline, to the stored vectors. We evaluate the method with ColBERTv2 and Jina v4 on MS MARCO and BEIR, and with ColPali2 on the ViDoRe v3 visual document retrieval benchmark. Experiments cover four training-free compression methods, multiple vector budgets, in-domain adaptation, and transfer from MS MARCO to BEIR. In the in-domain setting, the correction improves over baseline compression in 154 of 160 BEIR settings, while transfer from MS MARCO is more variable across models and datasets. We also find that matrices trained with synthetic queries can improve over baseline compression on ViDoRe v3. These results show that a small learned correction can recover some of the quality lost to compression without retraining the encoder or recompressing the documents.

</details>


### [293] [Mitigating Popularity Bias in Sequential Recommendations with Sparse Prior-Regularized Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17823)
*Anton Pembek,Anton Klenitskiy,Alexander Savchenko,Alexey Vasilev*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PR-entmax，一种带稀疏先验正则化的训练目标，将物品流行度先验注入稀疏概率映射，使上下文相关的流行度校正同时影响概率与梯度参与。方法不改架构、仅改损失函数，推理时移除先验。在多数据集与多序列推荐模型上声称能降低流行度偏差并保持或提升相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐中流行度偏差导致热门物品过度曝光、目录覆盖率下降，同时需要保持或提升推荐相关性，即在相关性与去偏之间取得更好权衡。

Method: 该工作不引入新的网络结构，而是在现有序列推荐模型的训练目标上做修改。输入是用户交互序列，经由序列推荐骨干网络（如 SASRec 类自注意力或 GRU4Rec 类循环网络）编码为上下文表示，再映射到物品概率分布。PR-entmax 在此映射中引入物品流行度先验：从广义变分原理出发，将流行度先验直接并入稀疏概率映射（类似 entmax 的稀疏化），得到流行度感知的 active support——每个位置只有部分物品获得非零概率，从而只有这部分物品在交叉熵类损失中产生非零梯度参与更新。由于支持集同时取决于上下文相关性和先验流行度，先验不仅改变物品概率，也改变哪些物品参与梯度训练，起到上下文相关的流行度校正作用。训练目标为在稀疏概率映射下最小化推荐损失，按概率分配给正样本与负样本梯度。推理阶段去掉先验项，仅使用学到的上下文相关性对物品打分并排序。整个流程只需替换损失函数，无需改动骨干架构或推理管线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 PR-entmax 稀疏先验正则化目标：在稀疏概率映射中直接引入物品流行度先验，由广义变分原理推导；流行度感知的 active support 机制：哪些物品获得非零概率与非零梯度由上下文相关性和物品流行度共同决定，使先验同时影响概率与训练中的梯度更新；训练时使用先验、推理时移除先验，仅用学到的上下文相关性排序，无需改变模型架构即可集成到现有训练管线

Abstract: Popularity bias is a well-known challenge in recommender systems, often leading to the over-exposure of popular items and reduced catalog coverage. In this work, we propose PR-entmax, a sparse prior-regularized objective that makes popularity correction context-dependent. We derive our method from a generalized variational principle in which an item popularity prior is incorporated directly into a sparse probability mapping, yielding a popularity-aware active support: the set of items receiving non-zero probabilities and hence non-zero gradients is determined jointly by contextual relevance and item popularity. As a result, the prior affects not only item probabilities, but also which items participate in gradient updates during training. The method requires no architectural changes and can be integrated into existing training pipelines by modifying only the loss function. At inference time, the prior term is removed, and items are ranked by the learned contextual relevance alone. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets and sequential recommendation architectures show that PR-entmax consistently reduces popularity bias while preserving or improving recommendation relevance, advancing the relevance-debiasing trade-off.

</details>


### [294] [More Reader Data or a Better Teacher? Updating a Model of Crowd Highlights in Late 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17768)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在社交高亮平台上，作者用固定开发集与密封集比较了‘更多读者数据’、‘更大LLM直接排序’、‘更强LLM当教师’三条路线：3B读者模型显著优于零样本LLM，增加读者数据带来稳定提升，而强LLM教师只带来+0.0013的微弱变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在读者高亮数据稀疏的页面上，是获取更多读者数据、直接使用更强的LLM作为排序器，还是用更强LLM作为教师来提升句子级排序模型？

Method: 输入为页面句子及读者高亮计数。系统有三个可比较的方案：一是直接用七个截至2026年10月发布的LLM对句子进行零样本重要性排序，并将阅读位置作为特征融合；二是用3B参数模型仅基于读者高亮数据训练句子排序器；三是将更强的LLM融合结果作为教师，在读者计数出现平局时打破平局，以调整训练模型。评估固定215个开发页面、预先设定的选择规则以及131个密封新页面，以平均精度（AP）对比模型输出与半数读者标记最多的句子。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average precision", "value": "≤0.3744", "setting": "七个截至2026年10月发布的LLM在215个开发页面上零样本句子排序，对比半数读者标记最多的句子"}, {"metric": "average precision", "value": "0.3931", "setting": "上述LLM融合阅读位置后在215个开发页面上的句子排序"}, {"metric": "average precision", "value": "0.4346", "setting": "3B参数模型仅用读者高亮训练后在215个开发页面上的句子排序"}, {"metric": "average precision 变化", "value": "+0.0013 [-0.0012, +0.0040]", "setting": "用更强的LLM融合结果作为教师打破读者计数平局，对训练模型的影响"}, {"metric": "average precision 变化", "value": "+0.0242 [+0.0179, +0.0301]", "setting": "训练页数从一半增加到全部对训练模型的影响"}, {"metric": "average precision 变化", "value": "+0.0225", "setting": "27B参数骨干网络采用仅读者数据配方在215个开发页面上的提升"}, {"metric": "average precision", "value": "均高于LLM融合", "setting": "131个密封新页面上，3B模型与27B模型的得分均高于LLM融合，二者差异未确定"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 固定比较集合、选择规则与密封集的前置实验设计；对比零样本LLM排序、LLM融合阅读位置、读者数据训练的3B模型三种方案；将强LLM作为教师打破读者计数平局，对比更多读者训练数据带来的增益

Abstract: A model trained on readers' highlights can show a newcomer where earlier readers converged on a page, even on pages with few readers. A year of stronger language models suggests two upgrades: use them directly, or use them as teachers. We compare both with a third, more reader data, on one social highlighting platform, with the comparisons, the selection rules and a sealed set of fresh pages fixed in advance. On 215 development pages, seven language models released by October 2026 rank sentences zero-shot at an average precision of at most 0.3744 against the sentences half the readers marked most, and 0.3931 when fused with reading position, while a 3-billion-parameter model trained on reader highlights alone reaches 0.4346. Used as the teacher that breaks ties among readers' counts, the stronger fusion changes a trained model by +0.0013 [-0.0012, +0.0040], whereas going from half to all of its training pages adds +0.0242 [+0.0179, +0.0301]. A 27-billion-parameter backbone on the reader-only recipe adds +0.0225 on the development pages; on 131 fresh pages, read once and too few for a confirmatory comparison, its difference from the 3-billion model is unresolved, both score above the fusion, and in an exploratory split their lead over the previous model sits on pages from domains that their fresh training pages also cover. In this recipe, more reader data improves a trained model where a stronger tie-breaking teacher yields no material gain, and frontier models used as rankers still trail a small model trained on readers.

</details>


### [295] [RALI@iKAT 2026: Retrieval-aware User Profile Denoising via Personalized Conversational Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17673)
*Yuchen Hui,Fengran Mo,Jia Ao Sun,Yuxing Tian,Wenhao Xu,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 面向iKAT 2026的个性化对话稠密检索方法PCDR，将对话历史与用户画像联合投影到检索专用隐空间，以少量标注实现检索感知的用户画像去噪，替代易损耗的“改写-再检索”管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户画像随时间累积引入噪声，现有基于LLM黑盒推理的个性化对话查询改写方法只能在稀疏词元空间显式选择画像短语，无法评估哪些用户信息对下游检索真正有用，导致个性化与相关性建模脱节。

Method: PCDR作为对话式AI Agent的核心检索组件。其输入为对话历史与累积的用户画像文本；模型将两者联合编码并投影到一个为检索任务专门学习的潜在嵌入空间，从而在表示层面实现用户画像去噪，而非在稀疏词元空间做显式短语选择。该设计继承自对话稠密检索器，复用其上下文去噪能力，因此只需少量个性化相关性标注即可微调训练。训练目标使嵌入空间对齐个性化检索相关性，推理时直接对候选文档进行稠密向量召回，避免“改写-再检索”两阶段管线中个性化与相关性建模解耦的问题。论文未披露更多模块细节、训练目标公式、编码器结构或服务延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出个性化对话稠密检索（PCDR），将对话历史和用户画像联合投影到检索专用隐空间，实现检索感知的用户画像去噪。；继承对话稠密检索器的上下文去噪能力，仅需少量个性化相关性标注即可训练，降低标注成本。；把个性化从显式短语选择转为隐空间联合表示学习，使个性化与下游相关性建模端到端耦合。

Abstract: Modern conversational information access systems continuously extract user information from conversations to build user profiles for personalized retrieval. As user profiles accumulate over time, they inevitably become noisy, making effective user profile denoising a fundamental challenge. Existing personalized conversational query reformulation methods rely on the black-box reasoning capability of LLMs to expand the original query with relevant user profile phrases, producing a verbose query for retrieval. Such rewrite-then-retrieve pipelines reduce user profile denoising to the explicit selection of profile phrases in the sparse token space, separating personalization from downstream relevance modeling. Consequently, they often fail to identify user information that is truly beneficial for retrieval. To enable retrieval-aware user profile denoising, we propose Personalized Conversational Dense Retrieval (PCDR), which learns to jointly project the conversation history and user profile into a latent embedding space dedicated to retrieval. By inheriting the context denoising capability from conversational dense retrievers, PCDR requires only a handful of personalized relevance annotations for training. We evaluate PCDR on the iKAT 2026 tasks, where it serves as the core retrieval component of a conversational AI agent assisting users with complex information needs.

</details>


### [296] [Computed, not Memorized: Bit-Factorized Identifiers for Efficient Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17467)
*I-Fan Lin,Zhen Zhang,Faegheh Hasibi,Suzan Verberne*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出比特因子化数值DocID，使生成式检索器无需额外训练即可对未见文档进行评分，在NQ320K和TriviaQA上未见文档Hit@10提升超20个百分点，推理延迟降低至多7倍，为文档标识符设计提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值型文档标识符（DocID）主要在索引阶段学习，难以泛化到未见文档，且需重新训练才能适应新文档。

Method: 该方法将每个文档标识符（DocID）表示为数值序列，每个数值令牌进一步因子化为多个比特。生成式检索器以语言模型为骨干，在解码时通过束搜索生成DocID序列。每个比特的得分由语言模型表示直接计算，令牌得分是其各比特得分之和。这种设计使得DocID的语义来自模型本身的表示，而非完全依赖索引训练时记忆的令牌嵌入。因此，对于未见文档，只要其表示能被模型计算，对应DocID即可被评分，无需重新训练索引阶段的DocID分配。训练目标沿用生成式检索的序列生成损失，但DocID空间的构建方式改变了泛化特性。推理时，模型按比特因子化的方式评分候选DocID，束搜索在更小的词汇表（比特级）上进行，从而减少解码步骤，降低延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Hit@10 (unseen documents)", "value": "提升超过20个百分点", "setting": "NQ320K 和 TriviaQA 数据集，相较于现有数值DocID设计"}, {"metric": "推理延迟", "value": "降低至多7倍", "setting": "NQ320K 和 TriviaQA 数据集，相较于现有数值DocID设计"}, {"metric": "性能下降", "value": "低于权重更新方法约3个百分点", "setting": "MS MARCO 数据集，无需额外训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出比特因子化数值DocID：每个DocID由比特因子化令牌序列组成，每个令牌的得分是其比特得分之和。；每个比特的含义源自语言模型表示，而非仅依赖索引训练时记忆的嵌入，使得未见文档的DocID无需额外训练即可评分。；在保持已见文档检索性能的同时，大幅提升未见文档检索效果并降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Generative retrieval is known for its efficient end-to-end indexing and retrieval. In particular, numeric Document Identifiers (DocIDs) are generally more efficient because they are shorter and generally fixed-length sequences over a small vocabulary, requiring fewer decoding steps during beam search. However, they tend to generalize poorly to unseen documents and must be learned during indexing-stage training. In this paper, we propose a numeric DocID design for generative retrieval that enables better adaptation to unseen documents without this http URL DocID design inherits the language model's knowledge: each DocID is a sequence of tokens factorized into bits, and each token is scored as the sum of its bits. Unlike existing numeric DocIDs, whose tokens carry meaning only through embeddings memorized during indexing training, each bit here derives its meaning from the model's representation. Therefore, the DocIDs of unseen documents remain scorable without this http URL on three benchmarks show that our DocID design substantially improves unseen-document retrieval while maintaining comparable performance on seen documents. It achieves over 20 percentage points higher Hit@10 on unseen documents and up to 7 times lower inference latency than existing numeric DocID designs on NQ320K and TriviaQA, while performing only 3 percentage points below weight-update methods on MS MARCO without additional training. These results show that an appropriate DocID design can heavily reduce retraining costs when adapting generative retrievers to new documents.

</details>


### [297] [Structural Segmentation for Brazilian Legal Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18622)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,João Alberto de Oliveira Lima,Hudson de Martim,Eduardo Seiti de Oliveira,Roberto Lotufo,Jayr Pereira*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究探索结构化分割作为长法律文档的表示策略，通过多层层级分块与核心块加元数据等四种策略，在巴西法律数据集上验证了结构化分割能改善稠密检索但未必改善词法检索，且完整层级并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长法律文档作为单一检索单元会稀释相关内容并限制检索效果，如何通过结构化分割改进巴西法律文档检索。

Method: 论文将每个法律文档表示为一组块（chunks），利用法律行为的层级结构。评估四种表示策略：1）多层层级表示，将文档按层级切分为多个块并保留层级关系；2）简化版本基于核心块，即条款（articles）和非规范性文档组件，并附加不同粒度的上下文信息。检索时分别使用词法模型和稠密模型对块进行索引与查询匹配。目标是通过结构化分块避免长文档内容稀释，提升相关块被召回的几率。实验在两个公开的巴西法律数据集（规范性法案和立法提案）上进行，对比全文索引基线，报告稠密检索在有分割时改善，词法检索无一致提升；且核心块加元数据即可达到与完整层级相当或更好的效果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索效果（未指明具体指标）", "value": "结构化分割相比全文索引改善稠密检索", "setting": "两个公开巴西法律数据集（规范性法案与立法提案），词法与稠密检索模型"}, {"metric": "检索效果（未指明具体指标）", "value": "对词法检索无一致提升", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于法律行为层级结构的多层文档表示与分块策略；比较四种表示策略：多层完整层级、核心块（条款及非规范性组件）及其不同上下文变体；发现核心块加文档元数据即可达到与完整层级相当或更好的效果，无需完整层级

Abstract: Long legal documents are often represented as single retrieval units, which can dilute relevant content and limit retrieval effectiveness. This study investigates structural segmentation as a document representation strategy to improve Brazilian legal document retrieval. Using the hierarchical structure of legal acts, we represent each document as a collection of chunks. We evaluate four representation strategies: a multi-layered hierarchy representation and three simplified versions based on core chunks (articles and non-normative document components), with varying contextual details. We test these strategies on two publicly available Brazilian legal datasets containing normative acts and legislative proposals, using both lexical and dense retrieval models. Results show that structural segmentation improves dense retrieval compared with whole-document indexing, despite substantially increasing the number of indexed units. However, it does not consistently benefit lexical retrieval. Finally, we find that full hierarchicy representation is not necessary: core chunks enriched with document metadata yield comparable or better results.

</details>


### [298] [AiSAQ-in-Filtering for DRAM-free Filtered Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17627)
*Kento Tatsuno,Daisuke Miyashita,Kazunari Sumiyoshi,Hiroaki Yamanaka,Kosuke Nakata,Jun Deguchi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Kioxia 提出 AiSAQ-in-Filtering，通过 LiPQ 将标签与向量联合量化，在不增加编码数据量的前提下实现无 DRAM 过滤式向量检索，单标签召回@1 最高提升 55%，DRAM 占用最多降至 1/78，并减少磁盘 I/O，可扩展至稀疏-稠密混合检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤式 ANN 搜索中同时存储向量数据及其标签导致 DRAM 占用随数据集增长而显著增加，需要在低内存/无 DRAM 条件下保持高召回并减少磁盘 I/O。

Method: 论文提出 AiSAQ-in-Filtering，在无 DRAM 的近似最近邻搜索框架 AiSAQ 上引入标签过滤能力。核心模块为 LiPQ（Label-inclusive Product Quantization），将非结构化向量数据与结构化标签信息合并为单一紧凑表示进行乘积量化编码，使编码数据量不随标签额外增长，从而避免存储向量与标签双重内存开销。检索时利用该联合编码同时完成向量相似度搜索与标签过滤，减少磁盘 I/O 并提高单标签搜索召回。系统保留 AiSAQ 的无 DRAM 检索特性，支持索引切换时间减少，并可扩展至稀疏-稠密混合检索（如语义与关键词检索）。摘要未披露具体网络结构、训练目标、损失函数及端到端数据流细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall@1", "value": "提升最多 55%", "setting": "单标签过滤搜索，与现有方法对比"}, {"metric": "DRAM footprint", "value": "降低最多 1/78", "setting": "保留 AiSAQ 主要优势，与现有方法对比"}, {"metric": "disk I/Os", "value": "更少（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "过滤式搜索，与现有方法对比"}, {"metric": "index switching time", "value": "更短（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "继承 AiSAQ 优势，与现有方法对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LiPQ（Label-inclusive Product Quantization）：将非结构化向量与结构化标签合并为单一表示进行量化，不增加编码数据量；AiSAQ-in-Filtering：在 AiSAQ 无 DRAM 检索框架上集成标签过滤，支持单标签过滤搜索；可扩展到稀疏-稠密混合检索（语义与关键词搜索）场景

Abstract: Filtering search is one of the crucial applications of approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS). In filtering search, label information is used in conjunction with unstructured vector data. ANNS provides efficient similarity search in large-scale datasets, however, the memory footprint of storing both vector data and their associated label becomes a significant cost factor as dataset sizes grow. To address this problem of increasing DRAM usage, we propose AiSAQ-in-Filtering, with a novel quantization method called LiPQ (Label-inclusive Product Quantization). LiPQ combines unstructured data and structured labels into a single representation, leading no growth of encoded data. Our proposal increased recall@1 of single-label searches by up to 55% and fewer disk I/Os compared with existing methods. This also retains the main advantages of AiSAQ, which can reduce DRAM footprint by up to 1/78 and index switching time comparing with existing methods. This proposal can also be applied to sparse-dense hybrid search beyond label filtering such as semantic and keyword search of documents.

</details>


### [299] [HASTE: Hierarchical and Attribute-aware Supervision for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17368)
*Tripti Shukla,Shwetha Somasundaram,Akshat Gupta,Rohit Garg,Paul Duff,Ian Anderson*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HASTE提出在生成式推荐中引入层级与属性级监督的统一训练框架，通过Hierarchical Loss在语义ID各层级施加监督使收敛约快2倍，并通过Attribute-Specific Losses利用语言模型语义知识改善长尾物品表示，在时尚推荐与Amazon多领域数据上提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型以标准因果交叉熵优化语义ID预测，未显式利用语义ID的层级结构，训练收敛慢；数据稀疏的长尾物品因监督不足导致表示质量差，长尾推荐性能弱。

Method: HASTE面向以语义ID表示物品并自回归生成语义ID的生成式推荐模型，在标准因果交叉熵训练目标之上引入两路监督。输入为物品的语义ID序列及其属性信息；模型自回归生成语义ID。Hierarchical Loss在语义ID的每一层级提供监督，使模型显式学习物品的层级结构，替代仅优化最终ID预测的标准目标，从而加速训练收敛。Attribute-Specific Losses要求模型在生成过程中保留细粒度物品属性，借助语言模型内在语义知识改善物品表示，尤其针对数据稀疏的长尾物品。训练目标为原生成损失与层级损失、属性损失的联合优化。推理时仍按自回归方式生成语义ID进行推荐，不改变服务路径。实验在时尚推荐基准与Amazon多领域数据集上验证跨数据集、跨物品频率区间的效果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练收敛速度", "value": "约2倍快于标准因果交叉熵目标", "setting": "Hierarchical Loss，时尚推荐与Amazon多领域数据集，离线实验"}, {"metric": "推荐性能", "value": "在多个数据集和物品频率区间上均有提升", "setting": "时尚推荐基准与Amazon多领域数据集，离线实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出统一训练框架HASTE，在生成式推荐中显式引入层级与属性级监督。；设计Hierarchical Loss，在语义ID的每一层级提供监督，显式利用物品层级结构，加速训练收敛。；设计Attribute-Specific Losses，鼓励模型保留细粒度物品属性，利用语言模型固有语义知识改善长尾物品表示与推荐。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models have shown promising results by representing items as semantic IDs and generating these IDs autoregressively. We introduce an approach showing that explicitly incorporating hierarchical and attribute-level supervision into generative recommendation can simultaneously improve training efficiency and long-tail recommendation performance. Existing approaches typically optimize semantic ID prediction with a standard causal cross-entropy objective, which does not explicitly exploit the hierarchical structure of semantic IDs and can require substantial training to learn effective item representations. Moreover, data-sparse tail items often suffer from poor representations due to limited supervision, resulting in weaker long-tail recommendation performance. We address these challenges with HASTE (Hierarchical and Attribute-aware Supervision for generaTive rEcommendation), a unified training framework that introduces hierarchical and attribute-specific supervision. HASTE's Hierarchical Loss explicitly exploits the multi-level structure of semantic IDs, providing supervision at each level of the item hierarchy and substantially accelerating training convergence. Its Attribute-Specific Losses encourage the model to preserve fine-grained item attributes, leveraging the language model's inherent semantic knowledge to improve representations and recommendations for tail items. We extensively evaluate HASTE on a fashion recommendation benchmark and across multiple domains of the Amazon dataset. HASTE improves recommendation performance across datasets and item-frequency regimes. Notably, the Hierarchical Loss achieves approximately 2x faster convergence than the standard causal cross-entropy objective, substantially improving training efficiency while maintaining strong recommendation quality.

</details>


### [300] [From a Large-Scale Teacher to Efficient Multimodal Students for Search Relevance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16569)
*Tomasz Gniazdowski,Mohammadreza Momenifar,Nadia Vase,Shahram Khadivi,Wiktor Walentynowicz,Nicola Ueffing,Jan Pawlowski,Piotr Szymanski,Abraham Bagherjeiran*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: eBay提出从大规模LLM教师到高效多模态学生模型的蒸馏框架，用于电商搜索相关性预测。先构建小规模人工标注真值，再通过LLM-as-a-judge生成大规模标签，最终训练紧凑多模态学生模型，在保持接近教师性能的同时显著改善延迟、成本和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工相关性标注昂贵、缓慢、难以扩展；大型商业LLM教师系统推理成本高、吞吐受限，难以在实际生产环境中部署。

Method: 该框架包含三阶段。第一，人工标注少量高质量查询-商品相关性数据，作为评估与校准的基准真值。第二，基于该人工数据集，通过提示工程和迭代优化开发LLM-as-a-judge流水线，输入查询和商品信息（含多模态信号），输出相关性判断；该教师系统被用于大规模生成相关性标签。第三，以生成的标签为监督信号，训练紧凑的多模态学生模型；学生模型接收相同的查询-商品输入，输出相关性预测。训练目标为使学生模型逼近教师系统给出的标签分布或判断。推理阶段部署学生模型，替代原本需调用大型商业LLM的教师系统，以减少延迟、降低成本并提升吞吐，从而满足生产环境对相关性的实时预测需求。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建小型高质量人工标注数据集作为可靠的真值基准；通过精细提示工程与迭代优化开发可靠的LLM-as-a-judge相关性判断流水线；利用该流水线生成大规模相关性标签作为训练数据；将LLM教师蒸馏为紧凑的多模态学生模型，在保持接近教师性能的同时显著降低延迟、成本并提高可扩展性

Abstract: Relevance prediction between user queries and items is a fundamental component of e-commerce search systems. Traditionally, high-quality relevance labels are obtained through human judgment, which is reliable but expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable automated relevance judgment at scale, achieving performance comparable to human annotators. However, such systems often rely on large commercial LLMs with complex prompts, resulting in high inference costs and limited throughput. In this work, we propose a framework that bridges this gap by distilling a high-performing LLM-based relevance judgment system into efficient multimodal student models. We first curate a small, high-quality human-annotated dataset to serve as ground truth. Using this dataset, we develop a reliable LLM-as-a-judge pipeline through careful prompt engineering and iterative refinement. This pipeline is then used to generate large-scale relevance labels, which serve as training data for compact multimodal models. Our distilled models achieve performance close to the teacher system while significantly improving latency, cost, and scalability. This approach enables practical deployment of high-quality relevance prediction systems in production environments.

</details>


### [301] [Tackling the Modality Inhibitance Effect with Wavelet-based Modality Integration in Multi-modal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16229)
*Rongqing Kenneth Ong,Andy W. H. Khong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态推荐系统中双模态转三模态时性能下降的模态抑制效应，提出 MMWave 模型，通过小波多尺度分解、结构感知图学习和自适应模态调节实现有效融合，在四个真实数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统从双模态扩展到三模态时，新模态引入的模态特异性污染和信息冗余导致现有融合方法性能显著下降，即模态抑制效应。

Method: MMWave 包含三个主要模块。首先，多尺度模态小波模块对各模态（如图像、文本、音频）的原始特征进行小波分解，从时间与频率两个尺度提取多尺度特征表示，过滤模态特异性污染并去除冗余信息，保留各模态的本质特征。其次，结构感知多模态图学习模块将物品的模态特征构建为图结构，通过图神经网络捕获物品之间的高阶潜在结构关联，增强物品表示。最后，自适应模态调节器采用跨权重集成策略，动态计算并调整不同模态特征在最终表示中的相对权重，实现有效融合。三个模块按顺序处理：小波分解→图学习→自适应融合，最终生成用于推荐预测的物品表示。论文在四个真实数据集上进行实验验证，但摘要未披露具体模型规模、训练资源、推理资源、离线指标数值以及在线 A/B 结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于小波分解的多尺度模态小波（MMWave）模块，从时频视角提取各模态的非冗余本质特征并抑制模态噪声；设计结构感知多模态图学习模块，捕获物品模态特征间的高阶潜在结构关联；提出自适应模态调节器，采用跨权重集成策略动态调制各模态特征的相对贡献

Abstract: Harnessing rich modality features has recently gained prominence in multi-modal recommendation systems (MRSs). To delineate user modality preferences, existing works focus on modality fusion, which can be achieved via attentive summation or concatenation. In this work, we introduce the modality inhibitance effect - a phenomenon which limits existing MRSs when these models transit from dual-modal to tri-modal configurations resulting in significant performance degradation. In particular, apart from the modal-specific contamination encapsulated in the newly introduced modality, the inclusion of new features results in excessive informational redundancy, thereby severely impeding effective integration within current approaches. To address the modality inhibitance effect, we propose a new multi-scale modality wavelet (MMWave) multi-modal recommender, which aims to fully capitalize on essential modality features and suppress modal-specific noise. This is acheived by employing wavelet decomposition from a multi-scale (time \& frequency) perspective, ensuring that only essential (non-redundant) features of each modality are extracted. We then account for the latent associations between item modality features by designing a new structure-aware multi-modal graph learning module that captures high-order latent structural information pertaining to items. To modulate the relative contributions of modality features and achieve effective integration, we then propose a new adaptive modality regulator component that employs a new cross-weight integration strategy. Experiments conducted on four real-world datasets validate the efficacy of our proposed model. The source code for this work has been made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [302] [Recall Begets Recall: Recurrent Memory Ranking for Personalized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16137)
*Pengyue Jia,Shida Yan,Shunyu Liu,Yingyi Zhang,Leszek Rutkowski,Chenyang Lyu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReMind提出一种循环自终止的记忆排序方法，通过复合线索逐步细化查询并利用充分性预测器动态停止，实验表明在公开基准上优于最先进的记忆排序器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆排序器单次评分并在固定top-K截断，无法捕捉需要多步推理才能关联的记忆，也无法根据查询需求动态调整所选记忆数量。

Method: ReMind是一个循环且自终止的记忆排序器。输入为查询和检索器返回的候选记忆集合。在每一步迭代中，系统将当前已选中的顶级记忆附加到查询上下文中形成扩展线索，然后使用该扩展线索对剩余候选记忆重新打分和排序，选出新的最高排名记忆加入已选集合。同时，一个充分性预测器在每一步评估已收集记忆是否足以回答查询：若充分则停止循环，否则继续下一轮。最终，已选记忆被输入下游个性化LLM以生成有依据的回答。训练目标可能涉及充分性预测器的监督学习，但论文未详细披露。推理时，循环过程自适应地决定选择记忆的数量，而非固定top-K。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 复合线索（compound cueing）：每一步将已选最高排名记忆加入查询上下文，扩大线索后对剩余候选重新排序，使后续步骤聚焦于还缺失的信息。；元记忆监控（metamemory monitoring）：通过充分性预测器判断已收集记忆是否足以回答查询，从而动态终止循环，自适应确定记忆数量。；循环自终止的记忆排序框架ReMind：将记忆排序建模为迭代检索过程，模拟认知理论中的记忆搜索机制。

Abstract: Memory ranking is the stage in personalized Large Language Models (LLMs) that decides which of the candidate memories returned by a retriever enter the limited context, so that the answer is grounded in the right parts of the user's history. Existing memory rankers operate in a single pass, scoring each candidate once against the raw query and cutting the ranked list at a fixed top-K. The single pass misses memories that the answer needs but that look unrelated to the query on their own, and the fixed cut ignores how many memories the query needs. To address both problems, we propose ReMind, a memory ranker that runs as a recurrent and self-terminating loop, following the cognitive theory of memory search: each recalled item joins the cue for the next recall (compound cueing), and recall stops once enough has been collected for the task (metamemory monitoring). For compound cueing, ReMind adds the top-ranked memory to the query context at each step and reranks the remaining candidates under the expanded cue, so that each recall makes the cue more specific and later steps look for what is still missing rather than for what merely resembles the query. For metamemory monitoring, a sufficiency predictor estimates at each step whether the collected memories are enough to answer the query and stops the loop once they are, so that the number of selected memories adapts to each query. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ReMind achieves the best results compared to state-of-the-art memory rankers.

</details>


### [303] [UniSGR-V2: Stepwise Target-Side Feature Anticipation and Crossing for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16132)
*Jun Yang,Jiasi Chen,Jiawei Sun,Ziyue Guo,Jianan Yan,Tao Luo,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TacGR，在生成式推荐的每个解码步骤预测目标侧连续特征并与语义前缀交叉，解决目标侧特征真空问题，在Lazada线上A/B测试中提升IPV 0.79%、订单5.55%、GMV 3.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐在解码时无法直接访问目标物品的丰富侧特征，导致目标侧特征真空，加剧条件熵高和级联错误，且最终排序时缺少目标侧特征交叉。

Method: TacGR在语义ID自回归解码框架中，每个解码步骤前，利用当前语义前缀和用户行为序列预测一个连续的目标侧特征向量。该向量与语义前缀及用户行为序列表示在解码器内部进行交叉，产生的交叉表示用于条件化当前码字的生成，并直接用于排序。为应对连续向量回归中目标坍塌问题，设计语义锚定辅助塔，稳定预测目标的表示；为避免预测噪声通过注意力传播到生成，使用单向可见性掩码限制信息流。对于超长工业行为序列，引入门控卷积去噪模块抑制噪声，并采用金字塔记忆漏斗适配推理以降低计算成本。整体架构端到端训练，目标函数同时优化生成损失和特征预测损失，推理时按步骤预测特征并交叉，最终生成推荐结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "IPV", "value": "0.79%", "setting": "Lazada线上A/B测试"}, {"metric": "订单", "value": "5.55%", "setting": "Lazada线上A/B测试"}, {"metric": "GMV", "value": "3.09%", "setting": "Lazada线上A/B测试"}]}

Conclusion: 提出目标侧特征预期（TacGR），在解码每个码字前预测连续目标侧特征向量，并与语义前缀和用户行为序列在解码器内交叉；引入语义锚定辅助塔缓解连续向量回归中预测与目标坍塌的问题；采用单向可见性掩码防止预测噪声通过注意力传播到生成过程；针对超长工业行为序列，设计门控卷积去噪抑制噪声，并适配金字塔记忆漏斗降低推理成本

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommendation unifies retrieval and ranking through autoregressive decoding but differs structurally from discriminative models, which can directly access rich target-item side features. The attributes are compressed into a finite codebook during quantization, while the target item remains undetermined during decoding and its features cannot be looked up. This target-side feature vacuum weakens generation at each semantic-ID level through high conditional entropy and cascading errors, and final ranking through missing target-side feature crossing. Existing methods interact with candidate representations or generate discrete attribute tokens, but none reconstructs a continuous target-side representation inside decoding to support both stepwise generation and ranking. We propose TacGR (Stepwise Target-Side Feature Anticipation and Crossing for Generative Recommendation). Before decoding each codeword, TacGR anticipates a continuous target-side feature vector and crosses it with the semantic prefix and user behavior sequence inside the decoder. The crossed representation conditions current-level generation and is directly used for ranking. Continuous-vector regression risks a learnable target collapsing with its prediction and prediction noise propagating into generation through attention. TacGR addresses these challenges with a semantic-anchoring auxiliary tower and a unidirectional visibility mask, respectively. For extremely long industrial behavior histories, it introduces gated convolutional denoising to suppress noise and adapts the pyramid memory funnel to reduce inference cost. Offline experiments on a large-scale Lazada dataset show significant gains over OneRec, UniRec, and UniSGR. Online A/B testing improves IPV, Order, and GMV by 0.79%, 5.55%, and 3.09%, respectively.

</details>


### [304] [OT-FlowRec: Optimal Transport-Guided Multi-Bridge Flow for Multimodal Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16038)
*Miao Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OT-FlowRec 提出基于最优传输引导的多桥流匹配框架，通过并行兴趣特定传输流建模多中心用户兴趣分布，并设计几何传输机制与渐进时间采样预热策略，在多个公开数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multimodal sequential recommendation methods using global attention inadequately capture concurrent multi-interest behaviors and fail to model multi-centric distribution of user intent.

Method: OT-FlowRec constructs a mixture of parallel, interest-specific transport flows to model multi-centric user interest distributions. Each flow preserves a distinct user interest cluster via an optimal transport plan. A geometric transport mechanism establishes minimum-cost paths from the latent multi-interest distribution to target preference prototypes. A progressive time-sampling warmup strategy dynamically biases optimization weights toward the final states of the generation process, bridging continuous global distribution fitting and practical top-k ranking metrics. The approach achieves explicit interest disentanglement, preserving multi-peaked preference representation instead of collapsing into a unimodal average. Experiments on three public datasets show outperformance over state-of-the-art baselines.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Optimal transport-guided multi-bridge flow matching framework for multimodal sequential recommendation；Mixture of parallel interest-specific transport flows to model multi-centric distribution；Geometric transport mechanism establishing minimum-cost paths from latent multi-interest distribution to target preference prototypes；Progressive time-sampling warmup strategy dynamically biasing optimization toward final states, bridging continuous distribution fitting and top-k ranking metrics

Abstract: E-commerce user preferences evolve dynamically within sessions, exhibiting concurrent and multi-centric interest distributions. While existing multimodal sequential recommendation methods leverage global attention to compress historical interactions, they inadequately capture the concurrent nature of multi-interest behaviors and fail to model the underlying multi-centric distribution of user intent. To tackle this issue, we propose OT-FlowRec, an optimal transport-guided multi-bridge flow matching framework that explicitly models multi-interest distributions to facilitate multimodal sequential recommendation. Unlike single-path generative models, OT-FlowRec constructs a mixture of parallel, interest-specific transport flows to model the multi-centric distribution characteristics, where each flow preserves a distinct user interest cluster via an optimal transport plan. Then, a geometric transport mechanism is designed to establish minimum-cost paths from the latent multi-interest distribution to target preference prototypes to provide multi-interest awareness. Moreover, a progressive time-sampling warmup strategy dynamically biases optimization weights toward the final states of the generation process, bridging the gap between continuous global distribution fitting and practical top-$k$ ranking metrics. This approach achieves explicit interest disentanglement, ensuring the preservation of a multi-peaked preference representation instead of collapsing it into a unimodal average. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that OT-FlowRec outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [305] [KhyontekAI at CheckThat! 2026: Multilingual Dense Retrieval and Cross-Encoder Reranking for Scientific Web Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15837)
*Bhargab Jyoti Bhuyan,Pritam Deka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向科学网络声明的多语言两阶段检索系统，结合微调稠密检索与交叉编码器重排，并系统评估多语言联合训练、元数据增强等策略，在 CLEF 2026 CheckThat! 任务上取得平均 MRR@5 0.5862 的离线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动为社交媒体中的多语言科学声明检索正确的科学文献来源，需要处理语义匹配、改写、噪声社交媒体文本以及跨域语言差异等挑战。

Method: 系统采用两阶段检索框架：第一阶段使用微调的稠密检索模型（包括多语言-E5、BGE、Contriever）将科学声明和科学文档分别编码为稠密向量，通过向量相似度进行候选召回；第二阶段使用交叉编码器对召回的候选文档与查询进行细粒度交互重排，以提升检索精度。训练中探索多语言联合训练、检索深度分析、元数据增强（将文档元数据融入编码或检索过程）以及语言平衡策略。推理时先进行稠密召回，再对排序靠前的候选进行交叉编码器重排，最终输出排序结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MRR@5", "value": "0.6222", "setting": "英语，最终多语言系统"}, {"metric": "MRR@5", "value": "0.5112", "setting": "德语，最终多语言系统"}, {"metric": "MRR@5", "value": "0.6254", "setting": "法语，最终多语言系统"}, {"metric": "MRR@5", "value": "0.5862", "setting": "多语言平均，最终多语言系统"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 多语言两阶段检索框架：微调稠密检索模型进行候选召回，再用交叉编码器重排提升精度。；对多语言-E5、BGE、Contriever 等稠密检索架构在不同元数据配置下进行系统评估。；研究多语言联合训练、检索深度分析、元数据增强和语言平衡策略对检索效果的影响。

Abstract: Scientific claims shared through social media platforms frequently reference research findings without explicitly linking to the corresponding scientific publications. Automatically retrieving the correct scientific source for such claims is a challenging multilingual retrieval problem involving semantic matching, paraphrasing, noisy social media text, and cross-domain language variation. In this work, we present a multilingual two-stage retrieval framework for CLEF 2026 CheckThat! Task 1 on Source Retrieval for Scientific Web Claims. Our system combines fine-tuned dense retrieval models with cross-encoder reranking for improving multilingual scientific document retrieval. We evaluate multiple transformer-based dense retrieval architectures including multilingual-E5, BGE, and Contriever under different metadata configurations. We further investigate multilingual joint training, retrieval depth analysis, metadata enrichment, and language balancing strategies for improving retrieval effectiveness. Experimental results demonstrate that metadata enrichment consistently improves retrieval effectiveness, while fine-tuned cross-encoder reranking provides additional gains in retrieval precision. Our final multilingual system achieves MRR@5 scores of 0.6222 for English, 0.5112 for German, and 0.6254 for French, with an overall average MRR@5 score of 0.5862.

</details>
